Chapter 901: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – Impossible to Stop Halfway

Bunches of water arrows shot, hail fell like rain, while simultaneously, all kinds of summons wreaked havoc across the ship decks.

The three magicians poured down their firepower in torrents.

The navy’s offensive was gravely hindered, and due to the magical bombardment, many people were wounded and forced to retreat, while others even lost their lives.

By contrast, despite bathing in the magic, the mercenaries were unharmed.

Divine spell [Mark Evil]!

As it turns out, Chi Lai had used his divine spell on the new silver level reinforcements, so the three magicians didn’t harm the mercenaries.

The three silver level magicians were able to fully display their skills.

It had to be said that the divine spell [Mark Evil] was really off the charts practical.

“Damn Chi Lai, I wish I killed him sooner!”

“We also have priests, what are we afraid of?”

“Shut up, you idiot!”

The navies were quite sullen.

They obviously had priests on their ships, but they couldn’t be used. Normal pirates almost never had priests, and if they appeared, it would provoke immense suspicions.

The navies sacrificed more and more.

When Tan Mo saw this, he once again sent Snow Bird Harbor City Lord an urgent communication, asking for the latter’s help.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stated his hands were tied.

“Sly snow elf!” Tan Mo cursed to himself.

He had information he could blackmail Snow Bird Harbor City Lord with, yet he only had himself to blame for the magic contract he signed with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

There was actually nothing he could do.

If a magic contract wasn’t signed, Tan Mo would learn Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had secretly killed [Gold Hand], while Snow Bird Harbor City Lord suspected Tan Mo had killed Hun Tong to silence the latter.

Both had done dirty deeds, and without an extremely strict contract between them, it would be impossible for them either to trust or work with each other.

Only by signing a contract of collaboration could their following plan feasibly reach this point.

“Worthy of being an arms merchant backer, those silver level magicians’ equipment is quite excellent!” Tan Mo huffed with rage.

The three silver level magicians’ equipment obviously surpassed the navies’

To make their disguise true to life, the navies gave up their regular armaments and were unable to use the battle skills, formations, and other military skills they were most familiar with.

These accursed mercenaries, damn it, damn it all!”

“No matter, send over the priests now, we cannot stare blankly and watch our comrades die!”

“Patience, in this situation, his Lordship will certainly act.”

The navy officers and soldiers fighting hard were also being struck quite sullenly.

They were soaked in blood from battle, and many were gravely wounded. Obviously they had better skills they could use, but they couldn’t.

Military orders must be obeyed!

The imperial regular navy had very strong military discipline.

Therefore, they obeyed military orders and deeply understood the grave consequences of violating military orders.

Thus, they got more furious, and most of that rage was stored in their thoughts.

Their rage was no match for the silver level magicians’ firepower.

The three silver levels used water, summoning, and ice as their main offensive methods. Their fighting styles were very similar, and all of them emitted a whiff of insanity, and were undaunted by danger.

Even when silver level navy fighters got near, they would use attacks as their defense. Even when the navy concentrated their long range firepower on them, they would seldom evade on the deck, and instead swim through the air constantly and bombard them back with all kinds of spells.

This kind of movement fought on the edge of life and death, fully exposing their utterly fearless fighting courage that greatly encouraged the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps members.

Seeing these fragile magicians all dashing in bravely, what qualifications did nobodies like them have to cower at the back?

The power of role models on the battlefield was considerably immense.

“I didn’t think our corps had such a formidable force in hiding.” Men Shi and Chi Lai fought back to back against the five silver levels, fighting ever braver.

“We can win; we must win!” Bai Ya was heroically fighting when he was suddenly dragged away.

A moment later, a navy machete chopped down where Bai Ya was, splitting empty air.

Bai Ya’s belated reaction was to coldly sweat in fright, and he suddenly turned around to see his life saving benefactor – Xu Ma.

The one who pulled him back, Xu Ma, replied, “Boy, you need to be observant in battle.”

Bai Ya’s eyes suddenly widened, “Be careful!”

A moment later, he swung his sleeve, a long grass flinging from his sleeve.

The long grass fell onto the navy person, then suddenly became slender. The grass tip turned into a snake head and the grass roots turned into a snake tail.

The snake’s head bit down!

The navy person was caught off guard, and after being bitten, they couldn’t help but scream.

The snake’s body seized the opportunity to twist around the navy person’s sternum, crushing it.

The snake grass!

Bai Ya had purchased this peculiar plant on Two Eyes Island.

Under Bai Ya’s utmost care and special training, he was able to use it against enemies.

Xu Ma had saved Bai Ya, and in the blink of an eye, Bai Ya had saved him.

Xu Ma couldn’t help but laugh and clap Bai Ya’s shoulder.

The young man he had intentionally or otherwise trained, had grown into a reliable partner that aided him.

Mu Ban hid in the corner of a room, his hands tenaciously holding a crossbow.

As an ordinary person, he couldn’t defend himself that much, and thus, he was very nervous.

But of all things, he heard a series of footsteps.

“Quickly, the battlefield situation is amiss, we need to bomb the magic power reservoir immediately.”

“I didn’t expect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to be so tenacious!”

“Damned military training is perpetual, endless, and painful, but it is very effective.”

A group of mercenaries quickly charged ahead.

They were ordered by Snow Bird City Lord to infiltrate the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Although Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stated his hands were tied to Tan Mo, he had secretly transmitted an order to mobilize these undiscovered traitors.

“Mu Ban, you can do it, you can do it! You could take on Mystifying Monster Island; this time you can do it too!” Mu Ban made an effort to encourage himself out of his nervousness.

A moment later, he took in a deep breath, and without sticking out his head, he fired the crossbow.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh.

The alchemy crossbow shot.

A dozen alchemy arrows of different attributes fired within a few seconds.

Screams arose continuously.

Then, the ship hold quickly grew silent.

After a good while, Mu Ban raised his trembling head and saw a corpse.

“I killed an enemy…oh!”

His eyes widened, astonished to see the corpse of “one of his own”.

“I, I killed the wrong person?!” Mu Ban’s eyes instantly widened as he suffered an enormous psychological attack.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ morale was constantly rising.

This was actually difficult. Pirates and mercenaries rarely had strong military accomplishments, and most of them were only good at fighting winning battles.

Tan Mo took a deep breath, the battle reaching this level had indeed stemmed from his expectations and it was impossible to stop halfway.

The navy officers’ and soldiers’ losses grew greater, and the growing amount was harder for them to bear.

Too many of the lower ranks dying in battle would destabilize his authority. Even if he could explain it away to his higherups with pirates, too many losses was out of the question.

Because he was doing illegal work.

The more naval personnel lost during this illegal work, the more doubts and hesitation they would have in future illegal work.

This would enormously increase Tan Mo’s commanding costs.

“Don’t tell me I have to personally act?” Tan Mo’s heart chilled.

His battle determination was not strong.

Like in the deep sea when Da Han and Qian Xing encountered Zhou Zhang and Teacher Si Ling, they decided to retreat as their battle determination was very feeble.

This was because their objective was merely to save Ba Qi and to get rich. When they noticed they would have to fight to the death, they naturally wanted nothing to do with it.

The gap between mental expectations and reality was too vast!

Now, Tan Mo’s side was in that kind of situation.

They originally wanted to get rich and didn’t think of fighting like their lives were on the line.

“These accursed mercenaries, why won’t you obediently let me rob you?”

“Resist? I want to see what you’ll use to resist me!!”

Tan Mo was entangled in his tempestuous fury, and he disguised himself and snuck into the battlefield.

He suddenly ambushed a Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps silver level, killing them on the spot.

The gold level aura suddenly leaking caused the navy’s morale and battle intent to surge.

By contrast, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ soaring morale was suddenly slammed and began to decline again.

After all, this was the world of the strong.

“A gold level pirate! He really exists.” Tripleblade’s heart couldn’t help but jump.

The thing he was most worried about had come to pass, but what could he do? He could only place his hopes in the “unreliable” Commander Long Fu.

Tan Mo disguised himself, and after killing a Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps silver level, decided to not charge at the flagship, but instead fly to the front lines all over the place.

He was also worried about Zong Ge’s one vs three method.

As a gold level power, even if he didn’t overtly use his equipment and battle skills, he possessed overwhelming dominance.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ silver levels on the front lines retreated in defeat or were slain.

It was rare for silver levels to withstand gold levels.

If it were common, then the dragonman youngster’s slaying of Teng Dong Lang would not set the entire nation aflame.

It was because it was so rare that the dragonman youngster’s reputation exploded and his name widely circulated.

“Want me to move?” Seeing Tan Mo devastate the battlefield, Hun Tong couldn’t help but say that to the dragonman youngster.

At that moment, on top of a three story building, the dragonman youngster and Hun Tong were camouflaged by a divine spell, no one could perceive them.

As for Dao Hen, he remained in the Ice Sculpture Capital.

After learning how to use the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] and the divine spell [Divine Domain Descent], the dragonman youngster knew how crucial believers were and that each qualified believer could be regarded as a portal.

The dragonman youngster teleported the vast distance between Ocean Mother Nest and the Ice Sculpture Capital through Divine Descent Domain and built a channel.

This time, he had returned to Snow Bird Harbor through the same method.

The believer who provided the coordinates was not Dao Hen, but rather the big guy.

Although the big guy did not believe in Charming Blue Goddess, it didn’t matter. So long as he provided the crucial coordinate, then divine spell [Divine Domain Descent] could be implemented successfully.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could never have expected that the saint seed he believed his exclusive property he did everything he could to hide would instead invite a gigantic calamity into his domain.

Of course, before teleportation, the dragonman youngster used many Charming Blue divine spells to camouflage the big guy and deceive everything around him.

Chapter 902: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – The Young Man’s Changes

The dragonman youngster teleported over, prepared for the worst battle possible, but the result was smooth winds and currents, with no twists and turns.

No one noticed their teleportation.

The dragonman youngster was also familiar with the place the big guy was locked up in.

He had once gone deep into the city government palace’s underground dungeon.

After getting out, he and Hun Tong immediately came to the port to observe the battlefield.

He didn’t act directly, instead he chose to do something else.

That bizarre and sudden alarm was caused by him.

But because he used a deception divine spell, no one present noticed where this alarm was coming from.

From this, it could be seen that when facing first class opponents like the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo, Charming Blue divine spells still possessed an extremely powerful practical nature. As for the Li Jian Clan noticing Zi Di’s shenanigans, it was not them seeing through Deceptive Disguise, rather it was them relying on their monitoring ability, where they noticed the flaws between the two “Long Fus”.

In military affairs, the dragonman youngster naturally couldn’t compare to Zong Ge, but through many large scale battles, he had practical experience.

The dragonman youngster watched on the sidelines, gratified to find that his mercenaries’ military qualities had obviously improved since his departure.

Of course, the regular navy had higher military accomplishments, and this led to the dragonman youngster’s advance warning not doing much.

But on the other hand, he did gain something,

Through divine spells and constant magical observation, he saw many hidden traitors and spies laid bare.

He also deployed an alchemy item to record these scenes, so that after this battle finished, he could settle accounts.

“Our ships were repaired in Snow Bird Harbor’s docks. Since Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is an imperial secret spy, he must have secretly infiltrated them and then instigated them.”

“Our new recruits are all suspicious, and those we recruited before might be incited into rebellion.”

“Weren’t two of the Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders not Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s arrangements? The Imperial Secret Spy Agency is the largest intelligence organization on the Main Plane.”

The dragonman youngster understood that this was an exceptionally good opportunity that wouldn’t be easy to come across.

He decided to exploit this rare opportunity to see who would stand out.

“No matter how it is, I will handle them!” The dragonman youngster had already determined his answer.

Of course, the dragonman youngster always gave the other survivors the greatest level of trust.

But he couldn’t rule out the chance some of the survivors had been bought off or deserted.

The dragonman youngster was constantly changing.

On Mystifying Monster Island, he used the Bloodcore to turn into different lifeforms. After leaving Mystifying Monster Island, he turned into a dragonman and then into a fishman.

His changes were not just limited to his appearance.

All kinds of experiences constantly molded his wisdom, evolved his worldview, values, life outlook, touched his spirit, and enlarged his horizons.

In the depths of the seafloor, he and Cang Xu helped each other, constantly investigated things, and he absorbed Cang Xu’s benefits.

He duped Charming Blue Goddess and then utilized Charming Blue divine spells to deceive the Main Earth Elemental God and Teacher Si Ling to save the big guy and to steal death divinity. All kinds of personal experience gave him ample appreciation and comprehension for the power of deception!

Afterwards, he discovered Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a secret spy and found that Tan Mo and Snow Bird Harbor City Lord were scheming against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and that stirred the fishman youngster’s thoughts even more – I deceived others, while others simultaneously deceived me! I have to always be careful because the world outside of Mystifying Monster Island is the same, filled with the cruel competition of survival.

In fact, the competition outside of that island was even more complicated and was a mix of countless conspiracies and plots. Superficial friends were in fact enemies (Snow Bird Harbor City Lord), or the relationship between enemy and friend was ever shifting (Hun Tong).

“Powerful beings can be schemed against too. Like the Main Earth Elemental God and Teacher Si Ling.”

“Besides, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is not a weak person, he has formidable strength!”

The dragonman youngster was quite prudent.

Carrying all kinds of stirred emotions, he left for the capital and after observing for a time, he decided to act. It yielded excellent results, and he deceived the Li Jian Clan. Although the results haven’t appeared yet, his main goal had already been achieved.

This reinforcement of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch was the same.

The present.

The dragonman youngster observing at the edge of the battlefield was not just the warrior from Mystifying Monster Island, the inspirational speaker from Snake Rat Island, or the valiant military leader that charged ahead on Two Eyes Island, rather he was a tactical master who gradually learned how to plan before acting.

The dragonman youngster had returned!

Not only had he returned with all new powers but also elevated wisdom.

He also returned to Snow Bird Harbor with Ku Feng, Qi Ling, Xiao Wang Zi, and Lady Sea Snake.

That’s right, Lang Xiao was Ku Feng.

Tripleblade did not recognize them because these people had many deception spells on them.

As for Lang Xiao’s dauntless bravery and fearless fighting style, the reason was that he was undead, and many mortal injuries were minor injuries to the undead.

Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, Lady Sea Snake, and Qi Ling were teleported into Ocean Monster Nest’s blood sacrifice then taken captive by the fishman youngster.

During the fishman youngster’s blasphemous prayer, his understanding of memory modification increased by a lot.

After Cang Xu’s cautious and rigorous investigation, the fishman youngster began to modify the memories of his prisoners.

The survivors had a memory crystal making device, one of the important inheritances from War Merchant.

The fishman youngster also knew many deceptive divine spells, and it went without saying, both brought the best out of each other in a perfect match!

The Memory Concealment spell suppressed their original memories and hid them away.

The Memory Switch spell was an ingenious method in the fishman youngster’s hands that blocked these memories.

Although the new memories were inadequate and not detailed enough, it was enough to make these captives willing to die for the dragonman youngster!

Then adding on a lot of equipment, they immediately possessed great combat strength.

What?

The source of their equipment?

Silver level equipment was widely circulated in the market, and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s armor prohibition only restricted equipment of the gold level and higher.

Money could be used to purchase it.

And nowadays the survivors…

Had money!

Zi Di’s arduously executed business plan had reaped extreme gains. Her immense contribution not only gave the survivors an abundant cash flow but also made their entire financial situation very healthy.

However, the equipment Ku Feng and the others was not things the dragonman youngster hastily purchased from the market after his return.

Rather, after the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had landed on the island, they had always been buying equipment.

Once prior, because of a lack of funds, Tripleblade, the secret decision maker of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch, wanted to stop this purchasing plan.

Now, the benefits had shown themselves.

Chapter 903: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – Performative Tears of Blood

“Although Qi Ling and Xiao Wang Zi aren’t using necromancy, and are instead using magical items and scrolls, they still have formidable firepower.”

“Ku Feng’s influence isn’t that big either. He has a camouflage ability and no one on Two Eyes Island noticed his true identity. His camouflage is from a battle skill and has nothing to do with the undead.”

The dragonman youngster evaluated the captives’ performance on the battlefield via magic images.

To be honest, using undead transcendents was very risky. If it was exposed, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ reputation would become repulsive. As the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ commander, the dragonman youngster’s reputation too would naturally suffer greatly and be called into question universally.

Therefore, all of the captives had muti-layered deception divine spells on them.

During the casting of them, the dragonman youngster also intentionally transformed into his fishman form while using the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale].

“Fake memories make these captives 100% loyal to me.”

“However, this also affects their performance.”

The dragonman youngster’s careful observation obtained some conclusions.

It was the first time these captives had seen real combat, and it was worth it to watch and study them.

Memory modification would counteract many individual sentiments and affect their performance.

Because things like talent and ability usually come from experience, study, and other accumulations. So long as memory modification was used, it would destroy these experiences to a degree.

“At the present stage, modified memories will render a brilliant person mediocre.”

“This is also my first time doing this work, perhaps in the future in the wake of more experience and increased skill, I can have a qualitative improvement in this?”

“Currently, the fabricating and modifying of memories is still quite crude. I can’t even let these captives be awake for too long, to prevent them from pondering too much and noticing the contradictions and flaws in their fake memories.”

“Making memories more logical, less flawed, and more finely crafted is the main direction to head towards in the future.”

The dragonman youngster watched as he thought deeply.

“But making memory modification better requires an enormous cost.”

“Perhaps Cang Xu can have a breakthrough on his end? The undead orthodoxy have been immersed in it over time, and they might have more mature alchemy techniques for it. If they do and we are able to get ahold of it, it will save us immense amounts of research costs.”

“If the undead orthodoxy don’t have it, or Cang Xu is unable to get ahold of it, what should I do?”

The dragonman youngster thought about it in passing, “Is there a simpler and less costly way of improving technology?”

His mind was suddenly struck with lightning as he thought of an answer, “Hold on, isn’t this how gods foster believers?”

Compared to the dragonman youngster directly fabricating memories, the believers gods fostered was a wide net, its imperceptible influence ultimately cultivated talents that wholeheartedly dedicated themselves to them. Not only would they be exceedingly loyal, but they also had ample experiences in religious organizations and possessed talents that surpassed others.

For a split second, the dragonman youngster had a new layer of understanding of gods and churches.

“Go die!” Tan Mo dashed left and right, then suddenly jumped in front of Xiao Wang Zi.

His hand brandished a blade, and he used a battle skill that cut Xiao Wang Zi’s neck into two.

Xiao Wang Zi’s head flew off, and together with his body, both fell into the sea in a “violent death”.

Not long after, Ku Feng was also beheaded by Tan Mo.

The dragonman youngster hadn’t acted yet.

Although they seemed to have died in battle, they actually weren’t.

The reason why their deaths looked “real” was due to deception divine spells and also because Ku Feng and Xiao Wang Zi really thought they had died! It was like when they had been taken prisoner, the fishman youngster had intentionally deceived them in the same way.

“A pity Ba Qi hadn’t fallen into my hands…” The dragonman youngster’s tranquil expression had a hint of regret.

He was a powerful gold level pirate whose reputation had been sowed far.

While Teacher Si Ling was resting between stealing death divinity, he convinced Ba Qi to turn into an undead lifeform.

Ba Qi’s biggest hidden danger was that he was seriously ill and at death’s door.

Because of this, he was chosen by Qian Xing – even if the subdued Ba Qi died not too long in the future, then Qian Xing could just smoothly take over Ba Qi’s pirate group.

Ba Qi also knew he was dying, and his bottom line was also flexible. He didn’t consider Teacher Si Ling’s advice for too long before agreeing.

Ba Qi’s life goal was to disseminate the Air Pressure battle qi technique, and to an extent, turning into an undead was beneficial to achieving this life goal.

After Teacher Si Ling transformed Ba Qi, he set him free.

Zhou Zhang didn’t make things difficult for Ba Qi, mainly for the sake of Teacher Si Ling’s face.

This development was quite in accordance with Da Han’s future divination via time divine spell.

“Want me to act?” When Hun Tong saw Tan Mo beheading two silver levels, he couldn’t help but ask to be sent into battle.

The dragonman youngster’s unorganized vision slightly converged.

He saw doubt in Hun Tong’s glance.

It was very clear that Hun Tong didn’t understand why Long Fu was standing by and watching his subordinates die in battle!

At the same time, the dragonman youngster also knew from experience the anger Hun Tong held in his heart.

Hun Tong deeply detested Tan Mo!

On the ship, he had witnessed Tan Mo frame him.

Wasn’t the situation before him a good opportunity for vengeance?

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “I have already made arrangements for this battlefield. You will go to the magician tower and be the true crux of that battlefield!”

Hun Tong’s heart shivered, he immediately realized that the dragonman youngster wasn’t just targeting the pretend pirate Tan Mo.

“But is doing this a good idea?”

“At least on the face of it, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ally.”

“Is it necessary to deal with two gold levels simultaneously, who are important imperial secret spies at that?”

Hun Tong hated Tan Mo, but he was ambivalent about Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

After all, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord didn’t scheme against his life, he only wanted to put his hands on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Furthermore, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hadn’t acted directly, instead he let Tan Mo do it and allowed the latter to play the role of a pirate and run around in circles.

Under these circumstances, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could still be utilized.

As an imperial noble, Hun Tong also understood compromise.

To him, it seemed the dragonman youngster was acting somewhat recklessly.

But he didn’t give any advice.

Although the dragonman youngster controlled him, he was still loyal to the Sheng Ming Empire. With regards to his current situation, Hun Tong was more inclined that it was fate playing tricks on him,

What he wanted to do most, apart from taking revenge, was to find a channel to make the truth known to the empire’s higher-ups and return to his Two Eyes Island.

That was the place where his lifelong foundation he had exhaustingly worked for lay!

But the dragonman youngster didn’t do as he wished, instead he was dispatched to the magician tower.

“Tan Mo has already left the magician tower, and Snow Harbor City Lord won’t appear in the flesh, the magician tower’s temporary shutdown means it is in its more vulnerable state.”

“Destroy this tower to the best of your ability.”

“If your operation fails, don’t return.”

The dragonman youngster turned his dragon eyes towards Hun Tong, his tone callous.

Hun Tong’s heart jumped, and he promptly bowed his head, “Understood!”

He was under his control and had no choice but to obey.

Reyling on deception divine spells and the magician tower’s current state under “pirate” control, Hun Tong easily snuck into the tower.

“What’s going on?”

“Someone is dead!”

“Enemy attack, an enemy! Find him now!!”

It wasn’t until Hun Tong had killed his way to the main floor did someone notice something peculiar.

It was as the dragonman youngster anticipated, the magician tower was at its most empty, and the navies that controlled it didn’t foresee enemies.

They were unable to see through the deception divine spells and could only passively defend themselves.

But silver level defense wanting to withstand a gold level assassin’s all out attack was extremely challenging.

After a moment, there was not a single witness left inside the magician tower besides Hun Tong.

“Hurry up! Go, go go!!” Hun Tong was in a race against time and moved like the wind.

After massacring everyone, he began to plant alchemy bombs everywhere,

Because he knew that if problems appeared in the magician tower, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord wouldn’t sit still. The performance would not continue and he would intervene personally!

The magician tower was a core defense; it was extremely important!

“What is the situation? Is there really an enemy taking advantage of this crisis?!” Like Hun Tong expected, the moment Snow Bird Harbor City Lord detected abnormalities with the magician tower, his look violently changed.

It had to be at least a gold level enemy to so quickly slaughter silver level navies!

This realization seemed to burn Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s buttocks with hot tongs, and he leapt up and rushed out of the city palace.

He had been spectator in Tan Mo’s attack on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

He couldn’t appear and needed to give Tan Mo enough time to carry out his looting.

But now, he had no choice but to appear.

“The magician tower is too important; it cannot be lost!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not act alone, he instantly mobilized the city guard, primarily the three magic battle squads.

The magic battle squads were still assembling, and unable to wait, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord rushed to the magician tower by himself.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord jumped across the roofs of buildings while simultaneously shouting, issuing warnings as their city lord.

His main purpose was to warn Tan Mo and the others that the situation had changed, stop performing, retreat without delay!

Tan Mo was caught utterly flatfooted, he hadn’t received the magician tower battle report and couldn’t help but glare furiously and thought, “This [Emancipator], what is going on with him? I am about to win, yet at the last moment, he actually showed up to ruin everything?!”

More ironic was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ reaction.

“Snow Bird Harbor City Lord has come to assist us! He has finally appeared.”

“Persevere, we shall be victorious.”

“If these wretched pirates want to leave, then they shall pay the price in blood!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was quite peeved to see that because of him, the morale of the enemy he wanted to deal with, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, had soared.

Tan Mo also bit his teeth.

He was irritated that Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s appearance had caused the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ morale to bounce back and many of its members had become dauntless and unafraid of death.

Tan Mo couldn’t help but be suspicious, “[Emancipator]. Are you really an ally?”

He really wanted to keep fighting.

But at that moment, he received the battle report from the magician tower.

Tan Mo wanted to bellow in anger, but rationality told him he had to retreat!

If he didn’t, he would have to fight Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Reaching this kind of development would be too hilarious!

Tan Mo was utterly sullen but had to withdraw.

He waved his arm and shouted to retreat, and the navies obeyed. They bit their teeth and after methodically arranging themselves, they retreated.

“This is actually a defeat!”

“But I won’t let you off Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!!” Tan Mo roared in his thoughts.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord burned with anxiety.

He rushed to the magician tower with all speed.

But the moment he was about to storm the magician tower…

Boom!!!

The magician tower exploded.

The fierce detonation burst apart the magician tower and blew up the entirety of the top floor.

The formidable air wave nearly blew away Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. The latter did his best to remain standing as he activated his defensive battle skill.

By the time the explosion finished, what Snow Bird Harbor City Lord saw when looked up wanted him to cough out blood in anger.

The magician tower…ceased to be.

Only the bottom floor remained, with another half floor on top of it.

The magician tower had been returned to ruins.

The disastrous loss caused Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s eyes to go dark.

“I…he…I…” He wanted to curse loudly but just stammered.

Hun Tong had left long ago and was hiding in some hidden corner.

Seeing Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s warped and pained expression, Hun Tong couldn’t help but sympathize with him. He and Dao Hen had personally investigated Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s and Tan Mo’s conspiracy.

These two wanted to play a trick, but in the end, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had beaten them at their own game, resulting in the magician tower getting blown up.

This loss was too heavy.

Were it not for the magician tower being “controlled” by pirates and shutting down the main defenses, how would Hun Tong be able to get inside? To say nothing of the magician tower getting blown up.

The magician tower was Snow Bird Harbor’s strongest defense and the core of it too. Because of a trick, it was so easily destroyed.

“I didn’t expect these alchemy bombs to be so powerful!”

“But why didn’t Long Fu wait until Snow Bird Harbor City Lord entered the magician tower to detonate the bombs?”

“He doesn’t want to deal with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord?”

Hun Tong’s mind sprouted many more suspicions.

He was unable to make sense of what the dragonman youngster was doing.

For a few seconds, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stood in place, rigidly gazing at the ruins of the magician tower, pain and animosity all across his motionless figure, like he was a statue.

After a few seconds, he became convinced of something and suddenly turned around to look at Tan Mo and the others.

“You, it was you!”

“You band of despicable pirates actually dared to destroy my magician tower!!!”

“Die, you will all die!!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s former grace was gone as he flagrantly charged the navies.

Tan Mo took a deep breath, then promptly ordered, “Retreat now, I will hold him back.”

Boom, boom, boom.

The two began to fight, trading extremely fierce and vile blows.

Hun Tong’s mind stirred, “Could it be that the reason why Long Fu didn’t kill Snow Bird Harbor City Lord in that explosion was that he wanted him to fight Tan Mo?”

“Hold on, I fear killing Snow Bird Harbor City Lord with that explosion is impossible. The reason why the magician tower was so frail was because its interior was damaged and it had no defensive mechanisms.”

“Long Fu this guy, don’t be fooled by his thick eyebrows and big eyes, he really is a bit…treacherous.”

At that moment, Hun Tong sympathized more with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord,

The latter obviously knew this wasn’t done by Tan Mo. But he had to continue the performance, and as far as the scene of the crime was concerned, these “pirates” must be the perpetrators.

Therefore, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had to act, he had to fight with his full strength, and he had to carry out fierce vengeance mixed with animosity.

This was the heavy responsibility of guarding his territory that he, as city lord, had to carry out.

Chapter 904: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – There Really Are Pirates

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held a short rod in both hands and used all kinds of spells and battle skills one after the other to suppress Tan Mo to take the upper hand.

His battle style was very gorgeous, and light and shadow changed in a spectrum of color.

A rod descended and pounded Tan Mo in the belly.

Tan Mo was smacked downwards, his body bending like a lobster as he coughed out spit.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smashed him with a thunderbolt, causing Tan Mo’s hair to stand up amidst black smoke.

“Hey, hey, hey, you’ve done enough! Why are your attacks so ruthless?” Tan Mo secretly transmitted.

Within a few short minutes, he had been badly battered.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s face was filled with hostility and anger, roaring “Pirate, I shall slay you here!”, while also transmitting, “Perform well, don’t expose any flaws. At this stage, this is all I can do!!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had to admit from the bottom of his heart that violently beating Tan Mo like this really was partially due to him venting his anger.

Were it not for Tan Mo, why would he use so much gold for bribes? Were it not for Tan Mo, he wouldn’t have planned to have Tan Mo pretend to be a pirate and attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Were it not for Tan Mo, his magician tower would have been running normally and would never have exploded!

The magician tower was gone!

This was too big a loss, and in accordance with kingdom law, as the top person in charge, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had to compensate for it. Because Snow Bird Harbor was not his territory, and the magician tower was not constructed and funded solely by himself.

The magician tower was built with public funds.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord didn’t just need to pay out a few hundred thousand gold coins, but at least a million!

His losses weren’t limited to the magician tower.

The pirate attack and the magician tower exploding, these were grave dereliction of duty that was bound to be an immense blotch on Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s record.

The future would be more annoying.

After the magician tower exploded, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord felt distressed about the future. Since the core defense that was the magician tower had been blown up, Snow Bird Harbor’s defenses had fallen to an all-time low, and it would surely attract actual pirates over that would plunder it.

In other words, after this battle, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s days would be very difficult.

The losses in front of him were disastrous, but the future would be even worse!

Before this operation, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had total control over Snow Bird Harbor and his beautiful achievement of cleaning up the Bayonet Gang gave him bright prospects.

But now, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s political prospects had taken a dramatic turn and been enveloped in haze.

“Hehehe..” In a distant corner, Hun Tong couldn’t help but laugh when he saw Tan Mo’s battered appearance.

“You wretched schemers!”

“He’s gone too much into his performance.”

“They are obvious allies, yet they have to beat each other up. What an original way of getting vengeance, it’s quite novel.”

Hun Tong joyfully thought.

Especially because he knew the inside story and understood that Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was currently swallowing his blood and tears, painfully putting on this show.

Tan Mo was getting worse.

He obviously had strength, but he couldn’t display it. He had to use widely known battle skills to disguise and mask it.

While he was unable to display his power, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was using his full strength to prove himself.

Through this back and forth, Tan Mo would naturally take a beating!

Hun Tong happily narrowed his eyes, watching this with ever more gusto.

He glanced at where the dragonman youngster was standing, suddenly thinking, “This arrangement of Long Fu’s, could it be that he is actually a very vengeful person?”

Hun Tong secretly analyzed it.

He had very complex emotions about the dragonman youngster and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In the past, he had a good opinion of the dragonman youngster and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Because during the battle of Two Eyes Island, Long Fu and the others had an impressive performance and were a cornerstone on the battlefield. Hun Tong appreciated them greatly and sincerely wanted to recruit them at a high price but didn’t succeed.

Afterwards, when Hun Tong was framed by Tan Mo, Dao Hen had saved him, something the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had nothing to do with.

However, when Hun Tong and Dao Hen were spying on Tan Mo’s and Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s conspiracy together, he knew that the massive wealth he kept in his mind constantly had ultimately fallen into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ hands.

Although he still didn’t know the truth, he believed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps seized it from the necromancer.

“But they were the ultimate benefactors.”

“They took my money.”

“Those were mine; they should have returned it to me. But they didn’t do that, instead they traveled to Ice Sculpture Island and disposed of them here!”

Could Hun Tong understand the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

Of course he could.

If he were the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he would have made the same choice, after all who would give such great wealth?

But while he could understand, Hun Tong couldn’t forgive them.

“Oh, how I awaited those riches!”

“Without it, my Two Eyes Island was left in ruins and oh how I suffered!”

“Tan Mo demanded bribes, and if I had that money, would I have been framed by him?”

Thinking this, Hun Tong automatically felt strongly aggrieved, secretly bitter, and resentful.

When Dao Hen got a divine oracle and after Hun Tong was forced to sign a contract upon meeting the dragonman youngster, his attitude changed again.

“What is the connection between the god behind Dao Hen and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

“A cooperative relationship? Or is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps a pawn of that unknown god too?”

“It has always been said that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was an agent of an arms merchant, but I have seen no evidence of that. Perhaps that was a disguise?”

Hun Tong began to realize that this matter was far from simple.

He also didn’t know which god Dao Hen believed in. But he was an intelligent person and through the limited clues, he could guess and analyze.

The trail was actually very clear.

It could be determined that Dao Hen likely believed in an ocean or a fishman god.

This unavoidably caused Hun Tong to make further connections.

“Hold on, is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps involved with the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan?”

When the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan attacked Two Eyes Island, a naga priestess and others from the Fishman Holy City had provided their strength.

Initially, Hun Tong rejected this connection.

After all, during the battle of Two Eyes Island, the fierce fighting and disastrous blood price paid by both sides meant it was unlikely to be a performance.

“But what if it was a trick of self-injury to gain the enemy’s confidence?”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan are actually on the same side. But using this trick of self-injury and establishing each other’s identities. These groups could split apart and use the residents of Two Eyes Island and even me as witnesses.”

“If it is like this, then the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is my foe! Tan Mo framing me and Dao Hen saving me was also within my enemy’s calculations!”

“Am I currently serving my foe right now?”

Hun Tong looked tranquil, while his heart was in chaos.

Bitterness, powerlessness, fear, suspicion, inquisitiveness, hostility…all these feelings were considerably complex, but outwardly, he showed an obedient bearing.

One under a roof was forced to lower their head.

Hun Tong chose to compromise, and he performed proactively and obeyed all orders completely. While secretly, he planned to reduce the other’s vigilance, investigate the truth, distinguish the enemy and him, and look for an opportunity to escape as much as possible.

Hun Tong’s attitude to this unknown god was reverence, wariness, and faint hostility.

His loyalty was still to the Sheng Ming Empire.

Ding.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s rod hit Tan Mo on top of the head, causing the latter’s head to shudder, and his body flashed back in retreat.

Tan Mo had a splitting headache, and he snarled, “[Emancipator], enough!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord immediately transmitted, “We have already signed a contract, what are you afraid of? Keep the show up, that is the correct path.”

Tan Mo glared back, “You fool! Our true enemy is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps! What are you thinking, not attacking the true enemy and instead making things difficult for allies?”

When Snow Bird Harbor City Lord heard that, he suddenly grew more furious, “As of now, do you really think I can attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as city lord with you ‘pirates’?

“Let me sober you up a bit.”

“Since this matter has reached this stage, even if your teeth are broken, you will have to take it! Our performance must go on!”

Tan Mo naturally didn’t want to do that, and he transmitted, “Be a bit gentler, hit a bit lighter, you’re attacking me too heavily!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord responded, “You believe I don’t want to stay my hand?! There are innumerable alchemy devices in the process of recording our fight. If I stay my hand even a bit, it will be an immense flaw.”

He thought, “Although this continued performance frustrates me, beating up Tan Mo still feels great! This opportunity must be treasured; it rarely appears!”

Tan Mo’s words to Snow Bird Harbor Lord instead made the latter’s attacks even more ferocious, causing him to promptly persuade from another angle, “The one who destroyed the magician tower must be the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, otherwise who else could it be?”

“They have silver level assistance not on record, they must be hiding a gold level. Only a gold level would be able to clear out the magician tower in such a short time.”

“If you expend yourself with me now, how will you deal with the gold level?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord transmitted a sneer back, “How will I deal with them?”

“Don’t I have you?”

“You are Tan Mo, a gold level imperial navy admiral. You have come to Snow Bird Harbor to rest and reorganize, and even if you are wounded right now, you can still appear!”

“No, you have to go into battle to confront these murderous ‘pirates’.”

“If you don’t do that, how can you justify yourself?”

When Tan Mo heard that, he suddenly went sluggish.

He wildly shouted in his mind, “What kind of mess is this?! Not only is the looting operation a failure, but I also have to return to my actual identity and attack my subordinates?”

When Snow Bird Harbor City Lord saw Ton Mo exposing a weak point, he seized the opportunity to get a cheap shot with his rod that badly bruised him.

Tan Mo covered his head and retreated again and again.

He was dizzy and his eyes were dim, and he felt like he needed to cough up blood.

This was utter nonsense!

He regretted this.

He really regretted this.

He felt immense remorse.

If he had known this matter would develop into this, he wouldn’t have pretended to be a pirate even if his life was threatened.

Who thought of this stratagem?

Stupid!

So stupid!

“Not I, it was you! You stupidly pressed for it.” Tan Mo scowled as he transmitted insults, “Without your suggestion, would I ever play pirate?!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was even angrier, thinking to himself, “If you weren’t such a greedy scumbag, repeated bribe solicitor, and so unwilling to let go of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, would I have been forced to think of that idea of you playing pirate?”

Anger was reciprocated with anger, but when Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was covered in wounds, he still decided to hold back.

He remined, “Now is not the time to look into who to blame.”

“We have a problem!”

“Get your people out of here now, my magic battle squads are coming!”

“Isn’t it because of you I can’t leave?!” Tan Mo wanted to bawl out but resisted in doing so.

He also knew the severity of this.

The magic battle squads going into battle meant the battle would truly slip from their control.

Tan Mo’s subordinates would suffer even more casualties!

This was because every city’s magic battle squad was organized and maintained through national funds. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s control over them was not deep enough.

Under these circumstances, the magic battle squads were completely in the dark and really believed pirates were invading Snow Bird Harbor.

Tan Mo transmitted, “[Emancipator]. Work with me now, let me wound you seriously so I can escape immediately!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord coldly snorted, why would he give Tan Mo a chance for personal vengeance?

He definitively declined, “If I suffer a ‘heavy injury’, how will we work together to face the enemy? Do you not have the alchemy puppet I gave you?”

“Use it to intercept me.”

Seeing Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was unwilling to cooperate, Tan Mo felt regret, but without other options, he was forced to use the alchemy puppet.

After the gold level alchemy puppet was activated, it “stopped” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Without stopping to rest, Tan Mo hurried over to reinforce his subordinates.

He saw his subordinates being surrounded and blocked by the magic battle squads and the city guard!

The elves were inherently good at magic, and to pretend to be pirates, the navies didn’t wear their regular equipment and had very low magic resistance.

Tan Mo hurried to the scene, his eyes cracking with fury. The navies’ immense battle losses caused his heart to bleed!

“These losses, these losses are too high.”

“Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps! I won’t let you off, I will return today’s losses a thousand fold!”

Tan Mo was an insatiable person, and it was rare for him to suffer losses.

Today, Tan Mo had eaten a massive loss, and he already bore a deep grudge for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But now was definitely not the time to retaliate.

Tan Mo could only push down his spite and lead his subordinates out.

With him as the vanguard, the city guard and magic battle squads were forced to evade and yield space.

The ‘pirates’ at long last had the pressure on them turn down, and they followed Tan Mo, doing their best to evacuate the battlefield.

On the face of it, they had no ships and were forced to retreat through the port and withdraw into the interior of Ice Sculpture Island.

Hun Tong returned to the dragonman youngster’s side.

“My Lord, I can intercept Tan Mo.”

“Can they keep performing if they don’t want to?”

“Hehehe, after putting in such effort for this grand performance, is it fine for it to end so abruptly?”

“It’s such a pity, I’ll help them out.”

The dragonman youngster glanced at Hun Tong, and smiled as he jested, “Hun Tong, I didn’t expect you’d be somewhat treacherous.”

Hun Tong: ???

He thought to himself: don’t tell me it wasn’t Long Fu who facilitated this situation?

The dragonman youngster continued, “There is no need to appear. I have a more suitable candidate.”

As he said that, he cast his gaze towards the port.

A moment later, all kinds of deception divine spells were undone.

A well trained pirate group suddenly appeared!

Their pirate flag was somewhat peculiar, it was an impressive wickerwork vegetable basket, and in the basket there was a skull.

Chapter 905: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – Another Explosion

“It’s the Vegetable Pirates!”

“Cong Mang, and his Hoodlum battle squad under his command!!”

When Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Tan Mo, and the others saw the pirates appearing, their hearts jolted greatly.

Why were there really pirates?!

Obviously before their operation, they had specifically used the magician tower and the patrol fleet to search the surrounding sea waters and found no traces of enemies, it was very safe.

“What method did they use? They actually had such a powerful camouflage ability?”

“This is completely inconsistent with the intelligence.”

“Wait a minute, at this time, Cong Mang is serving subordinate to Da Han as his vanguard official. The Vegetable Pirates appearing here, don’t tell me…Da Han is right behind him?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo had a formidable information advantage, and in a split second their minds churned, with each thought scaring them.

If Da Han really was targeting Snow Bird Harbor, then they couldn’t sojourn here!

The saint level and the gold level were two completely different concepts.

“If the magician tower was still here coordinating with all of the basic defensive magic formations found throughout Snow Bird Harbor, then we could last for some time…”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord looked at the magician tower ruins again, the corner of his mouth twitching.

Tan Mo led his troops out of the magic battle squad encirclement while his imagination ran wild, “Could it be the one who destroyed the magician tower was not from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

He was a bit dazed.

Hun Tong was camouflaged and acted very efficiently when he bombed the magician tower, he left no evidence behind that pointed towards the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Tan Mo even suspected Snow Bird Harbor City Lord!

The two of them really didn’t trust each other, and if it weren’t for their magic contract, they would never have been able to work together.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord also thought, “What a coincidence, everything is too much of a coincidence!”

Right as their performance reached a crucial moment, and after the magician tower’s defenses had been shut down, pirate experts were mounting a raid? Did they take advantage of that to blow up the magician tower?

Were the Vegetable Pirates just lucky?

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was not naïve.

He was the leader of one of Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s eight harbors, he had mixed in with the kingdom’s political circles for many years and was also a member of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency.

Therefore, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo simultaneously felt the strong stench of a plot.

The only thing they differed on was the suspected target.

The Vegetable Pirates’ appearance was undoubtedly a heavy blow.

Tan Mo became more cautious.

While Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was entangled with the alchemy puppet, he was giving orders to the city guard and the magic battle squads to move their focus to dealing with the Vegetable Pirates, indirectly letting Tan Mo and the others get away.

The pressure on Tan Mo and his navies consequently dropped and they were more easily able to escape the encirclement.

Cong Mang’s pirates rushed out of the pirate group, and several hundred people united in action as a grand black expanse charged into the city palace.

“Scramble quickly, that place must have the most money.”

“Nice trick, Commander. You destroyed Snow Bird Harbor’s magician tower!”

“Hahaha, our competitors have expended themselves ahead of us, we emerged at the right time.”

The Vegetable Pirates were very excited and had exuberant morale.

As they ran past Tan Mo and his people, some pirates bombastically thanked them.

The navies were green with anger!

To them this was undoubtedly a huge embarrassment.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord saw the Vegetable Pirates were shockingly going to attack his city palace, what kind of outrage was this?

He promptly abandoned trading blows with the alchemy puppet and rushed back to the city palace.

“Little ones, you must rob well for me!” When Cong Mang saw this, he lifted his gold level long rod and stopped walking, turning back to welcome Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The fight between the two immediately turned intense.

“Fuck off!” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was incredibly anxious, displaying his full strength and using all kinds of battle skills and spells in turn.

Cong Mang mainly defended himself and was pushed into a disadvantageous position. But on the contrary, he wasn’t worried and held the initiative.

As long as he stalled Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the pirates could steal an exceedingly considerable amount of wealth.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could abandon the alchemy puppet, but he was unable to free himself from Cong Mang. He resisted his anger, calmed himself, and sneered at Cong Mang, “Do you believe I don’t have arrangements in the city palace?”

Cong Mang burst into laughter, “Of course we know, but we know that it holds the most amount of money in Snow Bird Harbor!”

“Sacrificing some people for wealth is very normal.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord gnashed his teeth, “You damn pirates!!”

Since he couldn’t break away, he decided to attack madly with rage.

Far off, Hun Tong was amazed as he watched Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was in an abyss of suffering with Cong Mang.

He didn’t think that actual pirates would appear!

“All kinds of signs indicate that this is obviously Long Fu’s plan.”

“In that case, what is the relationship with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Vegetable Pirates?”

“Or perhaps the Vegetable Pirates are a pawn of that god too?”

Hun Tong slyly observed the dragonman youngster, increasingly feeling the latter was a profound mystery.

The reality was that after the dragonman youngster and Zi Di talked, he learned of the Vegetable Pirates’ connection with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance and that Elder Jiu Jin had invited Cong Mang over to the Ice Sculpture Capital to secretly open the Wisteria Cache.

Therefore, he used Elder Jiu Jin’s appearance to contact the Vegetable Pirates. The then publicly used a divine spell to win their trust and arrange for the Vegetable Pirates to attack Snow Bird Harbor.

“Pirates are always an adventurous lot.”

“Cong Mang is a pirate leader, and the fact that he went to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, a principal enemy of his, upon invitation, to conspire for the Wisteria Cache proves his extremely adventurous spirit.”

The dragonman youngster judged Cong Mang’s personality, allowing him to think of this plan.

After implementing it, sure enough it worked.

Looking at it currently, it seemed to have a great result.

The dragonman youngster imperceivably glanced at Hun Tong.

He clearly understood Hun Tong’s mood and thinking.

Despite having methods that could plant false memories into Hun Tong that could turn him into a fanatic for him like Xiao Wang Zi and the others, the young man believed that doing this was instead ineffective.

Hun Tong’s status, interpersonal relationships, and abilities were valuable.

Hun Tong’s current situation made the dragonman youngster resolve to attempt to deepen his subjugation of him.

“Charge!”

“This is mine, this is also mine.”

“Wa ha ha, so many gold coins, lots of gold coins.”

The pirates were extremely excited.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s look gradually shifted.

He noticed that the city palace’s defenses were minuscule, and that the person he assigned to stand watch over it, Mei Lan Du, vanished without a trace. None of the city palace’s traps had triggered either.

“Damn it!” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord wanted to charge in, but Cong Mang tenaciously stopped him.

The big guy, Da Dou, as well as Hong Zhu had already left the city palace and were watching from some calm corner.

Hong Zhu’s face was blushed red with excitement as she hugged a large amount of treasure.

The big guy held even more valuables.

Da Dou’s storage pouch had been packed full, and he was also holding a prisoner. It was Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s palace supervisor Mei Lan Du.

“It’s almost time.” Da Dou took out an alchemy device.

He pressed down on the device’s button.

Boom.

A moment later, the city palace exploded!

Hun Tong, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Tan Mo, as well as Cong Mang: ??!!

Only the dragonman youngster’s gaze was serene: the Vegetable Pirates deserved to die!

The dragonman youngster would not stand by and watch as pirates plundered the populace of Snow Bird Harbor after looting the city palace.

Besides, without his crew, Cong Mang’s strength would decrease, and then, wouldn’t it be easier to control him?

Chapter 906: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – A New View of Life

Cong Mang’s eyes went red.

The city palace’s explosion had buried most of his crew in the grave.

“The city palace had a bomb inside it!?”

Cong Mang’s people didn’t think they’d be killed while looting.

The Vegetable Pirates’ suffered serious battle casualties, not only was 70% of them gone, that number included some ringleaders and elites.

Even Cong Mang’s assistant, the pirate group’s first mate, was among the slain.

The only thing that brought Cong Mang joy was that the pirates outside the city palace were all experts, including the Hoodlum battle squad. They had the same duty as Cong Mang, holding back Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the city guard, and other forces from entering.

It instead let them survive this calamity.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s lifeless dazed face reflected in the burning flames.

“There is a conspiracy afoot here, there must be one afoot!” After reacting, he roared in his thoughts.

He was certain he hadn’t deployed an alchemy bomb inside the city palace, that was not his style!

The pirates wouldn’t blow themselves up either.

Tan Mo? He was a greedy character, and he was attacking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps for money. But he signed a contract, why did he want to blow up his ally’s base?

Adding on the coincidence with the magician tower, this caused Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to conclude that there must be a third party acting in the dark.

“A gold level appeared inside the magician tower, they are likely from that third party.”

“Cong Mang’s Vegetable Pirates don’t have a powerful camouflage ability, then suddenly they do. That might also be from that third party!”

“I have to ferret them out…”

Boom.

A moment later, Cong Mang’s battle skill violently swung at him.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s train of thought was interrupted, and he was forced to use a defensive battle skill.

Both sides collided head on, and Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was flung into the air and coughed out a lot of blood.

“Despicable snow elf, you’re going to hell!”

“I’ll kill you and bury you with my people!!”

The horrific losses made Cong Mang extremely angry, and he madly and ferociously retaliated.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord coldly snorted, and after regaining his footing, he counterattacked.

His short rod jabbed towards Cong Mang, but Cong Mang changed his fighting style and didn’t dodge it, instead he held his long rod tightly and swept it away with all his strength.

Battle skill – Sweep! Divine spell – Bear Strength.

Battle skill – Straight Stab. Spell – Haste.

Cong Mang battled Snow Bird Harbor City Lord!

After that outrageous clash, Cong Mang stood in place as his face quickly flushed red, then his laryngeal prominence rolled and he gargled blood.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was thrown into the air again, and because of the force of the impact, his slender elven body was violently bent backwards. He landed on his back, and upon landing, he immediately bounced back, nimbly somersaulted through the air, and landed on the ground in a kneel, his eyes fixed on Cong Mang.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s left arm slightly trembled, and just as he wanted to catch his breath, he saw Cong Mang’s body crackling with gold battle qi, his tense muscles looking like a bear.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord promptly used all kinds of spells to increase his defense and other attributes.

A moment later, Cong Mang widely attacked like a torrential gale that shrouded the former.

Unlike before, this time Cong Mang firmly held the upper hand and was beating down Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The dragonman youngster watched from a distant place, his dragon eyes reflecting the particulars of the fight between Cong Mang and Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“Snow Bird Harbor City Lord dual cultivates battle qi and magic, and within a short time span, he is able to use al kinds of small scale battle skills and spells to constantly accumulate an advantage in battle, until quantity leads to a qualitative change, making him victorious.”

“Cong Mang is a dual cultivator of battle qi and divine spells, his long rod is his main weapon, and his offensive is very wide ranging. He mainly uses divine spells to enhance his defense and achieve balance between attack and defense, at the same time because divine spells are comprehensive in all aspects, it can enhance his status overall, giving him a bear’s strength, a leopard’s speed, and a bird’s eyesight.”

Both had their distinct fighting style.

Cong Mang’s role was to use his personal combat strength to charge through the battlefield. The information the dragonman youngster collected had been proven when the Vegetable Pirates fought, Cong Mang normally took the lead and relied on his individual fighting strength to break through the enemy ranks, smash the battlefield equilibrium, then the Hoodlum battle squad would follow in soon after to stabilize the breach he tore open.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was more meticulous, actually he was more of an expert in one on one combat.

But the main reason he was conceding ground was that Cong Man was recklessly attacking in anger, eager to seek vengeance and willing to sustain injuries if he could inflict heavier wounds to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

While Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was unwilling to risk doing that.

It wasn’t that he lacked bravery, rather he discovered that there was a third party around, so naturally he wanted to conserve his strength as much as possible.

If he went all out now, perhaps he’d give that other power a petty advantage!

Cong Mang was going all out while Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was apprehensive and constrained.

Very quickly, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord understood Tan Mo’s pain – he was repeatedly beaten up by Cong Mang’s attacks.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s right eye was jabbed by a rod, swelling greatly and turning a black-purple color.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord howled at Cong Mang, “You idiotic pirate, wake up, do you really think I’d blow up my own home?”

Cang Mang howled back, “Why not?”

He didn’t stop attacking for even a second!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stared blankly for an instant, then was forced to retreat from this difficult situation and jumped away.

Seeing this, Da Dou couldn’t help but laugh.

As a gold level giant magic chef, he should have been an honored guest, but Snow Bird Harbor City Lord secretly arrested him and forced him to eat improved versions of the Deep Sigh of the Blue Ocean all day long. If it was another kind of magic medicine, it would have been fine. But these improved versions were a type of magic dish, which was a slap in Da Dou’s face.

Upon seeing Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s sorry figure running from a fight, Da Dou naturally took pleasure in it.

But at the same time, he still didn’t forget to be vigilant.

Snow Bird Harbor was sinking into commotion and chaos.

The pirate attack was too big, but fortunately the battlefield was limited and hadn’t expanded yet, so civilian casualties were minimal.

To the side, Hong Zhu was excitedly inventorying her valuables.

Da Dou looked at the big guy again.

The big guy was silent, his eyes leaking azure divine light. It was clear he was under Divine Descent.

It was precisely with this state that the big guy freed the three of them, stole an enormous amount of valuables from the city palace, and left behind facades that disguised countless valuables that baited the feverish pirates into a trap.

The dragonman youngster controlled both his body and the big guy, maintaining Divine Descent.

In the beginning, this would have been impossible for him.

But that was how things were, practice makes perfect.

The dragonman youngster naturally became proficient after many usages.

Of course, the divine blessings provided plenty of casting experiences that let him skip over a long process of inefficient individual accumulation cycles and was instantly able to divide his attention and mind to this level.

As a matter of fact, not only was the dragonman youngster controlling two bodies at once, but he was also evaluating things in his mind too.

“People are jaggedly different.”

Cong Mang’s temperament was impetuous; he liked to take risks and often gave no thought to if he lived or died. Therefore, the bomb and suicide explosion attack was very normal to him. He projected this onto others and maintained that Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would have done that. That caused a misunderstanding.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord? Planting a bomb in his residence is not his style. There was a high probability he wouldn’t have done such a thing.

He liked to plan then move, ensuring his chances of winning before acting. His style was to remain still, then kill in a single strike.

The dragonman youngster personally experienced this with the Bayonet Gang’s destruction, thus he had a considerable understanding of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Combined with the information he gained, his understanding of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had deepened.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s plot to dismantle the Bayonet Gang had begun a long time ago. He feigned compliance while planting spies.

He placed spies that became two of the Bayonet Gang’s six ringleaders. One third of the Bayonet Gang’s leadership was his people!

But he still didn’t act, rather he waited for an opportune moment, all the way until the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps appeared, who he saw as his shield. That is the reason why he made nothing of hardships and used great interests to spur the survivors into standing up.

Then, as city lord, he intervened as a third party, acting in the early stages to paralysis the Bayonet Gang as much as possible.

Afterwards, he utilized Mei Lan Du’s abilities to secretly tamper with Teng Dong Lang’s food and drink.

In an overall survey of the situation, he did his utmost to prevent direct confrontation and maximize his rate of success. All kinds of measures over a long period of time accumulated into an enormous advantage, until finally he moved openly.

When he moved, it was like a thunderstrike to an acupuncture point, instant results!

He was not a general, but a schemer, the way a politician behaved.

Would such a politician and schemer plant a bomb in his own home?

Few would do so.

Because a person with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s temperament couldn’t sleep under threat. He was immersed in the world of plots and was paranoid enough.

Putting a bomb in his own home would cause him to think daily: would the bomb accidentally explode; would an enemy find and exploit it?

A schemer like him wished to put himself in the safest environment, and barring unusual circumstances, wouldn’t give himself more stress!

“Furthermore, the reason why Snow Bird Harbor City Lord serves his post is because he is burdened with an Imperial Secret Spy mission.”

“Snow Bird Harbor is a coastal city and one of Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s eight large trading harbors.”

“The Imperial Secret Spy Agency controlling the city lord might mean they want to gain a deeper control over Snow Bird Harbor.”

“Therefore, the one assigned to be the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s is no mere figurehead, he is likely to be a member of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency.”

“Under these circumstances, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would never plant a bomb in his palace.”

The dragonman youngster pondered constantly, his mind freely inundating with all kinds of thoughts.

This was the first time he fought a battle as the hidden hand.

He profited considerably from this alteration of his customary style, along with the new and unique practical experience it brought.

From Cong Mang, he saw a shadow of his past.

From the misunderstanding between Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Cong Mang, and the conflict it brought, he had more of an appreciation of the immense influence personality had in conspiracies.

“Is this how a chess player feels when they lay things out?”

“It is truly unique, this feeling of control and achievement can’t help but make people soak in it, furthermore…it lingers in the memory.”

One could say that his exploitation of Charming Blue Goddess’ power in Ocean Mother Nest, receiving of divine oracles, and constant successful deceptions were a kind of guidance and teaching.

So that now, he was able to independently control an entire war situation by himself, he had studied so he could apply himself here.

Learning and applying, through practical application are studies validated and understood. All kinds of triggers let the dragonman youngster feel that a new door opened in his life, through which he had a completely new view of life!

Chapter 907: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – Hou Lang Appears

“This time, things have really gotten out of hand!” When the city palace exploded, even Tan Mo was dumbfounded for a good while.

“What’s going on with [Emancipator]?! He was convinced he had immense control over Snow Bird Harbor…”

“Is that it?”

Tan Mo was excessively greedy, but not foolish. On the contrary, he had outstanding investigatory abilities, or else not long after coming to Snow Bird Harbor, he wouldn’t have so quickly snooped out Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s shenanigans and forced the latter to submit.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord saw through this plot, and so did he.

The dragonman youngster’s first time was still too rough. If Cang Xu were here, it would be completely different.

Tan Mo’s reaction was different from Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

After breaking out of the siege he had time and space to revert to his original navy identity, but he didn’t rush out to battle.

“Let me stall for a bit.”

Tan Mo even poured himself a cup of red wine and slowly savored it.

Snow Bird Harbor had suffered heavy damage, with the magician tower and city palace being bombed in succession.

The situation was extremely critical.

“But this isn’t my city, right? I only came to make money!” Tan Mo cried in his thoughts. He drank his wine while he wiped the bloodstains off his face.

“This is not my responsibility, it is not my city, it is not my affair.”

“When I was attacked by pirates on the sea, I fell unconscious due to injuries, therefore, I had no choice but to come to Snow Bird Harbor to recuperate.”

“I have nothing to do with any of this, I am a victim, my injuries are very heavy, I have been secretly recovering. When the pirates attacked, I was in a deep sleep and didn’t know what was happening.”

“But the pirates were too noisy, and I was awoken by my subordinates’ reports.”

“Despite my heavy injuries, I am part of the navy, and I cannot turn a blind eye to the atrocities and sins before my eyes.”

“Therefore, I risked my life and dragged out my wounded body. Yes, this is important! In future reports this must be emphasized. I went to battle in the later stages of it.”

“My injuries were too heavy, and I hadn’t recovered well enough yet. Therefore, don’t blame my “halfhearted effort”, I had a terrible loss and cutting my losses at the right time is the right way of things.”

“Oh, I made the mistake of accidentally saying the truth.”

“In short, I will still go into battle.”

“Due to my injuries, I am barely able to fight.”

“What style is this? This is the noble style of the Sheng Ming Empire; I cannot bear to see sin run amuck and need to carry out naval justice. This is the Tan Tu Clan’s family customs, and I am one with the empire’s honor!”

“Alright, this is the report that will be sent, it’ll be enough to get at least an oral commendation?”

“As for the naval losses, am I able to cover it up?”

His avaricious personality let Tan Mo decide to stop his losses at the right time!

Therefore, when he regained his true identity and went into the battle, this is what he did:

Tan Mo went on stage with excessive grandeur and shouted to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, “City Lord, I have come to help you!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord coughed out, “You finally came!”

The city lord had complex feelings.

Despite Tan Mo forcing his submission, despite sinking into this plight because of Tan Mo, and despite him just beating Tan Mo up earlier (truth be told, he found it invigorating), right now, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord still looked forward to Tan Mo fighting.

Ding.

A moment later, Tan Mo was swept and tossed away into the distance by Cong Mang’s rod.

Tan Mo struggled to get up, he was kneeling, and his face was deathly white, but his expression was still firm, and his battle intent clearly remained.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: ?!

He realized things were anything but reassuring.

Tan Mo charged again and was once more flung away.

Tan Mo rose and fell time and again, fighting despite continual setbacks.

Tan Mo’s yells began to grow hoarse as he was soaked in blood, yet Tan Mo still fought on bravely.

His contribution to the battlefield was horrendous, and his most prominent thing was his “performance”.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord completely understood what Tan Mo’s plan was.

The help he originally placed his hope in actually turned up like this!!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord for a moment, simply wanted to spit blood. He bellowed in his transmission to Tan Mo, “What are you doing? What are you thinking?!!”

Tan Mo faintly responded on the sly, “City Lord, don’t be so hasty.”

“Don’t forget, when I requested to enter the harbor, my application clearly stated: I was seriously wounded in a naval battle and will be recovering in the harbor.”

“I…I…you…” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was furious, and he wanted to curse, but due to his anger, he was stammering.

Tan Mo was not worried about what Snow Bird Harbor City Lord thought of him.

The two of them had signed a contract, and he had information against Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, he could certainly destroy him!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord inhaled constantly, shouting from the bottom of his heart. “I need to calm down! Keep calm!”

He knew he couldn’t beat Cong Mang.

This pirate leader was impressively strong and was well known on the seas for charging in first, he was not weak.

Adding on that hidden third party, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord found that his best way forward was to win over Tan Mo’s true cooperation.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could only deal with Cong Mang while transmitting to Tan Mo.

Tan Mo sent a warning, “Be careful!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord saw a shadow flashing before him.

A moment later, Cong Mang’s long rod fell from the sky and smashed into his head.

The battle qi protecting Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s body loudly crumbled apart.

He howled in pain and was smacked through the air like a ball.

After falling to the ground, he wanted to stand up, only to meet Cong Mang’s next attack, forcing him to roll across the ground in sorry manner.

Tan Mo shouted and stepped forth to lend a hand.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord seized the opportunity to stand back up.

His head was broken.

Blood leaked from the top of his head, dripping past his forehead.

His eyes went dark for a spell.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord promptly took out a potion and drank it.

Tan Mo at that moment transmitted, “[Emancipator]! During battle, don’t be distracted. Fight well, this is your city, and this battle is depending on you.”

Pop.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord couldn’t stand it anymore, and he angrily coughed out blood, including the medicine he just consumed.

“Ahh!” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord furiously shouted, his hair disheveled and body covered in injuries.

He was extremely angry and irate.

Not bothering to drink the rest of the medicine, he threw himself at Cong Mang.

In this bout, his style suddenly changed to one similar to Cong Mang, he no longer dodged and weaved and was now fighting to the death with all his strength.

Seeing this crucial point, Tan Mo withdrew.

The two feverishly and wildly risked it all, each standing their ground and rarely evading as they attacked each other with all their strength.

“Amazing, truly amazing.” Tan Mo couldn’t help but draw a cold breath at this.

The incomparably fierce contest violently grinded at the two’s battle qi, magic, and divine power.

Pow.

With a sound, both the long rod and the short rod could no longer bear their burdens, and after countless intense collisions, they crumbled apart.
