The head of Cong Mang’s rod broke, while Snow Bird Harbor City Lord tossed away his useless short rod, continuing to fight with the remaining one.

“Strong enough, this is strong enough, hahaha.” Cong Mang grew increasingly intoxicated as he progressively displayed the bearing of a battle maniac.

“Die, go and die!” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord constantly harped on, his dreadful gaze was full of animosity, like he wanted to eat him.

But the two’s combat strength began to rapidly wane.

They were tired.

Stamina, battle intent, and transcendent energy had been depleted too severely and fell to rock bottom.

Seeing this, Tan Mo’s eyes shined, and he began to get restless.

“The crucial moment is almost here.” The dragonman youngster mumbled to himself.

Standing next to him, Hun Tong suddenly quivered, thinking, “Long Fu finally wants to tidy up the battlefield. But there was still one big worry, the saint level Tan Mo’s Treasure Chest was hiding. Up until now, they haven’t appeared, I wonder if they are still alive?”

Dao Hen and Hun Tong had been on the Treasure Chest for a long time and were quite familiar with the happenings on the ship.

But later on, Hun Tong and Dao Hen were deployed to the capital, and they weren’t able to keep investigating the Treasure Chest.

The dragonman youngster shook his head, extended his fingers, and clenched them, feeling his power.

In Hun Tong’s field of view, the dragonman youngster’s body suddenly lit up.

The luminosity was immense, and the sting of it forced him to narrow his eyes.

When the radiance dissipated, Long Fu’s form changed dramatically.

His towering body only became 1.6 meters, and he looked heavy and obese.

Layers of red dragon scales had changed into dark blue fish scales. The fish scales had a natural black ripple to them, forming the look of waves.

The once taut muscle lines became soft, and one could see increased fat superseding muscle.

The mighty dragon head turned into a fish head, the eyes became gigantic and split on both sides of the skull, and the pupils were as pure black as the abyss.

Hun Tong’s heart shook in the wake of that, and he unavoidably thought, “Could this be Long Fu’s true identity?!”

In a split second, he thought of Dao Hen, the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan, and of the many other indicators and clues.

The fishman youngster took a light breath, lowered his webbed fishman palms, and began to cast.

Spell – Bear’s Strength. Substantial Strength Enhancement.

Spell – Cat’s Agility. Substantial Dexterity Enhancement.

Spell – Leopard’s Speed. Substantial Movement Enhancement.

…

Divine spell – Strengthen Body. Comprehensive medium ranged constitution increase, including strength, endurance, recovery power, physical power, and other attributes.

Divine spell – Halo of Intellect. Increases intelligence and insight, making casters smarter and resourceful.

Divine spell – Lifeform Blessing. Substantially increases self-healing.

…

Ten divine and magic spells in a row enhancing the fishman youngster caused his personal transcendent aura to rise little by little.

Hun Tong was dumbfounded by everything he saw with his eyes!

Shock was across his entire face.

“How can he be enhanced by so many magic and divine spells?”

Enhancement divine and magic spells were doubtlessly good things, but a transcendent couldn’t enhance themselves infinitely.

It was like stacking iron sheets on top of a brick. The more iron sheets were added, the heavier the burden on the brick. There would come a day when the iron sheets would crush the brick.

No matter what kind of enhancement was used, it would temporarily boost a transcendent with external power.

A transcendent naturally needed to bear the burden of it.

Overburdening oneself would endanger a transcendent’s life.

Chapter 908: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – Shock!

In real life, even during fights to the death, transcendents would cautiously pick some limited methods of enhancing themselves. Because even a small burden could affect their displayable combat strength.

This impact was immense, and there were countless cases that told numerous transcendents that they had to treat enhancement methods prudently and not to covet them, as the gains don’t make up for the losses.

However now, the fishman youngster had smashed Hun Tong’s understanding.

Of course, Hun Tong didn’t think the fishman youngster was being foolish. If the fishman youngster was that stupid, he wouldn’t have been able to control the battlefield to this extent.

Therefore, there was only one answer.

It was that the fishman youngster had an insane tolerance and could bear so many enhancement methods.

And what kind of existence could do this?

Hun Tong was well aware, “A saint level bloodline!”

“Despite Long Fu only being a gold level now, his bloodline quality is obviously very high, it is at least saint level and has remarkable concentration.”

“It is because of that he is able to have such a formidable burden capacity and can simultaneously use so many magic and divine enhancement spells.”

In a split second, the fishman youngster became different in Hun Tong’s eyes.

“So long as he continues to grow, he will become a saint level in the future!” Hun Tong gasped in surprise.

Magic spell – Ring of Fire Barrier. A ring of fire instantly formed and revolved around the fishman youngster. So long as it was triggered, it could explode with immense tempestuous power in all directions, accompanied by scorching flames.

Magic spell – Whirlpool Shield. A water barrier covered the fishman youngster’s entire body, giving him omnidirectional protection.

Magic spell – Wind Wings. Light green wind elemental wings appeared half a meter behind the fishman youngster’s shoulder blades. When flapped they produced a speed as fast as a gale for him.

…

Divine spell – Saintly Healing Light. The fishman youngster’s scales began to glisten with white starry specks, and if he was injured, they would gather together and heal the wound vigorously.

Divine spell – Shadow Shelter. Underneath the fishman youngster’s feet, a shadow possessing defensive properties appeared. During a crucial moment, it could instantly cover his body and take a mortal blow for the young man.

Divine spell – Elemental Attractiveness. After the fishman youngster was enhanced by this, external natural elements would be greatly attracted to him, increasing his elemental absorption rate several-fold.

…

Hun Tong’s chin looked like a suspended anvil, his mouth wide open.

He saw the fishman youngster was armed to the teeth! His entire body was wreathed with all types of magic, divine spells, white starlight, a flaming ring, a blue water barrier, green wind wings, and more. They reflected each other in a neat and tidy manner.

Hun Tong was utterly stunned!

“This…”

“There is no way Long Fu just has a saint level bloodline.”

“He might actually be more…a legend level!”

“He is a legend level seed!”

“The saint level is not his limit; he is bound to be part of the legend level world.”

The fishman youngster’s transcendent aura increased sharply again, like it was a majestic peak slowly rising.

He was pleased to see Hun Tong’s changing expression as the latter took in the whole scene.

The reason why he changed in front of Hun Tong was to firmly awe the latter and change the impression in Hun Tong’s heart.

“Although I can still add more enhancements, there is no need to.”

“This is more than enough.”

The fishman youngster’s Surging Wave fishman form had already far surpassed the dragonman bloodline, it was a 100% god level bloodline.

In other words, his end point was the god level.

As for the dragonman bloodline, it was inherently the Flame Dragon King Bloodline. However, because the bloodline concentration was too low, there were many human characteristics remaining. If his bloodline concentration became high enough, then the young man wouldn’t be a dragonman but a gigantic dragon form.

The fishman youngster’s Surging Wave fishman form was strong enough to bear the weight of divinity, carrying these magic and divine spells was absolutely not a problem.

The fishman youngster’s mind stirred, and he slowly flew into the air.

Of the magics and divine spells on his body, at least three were helping him do this.

In the air, he undid several deception divine spells.

When that happened, his aura could finally be felt.

“Who is that?!” The embattled Tan Mo, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and Cong Mang seemed to be shocked similarly, their expression radically changed and they stopped fighting.

The fierce all out brawl came to a grunting stop.

It was simply because the fishman youngster’s transcendent aura was simply too powerful!

Despite only being a gold level, his transcendent aura was a boundless tempest. Compared to him, Cong Mang’s and the other’s auras were like fireflies to a light bulb.

“A gold level, but very strong. No, extraordinarily strong!” Cong Mang, the pirate leader with an irascible temper, was presently looking grave.

“The third party finally acted? So many enhancement methods, that, isn’t that too outrageous?” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s mouth and tongue were dry as he looked up at the dazzling figure in the air, it looked similar to Feng Lian.

“Damn it, there really was a hidden hand. Oh dear! If the secret saint level passenger were still here, how good it’d be!” Tan Mo was on the tenterhooks.

The fishman youngster hadn’t moved, yet his majestic transcendent aura had already intimidated the three gold levels.

Soon after, the fishman youngster slowly extended his palm, the center of which was aimed at Tan Mo.

“Hey, hey, hey, why do you want to deal with me first?!” Tan Mo felt the target on him, and he rushed away in retreat with astonishing speed.

At that crucial moment, he didn’t dare to keep up his performance.

Bloop.

With a light echo, a slender dark blue stream of water that appeared deceptively slow, rapidly streaked through the sky towards the springing Tan Mo.

Tan Mo was wildly shifting his position as he fled, assuming a random sidestepping running style.

But the stream of water followed his zig zagging, and even winded around all kinds of obstacles, then smoothly hit Tan Mo in the back.

Tan Mo shouted, activating all of his equipment and using his strongest defensive battle skill.

Ding!

An explosion that filled the entire sky with mist. Blood splattered, and Tan Mo looked like a dead dog as he was tossed a distance, then was smashed into the ground.

There was a massive cut in his back, almost cutting through his entire body. His backbone was completely gone, and watery blood was wildly bubbling out.

The defensive alchemy items used were totally ruptured and destroyed.

As Tan Mo sensed the signs of death, he subconsciously bit down on a modified tooth.

The tooth shattered, and life saving healing medicine quickly too effect, suspending him in life.

One blow, just one blow was enough to bring Tan Mo to the edge of death!

The fishman youngster’s offensive magic was obviously the ordinary water type magic [Water Stream]. But due to his incredibly enhanced state and god level fishman bloodline, it led to this average and ordinary Water Stream spell reaching a horrifying level of power!

When Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Cong Mang saw this devastation, their battle intent vanished.

The two glanced at each other, no longer thinking of carving the hated other into pieces.

They could see the other’s notion, that was to flee!

Thus, a moment later, Cong Mang and Snow Bird Harbor City Lord suddenly launched out in opposite directions, evacuating with great speed.

The fishman youngster laughed.

He gazed at Snow Bird Harbor City Lord in the distance.

Divine spell – Deterrence spell!

This type of divine spell was similar to Dragon Prestige; it utilizes a superior lifeform and transcendent aura to deter. A good effect could cause an enemy to freeze and be taken advantage of. A bad result could at least affect an enemy, deforming their movement and diminishing their battle intent.

The incorporeal Deterrence spell instantly covered Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

A moment later, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord felt his entire soul being crushed, like a giant mountain was suddenly crushing him.

He was unable to adjust his movement and could only watch helplessly as he ran into a wall.

He was a gold level, and it took running through three walls before he fell to the ground.

An instant defeat!

The fishman youngster’s second attack was more outstanding than his first.

“Is it because of bloodline enhancement?” The fishman youngster instantly understood why.

His Surging Wave fishman bloodline was the race’s strongest bloodline in the divine spell system, and it immensely enhanced divine spells.

This kind of amplification far surpassed the magic and divine spells enhancing him.

From this point, one could make out how important bloodlines were to transcendents!

Finally, there was Cong Mang.

This time, the fishman youngster decided to do the job himself.

He thought it, and space around him shook, forming a rippling circular wave.

Like he was merging with water, he squeezed into the spatial ripple and vanished from the air.

A moment later, he abruptly appeared in front of Cong Mang.

“Fuck off!” Cong Mang shrieked, and using all of his strength, he used his most powerful battle skill and formidable divine spell.

Battle skill – Great Evil Rod!

Divine spell – Divine Wrath!

Gold battle qi covered the broken long rod, swelling its volume several-fold, and he ferociously swung it down.

The Ring of Fire was first to resist it and was triggered.

A fierce air wave, mixed with scorching flames quickly swept all around.

Cong Mang was pushed back a step, most of the gold battle qi dissipating around his rod, but it still remained strong, breaking through this defense and continuing its attack trajectory.

Then it was the divine spell [Electric Chainmail].

Lightning shot out and curled upwards, bursting across the long rod’s battle qi, dissipating it completely, the long rod beginning to crack and burn.

Finally, the spell [Whirlpool Shield].

The water shield was thumped by the charred black rod, its surface forming a fierce whirlpool that wanted to disperse the collision’s power.

A moment later, the water shield collapsed into countless droplets that sprayed in all directions.

The long rod’s attack had broken through the limits of its defenses, but [Whirlpool Shield] displayed its deserving characteristic – it perfectly dissipated the long rod’s firepower and the fishman youngster wasn’t battered by it.

Ding.

The blackened long rod ruptured apart with a brittle crunch.

Cong Mang was covered head to toe in sprouts of burning fire (Ring of Fire), his muscles twitched with flickering lightning (Electric Chainmail), and his chest was covered in scarlet cuts (created when Whirlpool Shield burst apart).

All of his power was placed into that attack, and naturally he wasn’t balancing his defense.

Cong Mang’s transcendent aura fell to rock bottom.

On the contrary, the fishman youngster hadn’t even moved from where he stood.

Because Whirlpool Shield was dispelled, his true colors were exposed.

He gazed indifferently, “Pretty good, pretty good, you destroyed three levels of my transcendent defenses.”

His body still had a profusion of dazzling and brilliant colors; there were still at least 20 layers of transcendent defenses left.

Cong Mang glowered at the fishman youngster and was just about to bawl out something.

When suddenly, a hand formed from condensed water reached out and grabbed Cong Mang by the nape, lifting him up.

Cong Mang’s legs gradually left the ground, and he had a hard time breathing. With a red face and his shattered rod abandoned, he tried to push the giant water hand off of him.

He wanted to resist and throw off the bind that was gradually choking him, but the strike he just made took almost all of his remaining battle qi.

Although he still had some divine power left, he was unable to mobilize it – the fishman youngster effortlessly restrained him.

“I didn’t expect that I would die here! Cong Mang’s vision went black for a moment.

The fishman youngster faintly laughed, then the space behind him rippled again.

The ripple expanded, and the fishman youngster turned around and entered, vanishing.

Soon after, the big water hand holding Cong Mang also entered the spatial ripple.

The two disappeared together.

Thus, the battle of Snow Bird Harbor ended.

Chapter 909: Island Arrivals

Ice Sculpture Island.

One of the eight trading harbors – Munitions Harbor.

The pirate boss Hong Pa was sitting on a large chair, his mouth biting a watermelon.

He was sitting on his flagship’s deck, gazing at Munitions Harbor in the distance.

Munitions Harbor was already on guard, the magician tower was emitting a vast light, and a giant magic formation was activated which raised a magical light wall.

The light wall was as multicolored as an aurora, and it extended from the earth to the heavens, intercepting all enemy attacks.

The Dread Firepower Pirates led by Hong Pa were being blocked by this light wall.

“First round of bombardment, prepare the Baby Bird Mushroom Cloud shells.” The Dread Firepower Pirates’ first mate was located on the bow house, his hand holding an alchemy device, and directing the bombardment on behalf of the commander.

The Firepower Pirates’ ships were already in a single file line, with every cannon of all sizes and lengths densely packed and concentrated together.

Bang, bang, bang…

A moment later, artillery shells fired, and smoke sprung up everywhere.

Not all cannons were firing, only the longest ones.

After roughly 50 shells were discharged, steel wings popped out from the shells’ sides. The steel wings vibrated in the air, producing a warbling-like noise.

The artillery shells flew high into the air, then began to follow a full parabola as they slowly descended.

They all hid the multicolored light wall.

Boom, boom, boom…

Each artillery shell explosion issued an ear-splitting sound.

In the huge air waves, a white mushroom cloud quickly rose.

After the smoke dissipated, the multicolored light wall thinned by one third, yet it still remained massive.

“Munitions Harbor’s magician tower combined with seven different defensive magic formations to create the large-scale magic [Great Aurora Wall], its defense really is astonishing!” The first mate sighed ruefully.

After sighing, he calmed down, and continued to order, “Second round of bombardment, fire the Python Shot.”

This time, two long cannons fired simultaneously.

Approximately three hundred Python Shots exploded into the air, transforming into gigantic lightning pythons that charged into the [Great Aurora Wall].

These pythons looked like giant sea monsters that were constantly churning and stirring inside the great wall.

The [Great Aurora Wall] exploded with intense radiance, encircling and subduing the lighting pythons in it.

After a bout, the lighting pythons dissipated into nothingness.

The pirate first mate’s eyes flashed sharply, “[The Great Aurora Wall] has been weakened by two thirds.”

“Then let’s put in our energy into the third round.”

He suddenly raised his voice, ordering through an alchemy device, “Third round of bombardment, Space Breaker Shot, fire!”

This time, the short cannons were fired too.

Countless ball shaped artillery shells were loaded into the cannons, then fired into the air.

After they smashed into the [Great Aurora Wall], they instantly collapsed.

There was no gunpowder explosions, rather innumerable ball shaped spatial cracks began to quickly form.

The clumps of spatial cracks connected with each other and joined together. Quantity turned into a qualitative change, creating an immense spatial crevice.

The spatial cracks covered the [Great Aurora Wall], its light had reached its darkest and its transcendent aura was at its weakest.

But at that moment, magic power drained from the magician tower at a frightful rate, imbuing it into the [Great Aurora Wall].

The [Great Aurora Wall]’s depressed state was cleansed away, and it once again became vast, thick, and dazzlingly radiant.

The first mate was flabbergasted, “This, this…”

Clack, clack.

The pirate commander Hong Pa finally finished his watermelon, and he stood up clapping his hands.

The multicolored aurora illuminated his pig face.

His face was shrouded with displeasure and bewilderment, “Didn’t you say that after [Great Aurora Wall] is formed, it cannot be replenished afterwards?”

The fear from the alchemy device carrying the fist mate’s voice was palatable, “Lord, Lord Commander, this should be the casting technique [Spell Refresh]. I didn’t expect the Ice Sculpture Kingdom snow elves to be capable of applying a personal casting technique to an entire defensive magic formation.”

Hong Pa sighed, “I don’t care about any casting technique, the reason why we chose to stir up Munitions Harbor was because you said to first mate.”

The first mate promptly replied, “Right, according to my analysis, our group’s robust firepower, we were absolutely able to effortlessly deplete and dispel the [Great Aurora Wall]. Then, we could loot this trading harbor specialized in mainly arms to make ourselves stronger.”

“This was utterly unexpected, this was a lapse in information, not I!”

Hong Pa sneered, “When we came here, we made an agreement. You proposed we attack this place, yet currently, the outermost shell hasn’t even cracked. First mate, you should go.”

“No, no, Lord Commander, spare my life, please, please give me another chance!!” The first mate rigidly clenched the alchemy communication device, loudly begging for forgiveness.

But a moment later, the captain’s room door suddenly pushed open, and a few burly water pigmen pirate fiends walked out.

The first mate’s face was covered in fear, and without a trace of resistance, he slumped to the floor.

The water pigmen pirates grabbed the first mate’s legs and ragged him out of the captain’s room and to the rear of a cannon.

The first mate’s tears were overflowing as he frantically begged for forgiveness.

But the pirates remained unmoved, they opened the cannon and stuffed the first mate into it.

Boom!

Not long after, the first mate was shot out like a special artillery shell.

The first mate screamed as he streaked across the sky, then smashed his head into the Great Aurora Wall.

He was material, and the physically hard Great Aurora Wall stopped him.

A moment later, the aurora bubbled and completely melted the first mate.

Hong Pa glanced at the [Aurora Great Wall]. Then sighed, “Little ones, retreat.”

The Dread Firepower Pirates were following the Icebound Pirates and had participated in naval battles.

After the Li Jian Clan’s second naval fleet retreated in defeat, the pirate alliance dispersed into parts, Cong Mang’s Vegetable Pirates was one part, the Dread Firepower Pirates were another. There were other pirate groups like that too.

Pirates were not a regular army, and even the saint level Da Han found it difficult to constrain them.

As a matter of fact, he wouldn’t do that.

Because he understood the greedy nature of pirates. If he made a hardline demand, of course, he could restrict them, after all when faced with a saint level, who wouldn’t be afraid of dying?

But if that happened, then Da Ha’s reputation with the pirates would stink to high heaven. In future operations, it would be difficult for other pirates to willingly attach themselves to him.

Hong Pa decided to attack Munitions Harbor at his first mate’s strong suggestion.

They were unable to pierce the outermost defenses.

Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s eight great trading harbors were always important defensive regions. It would be a superb challenge for the Dread Firepower Pirates to storm it, and even after paying a disastrous price, they might not even succeed.

Therefore, Hong Pa chose to retreat.

Retreating put the pirates in a bad mood, and failing to gain a golden opportunity to pillage made them more frustrated.

Luckily, Hong Pa had dealt with the first mate ahead of time, and that would quell much of the public outrage to a degree.

“It seems we aren’t strong enough to force our way into the eight great harbor cities.”

“The most convenient thing to do is to follow behind the Icebound Pirates and draw support from Da Han’s strength.”

“Regretfully, the Icebound Pirates refuse any follower requests and have sternly ordered us to depart, saying anyone who followed them was their enemy.”

When Hong Pa thought that he couldn’t help but sigh as his heart filled with regret.

…

Da Han walked through the wilderness.

His movement speed was very normal, but after being enhanced with a time divine spell, his journey went by several-fold faster.

He was alone, he abandoned all of his Icebound Pirate subordinates, and none followed him.

Choosing solitude masked his whereabouts greatly.

He wore transcendent artifacts that provided him powerful invisibility, but they couldn’t compare to deception divine spells.

“These gold level camouflage devices cannot help me infiltrate the Ice Sculpture Capital.”

“To enter the Ice Sculpture Capital, I have to penetrate the ice lake and wait for an opportunity.”

“The National Ceremony Grand Duel is that opportunity!”

Da Han’s sight was profound as he walked alone.

…

The radiance of a teleportation spell violently dissipated in three short seconds.

After space stabilized, three people appeared from nowhere.

It was headed by Teacher Si Ling, who was followed by Cang Xu and Jian Gui.

“Where are we?” Cang Xu looked at a valley, despite not being a mortal being, he could still feel the frigid air.

That an undead lifeform could feel the cold proved how low the air temperature was.

Teacher Si Ling led the two out of this hidden cave.

Cang Xu and Jian Gui saw a snowfield.

Large snowflakes swirled through the air, the cold wind screamed, and heaven and earth mixed with each other.

Teacher Si Ling said, “This is the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s hinterland.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom?” Cang Xu was amazed.

Jian Gui was puzzled, “My Lord, why have we come here?”

“As a matter of fact, this is precisely my true destination for this trip. Follow me, there shall be a grand opera staged here.”

…

Plop.

The fishman youngster mentally controlled the large water hand to throw the captured Cong Mang onto the ground.

It was a forest outside the city.

Great numbers of pine trees stood in the blanketing cold, and there wasn’t much life around.

Cong Mang violently coughed, he had nearly been choked to death.

Having narrowly avoided death, he breathed as his life depended on it, still out of it and unable to reflect on why this mysterious and powerful enemy decided to let him go.

A moment later, a Wisteria Secret Order was thrown into the snowy turf.

When Cong Mang saw the Wisteria Secret Order, it took a full dozen seconds for him to react. His eyes widened and he glowered at the fishman youngster, “You, you belong to the Wisteria Merchant Alliance? Why did you attack me?!”

Ding.

The fishman youngster calmly waved his water hand.

Cong Mang was flung into a pine tree, then fell to the ground.

He coughed again; blood was coming out.

Because of this severe strike, branches and snow fell, covering Cong Mang and serving as a backdrop to how miserable he was.

The fishman youngster slowly approached.

After this, Cong Mang completely sobered up and recognized reality, “Hold on, wait a minute, we can talk about this!”

“I have always been working with your Wisteria Merchant Alliance; this you should know.”

The fishman youngster looked down, dully stating, “I understand that you received the merchant alliance’s help, but in the end, you didn’t contribute your strength, that proves your loyalty. You betrayed the merchant alliance.”

“No, when I accepted the subsidy, I was not subordinate to the Wisteria Merchant Alliance…ah.” Cong Mang shrieked.

Before he could finish speaking, the fishman youngster kicked him in the belly.

Cong Mang spat out blood and turned deathly white; the sharp pain caused him to look like a cooked lobster as he rigidly curled up into a ball.

After gasping for breath for a good while, Cong Mang recovered.

His eyes were dark, and his mind was abuzz. At this moment, then fishman youngster’s voice seemed to come from the distant heavens, “Those who betray the merchant alliance shall suffer punishment.”

“Your pirate group received the merchant alliance’s aid and grew from it.”

“Now, I used an explosion to collect some of that investment back.”

“As for you, you are also part of our merchant alliance’s investment…”

Cong Mang used a bloodstained hand to grab hold of the fishman youngster’s leather boot, and after drawing in several cold breaths, he croaked out, “I…I was wrong.”

“My Lord, give me another chance.”

“Let me correct my error, I will change!”

The fishman youngster’s tone eased somewhat, “Decent awareness.”

Cong Mang clutched at his last hope, hurriedly insisting, “I can create more value alive than dead, your Lordship!”

The fishman youngster grunted, “As a matter of fact, the higher-ups did say to give you one final chance.”

“The higher-ups?” Cong Mang anxiously gazed up.

He came to a horrifying realization, the fishman expert was actually not the supreme leader. There were still people above him!

A moment later, the fishman youngster opened his webbed fish hand and clutched Cong Mang’s shaven head, lifting him up.

“Activating the Wisteria Secret Order can indeed allow you to demonstrate your value.” The fishman youngster said.

Cong Mang swiftly stated, “I will do my utmost, your Lordship!”

“Good attitude.” The fishman youngster slowly nodded, “In that case, welcome to Ice Sculpture Island.”

Chapter 910: Capturing the Divine Bone

Snow Bird Harbor.

Inside a private house, there was a vast space.

This was where the fishman youngster used Divine Domain Descent.

Even if the magician tower hadn’t been blown up, it wouldn’t be able to detect the divine domain inside here.

Therefore, this place was very secure.

Of course, that wasn’t to say no magician tower was able to threaten the divine domain, as it would depend on its quality, level, and so on.

Hun Tong, Da Dou, the big guy, Hong Zhu, and Cong Mang got together.

The fishman youngster’s body glistened with blue light.

The blue light was striking at first, then soon waned, allowing the fishman youngster to transform back into a dragonman form.

Although the young man used the Bloodcore to change his form, he simultaneously used deception divine spells.

This was intentional.

In everyone’s eyes, it would unavoidably form a false conjecture: the fishman form was the young man’s true identity, while the dragonman appearance was a disguise.

This conjecture coming about was quite natural, especially because everyone present had been empowered with deception divine spells before.

It went without saying for Hun Tong, as deception divine spells had aided his spying on Tan Mo’s Treasure Chest and after making landfall on Snow Bird Harbor.

Cong Mang and his pirate gang had snuck into Snow Bird Harbor under the protection of deception divine spells.

Da Dou and Hong Zhu had seen the big guy unleash divine spells and were very familiar with the aura of that divine power. At the same time, during the Snow Bird Harbor Battle, they wantonly plundered the city palace while under the protection of deception divine spells.

The dragonman youngster didn’t explain anything and allowed them to hypothesize on their own.

The dragonman youngster slowly sat down.

As he moved, the divine domain space instantly condensed a high and expansive chair from divine power.

The chair slowly rose from the ground, gradually forming a throne.

The dragonman youngster sat with his limbs spread wide across the throne, looking down apathetically as he examined everyone.

“Was that Long Fu’s true identity?” Hun Tong had mixed emotions as he gazed up at the cold and detached dragonman youngster.

Hong Zhu had a similar impression as him.

Hong Zhu had worked with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps on Two Eyes Island. During the beast tide invasion, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps established close ties and friendships with many silver level experts.

At this very moment, the dragonman youngster’s strange posture and expression shattered Hong Zhu’s old memories.

She knelt down and used a reverent and appreciative tone to shout, “Lord Long Fu, you are Hong Zhu’s lifesaving benefactor. I request to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and ask that you accept my vow of loyalty and devotion! From now on, your orders shall be my objectives!”

Hong Zhu knelt down and did so candidly!

Everyone present was unsurprised.

This was the world of the strong.

Powerful transcendents naturally had enormous charisma in attracting subordinates.

The stronger one was, the more charisma they had.

Because in history, countless examples taught the world and formed a universal consensus – experts were the true foundation of any power or organization. Only by following the strong could one achieve twice the result with half the effort, save the gains of victory, and have enough to brave dangers.

With the fishman form, the young man displayed the beauty of an expert and had deeply shaken everyone present.

Da Dou, Hun Tong, and Cong Mang had all seen how effortlessly the fishman youngster had swept away three gold levels and cleaned up the entire battlefield.

If the three gold levels were that shaken, then the silver level Hong Zhu would be even more so.

It wasn’t just awe; the young man had also saved their lives.

After all, the fishman youngster’s divine spell aura was identical to the big guy’s Divine Descent divine spell.

Combined with the big guy’s deferential bearing right now, as well as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ authority, Hong Zhu already realized that within the unknown god’s subordinates, the fishman youngster was of higher status than the big guy!

There was also prior friendships, and Hong Zhu had plundered an immense profit from the entire city palace this time too…

These things combined made Hong Zhu willing to submit to the dragonman youngster cheerfully.

The dragonman youngster looked down at Hong Zhu, faintly laughing, “On Two Eyes Island, I could tell that you were an excellent treasure hunter, Hong Zhu. Because you had a pair of bright eyes that could find what the most valuable treasures were.”

The dragonman youngster ended with that, then swept his gaze over the others with deep intent.

But Da Dou and the others all had different expressions, none declared their position like Hong Zhu.

As for the big guy, he was no longer under Divine Descent and his bright eyes were fixed on the dragonman youngster, the corner of his mouth open in a silent and foolish laugh.

Seeing his father again made him extremely happy.

But due to the dragonman youngster’s repeated warnings, he stood aside not moving or speaking.

“Xiao Guai is obedient.”

“Xiao Guai will listen to father!” The big guy constantly cycled that thought in his mind.

The dragonman youngster turned back around, looking at Hong Zhu again, “Let me see what you gained from the city palace.”

Before Cong Mang’s Vegetable Pirates stormed the city palace, Da Dou and the other two had ransacked it.

Hong Zhu suddenly stood up excitedly, “Lord Long Fu, this time…we had an extremely generous harvest, an incredible haul!”

She stuttered somewhat in excitement.

Hong Zhu took out what she had taken and piled it up on the ground.

Da Dou also emptied his Packing Bag’s contents onto the ground.

The big guy was still looking foolish.

The dragonman youngster glanced at him, then used a divine spell to transmit his voice into his mind.

The big guy yelped, then began to take out his stuff too.

Gold coins were the most of it and was heaped into a small hill.

These were gold coins of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and were obviously money circulated inside its markets.

“Most of them were from the tax money storehouse.” Hong Zhu aptly presented.

The dragonman youngster indifferently nodded, of course he knew as his attention had been divided the entire time, participating through Divine Descent on the big guy.

But he didn’t show it.

He had already revealed a few secrets and wanted the rest to remain hidden.

The dragonman youngster wanted others to think that the big guy was receiving Divine Descent from a god and not from a gold level like him.

The level of deterrence between the two was like the difference between heaven and earth.

Because it was inside the divine domain, the dragonman youngster didn’t need to do inventory, with merely a glance, he could see the amount of gold coins was precisely 276,587 coins.

It was obvious this was a massive sum of money.

The small hill of gold coins firmly clutched Cong Mang’s eyes, as a pirate, he was most fond of these little cute things.

But very quickly, his eyes were drawn to a pile of ice cubes to the side of the gold coin hill.

These ice cubes were of different sizes, with the largest being the size of a teapot and the smallest the size of a child’s fist.

But their shape was consistent, all of them looked like a heart.

“Ice Hearts, these are actually Ice Hearts!!” Cong Mang exclaimed in his mind.

It was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s special local resource.

Ice Hearts had broad uses. People could swallow it raw; it could be used as a main ingredient for magic medicine (Ice Head Flower medicine was the most well-known type) and could also be made into all kinds of alchemy items (the Ice Headband was a classic product).

“Wasn’t it said the Ice Heart output was very low? How could Snow Bird Harbor have so many?” Cong Mang’s doubt was shared by Da Dou and Hong Zhu.

When Da Dou and Hong Zhu noticed them, they were quite amazed, almost believing they were seeing things.

The main reason was that there were so many Ice Hearts.

With regards to this, the dragonman youngster knew the truth, and as if seeing everyone’s doubts, he stated, “The kingdom’s Ice Heart output has actually always been very high.”

“But the Ice Sculpture Kingdom deliberately falsifies information and puts on a façade that the Ice Heart has a low output.”

“Secretly, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom controls the Ice Hearts more stringently than the armor prohibition.”

“These Ice Hearts are not completely transparent; at most some are translucent and are not of the highest quality. The highest quality Ice Heart is in the Royal Family’s hands and is never circulated.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord personally told him this secret.

Everyone understood this.

The Ice Sculpture Royal Family amassing wealth while keeping a low profile was something they could comprehend.

To be more precise, the Ice Hearts were a type of elemental heart.

Elemental hearts were a rare material that were better than elemental crystals. Apart from the Four Great Elemental Planes, elemental hearts always had an extremely low output.

Ice Hearts, to be more accurate, should be called Ice Elemental Hearts.

The lowest quality of these Ice Elemental Hearts was the silver level, with a few being gold level.

They were very valuable.

More crucially, was that there was demand but no supply. Under normal circumstances, even if one had a lot of money, it was very difficult to purchase so many on the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s marketplace.

To the side of the Ice Hearts was a large stack of antiques. There were oil paintings, statues, and famous weapons from across Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s history, which only had ornamental value.

Cong Mang was uninterested in these things.

The Vegetable Pirate Commander’s scholarly knowledge was next to zero.

Compared to the others, Hong Zhu had a higher insight, even higher than the dragonman youngster’s. To her, these antiques were extremely valuable, no less than a small mountain of gold coins!

Hong Zhu sighed, “Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had a very deep noble demeanor, he had so many art pieces put up inside his city palace.”

“But we were able to quickly gather all of these things in a short time, it was all thanks to this bone!”

Hong Zhu raised her hand a bit.

Her palm opened, and inside it sat a bone.

The bone was deathly pale, and looked irregular, making it rather difficult to glean which part of the body it belonged to.

The bone did not have any transcendent aura, and it looked like an ordinary and common thing.

Coing Mang fixed his eyes on the bone, then suddenly thought of something, shuddering out, “Could that be the Fourth Pirate King’s Greed God Bone?”

The dragonman youngster grasped at nothing, teleporting the Greed Gone Bone to him.

After he grabbed it, he used a Charming Blue divine spell to examine it. A moment later, the Greed God Bone emitted an extremely faint gold level aura.

The dragonman youngster was puzzled.

At the time, when part of his mind descended on the big guy, he saw how solemnly cared for this divine bone was and knew this “ordinary” bone was anything but ordinary.

At the time, Hong Zhu reacted faster than him and quickly identified the divine bone’s history.

The Greed God Bone’s function was that it could detect and point towards any valuables within a range.

It was a supreme implement to a treasure hunter.

As a treasure hunter, Hong Zhu of course yearned for it day and night.

This was the reason why she could so quickly identify it.

Because she saw an ordinary bone being so seriously kept, that was the first answer she associated with it.

Neither Hong Zhu nor the dragonman youngster knew why Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had such a rare thing in his treasury.

Chapter 911: Youngster – See My Resurrection Spell

“The Greed God Bone…” The dragonman youngster could feel the divinity the bone contained.

The Greed divinity!

The dragonman youngster was not unfamiliar with this divinity.

This was thanks to Teacher Si Ling’s teachings to Cang Xu, which allowed the dragonman youngster to learn about this hidden knowledge too.

In Ocean Mother Nest, he also stole four Death Divinities under Teacher Si Ling’s nose.

“But this bone doesn’t contain the greed divinity, not even a tenth of it.” The dragonman youngster analyzed it deeper, gradually finding how much divinity the bone contained.

“Perhaps, one divinity was plenty.”

“Is the divinity inside the Greed God Bone the normal state of divinity?”

“As for Ocean Mother Nest’s death divinity, was it from countless years of accumulations in White Hair Trench?”

It was very evident that the dragonman youngster needed to gain access to more divinities so he could more accurately judge them.

“The Fourth Generation Pirate King and I should be in similar circumstances. In those days, he was a gold level but had a god level bloodline (concentration unknown).”

“He risked everything to take the Pirate Throne. Perhaps his god level bloodline concentration was high enough or perhaps the Pirate Throne spurred him on. Anyways, when he fell, his bones were smashed into countless pieces, all bearing his greed divinity.”

“With a sufficient god level bloodline concentration, one is completely able to grow the lifeform’s corresponding divinity.”

“The Fourth Generation Pirate King was plotted against and slain. He was only a gold level, and wasn’t able to grow, yet he possessed the greed divinity and can be said to be a special case.”

“The Ten Millennia Ice Lake’s time dragon corpse is considered to be normal. It possesses enough god level bloodline, and after growing to adulthood, it had the time divinity.”

“If I kill myself now, my corpse won’t have divinity. However, I can bear divinity, and if the death divinity in the [Mermaid Fairy Tale] was moved into me, and then I died, then perhaps it can create a corpse that possesses the death divinity.”

The dragonman youngster gathered his diverged thoughts and once again stared at the Greed God Bone in his hand.

This bone was half a divine artifact in Hong Zhu’s and the other’s eyes, while to the dragonman youngster, it was just a god level material.

The reason it had miraculous properties was completely due to the greed divinity.

“If I extracted the greed divinity, and loaded it into my body, would I have the ability to sense treasure?”

“The Greed God Bone’s treasure finding has a systemic problem, it will point towards all valuable things and not the most valuable item within its range. Even more, it does not choose a target based on the user’s wishes.”

“If I controlled the greed divinity, could I be an even more insightful treasure hunter?”

The dragonman youngster just thought about it and didn’t act on it.

The theory suggested that since he had a complete Surging Wave fishman bloodline, he was qualified to assume godhood and become a divine lifeform.

But he didn’t take the risk.

Because he knew divinity had a massive influence on a person. In his current situation, bearing the greed divinity, despite it only being a tenth of one, might turn a person into someone like Tan Mo. Or even someone more despicable than him.

His humanity and personality would seriously deviate, his worldview, values, and life outlook, among other traits would radically change to an extreme degree in a radical upheaval.

This risk was not one the dragonman youngster wanted to brave.

But regardless of what was said, the dragonman youngster having a complete god level bloodline gave him enough qualifications to use god level materials.

This was the embodiment of the immense progress he had made since returning from his journey.

The dragonman youngster put away the divine bone and took a look at the other gains.

Besides gold coins, antiques, and the divine bone, there were some other valuable materials.

The most eye-catching was a snow white dragon talon.

The dragon talon still emitted cold air, it was a gold level white dragon talon.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a hunting ban, and the royal family had a firm alliance with the White Dragon Clan, so white dragon parts were only circulated on the black market.

No one expected that Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would have that in his collection.

The dragonman youngster knew that it was taken from the city palace’s secret storehouse.

Good things that weren’t fit to be seen in the light were placed in it.

The white dragon talon had extensive applications, it could serve as a gold level weapon, the main material in an alchemy item, and if used for magic medicine, the white dragon talon could produce ten times its value.

But in the dragonman youngster’s hands, there was only one use – food for the Bloodcore to increase his white dragon bloodline concentration.

Apart from the white dragon talon, there were also ocean magic beast parts, mithril, essence gold, obscure gold, and other rare magic materials. Although these materials could be purchased on the market, they were always expensive.

As for the magnets, obsidian, and other common materials, although the city palace had quit the inventory of them, Da Dou and the others didn’t take them.

There simply wasn’t room!

Their carrying capacity was limited.

Therefore, silver and copper coins weren’t taken either. The three only took gold coins.

The dragonman youngster conservatively estimated that this massive haul of goods was worth around 800,000 gold coins. Naturally this didn’t include the Greed God Bone!

That kind of sum was comparable to what was earned from Two Eyes Island.

Snow Bird Harbor was worthy of being one of the eight great trading harbors; just the things from the city palace was worth this much. It also had to be considered that Da Dou and the others didn’t take everything, and the expansive city district also held astonishing wealth in it too.

The dragonman youngster didn’t want to loot the commoners. The reason why he dealt with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was mainly because the latter wanted to scheme against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Compared to these assets, the dragonman youngster valued the captives more.

Da Dou and the others were locked up in the prison, and besides them, there were some other prisoners too.

Furthermore, when Da Dou and the others went plundering, they had taken the city palace personnel prisoner, such as Mei Lan Du and the Magnet Archipelago secret spy leader.

“These captives can be deceived, modified, and turned into combat forces like Ku Feng and Xiao Wang Zi.”

“The secret spy’s memories are very valuable, and I need to come up with a reliable method of extracting that information.”

At this time, there was a knock on the door.

The dragonman youngster waved a hand, and all of the things on the ground vanished as the door opened.

From the doorway stepped in a short goblin, it was Tripleblade.

On the face of it, Tripleblade had walked into an ordinary building, white in reality, he had stepped into a divine domain.

His face flashed with astonishment; he didn’t expect the room to be a separate space.

“Three gold levels!”

“Da Dou is really here.”

“Hun Tong…I didn’t expect that the Two Eyes Island Lord would actually become one of us.”

“There is also the pirate boss Cong Mang, to so quickly acknowledge allegiance. Ha, don’t be fooled by his tough fighting, his moral character is flexible.”

Tripleblade also noticed Hong Zhu and the big guy.

He remembered Hong Zhu. However, that silver level expert wasn’t qualified anymore for Tripleblade to attach much value to.

As for the big guy, Tripleblade let out a breath, like a heavy burden was lifted.

The big guy was lost under his supervision, and Tripleblade was responsible for it. He felt rather guilty of neglecting his duty.

Now, the dragonman youngster had not just found and brought back the big guy, but he also disciplined and reduced the kidnapper Da Dou to a slave.

This was beyond Tripleblade’s wildest imaginations!

Carrying this appreciation and other complex emotions, Tripleblade stoops and bowed to the enthroned dragonman youngster, reporting, “Lord Long Fu, the tasks you gave have been handled.”

“Most of the traitors and spies that popped up during this battle have been executed, with a few taken prisoner, they are being brought over.”

“In addition, in accordance with your directives, I have brought the corpses of our dead comrades over…”

When that was said, Tripleblade’s tone couldn’t help but be somewhat heavy.

In this battle, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps suffered great losses.

“Bring in the bodies of these heroic fighters, lay them out neatly, I will resurrect them!” The dragonman youngster made an astonishing declaration.

Not long after, corpses were lined up in several rows, each of them had mortal wounds, some were comparatively intact, and others were badly damaged.

The dragonman youngster stood up, slightly bowed his head, and began to loudly pray.

“Grand God Mist, You are the God of the Ocean, and a fishman God.”

“Your divine power envelops the vast waters and the abyss, Your radiance illuminates all things, Your mercy and Your power are equally infinite.”

“You are the guardian of the fish; You are likewise the divine forgiver of fishermen. You never forget to grant us grace, power, and fish and allow us to live in Your generous favoritism.”

“When my fighters bravely offered up their lives on the battlefield, we painfully experienced the shadow of death. Under Your divine power, they will live once again and return to our side.”

“God Mist, we are grateful for your grace and blessings under Your holy light.”

“Please acquiesce to our heartfelt esteem and praise, Your divine name will forever be put in splendor!”

After the prayer finished, the dragonman youngster used a major divine spell.

Divine spell – Resurrection!

Vast divine light fell from the sky and covered the corpses.

The divine power in the young man seemed to drain like a waterfall in sudden consumption. Reviving just a few people nearly drained his divine power.

But the dragonman youngster had foreseen this and calmly used necromancy.

He was capable of using necromancy. On the Deep Sea Monster Fish, he had personally used it to test Cang Xu’s bloodline quality.

After using several necromancy spells, he woke up the other corpses, turning them into skeletons, zombies, and ghosts.

However, they were layered in several deception divine spells.

Of course, these necromancy spells had the probability of failing. Therefore, there were one or two corpses that didn’t move.

It didn’t matter, the dragonman youngster was prepared.

He activated a few alchemy puppets and then covered them in deception divine spells too.

In short, to outsiders, it seemed everyone was resurrected!

“A miracle, a resurrection miracle!” Hong Zhu excitedly cried.

Despite knowing that the Resurrection divine spell could raise people from the dead, hearing about it and seeing it with her own eyes were two very different things.

She was shaken to no small degree.

Chapter 912: Young Man – I Come to Evangelize

Da Dou was flabbergasted.

As a member of a legend level power, and also as an outstanding gold level magic chef, he had traveled many places and had a very wide horizon.

“Resurrection has always been individual, this kind of communal resurrection is rarely seen, it simply captures the hearts and souls of people!”

“This God Mist, they should be a different race’s god, how magnanimous this god is!”

It was indeed rare.

To use Resurrection, one needed to be at least a bishop. Wanting a bishop to use Resurrection was hard, the starting price was at least 10,000 gold and there were other limiting factors too.

For example, resurrecting a person depended on if their corpse was intact and if their soul still remained.

In addition, it was also needed to examine the resurrection target’s allegiance, beliefs, and relationships.

Even if a resurrection target met all of the criteria, when some bishops used Resurrection, they needed the God’s (Godhead’s) approval, it was not purely decided by the bishop’s own determination.

All of these factors made just singular resurrections a rarity.

Let alone a communal Resurrection spell.

This kind of large scale Resurrection spell would only be used during world wars.

For example, during the demon invasion of the Main Plane several millennia ago, roughly 9 communal resurrections took place. That was the combined total of both sides.

Cong Mang was in a daze.

He watched as these corpses came back to life, and apart from his shock, he felt for the first time that perhaps relying on Long Fu was not such a bad thing.

It was always known that pirates lacked clergy.

Groups like the Divine Wind Pirates being outfitted with Sea Wind Church clergy was a rare example.

The vast majority, or it could be said, almost every pirate group wasn’t outfitted with extensive amounts of clergy.

Pirates were thus unable to enjoy divine spell healing during battle, and could only use magic medicine, alchemy items, or other methods to make up for this deficiency. (The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ White Bandage was a healing ship model)

But these treatment methods usually weren’t as effective as divine spells.

On the battlefield, every moment counted, and on the line between life and death, being two to three seconds slower to heal might cause these remedies to fail.

How could Cong Mang not want divine spell healing?

How could Cong Mang not want divine spell resurrection?

He really wanted them!

As a valiant pirate that always charged in and broke through enemy lines, having divine spell healing and resurrection simply solved his greatest worries.

Of the three gold level experts, Hun Tong was fairly tranquil.

He was constantly thinking over the name “Mist”, it seemed this god’s name had imbedded itself deeply into his thoughts.

Although Hun Tong had come into contact with the dragonman youngster and Dao Hen the earliest, the dragonman youngster did not make an introduction and Dao Hen specifically avoided Hun Tong when doing his daily prayers. This was the first time he had officially heard the god’s name “Mist”.

“God Mist…are they the grand master that is controlling everything behind the scenes?”

“They shouldn’t be a human god and are probably a different race’s god. A more brazen guess would be that they are a fishman god?”

Hun Tong glanced at the dragonman youngster, “to be able to unleash Resurrection, Long Fu must be at least a bishop.”

“No, he is not merely a bishop!”

“Why did the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps come to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom during the National Ceremony? This point in time is too subtle! This communal Resurrection spell entirely proves Long Fu’s status in the god’s heart! The God Mist surely must have a big conspiracy, and Long Fu is their most powerful chess piece in the immediate political situation.”

The three gold levels’ minds were madly shaking, to say nothing of Hong Zhu and Tripleblade.

Hong Zhu went limp and numb; she knelt down again and shouted blessings of how powerful Long Fu was and how divinely powerful the God Mist was.

Her excitement led to incoherent speech.

Everyone present could understand her.

As a silver level expert whose bloodline had come to its end, to join such a powerful power and hug the thigh of such an immense figure as Long Fu was a rare opportunity for her.

Hong Zhu was no greenhorn youngster; she had worked hard for many years and encountered innumerable dangers and struggled many times on the line between life and death. Compared to these dangers, she suffered much more discrimination, suspicion, and scorn. This time, she was arrested and thrown into prison by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and while suffering torture, she sank into the abyss of despair, believing more and more that she would lose her life.

The big guy was her only hope.

She clung obstinately to this hope and didn’t let the pressure strike her down, keeping the big guy’s secrets hidden.

That hope came to fruition.

Under Divine Descent, the big guy easily rescued her and Da Dou.

After that, she personally participated in deploying the alchemy bomb and witnessed Snow Bird Harbor’s city palace being blown up, realizing a small vengeance.

She also witnessed how the fishman youngster crushed Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo.

A single strike!

With just one strike, two aloof and remote gold levels that had caused her pain and despair were defeated.

The defeat was utterly thorough, like they were two dead dogs.

If it weren’t for the fishman youngster deciding to not kill them, how could Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo still be alive?

She had experienced Long Fu’s power when he saved her from despair and gave her revenge.

Now, Hong Zhu saw a communal Resurrection spell.

How could she not realize how important this stroke of luck was to her life!?

The communal resurrection had completely illuminated her eyes, setting aflame beautiful expectations of the future.

Her heart was constantly lingering on the divine name “Mist”. She looked up at the dragonman youngster and secretly pledged that from now on, she would firmly hug his thigh!

“Those comrades were really resurrected?” Tripleblade’s red eyes began to sparkle, and he nearly wanted to weep on the spot.

Tripleblade was once a mercenary commander.

While the dragonman youngster was away, he displayed utter loyalty to his duty, racked his brains, and exhausted his capabilities to keep the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch afloat. Although he was only an iron level, he made immense contributions.

He had feelings for the mercenary corps. If you were to ask who among the survivors desired to raise the mercenary corps’ rank and accomplish more in the corps, which would it be? It would be none other than Tripleblade.

When Tan Mo pretended to be a pirate and raided the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch, Tripleblade exhausted his capabilities to command the battle, and fight with all his strength.

But many people were sacrificed.

Ku Feng’s and Xiao Wang Zi’s unexpected assistance gave Tripleblade hope in the dragonman youngster’s return.

But ultimately, the one who arrived was the fishman youngster Hou Lang.

At the time, Tripleblade didn’t know who Hou Lang was. After the battle, he learned everything.

“That unfathomably powerful gold level fishman was the commander?!”

The dragonman youngster returning with such strength turned Tripleblade silent for a long while.

Soon after, Tripleblade learned of many more secrets, like how Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo were the leaders of this pirate raid.

Tripleblade realized that the dragonman youngster’s return had resolved the crisis that threatened to destroy the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch!

Finally, Tripleblade also learned about what the dragonman youngster roughly went through after departing.

The one who complained and grumbled about the dragonman youngster neglecting his duty was forced to concede he was wrong to blame the dragonman youngster!

Now, Tripleblade had witnessed a communal resurrection.

Within that split second, his body and mind were battered with excitement, both of his knees went soft, and he nearly knelt on the ground.

The reliable comrades had been resurrected.

The dead battle companions had returned!

Those placed as spies and revealed to be traitors during the battle had caused Tripleblade grief.

What else could prove loyalty?

Perhaps death could?

To Tripleblade it seemed that those comrades who died could absolutely be trusted!

Now they were alive again.

This kind of dream scenario, for the first half of Tripleblade’s life, was only possible in a fantasy?

But now, it had indeed happened before his eyes.

And the one who accomplished all of this was their leader, their young commander!

For the first time ever, Tripleblade thought that the dragonman youngster was more suitable to be the survivors’ leader than Zong Ge.

But a moment later, a thought came from the bottom of his heart, “Even so, I still wish to follow Lord Zong Ge more!”

The goblin was loyal to Zong Ge, and that loyalty was firm and hard to sway.

Inside the divine domain space, it slowly got noisy with voices.

“Is this the world of the dead?”

“Wait a moment, I’m still alive? I feel I’m alive!”

“Didn’t I die fighting? What is happening right now?”

The resurrected mercenaries gradually reacted, then a memory assaulted their mind.

Inside the dark blue ocean, an enormous shoal of fish formed into the look of a god, and every resurrected person had to raise their heads up at it so they could catch sight of the god’s towering cloud like chest.

Soon after, the god’s figure scattered, and the countless ocean fish moved to raise a turbulent tide that drowned the resurrected people.

At the same time, a vast voice rumbled in their hearts – I am the God Mist, recite my name and you can be reborn!

Thus, they said it.

“God Mist, God Mist!”

“Let me be reborn, I will believe in you!”

“Please, return me to life, I don’t want to die.”

It was as if this voice came from their innermost being, but as a matter of fact, this memory had been forged by the dragonman youngster.

He simply implanted them during the resurrection process.

“I’m alive, I’m really alive!”

“So that spectacle in the deep sea wasn’t a hallucination.”

“What, did you go into that deep sea illusion too?”

The mercenaries communicated with each other, then after mutual validation, their expression became more emotional.

“It was the Lord Commander’s Resurrection spell.”

“You guys really are lucky beyond belief!”

Tripleblade shouted, attracting many eyes.

Realizing they had been resurrected, they began to look around them.

They naturally trusted Tripleblade, and when some of the older members saw the dragonman youngster, they paid their respects.

The dragonman youngster waved his hand, “The one who truly saved you is the great God Mist.”

“They gave you a second life.”

“Come, join me in prayer, you will convert to that grand god together, my god, Mist!”

The dragonman youngster solemnly stood in front of everyone and put his dragon claws together on his chest in prayer

Everyone imitated at once.

The dragonman youngster began to evangelize for the first time in his life.

Chapter 913: The Basis of Belief is Acknowledgement

“Grand God Mist, ruler of the ocean and the fish.”

“Your divine power enveloped myriad fish and turns the vast seas.”

“Your mercy and power exceed imagination.”

“We are incomparably grateful for Your grace, because You brought us back from the misery of death. In our darkest moment, You gave us hope and strength.”

“Our fighters were resurrected because of Your power, now, they bravely stand before Your glory.”

“They will remain forever dedicating their loyalty and faith to You for their entire lives.”

…

“Grand God Mist, allow us to sense Your almightiness, and never be disconnected from Your favor and mercy.”

“We worship You and ask that You acknowledge our believers who fought for You.”

“Please admit them under Your banner, continue to show Your energy and strength to them. Let the resurrected continue to cross obstacles within Your grace and transcend our enemies.”

…

“In our prayers, we call out to You and hope You can hear our soft prayers.”

“We deem You the pillar that will never waver, because You seek to comfort us in our prayers, You are our sole hope we appeal to when plights grow wild.”

…

“Ocean God Mist, Fish God, we hope You will continue to protect us, give us the power and vision to get through storms, and point our way through the darkness. Thank You for your grace and blessings, we pray for Your blessings and that You will eternally navigate our journey forward.”

“We hope Your radiant light bathes the entire world and allows Your power and love to fill it!”

…

This was quite the verbose communal prayer.

The dragonman youngster was worthy of being a leader.

He first said a sentence, then everyone repeated after him. The ambience became solemn, respectful, and dignified, then in the wake of the dragonman youngster’s voice, his posture and expression became majestic and fanatical.

In this world, there were few nonbelievers, the vast majority of people had their own faith.

Many of them were not monotheistic, rather they believed in many gods. Therefore, general believers made up most of the population.

These people’s old faiths quickly swayed, even collapsing from the foundation.

A large-scale conversion was happening.

As a divine emissary in a divine domain, the fishman youngster’s perception abilities were at their apex, and could keenly and clearly sense everyone’s Charming Blue faith was sprouting, growing quickly, and constantly strengthening.

To him, this was both familiar and unfamiliar.

It was familiar due to the large amount of clerical experience he had obtained through divine blessings. Thus, although this was the first time the dragonman youngster officially evangelized, he had done so in an organized and well-trodden manner.

It was unfamiliar because this was the first time he had done it in real life, personally experiencing it was a layer different than experiencing it through divine gifts.

This would undoubtedly deepen the dragonman youngster’s understanding of it.

The dragonman youngster observed everyone’s hearts like a god was gazing at them.

They were experiencing the same thing Dao Hen once did when he was gazed upon.

It was as if they were insects looking up at a high mountain, realizing how grand the heavens and earth were, and how insignificant they themselves were.

This increased the reverence in everyone’s hearts, and they knelt to the ground, bent their waists, and hung their heads lower.

“Are we feeling God’s gaze upon us?”

“God is looking at us!”

“Heavens, it’s so great, so boundless…”

Of everyone present, the one preaching, the dragonman youngster, was actually the most tranquil.

His train of thought was even a bit unorganized as he mentally made other connections.

“Charming Blue Goddess’ main body is sleeping; I am actually drawing from the Godhead’s power.”

“Charming Blue Godhead, Dueling Godhead…”

“If I gain the Dueling Godhead in the future, will I also be able to pretend to be a god and conduct ministries?”

The dragonman youngster led everyone in prayer as he slowly turned around to look at the three gold levels.

His expression was flat, but the three gold level experts could sense that it seemed heaven and earth was suppressing them.

Divine spell – Deterrence!

This divine spell imitated divine prestige, strongly awing a target.

The fishman youngster once used this divine spell to easily intimidate Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. It was actually more appropriate to use it while preaching.

The first Deterrence spell was actually to help his preach.

From a certain point of view, almost all divine spells were bestowed by the gods for the purpose of evangelizing. Even healing divine spells, which could assert a god’s mighty power and benevolence, attracting believers to their religion.

Thump.

Under Deterrence, Cong Mang’s knees knocked against the ground in a kneel.

He had been beaten by the fishman youngster the hardest, was also a pirate, amenable to circumstances, and had a gangster temperament, so he bowed his head and began to recite the litanies.

Hun Tong glanced at Cong Mang, the depths of his eyes hiding bitterness. A gold level like him had kneeled too quickly, and now the pressure was concentrating on the other two.

“Oh dear!” Hun Tong sighed to himself, his mood heavy.

He had been subdued by the dragonman youngster already, and despite having his own faith that he deeply identified with joy, he also knew he couldn’t pull away from the powerful.

Therefore, he had no choice but to try to convert and followed the dragonman youngster in whispered recitation of the litanies.

This left just Da Dou.

After a few seconds, Da Dou was sweating profusely, and his expression was distorting as he struggled.

Although the young man was not in his fishman form and didn’t have the Surging Wave fishman bloodline’s formidable empowerment, it didn’t matter, this was the Charming Blue divine domain.

In the Charming Blue Divine Domain, Charming Blue divine spells would increase massively in power, far exceeding what the Surging Wave fishman bloodline could increase them by in the outside world!

The few seconds the young man was looking at him seemed like endless centuries to Da Dou.

After incessant suffering, he finally kneeled and hung his head, but his back still remained straight.

He tightly closed his lips and never opened his mouth to follow along with the dragonman youngster’s prayers.

On his body, the flame totems’ lines appeared, and faint fiery sparks spilled out.

Of the three gold levels, Da Dou had the most unflinching will.

His faith was very firm and singular, he had the haughtiness of the giants and did not want to believe in the god of a different race. He wasn’t willing to even try to pray along with the others.

In this extraordinary world, reciting another god’s prayers was not as superficial or simple as playing along with circumstances, it had genuine effects.

Da Dou did not want to “contaminate” his faith.

The dragonman youngster didn’t continue to force him into doing so.

After seeing Da Dou kneel, he shifted his gaze.

Faith could not be forced.

Being stubbornly tough would have the opposite effect. With this bit of knowledge and large amounts of clergy experience, the dragonman youngster knew this clearly.

Evangelizing to people like Da Dou should be done slowly.

The dragonman youngster was not in the least bit worried.

Because he did not believe in Mist, let alone Charming Blue Goddess!

He wouldn’t force everyone to believe in the God Mist. He had multiple goals in this evangelization.

As time passed, everyone’s convictions would change less and trend towards being fixed.

The dragonman youngster felt astonished, “Most people converted, but there are only four pious believers, and not a single fanatical believer?”

This outcome was quite unexpected.

In his original expectations, this group of people should be extremely grateful after they were brought back from the dead. Most of them should be at least pious level, while there should be at least two or three fanatical believers.

“Why was this conversion result so small?” The dragonman youngster asked himself.

Although he had received much experience from divine blessings, not much of it was on the Resurrection spell.

After all, Resurrection had always been seldomly used.

These limited experiences pertaining to Resurrection were all about singular resurrections of important figures of the Charming Blue Church.

After they were resurrected, their faith was substantially elevated.

It was precisely these experiences that gave the dragonman youngster great hopes for this preaching.

But reality did not answer his expectations.

“Faith…”

“It is not something that can be gained through immense benefits or compelled through fear or awe.”

“True faith comes from one acknowledging with their innermost being.”

“Therefore, believers nurtured from childhood have the strongest and most stable faith.”

The dragonman youngster thought of Xiong Ju at this time.

At the time, when he and Xiong Ju were publicly dueling, Xiong Ju would rather crush the Evil Eye than use it to gain an advantage in the duel.

This was Xiong Ju’s firm belief.

Xiong Ju’s deference to duels came from his innermost acknowledgement of it at the most fundamental level.

This conviction was firm and truly significant.

“Truly reliable faith cannot be spoken in general terms.”

“True faith does not need one to tell themselves to believe in it, but rather that they themselves believe in it.”

“Although these people have converted, they don’t acknowledge the God Mist. In fact, what do they know currently? From the prayers, they can only guess at the God Mist’s abilities or that he is the god of another race?”

“A lack of understanding will naturally lower acknowledgement. Even if the God Mist saved them.”

“In the end, people are unable to deceive their heart. A momentarily strong feeling can only nudge and catalyze, it is not the main source of faith.”

Practice and mental experiences were integrated together at this moment, giving the dragonman youngster penetrating insight into evangelization!

He subconsciously looked at the big guy.

The big guy was also kneeling, but his head was held high, and his chest was out as he impatiently gazed at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster laughed, “Xiao Guai is my fanatical believer.”

The young man once again realized how precious the big guy was.

He hadn’t met a fanatical believer of Charming Blue Goddess yet, while on the contrary, he had one of his own.

Adding on the big guy’s inherently exceptional bloodline, the dragonman youngster was more determined to focus on the latter’s cultivation.

The rate of faith change in everyone had already become imperceivable.

It could be said the outcome was visible.

This preaching ended up being a bit disappointing.

“Calculating it, my first public preaching was a devastating loss.” The dragonman youngster sighed.

His divine power investment was massive.

Almost all of his divine power had been spent to revive a few silver levels.

Chapter 914: Youngster – I’ll Make the Arrangements

The divine spell [Resurrection] severely depleted him! Despite the resurrection targets only being silver level.

Part way, the dragonman youngster swapped to magic power to use necromancy, raising the vast majority of the corpses into undead lifeforms.

The expenditure of magic and spiritual power was also an important cost.

Finally, the dragoman youngster activated a few alchemy puppets and imbued them with deception divine spells.

Planting false memories into the resurrected also required expenditure.

There were other multi-deceptive divine spells, tricking not just Hun Tong, Tripleblade, and others, but also those that were turned into the undead into believing that all of the dead had been truly resurrected.

The dragonman youngster had invested a large sum, yet the result was only a few pious level believers, the rest were ordinary level ones.

If this was put into business terms, it would be him investing a million gold coins for only a few thousand in revenue, it could even be said he lost his ancestral home.

“Preaching is so hard.”

“I am indeed a divine emissary, I have peak mastery over divine spells and the help of the divine domain, yet this is how it turned out. How do other missionaries fare?”

“The vast majority of clergymen preaching will spread their doctrine or perhaps utilize low level divine spells (at most healing spells), to display their god’s power to gain a populace’s goodwill and trust, a slow process.”

“Swaying the feelings of people is easy, and changing their opinions isn’t challenging. What is most crucial is that these people need to genuinely approve of a god and live according to their teachings. That is how believers of sufficient quality are fostered.”

The biggest thing the dragonman youngster felt was that evangelization was no overnight affair, rather it was an eternal and imperceptible long term undertaking.

“The ordinary and low level clergymen preaching methods are the cheapest.”

“The biggest cost incurred is time.”

“And since they are weak, their time is of little value. As for the gods themselves, they are not constrained by life span and have plenty of time to spare.”

“This is the best way to preach.”

The dragonman youngster had many regrets.

“When a lifeform’s evolution hits the god level, their life span is infinite, and at the peak of transcendent strength, the only true threats are existences of the same level.”

“Like Charming Blue Goddess, the reason why she was injured and sank into slumber was due to other gods of the same level besieging her?”

“Therefore, even if one is a god level, gods still need to constantly improve themselves. Only with power and the ability to defend oneself can one continue to survive.”

“And to get power, one needs to pursue faith!”

The dragonman youngster was currently still unable to determine whether the gods’ foundation was faith, but he already knew - faith absolutely could give gods more power, and the power of faith was a strong guarantee!

Even if faith was not the foundation of divine power, so long as one god pursued faith vigorously, the other gods had to imitate them and follow close behind or strive to surpass them.

If you don’t compete, while others do, then you will be the one who comes to grief.

By now, the dragonman youngster had a certain understanding of the gods’ living environment.

He had to understand it and gain an even greater comprehension of it.

The dragonman youngster was clear: don’t be fooled by the fact that he was drawing support from the mighty Charming Blue divine power to obtain immense benefits. Charming Blue Goddess was absolutely an immense future danger. After all, what he was really doing was blasphemous prayers, and as a blasphemous priest, he stole Charming Blue Goddess’ power and ruthlessly exploited it.

No, he didn’t just exploit it just once, it could be predicted that he would exploit it many more times in the future!

The more he exploited it, the more he understood it, and the more the dragonman youngster was afraid of the consequences Charming Blue Goddess would inflict on him.

He told himself: he had to be wary!

This wary mentality led to him, despite knowing the Resurrection spell, to not make it a priority to resurrect Zi Di – just in case Charming Blue Goddess tampered with it, he couldn’t tell if it was the case.
