Therefore, his resurrecting of everyone had many objectives, one of which was as an experiment.

He wanted to resurrect others first, then put them under constant observation, watching if their temperament, faith, and other things were tangibly and intangibly affected for the worse, and if that was the case, then to what degree.

The long sermon finally concluded.

“Stand.” The dragonman youngster proclaimed.

Everyone stood up, and looked towards the dragonman youngster, reverence and fanaticism across their faces as they fervently awaited the dragonman youngster’s next order.

Cong Mang and Hun Tong were the same too.

Hong Zhu went without saying, she was one of the new pious level believers.

Only Da Dou was different, wobbling as he stood up and pain still clearly remaining on his face. He persevered for a long time, and it wasn’t easy as his mind and willpower experienced a harsh ordeal.

The dragonman youngster already made arrangements for everyone.

He first arranged for the big guy, Da Dou, Hong Zhu, and some resurrected mercenaries to leave Snow Bird Harbor and join up with the Vegetable Pirates.

The big guy’s mouth gaped, gasping, he wanted to object. He was unable to make sense of why he had to be separated from his father after just reuniting with him, he didn’t want to do that!

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster glared at him slightly, his strict eyes cowed the big guy into shrinking his head, not daring to disobey.

He zipped his mouth, and his eyes quickly reddened, feeling very aggrieved.

Hong Hu also looked lost, and regretful. She made up her mind, she wanted to tenaciously hug the dragonman youngster’s thigh, but at this moment, she was to be dispatched into the ocean again,

Da Dou on the contrary was indifferent, and even somewhat happy. He was very afraid of the dragonman youngster and didn’t want to stay by his side. The dragonman youngster put him under too much pressure, especially during his sermon, that experience was etched into his memory. Keeping far away from the dragonman youngster could allow Da Dou to breathe.

Tripleblade also felt lost.

He still wanted to bring back these resurrected mercenaries, rouse morale, and reconstruct the mercenary corps.

His heart really was set on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But he quickly had a thought, and realized the dragonman youngster was right, “Bringing these people back alive is too garish.”

“Although we have Deceptive Disguise that can let them pretend to be other people, why would we expend valuable Pearl Bubbles like that?”

“These people are not strong enough to use Pearl Bubbles on.”

“Letting them go out to sea on their own is the right choice.”

Of everyone, Cong Mang was the most unwilling.

“The Vegetable Pirates is the foundation I painstakingly worked to build!”

Just now, the dragonman youngster took it over with a light word.

How could Cong Mang be willing to let that happen?

But what could he do?

The circumstances were stronger than the person’s will.

Cong Mang could only secretly console himself, “My foundation was originally subsidized and established by the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. Returning it to them is fine.”

He had second thoughts, “No, what is the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s relationship with Long Fu?”

“The Wisteria Merchant Alliance is destroyed already, when confronted with a foreign god, how could they possibly cooperate equally?”

“It is very likely the Wisteria Merchant Alliance has already been annexed!”

“The Long Fu before me is the leader.”

Cong Mang’s repeated visualizations quickly dissipated his stirred up emotions.

If someone in dire straits also saw someone meet the same fate, they would be in a much better mood. This was the approximate mentality – I’m not the only one that has bad luck, look, there are others who have the same terrible luck as me!

Like Cong Mang surmised, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was in equally terrible straits, so he didn’t have much beef with them.

He called out at once, “Lord Long Fu, I am truly honored that you can look after my people.”

He continued to express that he was willing to proactively conform and help Da Dou and the rest assume control over the Vegetable Pirates.

To this, the dragonman youngster merely nodded, expressing his approval.

Cong Mang’s reaction was something he expected.

The dragonman youngster analyzed Cong Mang’s temperament and considered how he would react.

As a pirate, he had a constant admiration for strength. Therefore, the pirate commander being the strongest within a pirate group was true with almost no exceptions.

In fact, this world belonged to the strong.

Every social class and all aspects of society had universal admiration for strength.

Cong Mang’s expression of this was also very obvious.

Just look at how he attached himself to Da Han willingly, led his entire pirate group as a daring vanguard, and obediently gave the Icebound Pirates a portion of his spoils of war.

Now that Cong Mang was actively helping the dragonman youngster’s annexation, he once again embodied his mentality of attaching himself to the strong.

On the one hand, he was already a captive, one under the yoke, and had no choice but to cooperate. On the other hand, the dragonman youngster had a foreign god backing him, an existence far surpassing saint level Da Han!

The dragonman youngster deliberately invoked the “God Mist” as deterrence and intimidation due to the considerable benefits it brought. Cong Mang’s active cooperation was one of those benefits.

The dragonman youngster continued to arrange things.

He had Cong Mang follow him to the Ice Sculpture Capital. While Hun Tong was left behind in Snow Bird Harbor to secretly defend and look after the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch here.

Cong Mang wanted to go to the capital. An important reason was the Wisteria Cache.

Now that he belonged to the survivors’ group, it would be very beneficial to Zi Di acquiring the cache.

Another important reason was that the dragonman youngster wanted him by his side so he could observe him at close range and understand him. The deeper the dragonman youngster’s understanding of his nature and thinking was, the greater his control over him would naturally be.

While Hun Tong was stationed in Snow Bird Harbor…

On the one hand, the dragonman youngster was relatively comfortable with Hun Tong. His hatred of Tan Mo, to some extent, was in a way loyalty to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

On the other hand, the dragonman youngster needed to give Hun Tong time alone.

This was because Hun Tong had experienced both the Snow Bird Harbor Battle as well as the dragonman youngster’s public evangelization, his mind was certain to have been immensely battered.

He was unprecedentedly loyal to the Sheng Ming Empire, this was due to his noble education from childhood, and it was how he habitually thought.

And now, he couldn’t help but ponder and compare.

On the one side was the empire, he had let a group alone far from the continent, painstakingly developed his territory, racked his brains to operate it, and then was blackmailed and framed by Tan Mo

On the other side was the enigmatic foreign power, one that had a god that magnanimously used a Communal Resurrection spell, and their higher-up Long Fu who was at least a bishop.

Which was better, returning to the Sheng Ming Empire or joining the dragonman youngster?

Chapter 915: Young Man – Leadership Ability Up ↑

If Hun Tong was next to the dragonman youngster, focusing his eyes and attention on the latter, he would feel omniscient pressure, he wouldn’t be able to think or be in an analytical mindset.

But if he was free to roam Snow Bird Harbor, during leisure time, he naturally would think more. The more he thought, the more he would hesitate and choose.

His hesitations and choices would constantly sway his loyalty, especially when he saw Tan Mo moving about right before his eyes.

Hun Tong was just a gold level and had reached the limits of his bloodline, his potential was exhausted!

He wanted to return to the empire and explain himself; he wanted to deal with Tan Mo and Tan Mo’s connections.

He didn’t have a good relationship between him and the clan behind him - the Duo Mu Clan.

But by genuinely relying on the dragonman youngster, he could have access to a bishop level higher-up and could even enjoy the super-benefit that was the Resurrection spell!

What would he choose?

The dragonman youngster looked forward to Hun Tong’s choice.

The dragonman youngster’s final arrangement was for Tripleblade.

He brought Tripleblade over for a private conversation, clearly informing him, “There are reasons why I left Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo injured but not dead.”

“First, they are both important members of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency. One is an irreplaceable arrangement in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and the other is an investigator. Killing them is bound to provoke needless attention and attract the spy agency’s powerful investigations and responses.”

“We don’t need to unnecessarily antagonize people, at least not ahead of time. Resisting all kinds of investigative magics and divine spells will intensify the depletion of Pearl Bubbles.”

“Second, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord has someone above him. His code name is [Emancipator] and his superior is code named [Usurper]. We must find out who [Usurper] is and uncover what the intention of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency is within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord wanted to unmask the arm merchant behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and so too did the dragonman youngster want to investigate who [Usurper] was.

The dragonman youngster continued to tell Tripleblade, “Therefore, you will go back and look for Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.”

“Make clear that I have learned of the bitter battle in Snow Bird Harbor and that I express my sympathies to him.”

Tripleblade couldn’t help but raise his brows, taking a new measure of his commander.

Tripleblade seemed to feel that the young commander had returned changed beyond recognition. In the past, the dragonman youngster would have never said that, let alone saying it without batting an eyelid.

The dragonman youngster added on, “If expressing sympathies is not enough, you will tell Snow Bird Harbor City Lord this: back then, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps received his help, and we jointly worked together to root out the Bayonet Gang, we have always remembered this camaraderie.”

“And this time, we wish to lend a hand to help the city lord out of his own crisis.”

“Our greatest sincerity is our capital.”

“Thanks to our magic medicine business, my corps currently has plenty of money and can give the city lord a large loan in order to rebuild Snow Bird Harbor’s defenses, and especially the core defense, the magician tower!”

“I know that in this battle, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s greatest breach of responsibility is losing the magician tower. He is sure to be held accountable strongly by the kingdom’s high class.”

“If he shoulders the task of rebuilding the magician tower of his own accord, then this blame will be alleviated a lot. This will have immense benefits for Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s political future.”

Tripleblade summed it up internally: we blew up the magician tower and robbed the city palace. Now, we are going back to help Snow Bird Harbor City Lord with a loan to him to help him rebuild the magician tower.

The goblin couldn’t help but be secretly speechless, he took another look and marveled at the young commander before him, shocked that he had changed so much after just one trip away from them.

“That said, I support this change.” Tripleblade was looking forward to how Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would react when he came knocking.

Seeing Tripleblade departing, the dragonman youngster’s gaze became deeply serene.

He knew what Tripleblade’s bloodline was and also had the Bloodline Purification formation, but he didn’t immediately purify Tripleblade’s bloodline.

If it were before, perhaps he would have done that.

After all, he was one of the survivors, a most trusted person.

But now, he had more notions and misgivings, so he decided to wait for a more mature opportunity.

The young man’s plans were immediately carried out, and he was soon alone in the divine domain.

But his plans weren’t finished yet.

He continued to use divine spells to secretly connect to the resurrected mercenaries, mainly the silver level ones.

He secretly gave these mercenaries a mission, empowered them with divine spells, changed their identities, and ordered them to lie low in various parts of Snow Bird Harbor.

After these mercenaries received the order, they trembled with excitement and accepted it without hesitation.

“Thus, the mercenary corps is divided into three parts.”

“The first is the main force in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom Capital, it will have me, Zi Di, Zong Ge, Dao Hen, Cong Mang, and the others.”

“The second part will have Da Dou, Xiao Guai, Hong Zhu, and the important resurrected mercenaries, and the tentatively controlled Vegetable Pirates remnants.”

“The third part is the Snow Bird Harbor branch. It will still have Tripleblade as the core, with Men Shi and Chi Lai as its backbone. They will shoulder some magic medicine production assignments, but will mainly be focused on deepening their connection with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and if possible, openly contacting Tan Mo. If they can build a relationship with the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, then that would be for the best.”

“If there was a fourth part, then it would be Cang Xu, but I don’t know where he followed Teacher Si Ling to.”

The dragonman youngster was unaware that Cang Xu and Jian Gui had been brought to Ice Sculpture Island by Teacher Si Ling.

“Furthermore, there is also Lan Zao. He is currently trapped in the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain.”

Previously, the dragonman youngster wanted to rescue Lan Zao, but now that he had a new goal of seizing the Dueling Godhead, he found Lan Zao’s position to be quite ingenious. He was a saboteur that had taken root in the Dueling God Divine Domain and perhaps in the future, he could be empowered!

The Ice Sculpture National Ceremony, the Warm Snow Cup, the Wisteria Cache, the pirate invasion, the Dueling Divine Domain, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency…the situation was still considerably complex.

As the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ paramount leader, the dragonman youngster had to shoulder and rise to his duty, steer their ship well, charge through the wind and waves, evade hidden reefs, and eventually make his way into the expansive seas.

These were things he, as their leader, needed to do!

He could never shirk them and had to exhaust his capabilities to carry these responsibilities.

The returned dragonman youngster was far more mature and outstanding than his past self.

The divine blessings had given experience in not only transcendent powers, but also mixed in experiences in administration, communication, and other fields.

The dragonman youngster’s vision had become farther.

He was no longer limited to a single battle.

In the Snow Bird Harbor battle, he used stratagems, borrowed power to attack, and even though he finished the job himself, the performance was composed of many real battles.

He successfully attained his multiple objectives.

First, he retaliated against Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo, damaging their strength and cutting down their greed.

Second, he intimidated Cong Mang and took him captive, then incorporated the Vegetable Pirates into his forces as bedcover for the Wisteria Cache.

Third, he left Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo alive for future relations, goodwill, false deference, and other bridges that could be built.

He didn’t hesitate to vigorously spend to conduct a public communal resurrection and evangelization, which had its own gains.

The first was that although his communal conversion didn’t have many results, it had achieved its main goal, which was to awe and entice everyone. The biggest thing was that he reinforced his own authority and united everyone together.

The second thing was that he had Charming Blue believers put into each of the three forces, making it convenient for him to teleport to each and maximize his control.

The third thing is that he started to experiment with divine spells, and the resurrected mercenaries became his observation targets.

Why did he choose Mist?

This had been pondered over by the dragonman youngster and came after careful deliberations.

Firstly, he could not take in faith personally, and it was easy to expose himself.

Secondly, he could not use Charming Blue Goddess’ name. That name would make known that the dragonman youngster was an expert at deception divine spells, which would involuntarily produce doubts, impact trust, and increase the dragonman youngster’s leadership expenditures.

Finally, the God Mist was one of Charming Blue Goddess’ visages and was not something the dragonman youngster came up with. Preaching with that name could still mold Charming Blue believers. These believers were valuable spatial coordinates. Divine Domain Descent made many matters easier for the dragonman youngster to deal with, and he had room to maneuver henceforth!

The dragonman youngster’s arrangements in the Snow Bird Harbor battle could not be looked at in isolation. One should treat the Snow Bird Harbor battle and the public sermon afterwards as an integrated whole.

Stratagems became an important skill of the dragonman youngster, for his leadership abilities had immensely improved.

Like Tripleblade realized, he had changed beyond recognition!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was nearly covered completely in bandages.

His injuries weren’t heavy, but he decided to appear severely wounded.

He was hard pressed and under pressure.

This was the truest portrayal of his current state.

“How did I become like this?”

“Why did the Vegetable Pirates suddenly appear? They controlled high level stealth techniques!”

“Most pivotally, who was that fishman expert who appeared at the end?”

“Why did the grand Imperial Secret Spy Agency’s information base not have a trace of him?”

“According to common sense, such an expert cannot be nameless, there will inevitably be some clues. But in reality, he seemed to appear from nowhere!”

“He defeated me and Tan Mo but didn’t kill us. On the contrary, he captured Cong Mang alive, then swaggered off. From this, I can see that he seemed to have come for Cong Mang in particular.”

“In that case, was the mysterious fishman part of the group who blew up the magician tower and the city palace?”

All kinds of misgivings entrenched themselves in Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s mind and continued to press down on him.

His current concern was not these suspicions, but how to deal with Snow Bird Harbor’s perilous situation.

Chapter 916: Snow Bird Harbor City Lord – I Can Make Deals With Long Fu!

Yes, that’s right, a perilous situation.

After losing the core defense that was the magician tower, Snow Bird Harbor’s weakness had been laid bare.

Snow Bird Harbor had been visited by the Vegetable Pirates. The city had suffered cataclysmic damage, and the city palace had been blown up. But on the other hand, other parts of Snow Bird Harbor still had its great wealth and hadn’t been plundered.

“I must rebuild the magician tower as soon as possible!!” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shouted in his mind.

Building a magician tower could be a long term project, or not. It all depended on whether money was in place.

Building a magician tower in two or three days probably wouldn’t work. But a week was completely doable. Because this was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the top country in alchemy technology on the Main Plane.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord estimated that if he assembled half the alchemy guild’s strength, he could rebuild the magician tower within a week.

Even though the new magician tower would be simpler and cruder than the old one, it was better than nothing.

Having a magician tower or not was the difference.

“The problem is money!” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord grasped the crux of the matter.

The city palace was blown up, and the money inside was gone.

This included last month’s tax payments and his own personal wealth.

“To be more accurate, I only lost the gold coins, the silver and copper coins are still there.”

However, the silver and copper coins were left melted or turned to metal fragments in the ruins because of the explosion.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had already ordered them to be recovered.

But he understood that even if all of it was recovered, this sum of money was like throwing a cup of water on a bonfire, it had little to no effect. The most valuable part of the treasury had been scurried away.

“It is almost certain it was that third party.”

“They schemed against me and schemed even more against Cong Mang’s Vegetable Pirates!”

They stole all of the gold coins and everything in my secret storehouse.”

The city palace explosion had destroyed almost all of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s accumulated wealth, and right now, he was wrapped up in bandages, pretending to be seriously injured when he reported to the higher-ups.

“Lord Clan Leader, I have no choice but to contact you, this is the situation…” Snow Bird City Lord decided to use a long ranged instantaneous communication device to contact his clan leader.

When the Xue Beng Clan Leader received the communication, he was grave, because he knew that this kind of long ranged instantaneous communication device consumed massive amounts of magic power every second.

Whenever this device was used, it indicated that the situation was serious and critical.

“Why is Snow Bird Harbor being attacked by pirates?” The Xue Beng Clan Leader hastily asked, “Do you need clan support?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord coughed, then threw up blood, “To be more accurate, the pirates have already retreated.”

“At the time, the battlefield was very complicated, I did everything I could, but I was unable to…cough.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord coughed out blood again, turning as pale as paper.

Seeing him so miserable, the Xue Beng Clan Leader suddenly felt his bad feelings being aggravated.

Then, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said, “Now, although we have luckily survived, the crisis has not waned. Snow Bird Harbor’s magician tower has been utterly blown up, and my city palace has met the same terrible calamity. I urgently need clan assistance to rebuild the magician tower!”

“What?!” When he heard the magician tower had been completely destroyed, the Xue Beng Clan Elder’s eyes went black, and his expression seemed to collapse.

Rebuilding the magician tower would necessitate massive amounts of funds.

Snow Bird Harbor’s current situation demanded a rapid construction of a magician tower, which would mean it would cost tenfold the amount.

The Xue Beng Clan Leader’s anger was scathing as he began to demand an explanation from Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. Why weren’t you careful? How did it come to this? If this battle had sustained such massive losses, then you as city lord cannot avoid responsibility! If this matter is not handled well, then your political prospects as city lord will inevitably go dark!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord bowed his head and received the scolding, occasionally coughing up blood and in short, playing up his miserable condition.

The Xue Beng Clan Leader scolded him for a spell before getting tired. He saw how miserable Snow Bird Harbor City Lord looked and deeply sighed, “Contacting me and the clan was the right decision.”

“Remember, regardless of your official position, you are Xue Yue, and from your birth name you are a member of the Xue Beng Clan, this is your symbol that cannot be wiped away!”

“At the moment, both His Majesty and the Lord Grand Magician are conserving their strength and waiting for a golden opportunity, the capital is in a delicate state.”

“If you report this directly to your superiors, the royal family is unlikely to act while the Alchemy Guild is focused on presiding over the Warm Snow Cup.”

“Due to the National Ceremony, so long as your Snow Bird Harbor hasn’t encountered Da Han’s Icebound Pirates, don’t expect any reinforcements.”

“We need to take our time making a decision in this matter.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord Xue Yue looked anxious, “Indeed! Right now, the most important thing is to rebuild the magician tower, this cannot wait, Lord Clan Leader.”

“Humph!” The Xue Beng Clan Leader looked annoyed, “Ordinarily, you don’t contact the clan, and in this matter, now you know who to depend on?”

“The clan is not your bank vault; you must understand this.”

“As a clan elder, I must consider everything, and not just one side.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord frowned, “However, of the entire clan, I am the only one who has authority in this region. Clan Leader, if I am held to account for my poor defense of the border, and am forced from the city lord position, then the clan will be negatively impacted to an immense degree.”

The Xue Beng Clan Leader sighed, his tone reserved, “Of course I won’t stand by and watch, but you can also take out some presentable things that will satisfy the other clansmen, do you understand?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s gaze went gloomy, he was silent for a time, then nodded, “Then please make your statement, Lord Clan Leader.”

What followed was a negotiation between the two.

Clan affection became a façade, a thin cloth covering up the icy contest over benefits that should not be known to outsiders.

After a short time, the negotiation finished.

Both sides hadn’t come to an agreement.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord Xue Yue cursed, his expression gloomy, but this outcome was not beyond his expectations.

He came from the Xue Beng Clan, but he was not on friendly terms with his clan.

“That old bastard!” The long ranged alchemy communication device’s metal surface reflected Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s ordinary looking face.

As a snow elf, how could he have ordinary features?

Although Snow Bird Harbor City Lord came from the Xue Beng Clan, he was of mixed blood, a half human, half elf mixed blood.

From his birth, he suffered ostracism and suppression.

The more he was suppressed, the more he worked.

But a half0blood wanting to grow in a prideful elven nation was really difficult.

It wasn’t until he became an imperial secret spy that he had a path to make true progress!

As for becoming Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, that was due to the empire secretly boosting his achievements. But on the surface, it was believed he was a benefactor of dumb luck when he obtained it after a political stalemate between the kingdom’s upper class.

At least this is what the Xue Beng Clan, including the clan leader, believed.

Following him obtaining the position of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the Xue Beng Clan tried to take advantage of him…to an unsightly degree for a time.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord deliberately utilized the Xue Beng Clan’s resources and contacts, but the latter was too greedy. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was loyal to the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, and he had to ensure his independence, or at least present an independent outward appearance. Therefore, many benefits and crucial posts could not be assigned to his Xue Beng clansmen.

At the time, both sides were having an unpleasant row.

The Xue Beng Clan in its entirety was greatly disappointed in Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, bawling out that he didn’t return the clan’s kindness, with some clansmen even manipulating things behind the scene, conspiring against Snow Bird Harbor City Lord in an effort to take away his position of city lord.

How could they succeed?

The Imperial Secret Spy Agency had painstakingly handpicked and raised up Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, so naturally they sought to protect him.

Therefore, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s external political crisis crumbled, and after he took office, it had vanished, preferring not to make mistakes than try, so gradually he sat steadily in his position.

Of course he didn’t opt out of the rat race, instead he was constantly trying to secretly save power and bitterly wait for an opportunity.

He was waiting for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to come.

Then, he mopped up the entire Bayonet Gang in a day, cleaning it out in a roll of thunder.

He pushed himself into the limelight, and the entire kingdom’s high class were shocked for a time, recognizing that he – Xue Yue, a Xue Beng Clan half elf, the incumbent Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was not just lucky, he was capable!

But now…

Pirates attacked Snow Bird Harbor and Cong Mang’s Vegetable Pirates easily landed. At the start of the battle, Snow Bird Harbor’s defensive core, the magician tower, was blown up, and even his city palace was blown to bits.

He was a disgrace.

Too much of a disgrace!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had gravely neglected his duty!

This was the greatest crisis since he had taken office.

He would surely be held accountable.

The reason why it wasn’t happening now was simply due to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom being faced with lots of ordeals already, the entire upper layer didn’t have the inclination to sort out a city lord like himself.

When the National Ceremony was finished and things recovered, he feared he’d be stripped of his title as Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“Unless I receive Lord [Usurper’s] help again.”

“But my relationship with Lord [Usurper]’s cannot be exposed, he cannot help me out fully.”

“I have to get myself out of this trouble!”

“I have to rely on myself to get through this crisis.”

“At least, I have to give it my all!”

The enormous pressure did not break Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, instead it stimulated his fighting spirit more.

When he left the communications room, he kept his spirits up, as if he had obtained the ideal outcome from the negotiation with the Xue Beng Clan Leader.

“We just didn’t come to an agreement.”

“How could such a matter possibly be concluded in one discussion?”

“Crucially, I have already obtained the Xue Beng Clan’s tentative support.”

“My major dereliction of duty this time means the Xue Beng Clan has to take care of things in advance, use their contacts and resources, and issue a good deal of preparations for them to save my position in a timely fashion hereafter.”

“It seems that my clan still has a bit of use in it.”

“Hehehe.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was already thinking of measures and began to implement them.

Exploiting the Xue Beng Clan was merely his first step.

“Next, I will invite my friends.” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord tore off his bandages.

His injuries were never serious.

As he tore off his bandages, he strode into a corridor.

The bandages he threw to the floor were naturally cleaned up by servants.

His insolent movement and heavy aura at this moment did not have a trace of elven grace, he looked like a raging bull.

Because he knew that this time was like countless times before, he had to do his utmost to make a stand and fight against his fate!

“I can borrow money.”

“A massive loan!”

“The Sea Bird Church is my ally and has fought alongside me.”

“The Clam Cloth and Secret Medicine Merchant Alliances, amongst others, have branches in Snow Bird Harbor. Due to a lack of security, they might rush to raise funds for the magician tower, it is our joint demand!

“Tan Mo…that damnable guy.” Thinking of Tan Mo, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hatefully itched his teeth, “But we can still cooperate, I can transfer a portion of Snow Bird Harbor earnings in the future to obtain funds from Tan Mo. Even if he opens with a high price…”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had completely calmed down already.

He was mentally prepared to be fleeced. He would rather agree to Tan Mo’s high price than compromise with the Xue Beng Clan, even if the latter’s opening price was lower than Tan Mo’s.

The reason was of course not due to his antagonistic mood.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a qualified statesman, and he thought deeply, “Tan Mo is merely an outsider, and a human, he came to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom on an investigation mission.”

“The fact is the Xue Beng Clan is a native power, and if I make an agreement with them, it will open up a hole that they will constantly seep through and become a large tail that obstructs me in the future.”

Even if he had to cede massive profits, it depended on who he was ceding them to.

Despite the pain of losing them making him breathless, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord deeply understood he had to concede!

This was the art of political compromise!!

If he didn’t concede assets while he was still city lord and there were assets to concede, he wouldn’t have a chance to do so in the future.

“City Lord, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has come to pay a visit.” Just as Snow Bird Harbor City Lord came to his study, a subordinate reported to him.

Tripleblade and Chi Lai came to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s suburban manor.

“Why did they come?” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord knew of “Di Bi” (Tripleblade).

After all, Snow Bird Harbor was his territory, and he also had the Imperial Secret Spy Agency’s intelligence. Therefore, he knew how the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ Snow Bird Harbor branch was organized. On the face of it, Men Shi and Chi Lai, two silver levels, were the elite heads, but that wasn’t true.

The real head leader was the iron level Di Bi (Tripleblade)!

This was a rare situation.

In this world of the strong, the strongest was generally the leader.

But Snow Bird Harbor City Lord understood the situation. He had given himself a rational explanation: since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were an agent of an arms merchant, then the people truly controlling them were their backers. Long Fu was like that, Di Bi too. Chi Lai and Men Shi did not swear loyalty to Di Bi, but rather to the arms merchant.

“Chi Lai and Men Shi merely joined in midway and are not considered to be their core.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps hides people, core members of the arms merchant. They should have been cultivated for a long time and are estimated to be very loyal. Long Fu and Shi Qi are certain to be like that, Hen Fa too. Now Di Bi should be one of those people too. That “Sleeping Man’ was an even more concealed trump card!”

Despite Snow Bird Harbor City Lord being far from the truth, with his limited information, he was nevertheless able to guess who made up the survivors in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Thinking of the big guy, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s gaze became dusky.

He felt intense cardiac pain.

The big guy was a future saint level, but he was killed in the explosion, with not even his skeleton being left behind!

A saint level, he was a future saint level.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord felt that all of the assets he had lost did not compare to losing the big guy.

After a moment, the study door bell rang.

“Please come in.” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord faintly adjusted his sitting posture.

Tripleblade and Chi Lai pushed the door open, entered, and simultaneously showed propriety.

“Please, have a seat.” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smiled.

He understood the internal situation in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch, and naturally didn’t look down on Tripleblade, and was curious at their purpose in coming.

Tripleblade was very candid, and after exchanging a few pleasantries, he immediately stated his purpose. The gist was that thanks to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s assistance, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch was saved. Lord Commander Long Fu had learned about the situation here and has dispatched them to discuss a new collaboration with you, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“What kind of collaboration?” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord exposed obvious interest.

Tripleblade continued his entreaties.

The moment he heard the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to provide 200,000 gold coins for a deal; Snow Bird Harbor City Lord felt his spirits rise.

This sum of money had emerged at a great time, too timely actually!

He really needed money.

He had no clue that this money came from his city palace treasury. Of course, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would not be so stupid as to pay with stolen coins, those were given to the group at the capital and the 200,000 gold coins came from the magic medicine profits.

“Then what does your side demand in exchange?” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord serenely asked.

“We need some materials.” Tripleblade handed over a list.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord read it, his brows gently creasing.

The vast majority of the materials enumerated on the list could not be purchased extensively on the market. Many of them were contraband.

For example, Ice Hearts and a large quantity of dragon corpses.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head, expressing it would be too difficult to procure.

Tripleblade thought: Who is he kidding? In the supplies the big guy stole, there’s a white dragon talon, from a pure white dragon, furthermore, there’s large quantities of Ice Hearts!

These indicated that Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had the means to get these things, perhaps the price was comparatively large, or it was very risky.

But in Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s current situation, regardless of the price or risks, they were secondary.

Tripleblade was prepared for this and didn’t relent on his conditions one bit.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord just wanted to raise the price, and upon seeing Tripleblade’s firm attitude, agreed to the terms.

“Truth be told, the Li Jian Clan’s contacts and resource channels are better than mine. Why didn’t your corps conduct this business with them?” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord inquired.

As for why the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted the prohibited materials, he didn’t ask.

To him it seemed that as arms merchant agents, what was wrong with gathering some alchemy materials? It was perfectly normal. The difficult part lay with the fact these materials were prohibited in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Tripleblade bitterly laughed, “Our collaboration with the Li Jian Clan went too deep, it wasn’t good.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s eyes rapidly flashed with vague glints.

He suddenly thought of some past information.

The Li Jian Clan saw the immense benefits in the mount potion business and wanted to completely bind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to them, becoming the latter’s sole business partner.

“Now it seems the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps doesn’t want to keep working with the Li Jian Clan.”

“That’s right. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ true loyalty is to an arms merchant. As an alchemy munitions merchant, they naturally don’t want to depend heavily on some power, as they will suffer under a yoke in the future, rather they want to benefit from both sides and the freer they are, the easier it is for them to earn money.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord instantly made out the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ requirements.

Tripleblade continued, “Speaking of which, it was thanks to you City Lord that we started to collaborate with the Li Jian Clan, you were the one who built the bridge behind the scenes.”

“Can you get in touch with the Jing Xiang Clan, your Lordship?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord quickly thought of the magic medicine market standoff.

The mount potion market was prosperous, but there were only two big players. One player was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan, the other player was the Jing Xiang Clan. The traditional hegemon, the Alchemy Guild, hadn’t done that much in the market, astounding many outside onlookers.

“The Jing Xiang Clan is the greatest magic medicine refiner of the kingdom’s nobility, but unfortunately, I am unable to help you two in mediation.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a mixed blood, his status was limited, and he had suffered veiled discrimination.

At the time, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was able to reach a collaboration with the Li Jian Clan mainly because of how strong the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was.

But now, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ true intent was to reach a reconciliation with the Jing Xiang Clan and even work together with them, this greatly exceeded Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s capabilities. Because if they wanted to do this, they not only had to put an end to past conflict between the two parties but also deal with the Li Jian Clan’s reprisal.

Tripleblade was unsurprised. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could investigate them, naturally they could do the same.

The selling price at the Thieves Guild for information that Snow Bird Harbor City Lord came from the Xue Beng Clan and was a mixed blood was very cheap.

Thus, a moment later, Tripleblade stated his primary purpose in coming, “In that case. We are here to ask Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to spread additional rumors.”

“What kind of rumors?”

Tripleblade’s tone and bearing were very tranquil, “It concerns the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan. The Li Jian Clan made a grave misstep and wanted to seize the largest share of the magic medicine earnings and bullied Long Fu’s party. Long Fu was unwilling to be humiliated, and he got quite furious, even threatening to work with the Jing Xiang Clan!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stared blankly.

Soon after, his mind seemed to flash with lightning, and he nearly blurted out, “Clever.”

This was extremely clever!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had made up their mind already and wanted to cut ties with the Li Jian Clan. The partner they wanted now was the Jing Xiang Clan, their former business enemy.

Disseminating this rumor would immediately uncover the conflict between the two. The crucial thing was that the Li Jian Clan was in a bad state currently. The kingdom’s high class were universally dissatisfied with the Li Jian Clan’s performance in the naval battle.

It could be said that the Li Jian Clan’s situation was much worse than Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to rescind their collaboration with the Li Jian Clan, then this was undoubtedly the best opportunity.

When the rumors were let out, the entire kingdom would know.

This was tantamount to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps giving a way out of this embarrassing situation and calmly extending an olive branch to all parties.

The mount potion market was too fiery, and so long as working together brought wealth, who wouldn’t want to do that?

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord also wanted to do that, but unfortunately, he was not strong enough and could not touch such enormous profits, he could only drool with envy.

After a half hour, Tripleblade and Chi Lai said goodbye.

The two sides reached a consensus and signed an agreement on the spot.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was quite satisfied.

“I can feel that Long Fu really wanted to help me. He didn’t exploit my circumstances, everything was based on market value, and he gave me capital when I most needed it.”

This was Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s time of most urgent need, and it could be said he had been provided charcoal during a blizzard.

“Of course, there are still risks to this collaboration.”

“After all, the things they want are contraband.”

But to save his city lord position, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was very determined to assume these risks.

“In comparison, disseminating rumors is far riskier, I might offend the Li Jian Clan.”

“But it doesn’t matter, I can utilize the Imperial Secret Spy Agency for this.”

“It has nothing to do with me, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord!”

“This guy, Long Fu. He’s pretty good, he handles matters in the open and stylishly, and he is loyal to his friends.”

“Unfortunately, once I get across this ordeal, I still need to deal with you and the arms dealers behind you.”

“In the future, if possible, I will help you out and reciprocate in kind.”

Chapter 917: Dragon Bloodline Rises Dramatically

Ice Sculpture Kingdom, a plain mountain range on the outskirts.

Inside a cave, within a divine domain, the dragonman youngster was currently using the Bloodcore.

A familiar scene played out as the Bloodcore’s light enveloped a white dragon talon.

Very quickly, the gold level white dragon talon turned to ash, and the Bloodcore’s blood light returned, increasing the dragonman youngster’s White Dragon bloodline by a bit.

The dragonman youngster experiencing this sighed to himself.

The Bloodcore only absorbed a gold level target, not increasing the bloodline by much. Most of the dragonman youngster’s bloodline had come from Mystifying Monster Island – the legend level Flame Dragon King bloodline.

He always wanted to collect dragon bloodlines and strengthen this aspect.

During his experience away from the corps, he had obtained a massive investment from Charming Blue Goddess, his Surging Wave fishman bloodline had emerged as a new force to be reckoned with, surpassing his dragon race bloodline and moved up as the top bloodline.

“Without divine blessings, I would have to collect bloodlines myself, it’s quite exhausting.”

“Perhaps I can do some blasphemous prayers to the Dragon God to receive his divine gifts?”

The dragonman youngster tossed his head, suppressing this notion.

At present, he didn’t want to do that.

It was too risky.

The hidden danger of Charming Blue Goddess hadn’t been dealt with yet; it was bound to be exposed in the future. Furthermore, the current circumstances made it untenable for blasphemous prayers.

“If I can obtain the Dueling Godhead, I will be classified as a high-end power, and perhaps I will have a higher assurance of blasphemous prayers.”

“Currently, the group has plenty of money and collecting dragon bloodlines is easier than ever.”

“Don’t rashly take risks. Although the earnings are immense for the risk, if I do it more, I will eventually die for it.”

The dragonman youngster criticized himself, finding his ego was somewhat inflated.

There was nothing that could be done, the treatment in the Ocean Mother Nest was excellent and Charming Blue Goddess personally looked after him. Now that it was his turn to work hard, there was an obvious gap in propriety.

The dragonman youngster promptly adjusted his mindset.

The dragon bloodlines still needed to be collected vigorously.

Because publicly, he was Long Fu who relied on the massive augments his dragon bloodline provided.

Since he was vying for Dueling Godhead, he had to battle foes and the safest way to do so was to use his dragonman identity.

Of course he could use his fishman form, then cover himself in a few Charming Blue divine spells, but doing that was very dangerous. If he came across something similar to the Truth Slate, there was a chance he’d be exposed.

Especially during the later stages of the National Ceremony, each duel was public. Don’t speak of the many spectators, there was Ice Sculpture Kingdom leadership watching these, along with many powerful figures not native to the Ice Sculpture Island.

If a slip up appeared, the dragonman youngster would ruin the enterprise for the sake of one basket.

Conversely, if he used the dragon bloodlines and just the Bloodcore’s abilities, the dragonman youngster would be extremely difficult to see through. Because his life form manifestation was just as it appeared, there was no need to disguise his appearance or form.

Right now, the dragonman youngster collected dragon bloodlines in mainly two ways.

The first was through Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s channels, where he could get hold of some pure dragon race contraband.

The second was from buying many wyverns on the market. Wyverns had scant dragon bloodlines in them, but so long as the quantity piled up, it was a considerable amount.

The first channel didn’t provide a lot and was risky, but the main risk was assumed by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The second channel had low quality but basically no risks.

The second channel was the main avenue.

The main thing was that there were many wyverns on the market at a cheap price.

This was mainly because the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a considerable white dragon community living in it. Dragons had a lascivious nature, and the white dragon community made many wyverns all year long. Wyverns usually had a high reproduction rate, to the extent that the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had to organize large hunting events at regular intervals to clean out the wyverns to prevent them from running rampant.

The dragonman youngster wasn’t just absorbing a white dragon talon.

Since he left till now, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been actively buying wyvern materials.

This purchasing had been so vigorous to the extent that when Tripleblade was nervous about finances, he wanted to cancel these purchases to alleviate the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ financial pressure.

When the dragonman youngster returned, the wyvern materials had become a small mountain.

Totally absorbed!

This was the benefit of having personnel and capital.

If he were by himself, doing this would be rather troublesome, take a great deal of time and effort, and would be done very inefficiently.

Nowadays, the number of dragon bloodlines in the Bloodcore has increased dramatically!

The Dragon Saliva bloodline, 18.3% - dragons with this bloodline often had stronger dragon breaths. The distinctive feature was that it was easy to drool while sleeping.

Dragon Talon Inheritance bloodline, 9.6% - dragon talons would become sharper and tougher. This bloodline’s heredity probability was very high, so long as one of the dragon race parents had it, their descendants would almost certainly have indicators of it.

Frost Hair bloodline, 21.5% - a wyvern’s fur seemed to be covered in a layer of white frost, it is not only capable of keeping it warm, but also able to absorb and store external ice elements and during times of crisis, cause death or injury.

Dragon Wing Dynasty bloodline, 2.7% - a dragon or wyvern with this bloodline will possess expansive dragon wings and have strong flying capabilities.

Ice Triangle bloodline, 33.7% - wyverns with this bloodline have formidable horns on their head, usually three of them. The dragon horns are incomparably hard and are mighty when used to ram something. At 70% bloodline concentration, natural runes will appear on the horns that possess constant ice magic (the specific spell depends on the dragon).

Cold Moon Tracker bloodline, 41.6% - one can easily hunt in the most biting cold environments, their silent footprints vanish in the snow and can fatally ambush enemies.

Second Ice Heart bloodline, 68.8% - this bloodline is the most commonly seen bloodline in wyverns. When bloodline concentration reaches 50%, it can form a second heart. This heart is similar to an Ice Heart and completely follows the composition of an ice crystal. It is entirely transparent, and when blood flows through it, it can clearly be seen from the outside.

Dragon Pack Heart bloodline, 24.9% - wyvern beast kings usually have this bloodline and have natural leadership with other wyverns.

The Cold Flesh bloodline, the Cold Wind bloodline, the Glacier Tusk bloodline, the Winter Tail bloodline, the Cold Wing bloodline…

The dragonman youngster’s foundation rose dramatically.

Due to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s ecosystem, the wyverns thriving here that were collected had two big traits: one dragon race trait and one ice trait.

“A bit inconvenient, my outside appearance is that of the Flame Dragon King, orange yellow dragon eyes, red dragon scales, so many bloodlines cannot be brazenly used.”

“However, they are still very useful.”

For example, the Dragon Saliva bloodline was a common bloodline. The dragonman youngster could secretly use it to increase the power of his dragon breath.

Another was the Second Ice Heart bloodline, when the dragonman youngster secretly used it, nothing could be discerned from the surface as a second heart formed inside his body and increased his ice resistance massively. If the concentration was higher, then he would gain a racial magic.

The Cold Flesh bloodline could be used in the same way.

But the Ice Triangle and the Dragon Wing Dynasty bloodlines were unusable. These two bloodlines would severely change the dragonman youngster’s form, provoking all kinds of suspicions.

The lower the concentration, then more precious the bloodline.

This was obvious with the Dragon Wing Dynasty bloodline. It was only 2.7%.

The ability to fly is an immense advantage. The bloodline has to reach 10% to gain a flying racial magic.

Historically on the Main Plane, the most glorious period of the dragon race’s hegemon era was known as the Dragon Wing Dynasty. At the time, it was purported that the wing shadows of dragon flocks could cover the continents and oceans.

As for now, the dragon race was rare, and the dragonman youngster could only glimpse at the divine might the dragon race had in ancient times from these bloodlines.

The dragonman youngster tested all of the dragon bloodlines once through, gaining usage experience and also producing all kinds of new notions.

“Unfortunately, Charming Blue Goddess has few believers with dragon bloodlines.”

“Otherwise, I’d be able to get all kinds of useful experience from divine blessings.”

“Perhaps I ought to have ‘faith’ in a dragon god?”

The dragonman youngster began to watch magic images.

The information in these images were records of past duelists fighting, training ground recordings, and other duelist related materials.

None were of silver levels, all were gold.

Upon returning, since the dragonman youngster wanted to go for the Dueling Godhead, the dragonman youngster naturally had to know himself and his enemies.

After watching for a while, the dragonman youngster had a deeper understanding of the National Ceremony Grand Duel.

“No saint level has participated in these duels.”

“Gold levels are the protagonists of the later duels, while silver levels are a level below them.”

“The experts participating in the duels are mostly Ice Sculpture Kingdom natives, but there is a minority of outsiders coming on account of its reputation.”

The dragonman youngster saw many gold level duelists, each of them had their own skills that were quite striking, and some would be very troublesome for him to deal with.

“The dragonman form is unlike the fishman form, and in public, if I don’t risk using a deception divine spell to mask myself, I can only use battle qi to fight,.”

Long Fu externally claimed to be a fighter that revealed a few racial magics.

Bing Yang’s Frost Aura had solved a big problem for the dragonman youngster, it was an acceptable explanation for the masses as to how a silver level suddenly became a gold level.

But this rational explanation would not help the dragonman youngster convince others when he told them: “I am not just a gold level; I am also an experienced caster and clergyman.”

Under the dragonman form, the young man’s combat strength was constrained, and couldn’t be fully displayed.

Using this form to duel, he would face a small number of gold levels that would be challenging.

“There is also that Long Meng, he is really strong!”

The dragonman youngster saw some of Long Meng’s duels, it shook his heart.

Each time Long Meng dueled, he held an enormous advantage and could efficiently resolve any battle without fooling around.

“He is absolutely not using his full strength!”

“With just a fraction of his power, he is able to dominate massively. His combat strength advantage is considerable, his combat tactics are implemented flawlessly, his mood is steady and stable, and he has no weaknesses or vulnerabilities.”

“It’s no wonder he is the most popular person in the National Ceremony Grand Duel!”

The dragonman youngster was unable to estimate Long Meng’s limits.

This feeling was like when he used the fishman form to deal with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo.

“If I use my fishman form, can I fight against Long Meng?” The dragonman youngster’s countenance was heavy.

He couldn’t calculate it.

Everything needed to wait until he personally traded blows to have a result.

Because key information was too scarce.

“Therefore, with regards to the entire mercenary corps, information is an immense weakness that needs to be complemented.”

The dragonman youngster thought of Zi Di’s situation when she met with danger inside the Wisteria store, she had been scared into a cold sweat. Recalling it now, she still had lingering fears about it.

Information gathering was lacking, and the survivors needed to develop an information organization, this was a consensus Zi Di and Zong Ge had come to earlier.

Now, the dragonman youngster endorsed it greatly.

“Information is very important.”

“Putting the rest aside, let me size up what’s in front of me. If I had formidable information gathering, I could scout out the strengths and weaknesses of gold level opponents, thereby focusing my guard.”

“Some gold level duelists, especially outsiders, often have some original skills that can win from a position of weakness and make a comeback from adversity.”

These skills were also a threat to the dragonman youngster.

When Zi Di was returning from the capital, the dragonman youngster was practicing battle skills.

After being promoted to a gold level fighter, the dragonman youngster always lacked time to practice.

The identity of Long Fu mainly still had to use battle skills. His prior suite of battle skills was reasonable, but they were silver level, and at the gold level, they needed to be supplemented.

The brunt of the burden was long ranged battle skills.

Gold level battle qi could condense outside the body, thus at this stage, a gold level fighter’s attack range rapidly expanded, and battle skills had all sorts of effects.

The dragonman youngster chose the Dragon Talon Strike battle skill as his main long ranged battle skill.

Then the important thing – a flying battle skill.

To the masses, flying battle skills were rare, but to the dragonman youngster, he was spoiled for choice.

Thank you again War Merchant!

The tower spirit’s database had many flying battle skills in it.

The dragonman youngster practiced his new battle skill a bit, then stopped after dipping into it.

“My progress is too immense; I can’t keep practicing.” The dragonman youngster sighed, worried at how fast he was improving.

Zi Di walked over, “The rumors we spread have gotten results already. This time, the Jing Xiang Clan has decided to send us an invitation letter.”

The dragonman youngster glanced at her, “The opportunity is ripe, transmit this message, the Jing Xiang Clan is bullying us around, and I Long Fu am unsatisfied, I want to challenge Mi Fang.”

The rumors merely gave the public an explanation. The dragonman youngster never thought about abandoning the Li Jian Clan to rely on the Jing Xiang Clan.

Cooperating with the Jing Xiang Clan still meant they were giving benefits to another party.

Why couldn’t he work on his own? Why couldn’t he alone enjoy the profits of the mount potions?

The dragonman youngster possessed peak gold level combat strength, had immense prestige, and could draw power from Charming Blue Goddess’ divine might.

The Li Jian Clan, an Ice Sculpture Kingdom top noble power, only had a gold level clan leader.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps nowadays were qualified to take over the mount potion benefits!

Now the problem was other people didn’t believe the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was qualified to hog all of the earnings.

Hehe.

The power revealed and painful beating that followed would wake them up and allow them to recognize who was strong and who was weak!

Negotiate with the Li Jian and the Jing Xiang Clans?

That would have to wait until after he defeated them.

Chapter 918: Mi Fang – What? Long Fu Challenged Me!

Through pure and lively magic glass, Mi Fang admired the snowscape outside his window.

This was the Ice Sculpture Capital.

Even though the wind and the snow was heavy, they did not fall here, for many alchemy formations reduced them to specks of pure white snowflakes.

These snowflake specks were minute, and outwardly emitted feeble gleaming radiance, and in the capital’s waning lights, they appeared tranquil, noble, and graceful.

Every time he looked at this kind of snowscape, Mi Fang would not want to leave.

He stopped in front of his wide window, hand on a hot drink, looking satisfied and content.

This house was located in the central zone of the capital, not only was the land extremely expensive here, but one had to have influence to purchase it.

And Mi Fang had struggled for over 20 years through his own power to be qualified to stand here and appreciate the snowscape.

At first, he had been a human who came from abroad to pursue his studies in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Mi Fang came from humble beginnings and understood how to climb his way up.

His luck was pretty good. Heaven had given him a gold level bloodline aptitude and a beautiful complexion. Even though he was amongst elves, he was very delicate, pretty, and handsome.

There were many kinds of beauty, and every race had different esthetics. Dwarfs held ruggedness in esteem, devils like malevolence and frightening looks, and elves like delicate and gorgeous looks.

Mi Fang’s features were very close to elven esthetics, and this caused numerous elven women to welcome him in.

When he was still a student, he sharply sensed that this was an advantage of his he could greatly exploit.

He maintained close relationships with many female elves, causing many people to be resentful. Many suitors began to challenge him in succession, publicly initiating duels.

At first, Mi Fang regretted it, thinking he went too far and drew in big troubles.

After hesitating for a good while, he summoned his courage and accepted a duel. After winning the duel, he realized he was being treated differently and suddenly realized there was a better shortcut than flaunting his male beauty.

“It was duels!” Mi Fang exclaimed in admiration.

The dueling culture seemed to have originated from the legend of the heroic duel, and it was quite popular in the Frost Continent and in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Duels weren’t just a widespread recreation amongst the masses, it permeated all aspects of life, and every social class was involved in them. Countless citizens used them to resolve disputes and decide how to allocate benefits.

Duels were so important, and from the beginning the Ice Sculpture Royal Family promoted them widely and often added fuel to the fire.

Mi Fang had a deep impression of them. He remembered that after losing, many challengers were not enraged over perceived humiliation, on the contrary they were pleased and acknowledged their allegiance to him, with a few even becoming his followers!

The dueling culture was the foundational gene of the kingdom.

So long as you won the duel, everything was easy to deal with.

Everyone would determine that you were stronger, admire you, serve you, and would back down willingly.

Even if you were an outsider and not an Ice Sculpture citizen, even if you weren’t a snow elf and were a human.

After learning how beneficial duels were, Mi Fang changed his style and engrossed immense energy into researching Ice Sculpture duels.

Each duel was both a challenge and an enormous opportunity!

Mi Fang wasn’t attending to one thing and losing sight of another, rather he was doing a two-pronged approach.

On the one hand he got close to snow elf women, and through all kinds of intimate relationships, he acquired plenty of cultivation resources, information, and more. While on the other hand, he was actively preparing to win the next duel.

Through the subsidies of his lovers, he overcame his lack of resources and enormously increased his dueling win rate. These dueling victories brought renown that projected his charm across the winds and waves. Because the many suitors he provoked from his pursuit of love became his dueling opponents.

Mi Fang firmly controlled both aspects of this, acting in concert with both to form a positive cycle that allowed his personal strength to rise little by little.

In the wake of his rising renown, he gradually caught the eye of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s high class.

When he became a gold level, his life reached its greatest turning point.

A gold level expert was the backbone of a region, and sufficient for the high class people to toss him an olive branch.

Finally, Mi Fang took a clan’s olive branch and became a member of the Jing Xiang Clan.

This was the outcome of his careful selection.

Although the Jing Xiang Clan was not as strong as the Han Yu Clan or the Li Jian Clan, they had a very strong foundation in magic medicine, and even though the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had high level alchemy, the Jing Xiang Clan was enormously competitive.

And Mi Fang was an outstanding pharmacist.

Of course, this was not the reason why he didn’t join the Han Yu or Li Jian Clan. Although these top clans were disposed to recruit him, they weren’t willing to let Mi Fang become a member of the clan and were more inclined to hire him to some important position. For example, the Li Jian Clan’s San Sha Man Tuo who held a position in the navy.

“If I wasn’t just a gold level, but a saint level, then my status would be entirely different.”

“A pity, I won’t ever have that chance in my life.” Mi Fang knew his bloodline potential had been completely exhausted and understood the gold level was the peak of his life.

The Jing Xiang Clan had many core gold levels, of high and low status.

For the next ten years, Mi Fang strived to climb the ranks of his clan.

He had become a son-in-law of the Jing Xiang Clan and greatly reduced his playboy lover flings. But he didn’t give up dueling, instead participating in them every so often to maintain his reputation.

He had participated in many duels, and in the wake of his accumulated experience, he gained many insights, seldom losing and often winning.

“In the end, duels have their limits. Or to be more precise, I have my limits.”

Mi Fang’s bloodline potential was exhausted, and after padding out his equipment to the pinnacle, his combat strength had hit a ceiling too.

There were far fewer gold level duelists than silver level ones.

Mi Fang also meticulously filtered his opponents, greatly reducing the available pool.

Duels were giving him less, so to make progress, Mi Fang had to turn his attention elsewhere.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ appearance allowed him to find a new golden opportunity.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps coming out with a dedicated mount potion opened up an unexplored market. When Mi Fang learned of this news, he felt like he was seeing a new world of great abundance.

He sallied out in full strength, maneuvered from top to bottom, and spent a large amount of capital and connections to become the Jing Xiang Clan’s provisional person in charge for this market.

Since then, the dragon rider potion was an instant hit and had fiery sales, exceeding industry expectations and attracting the eyes of countless people.

The Jing Xiang Clan reacted, wanting to get involved, but it was Mi Fang who seized that critical opportunity.

From the lowest rungs, Mi Fang had climbed to this height, so naturally he had talent.

He made an all-out effort and actively followed up, reversed engineered parts of the dragon rider potion with all speed and used it as a base to modify, obtaining the Jing Xiang Clan’s mount potion product from it.

Such magic medicine needed a large amount of testing before it hit the market.

But Mi Fang knew the importance of seizing market share, and chose to take a risk, standing his ground against the opinion of the masses and threw this mount potion into the market at once.

His gambling paid off.

The Jing Xiang Clan’s mount potion was comparable to the dragon rider potion. It was barely any different and didn’t have any undue side effects.

Thus, the Jing Xiang Clan business venture paid off, and Mi Fang stabilized his position, truly becoming the person in charge of this project.

This success motivated Mi Fang, causing him to become more proactive.

“The magic medicine business is the core benefit of the Jing Xiang Clan. I have become a son-in-law and have been a member of the clan for almost ten years before I was able to get into this field and become the person in charge of it!”

“The mount potion business has vast prospects, and now I have gained a foothold in it. I only need to take constant initiative to grab onto greater power, position, and benefits.”

“I can climb higher!”

Mi Fang knew his many shortcomings.

For example, he was a human, and in the end, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was led by snow elves. Even though he joined the Jing Xiang Clan, and even though he was in a loving relationship with his wife and children, he would never truly rise the ranks of the Jing Xiang Clan’s highest echelons.

He had already reached the limits of his bloodline, and attained the ceiling of his individual strength, and the Jing Xiang Clan limited his influence according to his capabilities, one he had reached.

Wanting to break through this limit was very difficult. Thinking of taking a small step beyond it was as difficult as ascending the heavens.

In the end, personnel of large high level organizations generally possessed enough political acumen to know that power was above all else.

The Jing Xiang Clan were nobles, and any noble family, including those of the Sheng Ming Empire, had the administrative consensus that the core should be strong while the branches were weak. Mi Fang was not a snow elf, and couldn’t even be considered a branch, the main members who controlled territory would never let an outsider like him in.

However, in reality, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gave Mi Fang a rarely seen golden opportunity.

Mi Fang did all he could to grab hold of this opportunity and break through his past limits!

Following the constant success of the Jing Xiang Clan mount potions, Mi Fang’s position and influence in the clan slowly rose.

This progress is beautiful.”

This kind of feeling intoxicated Mi Fang, and also spurred him on to climb higher, he wanted to tenaciously cling onto this unique chance and would never relinquish his grip on it!

Therefore, although he was a duelist, Mi Fang had complex emotions about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

On the one hand, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the duelists’ enemy, and to this day, the bodies of Jia Bing, Lin, and the Axe Gang Leader were still in their hands.

On the other hand, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gave Mi Fang an immense opportunity. Such an opportunity might only appear once in his life!

“I want to defeat these mercenaries and use them as a stepping stone for a higher position!”

Mi Fang’s greater motivation to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was complicated.

He already analyzed, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is the strongest in two areas.”

“First is the medicine they are selling. The three mount potions are of excellent quality; a professional can determine from a bit of reverse engineering and recipe analysis that they are refined and ingeniously designed.”

“Second is Long Fu’s enormous prestige. Long Fu killed Teng Dong Lang as a silver level. This wonderful start exceeds the hard work of many duelists’ lifetime achievements.”

Compared to Long Fu, Mi Fang’s battle record paled in comparison.

Mi Fang would have never though he’d be able to be in the limelight as a silver level.

Although his battle talent was excellent, he was not to the level of the dragonman youngster or Zong Ge.

“My bloodline potential falls well short of Long Fu’s.”

“His dragonman bloodline is clearly oriented towards battle and has a strong advantage. While my human bloodline is a production type bloodline.”

Therefore, in each duel, Mi Fang did not have it easy; to take steps forward, he had to secretly pay many things.

Every dueling opponent was prudently chosen, many tactics were planned, and he would arm himself with everything he had and deploy many magic medicines beforehand.

Each duel was an extremely risky opportunity, so he had to grasp certainty as much as possible.

He almost always fought at full strength, to the extent that he took too much medicine, causing a good deal of residual effects. Nowadays, those residual effects still tormented him.

This was just a small part of what he had to pay to reach the current position he climbed to.

Even though he prepared for battle with all he had, he could still lose.

He never had a winning streak like the dragonman youngster.

However, the consequences for losing were lighter than he envisioned. The Ice Sculpture citizens did not belittle or despise the losers that much, on the contrary, many times, many of them awaited the loser returning in a swirl of dust to bravely challenge experts.

“This is my dueling spirit!”

“Victory or defeat is just the result of a single duel, and in terms of the endless duels in one’s life, persevering with challenges was the best response.”

When Mi Fang grasped this concept, he was selected.

He became one of the duelists.

With regards to this important status, he did his best to keep it confidential, and not even his wife and children were aware of it.

Through his lovers, Mi Fang achieved a crucial start to accumulate assets through constant duels. The dueling spirit was how he made progress as an individual, and he was in complete agreement with it.

He had never been preached to, inspired, or received a divine oracle, yet he was a duelist with a dueling spirit.

It was precisely this intrinsic spirit that spurred him on and showed the way to constant improvement and advancement.

What he wanted to do now was to step on the heads of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to keep advancing!

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has formidable alchemy technology, and in the short time, I can only trail behind them.”

“But their other advantage enables me to make a move.”

Mi Fang, one who relied on duels to get rich, naturally knew that if Long Fu lost, his prestige would be reduced and that would be an immense attack against their mount potion business.

The Jing Xiang Clan could take advantage of that to rise and supersede the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to take hold of more of the national market.

Mi Fang moved actively in accordance with this notion.

Zong Ge was poisoned because of him. Until Zong Ge went through the Wine God Trial, this problem could not be resolved.

However, Mi Fang was intimidated by Zong Ge’s toughness and never dared to personally take the field.

Mi Fang believed the enemy was still formidable and needed to be weakened further, patiently waiting for a good chance.

He Gai Tou followed up, helping Bing Yang make things difficult for Long Fu.

As a duelist too, Mi Fang decided to come to his aid. Regretfully, despite Bing Yang’s reputation, he wasn’t strong enough. In their duel, Bing Yang wasn’t able to weaken Long Fu, instead it fomented the latter’s power.

Before Mi Fang could find a better method, he heard some new information.

Because of Bing Yang’s signature [Beautiful Future], the medicinal effect of the Frost Aura caused Long Fu to be promoted to the gold level!

Hearing this news, Mi Fang’s first reaction was to sneer. He believed that Long Fu was only pretending to be a silver level, he should actually be a gold level. Many people agreed on this viewpoint. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Tan Mo believed it was like that too.

Soon after, Mi Fang’s second reaction was that his eyes lit up, he could see a new opportunity from within this!

He suddenly noticed that after Long Fu became a gold level, there were now many more chances to deal with him!

Previously, Long Fu was only a silver level and finding other silver levels to not just publicly challenge him, but also defeat him, was too difficult!

Bing Yang was once a gold level, and when he fought “Long Fu”, he was suppressed the entire time and met a tragic end.

Now that Long Fu publicly declared himself a gold level, and had also directly beaten back “Hua Tang”, he had displayed his power.

This allowed Mi Fang to see many opportunities – other gold levels could challenge Long Fu directly. This wouldn’t incur gossip and spite, as the masses disdained the strong bullying the weak.

But Mi Fang would never do the job himself.

Even now, Zong Ge’s horrific achievement of killing three gold levels alone still severely deterred the duelists.

Mi Fang wanted to agitate ignorant gold levels to continuously impair Long Fu. But before he could start scheming, new information followed, one that overwhelmed him somewhat.

Overnight, it seemed the entire nation learned of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps having a falling out with the Li Jian Clan.

“Wonderful!” Mi Fang felt this was good news.

Putting himself into their shoes: Long Fu was a gold level who pretended to be a silver level. His name was being circulated nationwide right now, and the mount potion sales were flourishing and making a fortune every day. Now that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had a tentative foothold, would they need to keep on enduring?

The Li Jian Clan was not necessary for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

However, their benefits were not allocated in accordance with their contributions, who would put up with that?

At least Mi Fang believed that if he were Long Fu, he wouldn’t ever resign himself to that!

Additionally, Mi Fang analyzed that now was the best opportunity for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to have a falling out with the Li Jian Clan.

Because the Li Jian’s Second Navy had been routed on the front lines and their influence had cratered. The kingdom’s top brass was extremely dissatisfied with the Li Jian Clan!

“The Li Jian Clan has sunken to their lowest point, if they aren’t cut off now, when will they be?”

“These rumors should have been spread by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Soon after, Mi Fang inquired about a new rumor.

In the rumor, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to discuss collaboration with the Jing Xiang Clan!

“Wonderful.”

“It doesn’t matter who we work or don’t work with? The Jing Xiang Clan is better at magic medicine than the Li Jian Clan. It is only natural that it is more profitable to collaborate with us.”

Mi Fang did not think this was abrupt.

In fact, he always followed this with interest. According to the internal information he collected, the Alchemy Guild was close to acting and forced the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to sign a betting agreement.

Although the specific substance of the agreement couldn’t be determined, Mi Fang guessed this was the Alchemy Guild finally acting!

For a long time now, the strongest entity in the domain, the Alchemy Guild, hadn’t reacted much.

It was abnormal.

As he worked and managed, Mi Fang never forgot about the Alchemy Guild.

To him it seemed that because of unjustly distributed benefits, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps abandoned the Li Jian Clan and chose the Jing Xiang Clan to stand off against the Alchemy Guild!

“I didn’t expect this, instead of creating an opportunity, I waited for one to come by.”

As for Mi Fang, a duelist, collaborating with the enemy?

There were no problems whatsoever!

“I can work with them while also dealing with them secretly!”

Wasn’t feigning cooperation while targeting them surreptitiously not the easier way to render meritorious service?

“Wonderful, too wonderful for words.”

Therefore, after learning of this information, Mi Fang immediately dispatched a subordinate to send the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps an invitation letter. He invited Long Fu to a face-to-face meeting and stated he looked forward to them working together.

And now, he was waiting for his subordinate to return.
