Thump, thump, thump.

Not long after, the study door bell rang.

“Come in.” Mi Fang knew the subordinate with the invitation letter had returned, and he couldn’t help but smile at that.

However, the subordinate brought back a challenge letter.

Mi Fang opened it and read it, his expression changing, “Long Fu…wants to publicly challenge me?”

The subordinate also provided other information, “Lord Mi Fang, there are new rumors spreading through the capital. The propagators have already been confirmed to be the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“The rumors state: our clan has kicked around and looked down upon Long Fu during business negotiations as an outsider, forcing Long Fu to publicly challenge you Lord Mi Fang. He wants to use this duel’s outcome to advance this negotiation.”

Mi Fang: ?!

He soon reacted, subconsciously squeezing the challenge letter between his fingers, twisting the paper.

“This is Long Fu garnering support to force me into this duel.”

“Crucially…”

“I have no way to decline; I can only take up his challenge!”

“Damn it.”

In the public eye, Long Fu was just a newly promoted gold level with low combat strength. While Mi Fang was a long renowned and veteran gold level.

Therefore, this dueling invitation was the weak fighting the strong.

If an expert like Mi Fang declined, his prestige was bound to take a dive.

Pivotally, this duel was composed of complex elements, it concerned the lucrative mount potion benefits!

Pressure on Mi Fang not only came from the public, but also from the Jing Xiang Clan. After all, he was a foreigner, and the reason why he could sit stable in his current position was due to his outstanding achievements, which none could find fault with.

“If I shirk battle, I definitely suffer reprimand!”

“Malicious. Long Fu’s move this time is too malicious.”

“He is bullying me!”

Perhaps Long Fu wanted to negotiate, but only after he won the duel. If that happened, then the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would hold an enormous advantage in the negotiations.

“Long Fu is treacherous, while simultaneously being extremely overbearing!”

Mi Fang’s brows tightened, and anger turned his complexion white.

He was furious and resentful, but at the same time, he was even more nervous. Panic emerged from deep within his heart and quickly spread.

Because Mi Fang was very self-aware.

“If I rely on just myself, I will lose.”

“Them spreading such rumors means its impossible they are looking to lose this duel. They are very certain they will win!”

The pressure on Mi Fang was massive.

He thought of Zong Ge’s terrifying combat achievement.

He knew the Axe Gang Leader and the other two were no ordinary gold levels, all of them were duelists!

“With this method, just losing is the best outcome, I might lose my life!”

“More than one duelist has been slain by Long Fu’s hands.”

The study inside was as warm as spring, yet Mi Fang was overflowing with cold sweat.

This was the greatest crisis he had ever faced.

“I must arm myself fully!”

“I must draw support from the Jing Xiang Clan and ask the other duelists for help!”

“Damn it, we obviously have a lot of people, yet in this duel, I am the only one going into battle.”

“Damn it, why is there such a lousy rule!”

Mi Fang couldn’t sit still, he immediately got up, pushed the door open, and ran out at top speed.

Chapter 919: Mi Fang, Watch Your Strength, Don’t Kill Long Fu

Challenging Mi Fang was an overt plot, and the dragonman youngster was never worried about Mi Fang rejecting it.

Thus, after sending the challenge letter, he had mercenaries update the rumors.

Before Mi Fang could leave his study, these rumors spread throughout the capital.

The dragonman youngster was such a hot topic and his promotion to the gold level was very sudden, but it had basis in fact and a tinge of a legendary tale to it.

The news of him challenging Mi Fang rapidly disseminated.

“Did you hear? Long Fu actually challenged Mi Fang publicly!”

“I thought he would recover for a time after becoming a gold level. I didn’t expect he would be so dauntless and immediately make a move. I really look forward to it!”

“Most crucially, he decided not to challenge a silver level, nor an ordinary gold level, but Mi Fang. He is a veteran duelist and has outstanding strength.”

“This will be a good show.”

“What are Long Fu’s odds of winning?”

“Hic, very small. Although Mi Fang is not a snow elf, but a human, he is a veteran of a hundred duels, has been acknowledged by the high class, and is a member of the Jing Xiang Clan. Long Fu has just become a gold level, and it will be very difficult for him to deal with a veteran expert.”

“Saying that, if his probability of winning is small, why does he want to challenge Mi Fang?”

“Oh dear, you don’t know. This is about the collaboration between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan. You should know about the mount potions…”

The dragonman youngster didn’t do purposeful wide scale fabrications, rather they took advantage of the situation to make rumors.

These rumors were very close to reality, and very rational. They were primarily focused on the dragonman youngster’s individual figure – for the sake of his corps’ earnings, he had no choice but to bravely step forward as the weak challenging the strong and project bravery and responsibility!

This action couldn’t help but win the favor of the masses and elevate his reputation.

Especially the lower class, who loved to hear this kind of gallantry.

In a moment, Long Fu’s renown increased once again.

The gold level duel between him and Mi Fang quickly spread out of the capital and across the country.

Mi Fang quickly found out about these rumors, but he couldn’t bother with them, he needed to ask for help.

As a human and son-in-law of the Jing Xiang Clan, outside of his clan and himself, the biggest backer Mi Fang had was the hidden duelist faction.

Therefore, he first contacted Mei Lin.

Mei Lin’s prestige among the duelists was very high, and one could say it was second only to Long Meng. Outwardly, she was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s First Navy’s Rear Admiral.

Upon receiving Mi Fang’s plea, Mei Lin’s eyes contracted, “Long Fu launched an open challenge against you?”

“Yes.” Mi Fang was anxious, “I am certain to not be Long Fu’s match, and I am more worried that he’ll see through my duelist identity!”

Mei Lin pondered, “The probability is low.”

“Most crucially, I can’t find grounds to refuse this challenge. The masses view me as strong and him as weak. If I cower and refuse, I won’t have the integrity to raise my head.” Said Mi Fang.

He felt miserable. Obviously he was the weak one, but the entire nation believed he was stronger, that he was superior.

Mei Lin didn’t hesitate and replied, “We are duelists, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is our joint enemy. Regardless of whether Long Fu sees through you, that isn’t important at the moment. The important thing is we will spare no expense to arm you and maximize your combat strength.”

“This is an opportunity.”

“Before, Long Fu appeared to only be a silver level, and we didn’t know where to start.”

“Now that he’s ‘promoted’ to the gold level, our members can justly and honorably challenge him!”

“Mi Fang, you need to understand this: the outcome of this battle doesn’t matter. The important thing is that you keep yourself alive while simultaneously gauging Long Fu’s true skills. Especially since he won against three duelists alone, we need to know how he achieved that.”

“If you can get him to expose that trump card, our chances of prevailing over the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will increase by 20%.

When Mi Fang heard this, he couldn’t help but turn pale, thinking to himself, “If Long Fu used that trump card against me during the duel, would I survive?”

Regardless of what was said, Mei Lin’s full support was positive news.

Mi Fang took a deep breath, “I will fight with all I have, don’t hold any fantasies or extravagant hopes. As long as I can survive, it will be my greatest victory!”

Me Lin faintly wrinkled her brows.

She heard the unspoken implication in Mi Fang’s words.

When Mi Fang was stating his concerns, he said he was worried that Long Fu might unmask him as a duelist but didn’t provide enough evidence – this vagueness warned Mei Lin that if she didn’t save Mi Fang, he might “defect” and tell Long Fu the secret that he was a duelist.

Mi Fang’s final statement that ensuring his survival came first showed his pragmatic attitude, but on the other hand, it also illustrated his battle intent was quite weak. From this, Mi Fang tactfully refused Mei Lin’s request – he was unlikely to risk his life to expose the skill Long Fu used to win the 3 vs 1 battle!

“Mi Fang is not from the military, and he is mainly attacking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps for his own interests.” Mei Lin was very clear about this.

“But in the end, he is still a duelist member, and he proactively drew close to us, therefore, we have to help him fully!”

“Unfortunately, I am up to my ears at work right now.”

“I can only subsidize Mi Fang in other respects.”

Mei Lin was very busy with military matters.

Everything could be blamed on the Li Jian Clan. To preserve their strength, the Li Jian Clan did not openly clash with Da Han’s pirate alliance, instead they retreated without delay.

This caused many pirates to appear around Ice Sculpture Island and turned its coastline very treacherous.

Mei Lin was in the First Navy Fleet, and due to the situation, she had no choice but to undertake this maritime defense duty, working to deal with pirates large and small almost 24 hours a day.

Mi Fang’s business was more urgent and important, and after the communication finished, Mei Lin set aside her military affairs for a rare moment and mobilized her contacts and resources to make arrangements for Mi Fang.

“To outsiders, it seems that me and Mi Fang are unacquainted, therefore, this support needs to walk a winding road before being delivered to his hands.”

“I hope it can make it to him on time.”

Mei Lin went back to handling military affairs.

One of her trusted aides, the gold level Bei Jia, volunteered to fight. They had then sent a report that went roughly: Bei Jia caught up to the Vegetable Pirates and confirmed that their group had suffered grave damage and requested that they sally out and try to eliminate this pirate group.

Mei Lin’s heart rapidly soared at the related information: “Cong Mang’s pirates attacked on Snow Bird Harbor, consequently blowing up their magician tower, then Snow Bird Harbor City Lord brazenly exploded the city palace, making an unexpected comeback and causing them disastrous losses. Adding onto that mysterious fishman expert suddenly attacking, the Vegetable Pirates were forced to retreat…”

She rapidly evaluated the risks of this military operation, “Most of the Vegetable Pirates died in that explosion, but the Hoodlum battle squad is intact and there are other elites still on the island.”

“Cong Mang is unaccounted for, and the Vegetable Pirates lack a gold level expert overseeing them, Bei Jia has an immense advantage…we can attack!”

Mei Lin approved of Bei Jia’s program of action.

Mi Fang tried to find Gu Dong.

When he learned of Long Fu’s challenge, Gu Dong turned grave, “Mi Fang, you are not Long Fu’s match, you must be very careful.”

Gu Dong was from the army and had a straightforward way of speaking.

Gu Dong continued, “Take a look at my equipment, I will lend it all to you. In addition, you can come to the Dueling Divine Domain. I will do my best to play the role of Long Fu and use his fighting style to practice with you.”

Like Mei Lin, Gu Dong’s attitude was firm, and without hesitation, he fully supported Mi Fang.

Mi Fang was not reserved and asked to borrow Gu Dong’s shield on the spot.

He tried to find Bao Li Gen.

Bao Li Gen was busy and stated he’d get back to him later.

Mi Fang looked for Yi Jiu, the latter threw up his hands and said he was healing, impoverished, and lacked financial resources. If Mi Fang hired him at a large sum as an assassin, then he would act.

Mi Fang’s eyes lit up, “Are you willing to assassinate Long Fu?”

“Are you joking?” Yi Jiu’s tone jumped, “That is too risky. I meant I could help you assassinate other members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and distract Long Fu.”

Mi Fang’s face turned dark.

How could he possibly do that?

This duel was too hot and was bound to be covered nationwide. At this critical juncture, wanting to assassinate other members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would put Mi Fang into the greatest suspicion. Regardless of the evidence, his and the Jing Xiang Clan’s reputation would be sorely tested.

If Yi Jiu could assassinate Long Fu, then it would be easy to deal with, but simply assassinating anyone else would be more of a hindrance than a help to Mi Fang and stir up endless troubles.

Mi Fang severed his connection to Yi Jiu, then contacted He Gai Tou.

He Gai Tou had helped Bing Yang find trouble with Zong Ge and could secretly help Mi Fang too.

With this factor, after He Gai Tou learned of the news, he didn’t make any excuses and was willing to help.

He Gai Tou also said, “Old Brother Mi Fang, finding me was the right thing to do, I can get other duelists to help you.”

This sentence induced Mi Fang’s interest.

Mi Fang questioned him, closely.

He Gai Tou was somewhat proud of himself, “You know, I have many gambling houses. For this duel, I can manipulate the gambling joints nationwide. Before the duel, we can hype up your win chance and do our best to raise the masses’ hopes in you, then we can secretly bet a massive sum on Long Fu winning!”

Mi Fang: …

He Gai Tou continued to persuade eloquently, “No need to be so mentally burdened, Elder Brother Mi Fang.”

“You are certain to lose. But your loss needs to have value, isn’t that so?”

“Even if you lose, you can profit from the gambling and obtain a hefty sum. This is very sensible.”

Mi Fang: …

“Let me think it over carefully.” Mi Fang disconnected with He Gai Tou.

He was just about to contact Xue Tuan Zi when Bao Li Gen called.

Bao Li Gen’s voice was loud, and his temperament was fiery, speaking even more straightforward than Gu Dong, “What do you need help with, tell me. Don’t be afraid to kill him! One word, kill! If you can’t kill him, at worst you’ll die, what’s there to be afraid of?”

When Mi Fang heard Bai Li Gen’s loud voice bombarding his ears, he powerlessly ridiculed in his mind, “I am the one afraid to ‘die’, ugh…”

Apart from Bao Li Gen, Xue Tuan Zi also contacted him first.

Xue Tuan Zi, “I heard about your problem, Lord Mi Fang, I’m here to cheer you on.”

“I want to participate in the Warm Snow Cup Competition, so I can’t personally come to your aid.”

Mi Fang and Xue Tuan Zi’s relationship wasn’t deep, but at this moment, he was very blunt and directly asked, “I have plenty of equipment. My greatest deficiency is in life-saving items, if you have any, Xue Tuan Zi, lend them to me.”

Mi Fang contacted almost all of the duelists to gain as much support as he could.

He returned to the Jing Xiang Clan, and someone immediately notified him that the clan leader had summoned him and to contact him now.

After activating the emergency communication device, the Jing Xiang Clan Leader appeared as a three-dimensional magic image and floated in front of Mi Fang.

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader smiled and appeared calm, proceeding to say, “Oh Mi Fang, you are a talent worthy of my investment. I wanted to congratulate you ahead of time for distinguishing yourself once again.

Mi Fang: …

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader continued, “You submitted a list for me to look at, I am very satisfied. Even though you are facing a newly promoted gold level, you still maintain your cautious approach and are never negligent, asking the clan’s utmost support.”

“Anyways, your caution is somewhat excessive.”

“It is inevitable that others inside the clan will be displeased.”

Therefore, I can’t grant some of the things on the list, I hope you can understand.”

Mi Fang’s expression changed, and he promptly spoke out to tactfully fight for himself.

After talking in circles, the Jing Xiang Clan Leader’s patience vanished, and he waved his hand, “You applied for three pieces of gold level armor, that is really excessive.”

“Recently, you’ve been doing wonderfully, and you’ve obtained power and a clan position that matches your achievements.”

“But you shouldn’t treat this as a chance to probe at the clan’s bottom line for what you can get from us!”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader directly slapped down Mi Fang.

Mi Fang looked agonized.

The Jing Xiang Elder spoke in a conclusive manner, “This battle concerns the mount potions, and it concerns my clan’s core benefits. You need to win decisively, the more beautiful your victory, the more advantageous we’ll be in the following discussion!”

“But keep this in mind, remember to greatly restrain your power.”

“You must not kill Long Fu, if you kill him, our relationship with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will be reduced too enmity, and I fear there will be no chance of working with them.”

Mi Fang went agape, speechless from those words.

The Jing Xiang Leader had made a grave misjudgment of the situation!

First, Mi Fang had an irresolvable enmity with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Second, what Mi Fang was most worried about was Long Fu killing him.

Chapter 920: Long Fu vs Mi Fang – Long Fu Has Saint Level Potential?!

Ice Sculpture Capital.

The dueling arena was packed full, with even the corridors filled with standing spectators.

The capital’s residents’ enthusiasm for watching duels was naturally the highest in the nation.

Both sides entered the arena, immediately causing the audience to cheer.

The skies resonated with cheers as the fully armed Mi Fang, clad like a heavy armored knight, cautiously stared at the dragonman youngster, who had stopped walking.

Long Fu was carrying the Gold Flower Hacksaw, a metal glove (the Striking Shifter), his red dragon scales were left exposed, and the amount of transcendent equipment was not even a third of Mi Fang’s.

The two struck a pose, giving rise to heated debate.

“Is that Mi Fang? The layers of transcendent light around him are really blinding.”

“That’s a bit excessive, Mi Fang has at least three kinds of gold level armor, he obviously has the advantage in that aspect.”

“Lord Mi Fang…I have never seen Lord Mi Fang so heavily armed.”

The people were universally shocked by Mi Fang’s excessive equipment, especially the female audience. These people were the faithful and long-time fans who watched Mi Fang’s duels.

“Let the duel officially begin!” The dueling arena master personally presided over this duel.

Gold level duels were rare, so normally, the dueling arena master appeared personally for them.

The crowd’s passionate debating rapidly quieted, and everyone concentrated their attention, awaiting what kind of marvelous duel would play out between the two gold levels.

However, surprising the audience, the perceived strong one, Mi Fang, did not attack first, instead he suddenly retreated back.

He flew backwards while pouring all kinds of potions into his mouth.

Mi Fang’s armored faceplate could open up, not hindering his consumption of medicine.

Glug, glug…

His laryngeal prominence constantly rolled as he drank at least ten different potions down in a short span.

Rays of colorful light gradually shined inside and out of his body as all kinds of effects strengthened him.

“Look! That is one of Lord Mi Fang’s trump cards, being able to take many kinds of potions in a short span. The reason why he can do this is thanks to the special medicine he developed, the [Dregs Evaporation Compound].”

“Indeed, normally Lord Mi Fang uses this in the later parts of a duel, but in this one, he actually used it at the very beginning?”

“Nothing can be done about it, are the mount potion’s benefits that massive? Mi Fang must be under immense pressure from the Jin Xiang Clan; therefore, he is making an all-out effort from the start!”

“I hope Long Fu can withstand it, Mi Fang at full power is quite formidable.”

The audience were outsiders, and they were making guesses in real time, a bit worried for the dragonman youngster.

When the dragonman youngster saw Mi Fang retreating, he didn’t chase after him, instead he took out magic scrolls.

As Mi Fang was drinking potions, he tore open the magic scrolls.

As Long Fu, an expert battle qi cultivator, he only had some racial magics. Therefore, the dragonman youngster couldn’t use magic publicly and had to use magic scrolls as a substitute.

Like Mi Fang, the dragonman youngster’s body was quickly empowered by many enchantment spells.

Both sides actually made the same choice.

The difference was that Mi Fang was using potions while the dragonman youngster was using magic scrolls.

The differences between both quickly appeared.

When Mi Fang drank his 12th potion, he belched with satisfaction, then began to imbue his battle qi into all of his defensive items.

At this time, the dragonman youngster was still tearing scrolls.

The many enchantments empowered the dragonman youngster’s body. The myriad effects and multicolored radiance enveloped him to the point where his face was blurry.

Mi Fang was stunned.

The entire dueling arena rumbled.

“Long Fu is actually able to augment himself with so much magic?”

“Heavens, his bloodline definitely isn’t ordinary.”

“Far from unordinary? This amount of magical enhancements proves he absolutely has a saint level disposition.”

“I didn’t expect he’d unexpectedly be a saint level seed!?”

“It’s no wonder after Bing Yang’s Frost Aura, he could quickly become a gold level. So, the gold level isn’t his limit, it is just a part of his cultivation journey.”

For a moment, countless scorching eyes were cast at the dragonman youngster.

In the audience, the disguised Cong Mang sneered in his mind, “Gasping at the tip of the iceberg?”

He had personally witnessed the fishman youngster’s empowered state and knew the fishman youngster’s aptitude exceeded the saint level, in the future he might become a legend level!

Although Cong Mang wasn’t like Hun Tong, who had seen the fishman use divine and magic spells to enhance himself with his own eyes, the fishman youngster had beaten him up with his own hands, giving him a deeper perception of his state than Hun Tong.

When the whole audience exploded in shock, the dragonman youngster sensed he was done.

He looked serene and his mood was very stable.

He decided to expose his aptitude in front of everyone to prepare for all kinds of operations in the future.

When Mi Fang saw the towering dragonman youngster in the distance, he didn’t attack as he felt his limbs go stiff.

He had participated in many duels, but never one like this. They hadn’t even crossed blows, yet he felt complete pressure on him.

Mi Fang’s brain was a constant churn, “A saint level bloodline? Long Fu must have that kind of aptitude?”

“His arms merchant backer is willing to send out a saint level seed?”

Under normal circumstances, if an organization found a saint level seed, they would protect them by every possible means and cultivate them secretly with all they had.

Someone like the dragonman youngster running all over the place was really rare.

“Perhaps, Long Fu is unaware of his talent, and just discovered it recently? After all, bloodline testing is always unreliable.”

“The scariest situation is if they know.”

If it was the latter situation, then the arms merchant behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps must have made ample preparations before sending Long Fu out.

Mi Fang quickly thought of Zong Ge’s frightful 3 v 1 accomplishment.

To this day, the duelists had no idea how Zong Ge had accomplished this.

Mi Fang’s forehead began to sweat, “Perhaps we underestimated Long Fu, underestimated the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Since he is a saint level seed, then he must have ample trump cards that can save his life.”

“As for how he did that 3 v 1, perhaps that’s just a part of his trump cards.”

“Outside of attacks, he might also have defensive and recovery trump cards, and these trump cards are often greater than the offensive trump cards.”

“Right, there is also teleportation.”

“Saint level teleportation scrolls can achieve blinking.”

“Unlike other capitals, the Ice Sculpture Capital does not prohibit spatial teleportation.”

“Therefore, this guy Long Fu is secure in the knowledge that he has backing!”

Mi Fang was an astute man, and that shrewd head of his allowed him to climb from the bottom to his current position, he could think of many things.

And the more he thought, the greater the pressure on his mind.

Seeing Mi Fang entrenching in place and showing no signs of attacking, the dragonman youngster’s eyes quickly and sharply flashed.

“Psychological tactics are coming into effect.”

“As expected, deceptive methods can not only be used in war, but also in individual duels.”

“Hehehe, I have already seized an opportunity to win this battle!”

Realizing this, the dragonman youngster grew more confident.

Battle skill – Surge.

He activated his battle qi, and explosions under his soles thrusted him forth. He became a red artillery shell that shot at the distant Mi Fang.

“Come!” Mi Fang’s heart tightened, waiting at the ready.

Ding!

Ting, ting, ting…

The two crashed into each other, and soon after, they used their respective weapons in an intense clash.

The dragonman youngster manipulated the Striking Shifter, constantly turning it into all sorts of weapons that matched the different battle skills he used in a constant barrage of attacks.

Mi Fang held two shields, one large and one small, forming a solid defensive line that blocked the dragonman youngster’s assault.

The spectator universally had a weird expression on them.

“I didn’t expect the aggressor to be Long Fu and not Mi Fang.”

“Long Fu is very strong, how many weapon types can he use?”

“Lord Mi Fang’s battle intelligence gathering is remarkable, he didn’t underestimate Long Fu and prepared many defensive items, how useful!”

The capital’s residents had abundant spectating experience.

As the dragonman youngster and Mi Fang fought, most people could see that Long Fu was gradually losing the initiative.

Don’t be fooled by Mi Fang’s defensive posture, by using his shields, armor, and all kinds of alchemy items, he maintained his defense and grew increasingly calm.

Under these circumstances, when he began to officially counterattack, he might overturn the battle and start winning.

“Mi Fang’s attacks are steady, not bad.” Knowing the implications were profound, the Jing Xiang Clan Leader was personally watching.

At first, he thought that Mi Fang coming fully armed was making a big fuss over a minor issue, but afterwards, he watched with growing satisfaction.

He felt he figured out Mi Fang’s strategy, “This is a defensive and counterattack tactic.”

“First, he will let Long Fu go on the offensive to deplete his battle q and see through his offensive skills.”

“Then, when he finds a suitable opportunity, when he counterattacks, he will control the situation and keep Long Fu pressured until it ends in his loss!”

He wrongly judged Mi Fang.

From beginning to end, Mi Fang hadn’t thought about counterattacking, the many tactics he thought of only had one goal, to keep himself alive!

Only now…

“Did I misperceive it?”

“After fighting against Long Fu, and feeling the offensive pressure of Striking Shifter, it isn’t as big as I envisioned.”

Mi Fang’s ambition grew a bit.

His sense were not wrong.

Before, Zong Ge had used two weapons with the Striking Shifters, and after creating an opportunity in battle, attacked with all his strength and displayed the graceful bearing of a Great Weapons Master.

The dragoman youngster was not a weapon master.

Were it not for the divine blessings giving him many experiences and memories in weapon usage, he wouldn’t be able to pretend to be one.

Even so, he was no Great Weapons Master, and despite using his full strength, he was unable to match Zong Ge in that.

Mi Fang had been present and witnessed Zong Ge behead Bing Yang. For the purpose of this duel, he had watched every battle recording of Long Fu at least a hundred times!

“Perhaps I should counterattack?” Mi Fang licked his dry lips.

Chapter 921: Long Fu vs Mi Fang – Ruthless Explosion!

“Mi Fang is too conservative!” In the crowd, Hua Tang’s brows tightened.

Of course, he was excellently disguised and mixed in the audience, barely catching any eyes.

He had been watching and knew of Mi Fang’s defensive tactics, causing the latter to miss many battle opportunities.

“This guy, Mi Fang, he is too scared of Long Fu’s trump cards.”

“This caused him to choose such defensive tactics and during real combat, his battle will is also naturally constrained massively.”

“This kind of mentality is an immense influence; he is unable to conform with the threat his strength provides!”

“Hurry up and see the truth already.”

As if hearing Hua Tang’s shout, Mi Fang licked his dry lips, and the notion of counterattacking began to gush from the bottom of his heart.

At this moment, Mi Fang was wearing full armor, including heavy armor, inner armor, and soft armor.

His face was completely hidden by a cavalry type metal visor, and no one could see his expressions.

But the moment his heart began to get restless, the dragoman youngster’s mind suddenly flashed, “Oh? He wants to counterattack?”

Mi Fang had prepared fully and during their confrontation, the dragoman youngster didn’t display the oppressive power of a Great Weapons Master, so Mi Fang being inclined to fight was entirely natural.

The dragonman youngster suddenly halted his offensive and fell back a step.

This abrupt act immediately attracted everyone’s attention.

A moment later, the dragonman youngster’s next action surprised them.

He tossed away the Shifting Striker, the Gold Flower Hacksaw, as well as the alchemy crossbows at his waist!

The dragonman youngster straightened his chest and growled, “Mi Fang!”

“What are you doing?”

“Right now, you are not the opponent I was looking forward to.”

“Where is your courage? Your performance is too ugly and could never compare to your past battle charm.”

“Don’t be afraid.”

“Look, I have thrown away my weapons, I will only fight with my fist and feet now.”

“If you keep this performance up, then you only deserve my fists!”

Mi Fang was stupefied, “Long Fu…just did what?!”

Just as he had the urge to counterattack, it had been dispelled in a split second.

After all, the dragonman youngster had acted so bizarrely, so outrageously. Who would decide to discard advantages during a crucial duel?

This included Hua Tang, who for a moment was puzzled as his brows creased.

“No, this must be a plot of his!” Mi Fang once again grew nervous, and caution blinded him, “He is treacherous, sinister, he must have other schemes.”

“Is he thinking of luring me into attack, causing a weak point to appear in my defense?”

“Yes, that should be it!”

“I must keep up my defense, my powerful defense. He won’t be able to attack. If he uses that offensive trump card, perhaps the price paid will be very high.”

“I can’t fall into his trap!”

“Keep defending, I must not give the enemy an opportunity.”

Next, just as the dragonman youngster claimed, he abandoned his weapons and used his fist and feet to attack.

Without weapons, his offensive was weaker using just punches and kicks.

But this weakness on the contrary made Mi Fang more vigilant, and he was almost convinced that the dragonman youngster was luring him into his ruse.

All of Mi Fang’s energy was invested into defense, he barely moved and his entire body was as sturdy as an army fort.

In this confrontation, the spectators’ attitude clearly changed.

“Long Fu really is bright and open.”

“He actually decided to discard his weapons and only use his weaker fists and feet. It seems quite stupid, but I like him more for it.”

“Yes, although he is weaker than Mi Fang, he longs to fight to his heart’s content.”

“That’s right! Although he is an outsider, he possesses a powerful dueling spirit. He doesn’t want an ugly duel and isn’t worried about layered interests, on the contrary, Mi Fang…oh dear!

“Mi Fang changed his fighting style, and from the beginning, strongly defended himself and never had a decent counterattack. He is too dedicated to the mount potion benefits, isn’t that so?”

“Perhaps the Jing Xiang Clan put him under too much pressure?”

“Then again, the mount potion benefits really are immense!”

The audience gave their opinions one after the other.

They weren’t in the know, and were unaware of the truth, only able to guess and analyze at the surface level clues.

When Hua Tang heard these discussions, his heart gradually went chilly. Quite the discouraging opinion floated into his mind, “Could it be that everything was deliberately caused by Long Fu?”

“He deliberately weakened himself to build even greater renown?”

Hua Tang was a wholly indigenous Ice Sculpture citizen, and he understood that Long Fu’s bearing was extremely heroic and that won the hearts of the people!

“No, that can’t be determined just yet.”

“Long Fu might be luring in his opponent, and Mi Fang choosing defense is the most dependable option.”

Hua Tang couldn’t be certain; therefore, he was unable to give any warnings.

As for the dragonman youngster, he was already prepared to strike.

Battle skill – Dragon Talon Strike.

He opened his dragon talon, and constantly brandished it, with each wave, he could shoot out four golden talon prints.

The talon prints shot out into Mi Fang’s shield, splashing into countless golden light specks. The strong impact often caused Mi Fang’s shield to compress.

Battle skill – Dragon Wings.

The dragonman youngster’s gold battle qi condensed on his back, forming a pair of long dragon wings.

He flapped his wings, easily flying into the air.

He danced in the air, circling around Mi Fang, constantly bombarding him with his new long ranged battle skill.

These battle skills had just been learned by him.

The fighter experiences he obtained from Charming Blue Goddess was lacking in things for the dragonman form. Adding on that he only practiced a bit beforehand, only gaining a superficial knowledge of these skills.

Therefore, when these battle skills were used, it was clear he was unfamiliar with them.

However, as time passed, he rapidly became proficient in these new battle skills and made them passable in practice.

When all of the spectators saw this, they were rather dumbfounded.

“I was really looking forward to this duel, last night I couldn’t sleep from excitement. But I didn’t expect this fight to turn out to be so tedious. Long Fu is actually treating this as training!”

“The main reason is Mi Fang, alas, I had good prospects for him, but seeing his performance now, it really disappoints people. Long Fu is practicing battle skills, yet he hasn’t counterattacked, is he still a veteran duelist? Is he still Long Fu’s senior?”

“But then again, isn’t Long Fu mastering his battle skills a bit too fast?”

“No, it’s really fast! If I hadn’t seen it with my own eyes, I could never have envisioned that someone in this world could master a new battle skill in such little practice, and it’s not even just one!”

“Long Fu has extremely immense fighting talents! He was empowered by many enhancement spells too; he definitely has a saint level bloodline. His rapid learning speed must be his bloodline’s contribution.”

“As his practice continues, the stronger he will become.”

“Oh dear, will Mi Fang passively watch as his opponent gets stronger? That’s very unwise.”

The audience lamented for Mi Fang, frustrated that he failed to meet their expectations, while simultaneously having greater prospects for Long Fu.

This was precisely one of the goals the dragonman youngster wanted to achieve.

The reason why he deliberately hadn’t practiced his new battle skills was that he wanted to build his image as a genius in front of the masses.

This public persona was very useful, especially since he was a “newly promoted” gold level, and without having the public persona of a genius, how could he so quickly improve, how could he have the power to be the ultimate winner?

Mi Fang naturally sensed the audience’s attitude changing.

He was furious.

The dragonman youngster’s actions clearly showed he was trampling Mi Fang underfoot, to make him worthless, and then to serve as the backdrop to Long Fu’s heroism and talent!

“This is an overt plot.”

“He is forcing my hand, forcing me to attack at my own accord!”

“In order to do this, he deliberately put on a show, bringing out ‘newly learned’ battle skills, but because he is a genius, he appears to have rapid and astonishing progress.”

Mi Fang needed to cough out blood.

He felt that Long Fu’s acting was too good, it was duping the masses and also pressuring him!

“No, all of this is his stratagem.”

“I cannot let my mentality unbalance me and fall into his ruse!”

“Since I have already lost my face, then I am even more determined to carry out my tactic till the end.”

“He cultivates the Bursting Explosion battle qi technique, that battle qi is famous for not lasting long.”

“He is attacking at full strength, and I am defending with all my strength. Abandoning his weapons actually isn’t rational. By using boxing, he depletes his battle qi more!”

“His depletion rate is greater than mine, therefore I am actually holding an advantage in this. As time goes on, the greater this advantage will be.”

“By the time both he and I reach a qualitative change in depletion, I will attack, that is the most sensible choice.”

“Even though he possesses some formidable attack skill that can win a 3 v 1, it absolutely needs a lot of battle qi.”

“When his battle qi is depleted to a certain degree, then this offensive skill will be difficult to use.”

“When that moment comes, I will counterattack and be the ultimate winner!”

“So long as I am the final victor, the face I lost in this duel will return in full. The masses will completely realize that I was right, I was the most sober person!”

The dragonman youngster’s constant humiliation did not infuriate Mi Fang into losing his rationality, on the contrary, it was constantly increasing and solidifying his fighting spirit.

Mi Fang was no ordinary gold level, although he had drawn support from many external resources in his personal struggle, it was by his own active choices.

He was shrewd, his insight was original, and even though he was confronted with an expert like Long Fu, he could already make out the road to victory!

However, his way of thinking had been within the dragonman youngster’s calculations.

“It’s nearly time.” The dragonman youngster thought.

He suddenly stopped in the air, the dragon wings on his back freely keeping him hovering.

“Mi Fang, since you’ve decided to turtle up, then taste this battle skill properly!” The dragonman youngster said that as he raised his arms.

He straightened his arms and opened his claws as they were held above his head.

Surging gold battle qi bubbled out and quickly condensed into a gigantic ball.

The ball of battle qi was at first a small ball, but after a few breaths, it expanded to the size of a washbowl. Soon after, as it drew from the dragonman youngster’s battle qi, it quickly grew into a battle qi ball one meter in diameter.

“That is the Battle Qi Ball.” Everyone quickly recognized the dragonman youngster’s battle skill.

The battle skill – Battle Qi Ball was a classic move. Based on its foundation, fighters developed many kinds of battle skills that were spread widely. Thus, even ordinary people know about it.

“Long Fu has Bursting Explosion battle qi, therefore the battle qi ball he condensed should be [Battle Qi Ball – Bursting Explosion]. This will be a ferocious explosion.”

The essence of [Battle Qi Ball] rests with being able to hold a large amount of many kinds of energy types. Long Fu doesn’t have a way to harmonize a variety of energy sources, he singularly cultivates battle qi, and if he dual cultivated magic or divine spells, he could imbue it with his magic power or divine power, at least doubling its might!”

“This type of battle skill doesn’t suit him; it should be a newly practiced skill of his. Looking at the speed of his battle qi condensing, he is a novice at it.”

“Is Mi Fang going to turtle again? He must disturb Long Fu and stop the battle qi ball from being condensed as much as possible.”

Mi Fang decisively acted.

If he let Long Fu keep accumulating battle qi, then the battle qi ball would get stronger, far exceeding Long Fu’s conventional firepower.

Mi Fang used no less than three alchemy items to sufficiently interfere.

The dragonman youngster dodged left and right, and when he couldn’t dodge the final attack, he would rather use his body to endure it, saving the battle qi ball and continuing to condense it.

Mi Fang didn’t stop, continuing to use long ranged attacks and increasing his interference efforts.

The dragonman youngster shouted, then in spite of everything, stuck his talon, followed by his claw, into the battle qi ball, then dove down.

Everyone: ?!!

Mi Fang: !!!

Because the battle qi ball had formidable explosive power, after fighters used it, they would throw it from a distance.

It was like a bomb.

Who would fight with a bomb strapped to them? It was like a suicide bombing.

“Long Fu…is actually this dauntless?”

“The battle qi ball has already reached a diameter of 2 meters, and adding on Bursting Explosion battle qi, that bomb will cripple, if not kill Long Fu!”

“He must be relying on something.”

“Damn it, I can’t dodge!” Mi Fang was extremely anxious, he made a supreme effort to evade, but time and space would not allow it.

Boom!!!

Within the fierce explosion, the dragonman youngster advanced with his head high.

One of Mi Fang’s shields was blown away, while the other burst open wide.

As smoke and dust gradually dispelled, the audience subconsciously stood up.

Soon after, the whole audience saw this-

The dragonman youngster was soaked in blood and his exploded dragon scales fluttered in the air, exposing black skin and flesh.

His left arm, the one he pulled the battle qi ball with, was almost turned to charcoal, and his claws were blown off.

While his right hand was held high, his claws grabbed the nape of Mi Fang, tenaciously pinching the latter.

Mi Fang’s face was a dark reddish purple; he wanted to struggle yet had no strength to strike back.

Due to his intense will to live, Mi Fang resisted his vertigo and struggled to cry out, “I, I concede…”

Just as the dragonman youngster was about to choke him to death, he suddenly relinquished his dragon claw.

As Mi Fang fell, he fell unconscious.

The dragonman youngster raised his right arm, firmly making a fist, faced the assembled audience, and roared.

After a silence, the entire audience’s cheers shook the heavens.

Chapter 922: Long Meng’s Invitation

A divine spell’s radiance lingered on the dragonman youngster’s body, waning from bright to dark, then fading away.

The Glacier God bishop coughed, exposing a weak expression.

The priest behind him had been staring at the dragonman youngster the moment he saw him until it finished, and despite how long the treatment was, the excitement and veneration in his face was not reduced by even a hairsbreadth.

The priest was quite young, and from his appearance one could see he was similarly looking to the bishop.

“Lord Bishop, many thanks.” The dragonman youngster moved his dragon claw as he expressed his thanks.

This treatment had fully healed the injuries he had incurred during the battle.

Of the three transcendent systems, divine spells were the best at healing. Magic could not imitate it, nor could battle skills supersede it, rather it was divine power, a universally applicable energy source, that gave healing divine spells a very broad range of applications.

The Glacier Bishop was solemn and respectful, expressing that he really wanted to treat the dragonman youngster. If he required his aid in the future, he would be willing to do it again.

Soon after, when the dragonman youngster wanted to pay for the treatment, the Glacier Bishop gave him a discount of 20% as a friendship price.

As they left, the young priest behind him finally couldn’t restraint himself, and he said to the dragonman youngster, “Lord Long Fu, I personally watched your decisive battle with Mi Fang. Your attack was truly bold and unconstrained!”

The young priest gave the dragonman youngster a thumbs up, remarkably like a fanboy.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “So, you were a supporter of mine.”

“Come here I’ll give you a small gift.”

The dragonman youngster waved his hand, and an attendant sprang to action, taking out an ice sculpture.

“Your distinguished country’s alchemy technology is the best in the present age. It is especially good at ice sculptures, and I too follow the local customs, so I hired an ice sculpture great master to carve some gifts.”

“After every major duel, regardless of the result, I will make some ice sculptures and use them to repay my supporters.”

“Please accept it.”

The Glacier bishop slightly frowned, he had a wide field of view and immediately noticed that this ice sculpture was made out of high grade materials and that its workmanship was extremely exquisite. It was made from many kinds of gold level materials, and from the engravings, one could see the alchemy techniques in it, it was certain to be some great master’s style.

“This is too precious…” The Glacier bishop was just about to refuse when the young priest seemed to move like lightning to take it.

“Are you really giving it to me?”

“This is wonderful, so wonderful!”

“Oh, it truly is excellent!!”

The young priest was utterly delighted as he excitedly held the ice sculpture of Long Fu, his eyes shining with overwhelming joy.

The posture of the Long Fu ice sculpture was of him throwing the gigantic battle qi ball as he swooped downwards. The ice sculpture was true to life, perfectly depicting the dragonman youngster’s bravery, ferocity, determination, and grace.

When the Glacier bishop saw how red with excitement the young priest was, he let out an exasperated sigh and expressed his sincere and cordial gratitude to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster accompanied the two people to the inn’s doorway.

This hotel had an astonishing backer, it was the Han Yu Clan’s property, and it represented the Han Yu Clan’s face to a degree, and when the dragonman youngster chose it, it became their temporary residence in the capital.

The grand noble Han Yu Clan was only second to the Ice Sculpture Royal Family in the kingdom and was far stronger than the Li Jian Clan.

The two clergymen the dragonman youngster sent off belonged to the Glacier Church.

The Glacier God was one of the main gods of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and the Glacier Church was the largest and strongest religious institution in the entire nation. Although they hadn’t set up their religious headquarters here, to serve as the Ice Sculpture Capital’s Bishop, an organization naturally had to be extraordinary.

“My identity as Long Fu, my promotion to the gold level, and my victory over Mi Fang is sufficient enough to face the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s high class squarely.” The dragonman youngster thought.

He was himself a cleric and simultaneously a gold level magician, completely capable of healing himself.

But he didn’t do that, instead he specifically asked the Glacier bishop to come over.

Of course, this was no random decision.

The young priest was the Glacier bishop’s son and was usually a favorite of the latter. During the duel between the dragonman youngster and Mi Fang, he shouted enthusiastically from the sidelines.

“With the Glacier bishop’s healing, my injuries can be confirmed, and I will also get a witness to my saint level aptitude.”

“But my fighter strength can still be improved.”

The dragonman youngster knew that he was able to defeat Mi Fang mainly due to psychological warfare. If Mi Fang really fought, with just his fighter skills, it would be a narrow win for him.

“As Long Fu, I cannot use magic or divine spells.”

“Equipment can’t also constitute and advantage.”

“Only bloodlines can be focused on.”

“A saint level bloodline can spurt a massive growth rate, and it is feasible for ‘Long Fu’, a newly promoted gold level, to rise to his peak combat power rapidly.”

“The question here is my saint level aptitude is from the Bloodcore’s compound transformation, and the growth rate isn’t ideal with Bursting Explosion battle qi.”

In the Snow Bird Harbor battlefield, the young man used his Suring Wave fishman form, its 100% god level bloodline amplified his divine spells immensely, and that left a deep impression on the young man.

But during his duel with Mi Fang, although his Flame Dragon King bloodline was legend level, its concentration was not high enough and its power amplification could not compare to the Surging Wave fishman bloodline’s terrifying amplification of divine spells.

To display a saint level disposition, the dragoman youngster surreptitiously used the Bloodcore to turn into other dragon species. These altered traits were internal, invisible to the outside, and concealed from the entire audience.

If it was just the Flame Dragon King bloodline, with the dragonman youngster’s current bloodline concentration, it would not be enough to bear so many enhancement spells.

Now the problem was the other transformations were interchangeable dragon bloodlines or ice type dragon bloodlines, while the bulk of his form was the Flame Dragon King bloodline, and the Bursting Explosion amplification rate was not high in other respects (such as ice resistance), and he had to make up for this deficiency.

“Therefore, I have to raise the Flame Dragon King bloodline vigorously.” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

With regards to this, after he returned, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ efforts doubled. Under the pretense of alchemy and medicine refinement, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps went on a crazy buying spree.

Regretfully, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s natural environment had few times of wyverns reproducing that had bloodlines close to the Flame Dragon King.

As far as this was concerned, the dragonman youngster was without a solution, and could only throw a wide net.

However, with the Bloodcore’s compound transformations, his aptitude was enough for the time being.

“Hm?”

The dragoman youngster’s eyes flashed a different color.

His face did not reveal anything as he serenely turned around and returned to his room.

After reaching his bedroom, he secretly crushed a Pearl Bubble and used a divine spell to replenish his disguise.

Since officially returning to the capital and repelling “Hua Tang”, all parties were investigating and divining him.

Before, the dragonman youngster was not strong enough, but after he returned, he mastered divine spells and magic, becoming a triple system gold level transcendent, thus he could observe deception divine spells being depleted.

At the hotel doorway, he felt the deception divine spell on him suddenly depleting rapidly.

As he returned to his room, that depletion hadn’t stopped.

“This is a Han Yu Clan hotel, and it has tight defenses.”

“It seems this is not an observation method, but a divination method. Some expert or power is directly divining about me.”

Since the dragonman youngster’s duel with Mi Fang, these kinds of divinations and investigations clearly increased and strengthened.

The dragonman youngster had no way of confirming who was doing these investigations and divinations.

There were too many people and powers that had motive to do so.

The Alchemy Guild, the Li Jian Clan, the Jing Xiang Clan, Tranquil Hill’s duelists…

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ entrance into the capital also created a whirlpool of tussles with the powers here.

The dragonman youngster sensed the deception divine spell being expended, yet his body and mind were very tranquil.

He now had many Pearl Bubbles on hand and wasn’t worried about this kind of expenditure. In fact, on the contrary, it was advantageous to him because any divination was oriented towards the façade the dragonman youngster deliberately set up.

The dragonman youngster’s thoughts were now on the Glacier bishop and his son who just departed.

“His young son is a Glacier Church priest, yet he is unexpectedly enthusiastic about duels.”

“I can sense that this young chap thinks highly of me. This is a gain from my duel with Mi Fang.”

“As expected, my meticulous choice of a heroic battle style has been favored by the Ice Sculpture citizens.”

“Although the bulk of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is composed of snow elves, the popular customs are quite plucky.”

From the young priest’s passion, the dragonman youngster could surmise and get a deeper feel of how duels permeated popular sentiment.

“If even a cleric who believes in the Glacier God is like this, to say nothing about others.”

“That priest is relatively young and perhaps has no appreciation of the gravity of things, but his father, as a bishop level figure, was there the entire time and saw how his son acted. If the ramifications were huge, he would have given the priest a stern lecture.”

From this observation, the dragonman youngster could make out what every large church’s attitude to duels was.

“If in the future the Dueling God appeared, what would happen?” The dragonman youngster was no outside passer-by, the divine blessings had given him abundant clerical experience.

If there really was a Dueling God, then every large church would have an entirely different attitude towards duels!

The gods had to fight over faith for their own benefits.

“Now the question is, does the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s high class now about the Dueling God, the duelists on Tranquil Hill, and other such secrets or not?”

The dragonman youngster’s eyes were indistinct.

He learned of the Dueling Godhead in Tranquil Hill because of Charming Blue’ divine oracle.

But the divine oracle did not tell him what other people or powers were conspiring for the Dueling Godhead.

Did they exist or not?

Did the Ice Sculpture high class know? If they didn’t, the situation would be relatively simple. The real fear was that the high class was well aware and, most importantly, involved in it.

The dragonman youngster consulted a lot of information and discovered that duels were always supported and popularized by the Ice Sculpture Government, they wholly treated it as a national policy that was energetically carried out.

In this aspect, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom never changed the slightest.

This inevitably caused the dragonman youngster to break out in misgivings.

“Even if the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s high class is unaware, those duelists in Tranquil Hill at minimum are a big hindrance.”

“If only I could get in contact with Lan Zao…”

Regretfully, when the Dueling Divine Domain spatial crack was closed up, the dragonman youngster found reaching Lan Zao was very difficult.

“There will eventually be a chance later.”

The dragonman youngster was following Lan Zao, the big guy, and Snow Bird Harbor closely as he waited for public sentiment to develop.

The main thing he was waiting for was the Li Jian and Jing Xiang Clans’ reaction.

Although he had decided to take all of the profits from the mount potions alone, he didn’t want to immediately brave forth in spite of the obstacles.

Direct force would cause immense resistance, and it was far better to feign cooperation and paralyze the other powers, perhaps provoking the outside powers into depleting each other and alleviating the resistance on him.

One day later, the dragonman youngster hadn’t gotten any envoys from the Li Jian or Jing Xiang Clan, instead he received an unexpected invitation.

“Oh? Long Meng sent a person over to invite me over for a meeting?”

Long Meng!

The first among the duelists and publicly known as the strongest gold level in the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

He naturally was the most popular candidate for being the champion of this year’s National Ceremony Grand Duel.

The dragonman youngster knew that Long Meng’s distinguished name was engraved on some tombstone located on the top of Tranquil Hill.

He also understood that Long Men was quite strong, and even if he was in his fishman form, he wasn’t sure if he’d win, it would take a genuine battle to ascertain that.

To say nothing of his current identity as Long Fu right now.

“Why is Long Meng inviting me over? What is his aim?”

Chapter 923: Let Me Teach You, Long Fu

The envoy with Long Meng’s invitation was a dragonman too.

The dragonman envoy led Long Fu into a carriage, then after going through a few streets, came to Long Meng’s residence.

After entering and going up two floors, the dragonman emissary arrived at a door, “Lord Long Meng is training inside.”

After saying that, the dragonman envoy lightly pushed it open a chink.

The training room door was never locked and always open.

The dragonman youngster stopped, and in the wake of the door being opened a crack, he sensed a vague spatial fluctuation.

The dragonman youngster was a gold level magician and possessed a lot of casting experience. Before entering the room, he knew this training room must be using some alchemy technology to add internal space to this room.

He pushed the door open and strode inside, and sure enough, he found the space inside was abnormally wide, it was no less than two to three hundred square meters big.

The walls, roof, and floor were white, and blended into one another.

There was no training equipment, only Long Meng inside the pure white space.

The dragonman youngster glanced over, seeing it was indeed Long Meng. He had seen him in many magic images and was quite familiar with what Long Meng looked like.

Matching the young man’s memories: Long Meng was covered in unblemished white dragon scales. He had two dragon eyes as blue as the sea, giving people a feeling of peace and depth.

At this moment, he was wearing a set of wide blue-gray training clothes, the upper cuffs and lower trouser legs were very spacious.

Long Meng only had two pieces of transcendent equipment on him.

There was a white headband on his forehead, the white headband had a dark blue gem inlaid in the middle of it.

The second was on the tip of his dragon tail, it was a golden ring.

The dragonman youngster knew a lot of information on Long Meng, among them was information on these two pieces of equipment.

The sapphire headband was called the Blue God Headband. It could be treated as an improved version of the Ice Heart Headband, providing not just formidable mental defenses, but also could be enchanted and allow the user to cast a mental barrier.

The gold ring on his tail was the Gravity Ring. After putting on this equipment, the dragon tail would become heavier. During training, it would increase results. When used for combat, it could increase the strength of attacks. As for Long Meng, it was not just worn for training, and according to the reports, barely anyone has seen him take off that gold ring.

Long Meng naturally sensed Long Fu, but he didn’t stop training.

His movements were slow and heavy, his attacks following a prescribed pattern to the letter. Because of his internal strength, each time he moved, the hems of his clothes would flutter, and his attacks would create light echoes.

He didn’t wear shoes, and the area where his gigantic soles stepped on seemed to be a uniform circle, and from beginning to end, he didn’t step a meter out of its radius.

Long Fu watched for a spell, then appeared impatient, “Hey, Long Meng, did you call me over to watch you perform?”

Long Meng stopped moving, and smiled at the dragonman youngster, “Did you watch clearly?”

The dragonman youngster coldly snorted, lifting his head, “Why did you ask me to come? State the matter you want to discuss.”

Long Meng shook his head, “You truly possess the fire dragon bloodline’s temperament.”

“Come.” Long Meng waved, “Let us do a small spar first.”

The dragonman youngster grinned, “That’s a good suggestion!”

He gladly stepped forth, ready to fight Long Meng unrestrained.

The dragonman youngster wanted the Dueling Godhead and to be first in this year’s National Ceremony Grand Duel. The powerful Long Meng was his greatest enemy.

The dragonman youngster was worried he didn’t have enough information on hand and had been unable to probe intel on Long Meng.

Even if Long Meng hadn’t said so, he had accepted Long Meng’s invitation with the strong intention to spar with Long Meng.

Long Meng’s proposal was what the dragonman youngster wanted to hear.

As for whether Long Meng would harm him, the dragonman youngster wasn’t worried.

He was a triple system gold level, and when he came over, he decided to bring the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale]. The strongest combat personnel in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was a saint level, while Long Meng was a gold level, so the dragonman youngster wasn’t worried about being in danger.

Long Fu and Long Meng began to spar unarmed, swapping between offense and defense frequently.

Long Meng’s martial skill was consummate, and the dragonman youngster quickly began to lose.

Battle skill – Surge.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Battle skill – Dragon Talon Strike.

The dragonman youngster used three battle skills, increasing his speed sharply, striking like an artillery shell with his powerful exploding fist. After sending Long Meng flying, he used Dragon Talon Strike to attack from range, tying down Long Meng as the latter warded it off.

The dragonman youngster immediately gained the upper hand with the help of battle skills.

Weirdly, Long Meng didn’t use any battle skills and was just relying on battle qi enhancing his basic unarmed skills to fight.

Although the dragonman youngster held the upper hand, he was unable to shift that dominance into a victory. On the contrary, the more he attacked, the more apprehensive he got, “This Long Meng might be a [Great Martial Arts Master]! His unarmed techniques are very strong, and he was actually able to withstand my consecutive battle skills attacks without losing his footing in the slightest.”

Long Meng hadn’t used battle skills at all, yet the dragonman youngster felt how intractable he was.

After a few more rounds, the dragonman youngster stopped, “Hey, what’s the meaning of this?”

He directly asked Long Meng, “You aren’t using battle skills, are you looking down on me?”

Long Meng shook his head, both he and his aura was serene, “Just the opposite.”

“Long Fu, do you dare to just use basic martial arts to fight me?”

“As long as you last 30 rounds, I’ll consider it your win.”

“If you win, I will give you a battle skill. Believe me, this battle skill suits you well.”

The dragonman youngster narrowed his eyes, sizing up Long Meng, then bellowed, “Your punching and kicking are far better than mine, but I can absolutely last 30 rounds! Hehe, I’ll take any battle skills you got!”

After saying that, he roared and stepped forward to attack Long Meng.

After this, the dragonman youngster didn’t use a single battle skill, only using his internal battle qi to accelerate his movement and increase the power of his punches and kicks.

He lasted 29 rounds, but in the 30th round, he was knocked to the ground by Long Meng.

Long Meng breathed flatly, and smiled, “Well that’s unfortunate, try again?”

The dragonman youngster coldly snorted, “I’ll try again!”

The second time, at the 30th round, Long Meng used a floor sweeping kick to trip the dragonman youngster to the ground.

He smiled, “Again?”

The dragonman youngster resolutely hammered the floor, resentfully replying, “Again!”

The third time, at the 30th round, the dragonman youngster was shoulder thrown to the ground by Long Meng.

But just as he was about to suffer defeat, he lashed out his dragon tail, the momentum spinning him around into an ugly posture, in a supreme effort to turn his body around, narrowly freeing himself from Long Meng’s grasp.

The dragonman youngster landed on the ground with both feet with a thud, taking a few strides backwards to stabilize his footing.

“You’ve won, congratulations.” Long Meng grinned at him.

But the dragonman youngster snarled, spitting out caustic resentment, “You dare toy with me?”

“I could sense that you deliberately slackened your strength.”

“If you didn’t throw the round, I would have lost again!”

“This doesn’t count, again!!”

The dragonman youngster growled, he was just about to charge when Long Meng waved his hand, “Stop for a bit.”

Long Meng gently chuckled, he sized up the dragonman youngster, not concealing his admiration, “You really are a genius!”

The dragonman youngster was stunned.

Long Meng continued, “I saw the recording of your duel with Mi Fang. You are a newly promoted gold level and the few gold level battle skills you used were horrendous to watch.”

“However, in a short time span during the fight, you swiftly learned how to use these battle skills.”

“You not only have natural gifts in practicing battle skills, but you also have a fundamental aptitude for martial arts ordinary people find hard to strive for.”

“You have already mastered many essentials of basic fighting and had immense progress. Think back carefully, isn’t that right?”

The dragonman youngster thought, “Of course that’s true! Otherwise, why would I be so willing to fight you?”

He realized the benefits of doing this already.

Although the dragonman youngster had obtained many fighter experiences from Charming Blue Goddess, these fighters were fishmen, seafloor lifeforms, there was barely anything for the dragonman form.

These experiences were also focused on how to use battle skills and didn’t touch on combat techniques.

But like Zong Ge once said, the dragonman youngster indeed possessed extraordinary fighting talent. In the past, the dragonman youngster effortlessly learned how to use Penetration Force, and after fighting Long Meng, he quickly improved his basic martial arts techniques easily.

Long Meng was unaware of the truth, but this didn’t hinder his growing admiration of Long Fu.

Long Meng watched as the dragonman youngster sank into contemplation, as he continued, “Unarmed techniques are the basis of combat.”

“In many battles, it isn’t necessary to use battle skills, you can just use unarmed techniques.”

“Believe me, if I was the one fighting Mi Fang, I could break through Mi Fang’s shields with just unarmed techniques.”

“Although battle skills are powerful, each time they are used, they need to be charged by gathering battle qi. Your [Battle Qi Ball] takes a very long time to charge up. This is actually an enormous weakness.”

“When true experts fight, it is unlikely you’ll have such time to spare.”

“Usually when you have such a long time to charge, it proves you completely hold the upper hand. In your fight with Mi Fang, it was mainly because Mi Fang made an immense mistake in his tactics.”

Long Meng had hit the nail on the head when he analyzed the duel.

The dragonman youngster was silent for a bit, then gave Long Meng a weird look, “I admit your words are reasonable, but exactly what are you trying to do by saying all of this?”

Long Meng smiled, “Our dragonman bodies are vastly superior when compared to the other intelligent races.”

“And basic unarmed fighting can magnify this superiority.”

“You are very gifted, Long Fu.”

“Your bloodline is at least saint level, and you possess a natural talent for battle.”

“You are a genius, a true genius!”

“However, you lacked a suitable teacher and training. As a dragonman, I can’t bear to see such a situation persist.”

“Let me teach you, Long Fu.”

The dragonman youngster was flabbergasted when the truth was revealed.

He stared at Long Meng; he never expected that this was why the latter had invited him here.

The dragonman youngster still couldn’t believe it, “Long Meng, what are you really up to?”

“You want to teach me and improve my strength?”

“What are you trying to do?”

“Lest you forget, I am participating in this year’s National Ceremony Grand Duel too. We are rivals, are you giving me benefits beforehand, so I’ll withdraw from the grand duel?”

“There is no need to be so wordy, that’s impossible!”

Long Meng raised his head, then burst into laughter, “What am I trying to do?”

“Get you to withdraw? I’d never consider that.”

“In fact, I look forward to your improved strength, as my opponent.”

“As it is now, the National Ceremony Grand Duel looks very boring. If you grow fast enough and become my match, then even if you defeat me, I will feel excited and won’t feel any regret!”

Long Meng pointed a finger at the dragonman youngster, his eyes shining, and said with a faint arrogance, “Can you understand me?”

“You should be able to understand.”

The dragonman youngster’s mouth fell open, showing his sharp teeth, his orange dragon eyes seemed to ignite with flames, “I do!”

“Your arrogance makes me really want to beat you up with just basic techniques.”

“You got guts, you shall have everything I can teach you! In this National Ceremony Grand Duel, I want to defeat you before an audience of millions and let you taste the bitter fruit of your arrogance.”

“I believe that you won’t forget it for the rest of your life!”

Chapter 924: The Big Guy’s Real Combat Training

The moon and stars dotted the night.

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di walked out of the Ice Sculpture Capital, came to a snow mound in the outskirts, and once again entered the divine domain spatial interior.

This was their true encampment.

During the day, they would temporarily rest in the Han Yu Clan hotel but did their best not to stay in it overnight.

At night, they would hurry here.

The Charming Blue Divine Domain was the place they felt most relaxed and was the best place to withstand outside threats.

Under the current circumstances, the main threat was not Hua Tang or the assassin Yi Jiu, the possibility of them making a fuss was low. The dragonman youngster was relatively afraid of the Li Jian Clan’s surveillance methods.

Based on what he and Zi Di surmised, this surveillance method should be rooted in the Li Jian Clan’s spatial bloodline superiority.

Therefore, the divine domain was necessary.

So long as there was the slightest fluctuation in the divine domain, the dragonman youngster could instantly sense it.

After returning to the divine domain to rest, the dragonman youngster once again scrutinized the Ultra-energy Dragon Pearl.

This [Ultra-energy Dragon Pearl] was the size of a fist, completely white, and felt ice-cold, it was a type of alchemy item.

When he bid farewell to Long Meng, the latter had gifted it to him.

Long Meng told him, “Sire Long Fu, your acceptance of my teachings went beyond my expectations, it was wonderful.”

“I will teach you wholeheartedly.”

This battle skill [Dragon Pearl] is the most classical battle skill for us dragonmen, it suits you well so I want to give you this as a present.”

“It is even more perfect when paired up with this, the [Ultra-energy Dragon Pearl].”

As he mentioned it, he gave him a magic image record of the Dragon Pearl battle skill as well as the [Ultra-energy Dragon Pearl].

With regards to [Dragon Pearl]. The dragonman youngster knew about it – War Merchant had it in his database.

This battle skill was used by condensing battle qi into pearls that circled through the air around the user. When it came time to do battle, the fighter could spiritually control them and smash them into an enemy.

Long Meng’s words were correct, [Dragon Pearl] was a very classic battle skill. From its foundation, many subcategories and branches were derived from it.

Some people liked to condense one giant dragon pearl, using it for offense and defense. Others were more proficient in multiple condensed dragon pearls that would hover around them like flying swallows, expending them as long ranged attacks, or flying back to the fighter to replenish their battle qi.

“Long Meng recommended the [Dragon Pearl] battle skill to me; he must have seen my usage of the battle skill [Battle Qi Ball] during the duel.”

“To a dragonman, [Battle Qi Ball] cannot compare to [Dragon Pearl].”

“The Dragon Pearl battle skill was specifically developed for dragonmen, becoming Battle Qi Ball when enlarged and becoming other battle skills when shrunk.”

Because gold level battle qi could leave a fighter’s body, the battle skill [Dragon Pearl] was a gold level battle skill.

In fact, when the dragonman youngster was picking a long ranged attack method, he considered this skill. But time was too short and he decided to pick the battle skill Dragon Talon Strike to see its effects.

Because there were so many derivations of the [Dragon Pearl] battle skill, practicing it was not as simple as [Dragon Talon Strike], so the dragonman youngster decided to pick it up later.

As for [Battle Qi Ball], it on the contrary was a priority and one of the main things he trained on.

“Speaking in terms of myself, [Battle Qi Ball] is more suitable for me than [Dragon Pearl].”

The reason was simple, the dragonman form was just one life shape, he had many bloodlines in the Bloodcore and had many life shapes.

Therefore, to him, [Battle Qi Ball] was a more usable and suitable battle skill. If he energetically practiced [Dragon Pearl], then it would only strengthen the dragonman form. But mastering [Battle Qi Ball] would benefit both the dragonman form and the fishman form.

“However, if the alchemy item [Ultra-energy Dragon Pearl] is added in, the battle skill will be immensely improved and make it much more tempting.”

The [Ultra-energy Dragon Pearl] could store dragonman battle qi in it, and after a dragonman used the battle skill [Dragon Pearl]. Battle qi would instantly bubble out of it and expand into a large pearl, fully forming a dragon pearl.

There were other benefits.

For example, the item [Ultra-energy Dragon Pearl] could act as a core for a dragonman to command many more battle qi dragon pearls, and regardless of their smarts, their control range would substantially increase.

“This alchemy technology improves the quality of [Dragon Pearl]!” The dragonman youngster lamented.

This pair up was not in War Merchant’s database. Nor was it in War Merchant’s records.

Based on what Long Meng said, this technology only started getting popular in the last few years.

This kind of pair up caused the dragonman youngster to make a mental connection to Lan Zao’s Air Cannon and alchemy projectile cores.

“It seems it has formed a kind of fad…some alchemy items being developed for battle skills that can increase their power.”

“If battle skills are like that, then it doesn’t make sense if magic and divine spells can’t be amplified similarly.”

The dragonman youngster had an unorganized train of thought, and he pondered over Long Meng again.

“Why is Long Meng doing this?” This was the question he had been pondering on the way here.

“Is Long Meng coveting my reputation? After all, giving me advice can indeed raise his own renown.” A new possibility came to the dragonman youngster at this moment.

But he quickly rejected this notion.

Although the dragonman youngster was currently the hottest celebrity around, his reputation could not compare to Long Meng.

If news got out that Long Meng was teaching him, then the dragonman youngster would be the one that benefited the most from his connection with Long Meng.

The dragonman youngster’s mind suddenly stirred, sensing something.

“An opportunity.” He immediately summoned Cong Mang.

After the Vegetable Pirates boss saw the dragonman youngster, he instantly kneeled and expressed his readiness to serve.

After coming to the Ice Sculpture Capital, he was less inclined to be presumptuous.

If he was exposed, the Ice Sculpture Capital guards would definitely come for him, and if he wasn’t beheaded, he’d be locked up.

“Follow me, our pirate group needs assistance.” The dragonman youngster glinted with blue light, and after a few breaths, he turned into a fishman.

He grabbed Cong Mang’s shoulder and walked ahead.

Space violently undulated, then calmed down in an instant.

Cong Mang and the fishman youngster had traveled through space, and within a second, arrived on Cong Mang’s flagship.

The night wind whistled, and the moonlight was clear.

A naval battle was already underway here.

Cong Mang’s pirates were running away in a sorry manner, with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s First Navy Fleet at their heels.

Of course this was not the main force, just a small detachment.

The head of this detachment was Bei Jia, a gold level snow elf fighter.

Since the Li Jian Clan’s Second Fleet had been routed by Da Han’s pirate alliance, the First Navy Fleet had strained itself to clean out the calamitous flood of pirates across Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s ocean defenses.

As Bei Ji a was commanding his fleet, he had the “good fortune” of noticing the Vegetable Pirates.

At least, he believed it was his good fortune.

According to the intelligence reports, The Vegetable Pirates had suffered disastrous losses when they were plundering Snow Bird Harbor, and Cong Mang was heavily wounded and carried away by a mysterious fishman expert. Therefore, Bei Jia immediately reported this positive battle report.

Mei Lin approved of his military operation, and so Bei Jia led his fleet day and night at double speed to relentlessly chase down the Vegetable Pirates, vowing that he would scoop up the whole lot of them.

After the Vegetable Pirates noticed they were being pursued, they instantly chose to accelerate their departure.

But Bei Jia’s naval fleet used methods, such as condensing seawater or tampering with the ocean currents to slow down the pirate ships.

The Vegetable Pirates had suffered immense losses to their crew, but not much to their ships, and after appropriation of personnel, there weren’t many people on each ship.

After sinking into this crisis, Hong Zhu immediately started praying.

The big guy was a fanatical believer in the dragonman youngster, and after the young man’s large scale preaching, Hong Zhu had become a pious believer in the God Mist.

Added on to the fact that the dragonman youngster always paid attention to them, not long after Hong Zhu started praying, he realized what kind of situation they were in.

He gave the order, making the pirates sail at top speed, disregarding the energy consumption. If necessary for time, they could abandon some ordinary pirate ships to stall for time.

As Bei Jia approached the Vegetable Pirates at dusk, he had used all kinds of methods to slow down the pirates, while the pirates tried various methods to stall for time.

The two sides’ fierce battle of wits lasted until 2 am.

“These wretched snow elves, they actually don’t want to let us go!” Cong Mang was familiar with the latest situation and gnashed his teeth in rage.

He impatiently gazed at the fishman youngster next to him, his desire to be sent into battle evident in his words.

The fishman youngster smiled, “It is time to counterattack.”

“You can charge.”

When Cong Mang heard he could act, he ecstatically raised his arm and shouted, “Little ones, follow my charge!”

Not long after retreating from Snow Bird Harbor, the Vegetable Pirates and Cong Mang were overtaken by the fishman youngster and easily taken over by the latter.
