Therefore, the pirates were mentally prepared for Cong Mang and the fishman youngster.

The pirates admired the strong, so when the Vegetable Pirates’ old captain defected, the pirates followed without objection.

“What?” Seeing Cong Mang appear, Be Jia was flabbergasted, “Cong Mang is here? He was hiding in the pirate group?”

Cong Mang showing himself greatly increased pirate morale.

He and the Hoodlum battle squad began to do boarding actions on navy ships.

The fishman youngster didn’t show himself, instead he was in the ship hold, putting his hand on the big guy.

“Father!” The big guy knelt to the ground, shouting that word in his thoughts, his expression was filled with joy.

Although he was living on a pirate ship, his days were quite comfortable.

Da Dou became his personal chef, giving him meals that filled his mouth with grease, his happiness swelling like his belly.

As for fresh ingredients, the dragonman youngster delivered them constantly through Divine Domain Descent.

The fishman youngster examined him, confirming that with the big guy’s extraordinary bloodline and lightning fast progress, he was closing in on the silver level.

“Relax a little, I have come to give you something.” As the fishman said that he began to use divine spells.

After three divine spells, in addition to a massive expenditure of divine favor, the big guy’s aura underwent a qualitative change, going from iron level to the silver level!

Upon seeing this, Hong Zhu was very excited and envious, Da Dou too also revealed a look of amazement.

As a [divine emissary], the fishman youngster naturally could promote a believer’s transcendent level. There were two principal restrictions to this, one was the receiver had to possess room to improve, and the second was faith in Charming Blue Goddess or the fishman youngster.

“Get out there, get out and cause havoc on the navy ships.” The fishman youngster lightly clapped the big guy’s shoulder, encouraging him.

“Father, Xiao Guai, afraid!” The big guy shouted in his heart, then lay on the floor, hugging the fishman youngster’s thigh and shivering.

Hong Zhu: …

Da Dou: …

The fishman youngster gave an exasperated sigh.

He had dedicated himself to cultivating the big guy, and this was a good chance to get live combat training, but the big guy was naturally timid and that hadn’t changed.

“It doesn’t matter.”

“Let me help you.”

“Come, look into my eyes.”

An intense blue light flowed from the fishman youngster’s eyes and into the big guy’s eyes, rapidly permeating through his entire body.

A moment later, the big guy stood up, and boldly slapped his chest, like he was a gorilla!

Chapter 925: Training a Ravenous Wolf

In the night sea, the Vegetable Pirates and the First Navy Fleet detachment were fighting fiercely.

Cong Mang took the lead, holding a gold level long rod in a truculent manner.

Bei Jia stepped forward bravely, draped in heavy armor to intercept Cong Mang.

The two gold levels were playing tough, not retreating from this desperate struggle.

At that moment, the big guy, eyes emitting blue light, roared as he jumped onto the deck.

He was already under the fishman youngster’s Divine Descent. His consciousness curled up as he calmly watched his father help him.

“Xiao Guai, experience this carefully, I am here to teach you how to fight.” The fishman youngster secretly transmitted to the big guy.

The big guy was very scared and weakly agreed.

A moment later, the fishman youngster took control of the big guy and transferred iron level battle qi to his legs and feet.

With this battle qi boost, the big guy’s jumping ability rose dramatically!

The big guy jumped a few hundred meters and landed precisely on a kingdom warship.

The kingdom’s navy was in the middle of a firefight, when suddenly an alarm rang loudly. They raised their eyes to see a shadow covering the full moon in the sky.

The huge shadow suddenly descended in a massive gust of wind.

Ding!

The big guy was like a boulder toss from a catapult that smashed into the warship’s afterdeck.

The afterdeck immediately caved in, causing the big guy’s lower half to get stuck, in addition, because of the big guy’s weight, the entire ship slightly tilted.

The navies had gone through fine drills, and after a momentary confusion, they gathered together to encircle the big guy.

The big guy roared repeatedly, brandishing both arms and stepping out. From the aft to the front, he left a complete mess wherever he went.

The navies that dared to openly obstruct the big guy had their bones smashed and muscles torn as they were tossed aside.

The deck was constantly being ruptured, and smashed blocks were being flung everywhere.

None could stop the big guy’s charge, the warship’s mast was snapped by him, and the ship house was penetrated when he collided with it.

The big guy curled up consciousness shivered as he witnessed the dreadful scene before him. His corporeal body was under the fishman youngster’s control and became a deadly weapon on the battlefield.

In less than a minute, he had killed his way from the aft to the bow, tore the first deck in half, and left behind a huge gorge in the ship.

The navies suffered disastrous casualties and corpses littered the deck.

Clang.

The catastrophic destruction caused the hull to violently shake, and it finally toppled over completely.

“Amazing!”

“He obviously just got promoted to the silver level, yet he is stronger than most gold levels.”

“When he stabilizes his silver level, his constitution will be substantially increased as a result, at the time, it will surpass my dragonman form.”

Although the big guy’s body was weaker than the dragonman youngster’s body right now, the fishman youngster could already sense the future clearly.

The warship the big guy devastated lost all fighting strength.

The fishman youngster was not addicted to killing, and immediately had the big guy jump onto another navy warship.

Under his control, the big guy wreaked havoc all over the place, throwing the navy into complete confusion and utter suffering.

Cong Mang and Bei Jia were at a stalemate.

The Vegetable Pirates were outnumbered by the navy and were at a slight disadvantage.

But the big guy’s lively performance allowed the pirates to quickly suppress the navy.

“Where did that small giant come from?” The navies had incessant headaches but couldn’t discern anything about the big guy.

The big guy, Hong Zhu, and Da Dou were imbued with deception divine spells, and when observed by outsiders, the big guy was a strange small giant.

“We have to get rid of that troublesome guy!” The navies quickly came to a consensus.

Taking advantage of the big guy being on a damaged warship, they used the cannons on the surrounding warships to rapidly open fire on the big guy.

Alchemy cannons – Snow Ice Light Cannons!

A large amount of honey snow filled these cannons, then an alchemy formation rapidly compressed the honey snow into a pure white beam of light that was discharged.

Perfectly straight and thick white beams of light accurately shot towards the big guy.

Under the fishman youngster’s control, the big guy moved extremely sharply, dodging left and right while simultaneously moving his hands and feet about.

When these light beams hit the deck, they instantly turned into icicles.

The few that hit the big guy caused his body to shake.

The fishman youngster had no choice but to deplete a large amount of iron battle qi to withstand the light cannons.

Iron level battle qi was severely depleted, but that was precisely what the fishman youngster wanted. Very quickly, he extracted newly advanced battle qi from every place in the big guy’s body.

Because the big guy had been promoted to the silver level, his new battle qi was not iron level, but rather the qualitatively different silver level.

After the big guy gradually possessed silver level battle qi, his combat strength rose again by a lot.

This time, the navies had even fewer ways of dealing with him.

As the big guy’s destruction grew more serious, Bei Jia anxiously fumed.

He knew that if he disregarded the big guy, this naval battle would be lost. He willingly endured several thumps from Cong Mang, coughing out blood as he attacked towards the big guy.

“Father, bad guy, very scary!” The big guy’s soul shouted in a flurry.

The fishman youngster’s eyes nevertheless lit up, “Xiao Guai, this is the most valuable part of this real combat. Don’t be afraid, in the future you will be even scarier than him.”

He maneuvered the big guy into a counterattack and didn’t retreat, instead deciding to attack Bei Jia.

Bei Jia wore heavy armor and was a gold level, he was not someone the newly promoted silver level big guy could smash away.

Bei Jia attacked, immediately suppressing the big guy and alleviating the battlefield situation.

Cong Mang wanted to rush over to interfere, but the fishman youngster immediately transmitted that he was to withdraw.

The fishman youngster didn’t empower the big guy either, instead he only used the big guy’s fighting skills to contend with Bei Jia.

After fighting for a few rounds, Bei Jia beat and badly battered the big guy.

The big guy’s constitution was very strong; he could quickly recover and was tough.

Under the expert Bei Jia’s suppression, the fishman youngster controlled the big guy more and more skillfully, the constant experience and practice from this solidifying the big guy’s combat martial arts.

The big guy’s outstanding constitution, paired up with the dragonman youngster’s extraordinarily exceptional fighting and learning talent allowed the big guy’s performance on the battlefield to improve at a rate visible to the naked eye.

Bei Jia was also experiencing how horrifying his enemy’s progression rate was and was secretly apprehensive.

He took a deep breath and eliminated all thoughts that this was a fluke. Now attaching actual importance to the big guy, he didn’t hesitate to spend battle qi and use powerful battle skills.

The fishman youngster chose to scurry away from these sharp attacks.

If he couldn’t dodge, then he’d use a defensive battle skill.

Bei Jia displayed his greatest firepower, and the big guy repeatedly retreated in defeat coughing out a lot of blood, with several of his ribs being broken too.

Seeing the crucial moment about to arrive, the fishman youngster called out to Cong Mang, the latter would stop Bei Jia as he healed the big guy with a divine spell.

After a few seconds under the divine light, the big guy fully recovered and his combat strength rose back to what it was at the beginning of the battle.

“They actually have a cleric?!” When Bei Jia saw this, his eyeballs nearly swelled from their sockets. Then after his shock, he felt a strong chill spreading from the bottom of his heart.

Such a powerful healing divine spell meant the pirate ships had at least one silver level cleric.

If it was a one on one, Bei Jia wouldn’t be afraid of a clergyman. But this was reality, and the gold level Cong Mang was reaping devastation while the strange small silver level giant was very tough and was improving extremely rapidly.

“This fight cannot continue, withdraw!” Bei Jia decisively ordered the retreat.

But how could he retreat?

The fishman youngster personally acted.

A second later, the sea billowed up and rolled a myriad of ocean spray.

The seawater formed a whirlpool that sucked in the warships, making progress difficult.

Divine spells by nature were powerful, and with the terrifying amplification of a god level bloodline, he instantly altered the battlefield environment. An ordinary divine spell in the fishman youngster’s hands became an awe-inspiring battlefield level power!

The navy’s retreat failed, and their battle intent slumped. Bei Jia decided to stay behind, wanting to cover the navy’s retreat.

The thought was laudable.

It really was touching.

After a half hour, the curtain closed on this battle.

The Vegetable Pirates seized total victory, encircling and subduing the vast majority of the warships, with only two escaping, as the fishman youngster had ordered they be allowed to leave.

Bei Jia had been captured alive and was carried over to the fishman youngster by the big guy.

Seeing the gold level fishman youngster and Da Dou, Bei Jia looked startled, then turned dark.

The fishman youngster tried interrogation, but Bei Jia was a model soldier, tough and fearless of death.

The fishman youngster didn’t want to humiliate him, Bei Jia’s decision to stay behind was something he admired.

“Put him into custody, don’t dock his food and water, and be properly hospitable to him. He is a very good sparring partner.” Ordered the fishman youngster.

Bei Jia wore heavy armor and specialized in defense. He had a gold level sea horse mount and was a heavy cavalier.

The fishman youngster planned on keeping him as the big guy’s training partner.

He undid Divine Descent, and the big guy once again took control of his own body.

“Hurts, hurts, hurts!” The big guy cried out, the wounds all over his body conveyed a strong sense of presence.

“These wounds were intentionally left behind by your father.” The fishman youngster smiled, placating his son.

“You need to feel this pain, adapt to this pain, and allow your body to remember it all so you can grow faster.”

The big guy was weeping in pain, “But Father, I, I can’t stand it.”

“Make an extra effort!” The fishman youngster bit his teeth, hardened his heart, and lightly slapped the big guy’s shoulder, sincerely and earnestly educating, “These are small injuries, far from what can kill you in battle.”

The big guy thought to himself, “I don’t want to fight like this one bit.”

But seeing his father’s hopeful gaze, he closed his mouth and stopped crying out in pain.

The fishman youngster sighed to himself.

The future could not be guessed, and compared to the huge powers, the survivors were still too weak. Every member had to grow, and substantially!

The big guy was trained like this because the big guy’s bloodline was very high, and it had a lot of space to unearth.

“This time I strive for the Dueling Godhead, and danger lurks on every side. If in the future something happens to me…I hope Xiao Guai will be able to survive better.”

To the side, Da Dou had witnessed everything.

He kept his eye on the big guy and endorsed the fishman youngster’s way of handling him.

This was the world of the strong.

The giant’s world was the same.

Everyone wanted to become strong! Da Dou had accepted this idea from childhood.

Da Dou didn’t think the big guy was miserable, on the contrary he envied the big guy’s treatment. He knew what the big guy’s circumstances were, and the fishman youngster’s training method was simply tailor-made for him, it really fit him!

Before leaving, the fishman youngster put into place some arrangements.

“Transmit this letter to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, say our Vegetable Pirates took Bei Jia captive.

“Let them raise a ransom.”

“Are the Brambles Pirates nearby?” He pointed a finger at an ocean map and asked.

“Yes.” The Vegetable Pirates’ newly promoted first mate promptly replied, “Their pirate commander was killed by Feng Lian, and the just elevated gold level is very prudent. Not long ago, they invited us to combine our forces and plunder White Mist Harbor.”

White Mist Harbor was a small sized harbor.

The fishman youngster smiled and clenched his hands together, “Converge with them, and seize that opportunity to annex them.”

“We are a little short on people, right?”

This was the statement of a pirate!

Those words caused everyone’s eyes to light up.

Everyone looked at the fishman youngster, all thinking: was the fishman expert before them originally a veteran ocean bandit?

Cong Mang licked his lips even more, he stood up with excitement and asked him to be assigned to this, loudly shouting he wanted to go with the ships.

The fishman youngster calmly listened for a while, then suddenly slapped Cong Mang into the air.

Plop.

Cong Mang coughed up blood and spat out a tooth. After being thrown to the floor, he lay on the floor for a while

Or perhaps one could say, he didn’t dare to crawl back up.

The fishman youngster’s eyes glinted callously as he leisurely asked, “Do you listen to me, or do I listen to you?”

Cong Mang promptly kneeled, his forehead on the floor, “Of course I’ll listen to you, boss!”

His agitated heart had been quelled instantly by the fishman youngster’s heartless slap.

The young man’s power made Cong Mang even more obedient.

The pirates were the ravenous wolves of the ocean, and one could not be lenient on recently tamed wolves.

Baring your teeth?

Even if it was baring another’s teeth, that kind of attitude would offend the owner.

I didn’t let you bare your teeth!

You will listen to me.

After returning from the Ocean Mother Nest, the fishman youngster had an increasingly subtle grasp of human nature. While training the big guy, he was simultaneously training the ravenous wolf Cong Mang.

The pirates kept quiet out of fear, thoroughly recognizing who really was in charge.

As for Cong Mang?

It had to be said that this was the best way to deal with him!

Through Divine Domain Descent, the fishman youngster took Cong Mang from the ocean and returned to the Ice Sculpture Capital.

Things weren’t finished just yet.

He secretly notified Tripleblade in Snow Bird Harbor that he was to contact Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and asked him to help in a small favor – to spread a piece of news.

The news was that Long Meng really admired Long Fu. He personally taught the latter and also gifted them a battle skill and an alchemy item.

Chapter 926: Two Clans Join Hands

In a vast snowfield, there stood only one city.

The city had been meticulously laid out, was made mainly from light and dark blue ice bricks, had round roofs, round arched doors, and other similar architecture that filled the city with elven grace and artistic flair.

These gentle and beautiful buildings alleviated the ice-cold and harsh environment, the vitality of the vegetation and people adorning the white-blue city.

This was Igloo City, the Li Jian Clan’s main city.

As a great noble clan of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the Li Jian Clan perfectly controlled this city, like how they controlled the Kingdom’s Second Navy Fleet.

Mian Li Zang was the Li Jian Clan’s current elder, and he was currently in his study, handling onerous affairs.

The window to the outside showed a tranquil scene, sunlight sprinkled on the city and there was no wind or now.

However, the Li Jian Clansmen knew that an invisible blizzard had engulfed the Li Jian Clan already.

This powerful clan seemed as frail as a tiny boat in this blizzard.

And Mian Li Zang was the only one steering this boat, the one facing this immense challenge!

During the battle with Da Han, the Second Navy Fleet retreated in defeat, and although the main force was intact, the Li Jian Clan was in a terrible situation, almost the entire kingdom hated them, pushed them aside, and even were hostile towards them.

While this was happening, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ conflict with the Li Jian Clan was made public, adding insult to injury.

“Lord Clan Leader, this is the information you were asking for.” An attendant opened the study door and came in with a magic crystal ball.

Mian Li Zang nodded, indicating that the attendant put the crystal ball on the desk.

The attendant respectfully withdrew from the study, and after the door was closed, Mian Li Zang stroked the crystal ball.

A moment later, a magic image appeared in the crystal ball.

A large group of people were surrounding a store.

The store sold only three magic potions, Dragon Rider, Spirit Stimulation, and Smooth Stride.

“Line up, please everyone line up, one customer at a time!” A salesclerk did their best to maintain order but didn’t have much impact.

The customers swarmed like bees into the store, snapping up the magic medicines.

“I bought it, I bought it!!”

“Don’t take mine, are you courting death?!”

“I want to buy 60 sets, no matter what kind, just take my money.”

“Here is the money, I want to buy 120 sets!”

The customers frantically clawed at the mount potion stock. The reason was because of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ new marketing strategy: so long as someone bought 60 or more sets of magic medicine, they could receive an ice sculpture of Long Fu.

The customers who managed to buy them clutched or held high their ice sculpture of Long Fu in great delight, squeezing out of the store with uplifted spirits.

The magic image was magnified, giving a close up of an ice sculpture of Long Fu.

Mian Li Zang looked at it closely, and even he had to concede that the ice sculptures had extremely remarkable workmanship. The ice sculptures were not just made of carefully selected materials, but it was also sturdy and engraved superbly, vividly depicting the dragonman youngster dragging his battle qi ball as he dove from the sky.

The magic image continued to play.

Blood Kin City, Snow Bird Harbor, Munitions Harbor, Snow Rabbit City, Rime City, Ice Sculpture Capital…

The stores in every town and city were virtually the same, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ mount potion sales were booming!

“After Long Fu defeated Mi Fang, his renown increased sharply.”

“Based on the latest information, Long Fu even got Long Meng’s eye, the latter really admires Long Fu and personally advised and trained him. This news further fostered Long Fu’s renown, like pouring oil onto a fire!”

“It is very clear this information was deliberately disseminated. It would have taken a lot of energy to spread this news in so short a time.”

“Was this done by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps themselves, or were they working with another power?”

Mian Li Zang deeply sighed.

When it came to dealing with their relationship with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Mian Li Zang had personally shown interest in it.

Previously, he decided to propose and demand that the Li Jian Clan be the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ sole partner when he saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps quickly establishing themselves, and the impact and value of the Li Jian Clan was rapidly being reduced. He prepared for the rainy day and decided to respond in advance.

Regretfully, the dragonman youngster secretly returned, used Hun Tong to act as Hua Tang, and ruthlessly smacked the Li Jian Clan back in a smooth comeback.

After this series of operations, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ conflict with the Li Jian Clan was made known to the masses.

If it was during normal times, then Mian Li Zang could calmly act. But after the Second Navy Fleet was defeated, it was now a hundred times more difficult for Mian Li Zang to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Mian Li Zang finished looking through the images in the crystal ball, then bowed his head in contemplation, his brows locking up as he pondered how he should cope with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ tutting offensive.

Some clansmen proposed that they simply stop mount potion production at all of their clan’s workshops and factories.

Since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to obviously separate themselves, the Li Jian Clan should stop producing magic medicine, despite every part of the country having a massive demand for it.

Mian Li Zang didn’t think much before rejecting that suggestion, on the contrary he ordered to increase production, including all night overtime and increasing staff, striving to achieve production 24 hours a day.

As a remarkable politician and manager, Mian Li Zang knew that “cooperation benefited both, and division harmed both”. Currently, both sides were still cooperating, and if it continued, everyone would benefit.

If magic medicine production was stopped, the Li Jian Clan’s benefits would suffer. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be more pressed to extricate themselves from the Li Jian Clan and accelerate their ties with the Jing Xiang Clan and other powers.

Therefore, stopping production seemed like a punishment, and would put a spotlight on the fact that the Li Jian Clan was essentially venting their negative feelings.

If that was done, then this entire incident would be even less conducive to the Li Jian Clan’s growth.

As an experienced clan leader, Mian Li Zang would never do that.

“To deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, I can adopt other measures.” Mian Li Zang’s brows smoothed out a little; he had thought of some already.

He transmitted an order to a sentry.

After a moment, he came to another room.

Inside the room was a gigantic alchemy module, it nearly took up all of the space in it.

It was an emergency communication device.

The Li Jian Clan was one of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s great nobles and had plenty of political resources that could obtain this device, said device could contact almost all of the high class powers.

This time, Mian Li Zang was not contacting just anyone but the Jing Xiang Clan itself.

The alchemy device turned on, and not long after, the Jing Xiang Clan Leader’s magic image condensed and appeared.

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader looked gloomy.

For the last several days, he had been in a bad mood.

His favored Mi Fang had been defeated by Long Fu in a humiliating fashion.

This caused the Jing Xiang Clan’s business negotiation with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to be enveloped by a dark shadow.

The sad news was not limited to just this.

Very quickly, the Jing Xiang Clan heard that Long Meng took Long Fu under his wing.

This put the Jing Xiang Clan Leader on the spot, and he decided to suspend the clan’s talks with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. If talks were carried out under these circumstances, they were bound to get the worst of it!

Anyways, the Li Jian Clan Leader Mian Li Zang decided to contact him, and when he saw the former, the Jin Xiang Clan Leader’s expression allayed a lot.

When someone with bad luck came across another with even worse luck, then naturally their mood would improve.

“Li Jian Clan Leader, why have you contacted me?” The Jing Xiang Clan Leader appeared somewhat haughty.

Mian Li Zang smiled, “Because of a mercenary corps, or to be more accurate, an outsider. He has given us both a lot of trouble, right?”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader coldly snorted, “Long Fu has indeed given my clan some trouble, but he will produce far more benefits. Mian Li Zang, do you really think I’d stand with you against the Dragon lion Mercenary Corps?”

Mian Li Zang shrugged his shoulders, “Why can you not?”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader was just about to sneer, but a moment later, Mian Li Zang stated his intentions, “In that duel, Long Fu displayed a saint level aptitude. He could break away from my clan today, and even if he works with your Jin Xiang Clan, what about in the future?”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader’s pupils contracted, and he became even more gloomy, sinking into silence momentarily.

Mian Li Zang’s words had struck at the deepest concerns in his heart.

Saint level…

There were only three experts of such level in the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom. The reason why Da Han was such a significant threat was because he was a saint level pirate!

During Long Fu’s duel with Mi Fang, he took on many enhancement spells, revealing his strong bloodline talent, and showing the shining qualifications of a saint level!

In other words, given enough time, he might become a saint level.

If the Jing Xiang Clan had an inseparably close cooperation with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, then when Long Fu became a saint level one day, how would they conduct themselves?

Long Fu could use his saint level to suppress and force more benefits out of the Jing Xiang Clan, how could the latter resist?

It didn’t seem possible.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could even annex the Jing Xiang Clan; stopping that would be hard.

When that happened, their cooperation with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would not be happiness, but a disaster!

“All right, Mian Li Zang. You don’t really think using some nebulous future calamity can persuade me to stop my collaboration with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps so yours can continue?” The Jing Xiang Clan Leader sneered.

He continued, “If you are afraid, why aren’t you breaking away from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, why are you increasing magic medicine production?”

Mian Li Zang’s smile dwindled somewhat, and he cordially replied, “Because that is just one possibility.”

“Growing to the saint level is no easy thing.”

“Please don’t misunderstand me.”

“I don’t want to throttle Long Fu’s plans ahead of time. Since he has been deployed by his arms merchant backer, he must have powerful protection methods.”

“Leaving the rest aside, a saint level teleportation scroll is instantaneous. The Ice Sculpture Kingdom does not forbid spatial teleportation, and we don’t need to try anything meaningless.”

“To tell the truth, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is our friend, and if we work with them, we can earn a lot of money.”

The Jing Xiang Clan Elder narrowed his eyes, “Do you want our clans to work together?”

Mian Li Znag nodded, “Everyone will get wealthy together, isn’t that good?”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader coldly snorted, “Even if I agree…the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps might not, then what?

Mian Li Zang smiled, “Then let Long Fu taste our Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s power. We don’t need our clans on the forefront; I heard that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps made a betting agreement with the Alchemy Guild.”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader’s eyes suddenly shined, exposing a smile for the first time, “Hmm, that’s a decent measure.”

Chapter 927: Second Exam Question

Despite the Li Jian Clan’s challenging situation, Mian Li Zang still could depend on his outstanding abilities and secretly reached an agreement with the Jing Xiang Clan Leader.

Very quickly, the Li Jian and Jing Xiang Clans dispatched clan members to the Ice Sculpture Capital to pay a visit to Hua Ni.

Hua Ni was a genuinely powerful elder of the Alchemy Guild and controlled the guild’s magic medicine sphere.

The Li Jian Clan and Jing Xiang Clansmen conveyed to Hua Ni that they wanted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ Magician Yao Ma (Zi Di) eliminated.

And they brought with them heavy gifts and also made clear their sincerity to Hua Ni.

“Long Fu has saint level qualifications, compelling the Li Jian and Jing Xiang Clans to secretly reach some mutual understanding and have sought to negotiate with me. Hehehe.” Hua Ni naturally accepted the two heavy gifts on the spot, and replied she’d do her best to eliminate Yao Ma.

Zi Di had put out the mount potions and broken new ground in an uncontested part of the magic medicine market. At the beginning of this, Hua Ni knew how immense this business would be, but regretfully, she was up to her ears in work and had heavy responsibilities.

By the time she had free time and energy, Zi Di had come out with a second and a third potion, which had even more repercussions in the market.

Then, Elder Jiu Jin acted as envoy and was exploited by Zi Di, forcing the Alchemy Guild representative to sign a betting agreement with Zi Di.

So long as they won the bet, the Alchemy Guild would surpass these early birds, steal the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ early gains and jump ahead, possessing the greatest mount potion domestic market share.

Therefore, eliminating Yao Ma (Zi Di) was what Hua Ni wanted to do.

The first exam question was made substantially more difficult by Hua Ni’s directive.

The Li Jian and Jing Xiang Clansmen looking for her had strengthened Hua Ni’s feelings.

After thinking about it, she called over Elder Jiu Jin and told him to alter the second exam question.

After Elder Jiu Jin listened to her, he was stunned, “You want it to be the construction of an autonomous combat puppet? You also want that puppet to fight an ice sculpture guardian of the capital, and only by winning can they advance to the next round?”

He stared at Elder Hua Ni, “This is too hard! You’ll eliminate 99% of the competitors, and even if 1% is left, the winners would have just been extremely lucky.”

Although Elder Jiu Jin had the worst alchemy attainment of the elders, he was still knowledgeable.

Hua Ni’s exam question most crucially specified “autonomous combat”.

This implied that no matter what the size of the puppet was, it would absolutely be an enormous engineering undertaking and an immense challenge.

Then it had to defeat an ice sculpture guardian, that skyrocketed the difficulty!

Hua Ni smiled, “I know how difficult it is.”

“Therefore, this alchemy question is a collaborative project, the competitors can make up to groups of three.”

“So, it’s collaborative alchemy, in that case, the alchemy workload will be drastically reduced, and it is acceptable.” Elder Jiu Jin sighed.

He was still worried, “In that case, what is the standard for the examination-use ice sculpture guardian?”

Hua Ni replied, “Silver level.”

Elder Jiu Jin’s brows tightened, and after lowering his head in reflection, he still shook his head, “Even so, this exam question is too difficult. I am worried the pass rate will be less than 30%.”

“If we conduct such an exam, then the spectacle will be quite unsightly.”

“Outsiders will say that our Ice Sculpture Kingdom are sore losers who deliberately raised the difficulty to open a back door to our own.”

Hua Ni contemplated for a moment, “Then let’s ease the restrictions a bit and allow the competing magicians to use an external material.”

When Elder Jiu Jin heard that, his brows relaxed a bit but still shook his head.

However, Hua Ni raised a hand and overrode him, “Stop talking, this is how we’re doing it.”

Jiu Jin forced a smile, “Elder Hua Ni, if you’re going to alter the exam by so much, shouldn’t you at least inform the Lord President? If he has differing ideas, we will be hard pressed to accomplish this.”

Hua Ni replied, “I will do that.”

Elder Jiu Jin said his goodbyes, then looked for Zi Di, deeply worried and sick at heart.

When Zi Di heard this information, she bitterly exclaimed, “I didn’t expect Great Master Hua Ni to be so determined to deal with me!”

Jiu Jin anxiously looked at her, “Your Lord Commander Long Fu revealed saint level qualifications, that should be the main factor that prompted Elder Hua Ni’s decision.”

“Furthermore, I also asked around, the Li Jian and Jing Xiang Clans’ envoys paid Elder Hua Ni a visit.”

With Elder Jiu Jin on her side, Zi Di easily acquired inside information.

Zi Di suddenly turned apprehensive, “Have those two clans unexpectedly joined hands secretly?”

Of course, the Jing Xiang Clan and the Li Jian Clan should be rivals, but now they were working together behind the scenes.

At least, they reached some mutual understanding.

Elder Jiu Jin was being blackmailed by Zi Di, so he had no choice but to give advice, “Magician Yao Ma, it will be very difficult to pass the second examination.”

“Working just by yourself, you have no chance.”

“The examination will allow up to three people in a group, actually just pairing up is untenable.”

“Therefore, you need to find at least two competitors and ensure they are not clan or guild spies.”

Zi Di looked grave, this kind of alchemy exam question really targeted outsiders like them.

Zi Di sank into vexation.

In so short a time, how could she find people worthy of trust?

Ten Millennia Ice Lake.

Millennium ice layer.

An alchemy laboratory was set up above the ice layer.

Through the alchemy laboratory window, Cang Xu’s eyes were bright, he could admire the ice lake’s distinctive ecology as much as he liked.

Suddenly, a ray of light flashed in the pale lake water.

The golden light flowed just like a blue dragon, seeming to flow and meander in the seemingly boundless white fog.

The golden light emitted an intense and vast aura that made Cang Xu incessantly apprehensive.

Teacher Si Ling appeared behind him at this moment, extending a hand on Cang Xu’s shoulder, placating him, “Don’t worry, the Millenium Dragon Formation has just been built, it currently isn’t a threat to us yet.”

Teacher Si Ling had teleported Cang Xu and Jian Gui from White Hair Trench and secretly infiltrated the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

They had moved stealthily the whole journey and teleported many times to arrive under the Ice Sculpture Capital.

The Ice Sculpture Capital’s Ten Millennia Ice Lake’s ice layer had many alchemy laboratories, shrines, and even magician towers built here.

It was because the Ice Sculpture Capital did not block teleportation that Cang Xu was able to effortlessly get into the ice lake.

After getting some information about the situation from Teacher Si Ling, Cang Xu learned that Teacher Si Ling had been impersonating someone for a long time and was able to successfully petition the capital for the construction of his own alchemy laboratory in the ice lake.

But this was the first time Cang Xu had heard of the [Millennium Dragon Formation], and he asked for more details at once.

Teacher Si Ling was willing to instruct Cang Xu and with a sigh, he explained, “The Millennium Dragon Formation is an ultra-formation, secretly built a long time ago at the orders of the royal family.”

“By the time of this generation’s king, they finally built the initial formation. What you just saw was the formation being activated.”

“The royal family primarily built this formation to control the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s national policy on the ice lake is that it should be the core of the nation’s alchemy policy. This national policy had a large effect on the kingdom possessing such a top quality alchemy level.”

“But as time passed, the systemic problems of this national policy gradually accumulated and grew more severe.”

“Take us for example, it was very easy to get here. It goes without saying, in the buildings across the Ten Millennia Ice Lake, there exists many dark powers that greatly threaten the Ice Sculpture Royal Family’s control.”

“On the other hand, speaking in terms of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s domestic political structure, the royal family urgently needs to gain total control over the Ten Millennia Ice Lake, this super-resource point, to hold dominance and coerce the Han Yu Clan and other great noble powers.”

Teacher Si Ling had a very clear understanding of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and explained it in a very frank manner.

Cang Xu asked, “What kind of powers does the Millennium Dragon Formation have?”

Teacher Si Ling exposed a rarely seen serious look, “This is a god level super-formation! Because the main material it uses is the Ten Millennia Ice Lake’s water, and it possesses immense power over the aspect of time.”

“According to my current investigation, it contains a magic rune combination that forms at least 80 different types of time magic.”

“The Ice Sculpture Royal Family has finally achieved this after generations of hard work.”

“With it, the Ice Sculpture Royal Family can confidently contend with gods.”

“But right now, the Millennium Dragon Formation has just been built, and is still in its testing phase, it hasn’t been completely activated yet.”

“We are still safe as of now.”

“After this National Ceremony, we will no longer be able to come back to this alchemy laboratory.”

Cang Xu pondered, offhandedly said, “How will the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s domestic great nobles respond to the royal family wanting control over the Ten Millennia Ice Lake?”

“Hehehe.” Teacher Si Ling clapped Cang Xu’s shoulder, “That is an interesting topic.”

The Alchemy Guild President was exhausted in both body and mind, and after quietly leaving the Millennium Dragon Formation control hub, he teleported back to the Alchemy Guild.

The super-formation testing workload was growing larger, and he sensed it was getting more difficult to manage.

The Alchemy Guild President did not have outstanding individual talent; his most outstanding characteristic was his loyalty!

As a result, the royal family appointed him many times and made him an important member of the testing team.

Just as he sat down, the study doorbell rang.

The guild president sighed, “Please come in.”

Elder Hua Ni walked in, and got right to the point, “It’s about the Warm Snow Cup’s second exam, I think I should change it somewhat.”

After saying that, Hua Ni handed over a plan document.

The president quickly skimmed it, his brows creasing, he looked at Hua Ni, “There has never been such a large change before. Is it worth making such a big fuss over the insignificant Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

Hua Ni simply said, “Think about Long Fu, he has saint level qualifications.”

The president took a deep breath, and put down the plan document, “I approve.”

He continued, “Not long ago, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps petitioned the guild for a large amount of alchemy laboratories, I have done my best to delay that.”

Hua Ni laughed, “They are in the process of cutting ties with the Li Jian Clan. They are likely applying for capital alchemy laboratories to produce magic medicine.”

These people are ambitious, we must pinch their attempts in the Warm Snow Cup, and by winning this bet, we can control them in the future.”

The president nodded, “You are right.”

Chapter 928: Incorporating the Bramble Pirates

The Warm Snow Cup’s second exam details were formally announced two days later.

Even though Zi Di was mentally prepared, when she genuinely saw it, her mood was inevitably weighed down.

“Refine an autonomous combat puppet. After activating it, it has to defeat an ice sculpture guardian to advance to the next round?”

“An alchemy puppet manufacturing cycle is at least one month. Even if it is a small sized puppet toy, making it functional will take at least a week.”

To build it on the spot meant she only had 12 hours, that was overwhelmingly difficult.

Perhaps a gold level master alchemist could do it, but most of the participants in this year’s Warm Snow Cup were silver levels like Yao Ma.

Relying only on herself, this was nigh impossible to do.

Luckily it was made known that there was an addendum – it would allow teams of at most three, and at minimum two.

How could there be teams of two?

A team of three could barely accomplish this immense workload!

“That isn’t even the hardest part!” Zi Di’s brows tightened.

The alchemy puppet created had to defeat an ice sculpture guardian.

“A silver level ice sculpture guardian…this requirement means the alchemy puppet refined needs to be at least silver level too.”

This was the most basic criterion.

If you can’t achieve the silver level, don’t bother comparing it to an iron level.

Because the ice sculpture guardian’s true fighting strength surpassed most silver level puppets!

The ice sculpture guardian possessed extraordinary intelligence, this was dependent on its main material, the Ten Millennia Ice Lake’s ice.

Because of the time dragon corpse, these ice chunks were irradiated with divinity and possessed ample spirituality.

A simple carving could form a living spirit and image. A constructed framework could allow an ice sculpture to freely move. The results were simply unbelievably good.

Otherwise, how could the Ten Millennia Ice Lake be a super-resource point?

It was the foundation from which the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom developed.

“Therefore, just refining a complete silver level alchemy puppet is not enough, it also needs to possess formidable fighting strength that can defeat an ice sculpture guardian.”

Zi Di felt that breathing was hard.

She couldn’t think of a way to handle this.

Her alchemy talent wasn’t high.

Just relying on herself, she would have failed the first exam. By exploiting Jiu Jin to cheat to get the exam questions ahead of time, followed by wild preparations and a large amount of secret practice, she was able to pass.

Although she came in first place, it was all in vain.

Not to say Zi Di was utterly lacking in alchemy talent.

She had some attainment in magic medicine, but in the high standards of the Warm Snow Cup, it was insufficient to look at.

At present, faced with the second exam question, Zi Di had no idea where to start.

“But I have to pass…”

Zi Di’s eyes were filled with resolve.

The dragonman youngster wholeheartedly wanted to cut ties with the Li Jian Clan and had no intention of working with the Jing Xiang Clan. The rumors of him collaborating with the Jing Xiang Clan were just a façade used as a pretense to stall for time.

During this period, the dragonman youngster wanted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to truly stand up. With his current strength, why couldn’t he take all of the mount potion profits? Why would he share with anyone else?

Selling channels were not a problem, and thanks to Zi Di borrowing a chicken to lay an egg, the Li Jian Clan was exploited to make that possible.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps really lacked places to manufacture magic medicine.

To respond to the entire kingdom’s magic medicine demand, they needed enough production capacity. More importantly, it had to be secure!

Like before, when Mei Lin sacrificed people to breach the Snow Bird Harbor workshops.

This wouldn’t do!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to take everything for themselves, and they faced the Li Jian Clan, the Jing Xiang Clan, and also the Alchemy Guild.

The survivors had to take preventative measures ahead of time.

And the best way to prevent things was to build a magician tower.

A magician tower had outstanding defenses and was often treated as a defensive hub.

So long as it was built, even one floor, it was easily guarded and hard to attack.

After using spatial alchemy technology, each room in a magician tower could be a vast space and building an alchemy factory in one was no problem.

“As long as one gets to the top three in the Warm Snow Cup, they will be subsidized in the construction of a magician tower.”

The funds were actually secondary.

The survivors had prosperous mount potion sales and weren’t worried about capital.

Zi Di valued the qualifications!

The Ice Sculpture kingdom required heavy vetting to get a piece of gold level armor, let alone a magician tower.

Wanting to covertly build a magician tower was nearly impossible.

This was a large-scale engineering project.

By getting top three, the foreign Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be qualified to build and possess a magician tower, that was very significant!

Despite the extreme difficulty, Zi Di was still very motivated.

The Commander has invested so much, I have to redouble my efforts!”

The ghost girl always firmly believed: the harder she worked, the happier she’d be.

And she was intoxicated solely by the joint struggle with her beloved.

During the dragonman youngster’s duel with Mi Fang, he sustained heavy injuries, with his fingers all being blown off his hand when he threw the battle qi ball. Zi Di was incomparably distressed by that.

Obviously the dragonman youngster could have won at a lesser cost, but he didn’t. For the sake of the bigger picture, he strived to disguise his own combat strength while simultaneously fighting for more renown, thus, he intentionally threw the battle qi ball and bore the great suffering it inflicted.

Zi Di recalled fondly the price the dragonman youngster paid, and she needed to do her best in her objective!

This kind of energetic zeal was far greater than it was during her partnership with Zong Ge.

In the beginning Zi Di had many conflicts with Zong Ge and was very wary of him, but since then, step by step, she comprehended Zong Ge’s nature and had a lot of admiration for him. As they fought alongside each other, it increased the camaraderie between them.

Being together with the dragonman youngster was completely different. Zi Di enjoyed every moment, wanting to do her best from the bottom of her heart, so much so that she was undaunted by the dangers like a moth that flew into the flames that ignited her enthusiasm.

“The Warm Snow Cup actually only has two phases.”

“The first stage is the Alchemy Guild using some test questions to wash out some and filter out the true elites.”

“The second stage is displaying one’s alchemy achievements. It is the main feature!”

“As long as I am able to get through the first stage, I’ll be able to display my great skills in the second stage!”

Zi Di had great faith in her second stage performance.

To be more accurate, it wasn’t in herself, but in War Merchant.

War Merchant was a legend level and a great grandmaster alchemist. Others would be using all they had to display the most recent years’ alchemy achievements while Zi Di only needed to choose something from War Merchant’s database to do the same.

“Therefore, I have to get across the first stage!”

Zi Di secretly sucked in air, then gazed at Elder Jiu Jin, her eyes emitting cold light.

She calmly said, “Elder Jiu Jin, I don’t want to make things difficult for you. But why did you nevertheless make things difficult for me? I joined the Warm Snow Cup merely for the sake of our mercenary corps. Why is getting through the first round so difficult? Is it worth it to target me so heavily?”

Jiu Jin promptly explained, “This is not me making things difficult for you, Magician Yao Ma! It is Elder Hua Ni! She is very cautious and in fact very stingy. Adding on the Li Jian and Jing Xiang Clan’s urging…your corps is really powerful and that they are afraid of the consequences.”

“I don’t care!” Zi Di waved her hand, displaying a crafty and unruly side, “If I can’t get through this, then your sin will be revealed.”

“Therefore, you had best think of a good way to deal with this, otherwise, you won’t be an Alchemy Guild elder any longer.”

Unable to think of anything, Zi Di forced that task onto Elder Jiu Jin.

She held definitive proof that Elder Jiu Jin had close ties to a necromancer.

If this evidence was published, Elder Jiu Jin would no longer keep his position and might have to accept investigation. An Ice Sculpture Kingdom internal investigation was easy to deal with, Elder Ju Jin could mobilize his contacts and use bribes to perhaps get through it, the real fear is that the Blood Light Punishment Institution would get involved. Everyone knew that almost every power on the Main Plane worked together to round up necromancers, and the Sheng Ming Empire mainly relied on the Blood Light Punishment Institution.

Jiu Jin suddenly became worried; he was afraid of exactly that. Unable to secure that evidence, he was yoked and forced to put forth plans and ideas for Zi Di’s sake.

“It will be impossible for you to do this alone Magician Yao Ma.

“To pass the second exam, we need to focus on other people.”

Although Zi Di had exceptional grades in the first exam, as one of the people involved, Jiu Jin knew what Zi Di’s alchemy abilities were.

He was of the opinion that Zi Di alone was out of the question, so instead find two experts to carry Zi Di along. Even if Zi Di was deadweight, they could still drag her across the finish line.

Zi Di also had that opinion, it’s just that there was immense hidden dangers in this.

She continued to gaze at Elder Jiu Jin, “I don’t trust outsiders that much, and I am very worried that Hu Ni, the Li Jian Clan, or the Xing Jiang Clan have planted spies. When the time comes, the spies will tamper with things, and it’ll be worse than me facing the second exam alone.”

Elder Jiu Jin also considered that, and said at once, “Therefore, we must start in advance and contact some former years’ alchemists and not those in the current year. We can secretly contact them and use a large sum of money to purchase their services and use magic contracts to restrict them!:”

The Warm Snow Cup’s second round was not just available to those who passed the first round.

So long as a competitor had a relatively exceptional showing in past Warm Snow Cups, the Alchemy Guild gave them the right to skip sections in future Warm Snow Cup Grand Alchemy Contests.

Because inherently, the Warm Snow Cup’s first stage was mainly to be a filter.

The alchemists that made it through the filter in past years naturally didn’t need to be filtered again.

Of course, there were many bylaws, and based on their achievements, some magicians could skip the first exam, others could skip the second exam, and there were some that could be immediately qualified to participate in the second stage alchemy achievement exhibition. Former champions could immediately display their alchemy results and could also show them in the best spots in the exhibition hall.

This kind of competition skipping had a time limit. The longer time went on, the more these qualifications weakened. If too much time passed, the alchemists had to participate in the first exam once again.

However, there were special cases. For example, some alchemy masters won success and recognition outside of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and when they came back to compete, the Alchemy Guild gave special approval to automatically advance them into the alchemy showcase.

In short, the Alchemy Guild had considerable margin in this respect.

With regards to the fact that “former master alchemists that could skip exams”, Zi Di knew about it.

She questioned closely at once, “Elder Jiu Jin, who do you recommend?”

Elder Jiu Jin came prepared, and he took out a list, “This is a list of those who can participate in the follow-up grand contest, this information came from my guild’s database, copied word for word, it is definitely complete!”

“Of course, if a foreign great master alchemist suddenly appears to compete, that is something we can’t anticipate.”

Zi Di took the information, but didn’t let Elder Jiu Jin off easily, rather she kept questioning, “I asked for who you recommended?”

At first Elder Jiu Jin didn’t want to shoulder that responsibility, declining tactfully and unwilling to issue his opinion.

Zi Di coldly snorted, reiterating, “If you don’t say anything, and if I can’t pass, you’re done for.”

Jiu Jin wiped cold sweat off his forehead, and with no other choice, was forced to give a few names, evaluating them as outstanding.

Zi Di found the people in the information.

The information was detailed and accurate, not just limited to names, but also included a summary and most importantly, their performance in past competitions; all of this was recorded correctly.

After Elder Jiu Jin left, the dragonman youngster’s figure appeared in the corner of the room.

He knew that Jiu Jin was under Zi Di’s thumb and had exclaimed in admiration at Zi Di’s skill. When he was observing this play out, he felt that Elder Ju Jin was still on their side, and his words were relatively honest and trustworthy.

Seeing Zi Di’s worried face, the dragonman youngster said understandingly, “You are worried there are problems with those recommended?”

Zi Di nodded, “If we could think of this, there is no reason this hasn’t occurred to our adversaries. The people Jiu Jin recommended have high assessments, won’t they be paying attention to these people?”

“They are the local bully, maybe they’ve already contacted these people.”

“If we can’t elucidate the truth, and devote our energies to this, we’ll be stepping into a trap.”

If they used lots of money to buy them, then the others would use even more to buy them away.

Magic contracts were not omnipotent, and like the conflict between the strongest spear and shield, there would inevitably be a way to resolve them.

The dragonman youngster deeply had the same feeling.

Before, when he returned, he used Charming Blue divine spells to resolve the magic contract Zi Di and Zong Ge signed with the Li Jian Clan.

In White Hair Trench, the stronger Magician Si Ling painstakingly condensed and gathered death divinity, all for the fishman youngster’s benefit. Even though Teacher Si Ling had the divine artifact [Truth Slate], what use was it? Even with unintentional planning, it would still achieve the desired result.

The dragonman youngster thought about it, “Do you remember what I told you about Xiao Wang Zi?”

“If everything else fails, we can use a large sum to buy off a former year’s alchemy master, secretly control them, then have Xiao Wang Zi pretend to be them in the competition.”

The necromancer Xiao Wang Zi surely had the fundamentals of alchemy.

The teleportation magic item, the Eulogy Tombstone, had been created by him independently.

Zi Di shook her head and sighed, “That can only be used as an alternative plan.”

Xiao Wang Zi’s true alchemy skills were left open to questions.

He was a necromancer, and if he was spotted during the grand contest, then it would be disastrous.

There was another hidden danger, Xiao Wang Zi’s memory had been modified.

Tense battles were easy to deal with, as Xiao Wang Zi didn’t have to think foolishly, and was only able to focus his attention on dealing with the powerful enemy before him.

In alchemy, this was different.

Alchemy needed rapid thought, the constant detection and resolution of problems. Under these conditions, one’s thinking would be very active and needed to extract knowledge from their memories, increasing their success from their experiences.

This was not secure, and Xiao Wang Zi was likely to notice his forged memories were false, easily causing chaotic cognition and let him see the truth.

The dragonman youngster sighed.

He also knew this suggestion wasn’t that good.

He also wanted to intervene personally.

Regretfully, although he was a gold level magician, the divine blessings were focused on casting experience, and a pitiful few were on alchemy.

He was unable to help Zi Di.

Zi Di saw her beloved’s exasperation and depression, and she extended a hand to stroke the young man’s chest, tenderly consoling, “My Lord, you still have more important business.”

“The Warm Snow Cup is mine to handle.”

“If I fail, that is still acceptable.”

“But if I can pass, it will be even better.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, he really did have more important business to attend to.

After saying goodbye to Zi Di, he called Cong Mang over.

After transforming into a fishman form, he pulled open a heavy curtain in space through Divine Domain Descent, and with a step, teleported thousands of kilometers out to sea.

On the ocean surface, the Vegetable Pirates and the Bramble Pirates were already confronting each other.

The two flagships slowly came side by side of each other.

The fishman youngster did not appear, instead Cong Mang took the lead, jumping onto the other side’s flagship.

He looked at their leader, suddenly looking disdainful, “Are you the Bramble Pirates’ current captain?”

“Hehe, an insignificant silver level, for what reason?”

“What qualifications do you have to speak of collaboration with me?”

The Bramble Pirates’ original commander was called Jing Wen, Feng Lian had cut him down in one slice. The new commander was just a silver level human with a short and wizened stature, long curly hair, a gloomy face, and very baggy eyes. Something happened to his skin, as it was a weird dark green.

He raised his head to look up at the taller Cong Mang and laughed grimly, “I am called Ci Ji. Regarding my qualifications for working together with you, it would be better for us to just fight it out.”

Cong Mang stared for a bit; he didn’t expect Ci Ji to be so direct.

He seemed to look at him with new eyes and nodded, “Good, we’ll fight first.”

Ci Ji was prepared, and after taking a few steps, he jumped into the sea.

Both feet stepped on the water, but he didn’t sink, walking as if on level ground.

Cong Mang took out a new gold level long rod (to replace the one that had been destroyed in the battle with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord) and also landed on the ocean surface to battle with Ci Ji.

Ci Ji’s weapons were two daggers.

Cong Mang waved his long rod, dashing with a rush of wind, his offensive very impressive.

Ci Ji moved nimbly and was very good at ocean combat. He occasionally ran and shot, occasionally throwing himself into the ocean then ambushed Cong Mang from the bottom of the latter’s feet.

Cong Mang was a blunt and charging type of fighter, that was the kind of battlefield that suited him most. For a moment, he was flustered somewhat.

The fishman youngster secretly observed, amazed to notice that although Ci Ji was a genuine silver level, he displayed a gold level constitution.

Not only that, but his speed was greater than Cong Mang’s.

However, when Cong Mang used divine spells to amplify himself, he became faster than Ci Ji, causing the latter to sink into a disadvantageous position.

Don’t be fooled by Cong Mang being a valiant charger and breaker of lines, he was actually a druid that majored in divine spells, battle qi was his second cultivation.

Cong Man’s battle aura grew more flourishing.

When he followed the young commander to the Ice Sculpture Capital, he naturally didn’t dare to be neglectful and was very cautious. When he returned to the seas and fought a fierce battle, this was precisely his familiar and favorite setting, this made him exhilarated.

“Come again, come again!”

“Can you go faster? Don’t hide, do you dare to take my rod head on?”

“If this is your limit, then for this operation, you’ll listen to me!”

Cong Mang continued to shout, tentatively approving of Ci Ji’s strength.

As a silver level, being able to fight a level above him and commended by Cong Mang like this was very rare.

But the fishman youngster nevertheless saw that Ci Ji had energy left.

He used Divine Descent, controlling the latter and having him step forth.

“Cong Mang, withdraw now.” The big guy’s eyes emitted blue light, his voice wasn’t loud, but everyone could hear it.

Cong Mang didn’t dare to disobey, and he promptly put away his rod and withdrew from the fight, returning to the ship in a servile manner.

The Bramble Pirates were stunned.

Cong Mang was publicly known as the Vegetable Pirates’ commander. Yet he unexpectedly heeded the orders of a silver level small giant, what was going on?

Ci Ji looked bewildered, thinking of the information where “during the Snow Harbor Battle, Coing Mang was taken away by a fishman expert”.

As for the Vegetable Pirates, they had been incorporated in already and were well accustomed to this.

The big guy gazed at Ci Ji, the blue light in his eyes growing a bit more vigorous.

Ci Ji felt something powerful staring at him, like it was stripping him naked and tossing him into the water. He immediately snorted in cold displeasure, “Who are you?”

Da Dou appeared next, “Impudent! You’re making a big fuss, small pirate. My master’s name is taboo; do you really think you can easily learn it?”

Da Dou naturally had been blessed by deception divine spells and looked radically different. But his gold level aura hadn’t been tampered with.

Seeing a gold level giant, who also professed to be a servant, the Bramble Pirates were alarmed.

In a split second, they looked at the big guy with new eyes and formed a common understanding – the big guy had extraordinary origins and might be the core member of a large force.

The big guy under the fishman youngster’s control spoke deeply, “Do you have a totem on your body?”

Ci Ji’s expression changed, “Since you can see it, then there is no need to conceal it.

“It is indeed a totem, it is Commander Jing Wen’s Magic Spirit Totem, it is because of it that I have gold level battle power!”

The totem was one of the Shaman Church’s characteristics, and in the current age of the Main Plane, it was popular with the beastmen. But it was not limited to the beastmen, it widely existed amongst the other intelligence races.

For example, the dragonman youngster saw the Flame Totem on Da Dou’s body.

The Bayonet Gang remnants escaped Snow Bird Harbor through rocket assault boats, which had many totems in them.

The big guy’s head and chest were held out high, he stepped into the air, and looked down at Ci Ji, “What is the Magic Spirit Totem? I can see you haven’t completely displayed this totem’s power.”

“I will allow you to do that!”

“Next, so long as you can block my fist once, the Vegetable Pirates and your Bramble Pirates will work together, I swear by the Pirate Throne that all loot acquired will be divided evenly.”

But Ci Ji turned very gloomy, “If I can’t withstand it, what will happen?”

The big guy looked up, using his nostrils to glance at Ci Ji underneath him, “You will serve me, and I will still allow you to command your people.”

Ci Ji became furious, raising his head to shout, “Pirates are free!”

The big guy rubbed his fists and wiped his palms, “In that case, I will grant you a greater freedom – death.”

Ci Ji gritted his teeth tightly.

He now felt immense regret, why did he want to work with the Vegetable Pirates?

It really was wretched, the difference between the information and reality was too large!

Circumstances forced him, what could he do?

Ci Ji glared gloomily at the big guy, then at Cong Mang and Da Dou, then finally back to the big guy.

“He coldly snorted, “I accept!”

“I’ll let you see the Magic Spirit Totem’s full might!”

After saying that, he bit the tip of his tongue, and sprayed blood onto his palm.

He wiped his palm across his chest and arms.

The blood was rapidly absorbed, and his dark green skin suddenly became “alive”, changing into waving dark green vines.

In the blink of an eye, Ci Ji was wrapped by layers of vines, several hundred vines twisted with each other, wrapping him up into a giant spheroid.

A gold level aura was raised to its peak, nearly suppressing Ci Ji’s transcendent aura almost completely.

“Come!” The vine ball transmitted Ci Ji’s muffled voice.

The big guy lightly laughed, slowly stepping down from the air.

He came to the vine spheroid, slowly extended his palm, and placed it on the spheroid.

Battle skill – Giant Wave Palm.

Enhancement – Penetration Force!

The palm’s force was like an immense wave, bearing enormous gravity and thrust, evenly spreading across the vines. With the enhancement of Penetration Force, the attack penetrated into the middle of the vine sphere, into Ci Ji.

Regardless of how substantial his defenses were, Ci Ji was just a silver level.

The big guy’s battle qi was fully silver level now, his constitution was supported by silver battle qi, and with Da Dou’s meticulously cooked food, he had explosive growth in all attributes.

The big guy surpassed the human Ci Ji in all attributes. He was able to bypass the totem’s defense and hit right into the heart.

Plop.

Ci Ji seemed to feel a giant wave slap into him, and after he coughed out blood, he sank into unconsciousness.

When he lost consciousness, the Magic Spirit Totem was no longer being controlled, and it quickly vanished back into his body, turning his skin a dark green once again.

A one hit victory!

The Bramble Pirates were a deathly gray, while the Vegetable Pirates broke out into a deafening cheer.

Chapter 929: Long Meng Teaches “Force”

Ice Sculpture Capital.

In a training room, two dragonmen were currently fighting in close quarters.

One was white scaled and blue eyed, the other was red scaled and orange eyed, the former was mainly defending, while the latter was mainly attacking.

It was Long Meng and the dragonman youngster.

“A chance!” The dragonman youngster slid.

Long Meng lightly jumped and evaded.

The dragonman youngster exerted his tail, powerfully bracing himself, this caused the force of his slide to suddenly change!

Long Men ‘s expression was unchanging as he swung his palm like a blade, instantly chopping in a fierce clap of wind.

The two fought in the air, and because they weren’t using battle skills, they weren’t putting any effort into those, and this became a tussle of strength.

Both sides were gold level, and although Long Meng wasn’t as strong as Long Fu, his technique was better.

Long Meng withstood the dragonman youngster’s punches and kicks, exerting his waist, twisting his body, and turned the dragonman youngster off balance, throwing him through the air.

The dragonman youngster flew some distance, and just as he hit the ground, he saw Long Meng charging over!

Long Meng turned from defense to attack, a string of continuous unarmed attacks created a gale that assaulted the senses, his spacious clothes’ hems also clapped with booming echoes of wind.

The dragonman youngster’s footing was unsteady, as parrying the blows became increasingly strenuous.

He spared no efforts to fight, utilizing every part of his body and did his utmost to defend himself. However, the wild dragon claws cutting and the dragon teeth biting did not help him flip this losing battle.

Ding.

A dull echo.

Long Meng struck the dragonman youngster’s chest dead center, throwing him into the air.

The dragonman youngster landed on his back, straining to straighten himself and bounce back up.

Battle skill – Dragon Pearl.

He extended his right hand, and opened his dragon claw, from which gold battle qi bubbled out. The battle qi rapidly condensed into a reddish-gold dragon pearl.

The dragon pearl boiled, rapidly increasing the local air temperature.

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster coldly snorted, and threw his claw, revoking the Dragon Pearl battle skill, “Hump, it was agreed upon that there was to be no battle skills or external battle qi, I lose!”

Long Meng nodded, then looked down at his training clothes.

The clothes had just one tear in them; when the dragonman youngster was struggling, he cut it open with his dragon claw.

“How many training sessions has it been?” When Long Meng saw the cut in his clothes, he gasped in surprise mentally.

Since he began advising the dragonman youngster, the latter’s unarmed combat skills have soared, making rapid progress daily.

Once Long Fu’s basic combat skills were horrendous, he had a powerful body but was unable to truly exhibit it.

The current Long Fu could not only display his powerful constitution but could also move unpredictably and agilely.

If he hadn’t experienced it personally, Long Meng would never believe there was such a gifted dragonman in this world.

He coughed, then Long Meng stared at the dragonman youngster, “It seems you have practiced the Dragon Pearl battle skill proficiently.”

“However, what I like more is your act of cancelling the battle skill.”

“Let’s sit down and chat.”

Long Meng reached out to indicate that, then sat in a kneeling posture. His buttocks didn’t touch his legs and feet, while his upper body was straight as a log.

The dragonman youngster sat on his buttocks, his legs bending into a cross-legged position. His dragon claws rested on his knees as the young man carefreely said, “Long Meng, you’re praising me a bit too much, hehehe.”

Long Meng looked tranquil, his tone was slow and steady, “Indulging on one’s desires is easy, but restraining oneself is usually at least ten times harder.”

“The essence of martial combat does not lie with attacking, but in not attacking.”

“The martial goal is not in killing or injuring but lies in stopping conflict.”

The dragonman youngster’s mind moved, but on the surface he just asked, “What does that mean?”

Long Meng faintly smiled, “This is the philosophy of the eastern martial arts, it really is somewhat shrouded in fog. Let me explain this in another way.”

“Did you know our dragonman bloodlines come from the dragon race?”

The dragonman youngster replied, “Of course.”

Long Meng continued, “The earliest dragonmen were produced from dragons and humans mating.”

“Dragonmen then mated with each other or other races, progressively developing to form their own fixed ethnic group in current times. As dragonmen, we both enjoy the dragon race’s superior bodies, while also having the corresponding systemic problem – the draconic nature disturbs us.”

“Draconic nature?” The dragonman youngster frowned.

Long Meng kept going, “Dragonmen are universally arrogant, they look down upon other lifeforms, and the stronger they are, the more egotistical they are. This is not rare, but in the general situation, this is the draconic nature.”

“Naturally, the specific dragonman bloodlines are different, and so too are there differences between draconic natures.”

“For example, my bloodline originated from a white dragon, thus I am inclined towards callousness and indifference.”

“As for you, it is obvious you have red dragon bloodline traits and are disposed towards irascibleness and impulsiveness. Red dragonmen are normally bold and fearless, but from another point of view, they blindly rush indiscriminately into action.”

“On the battlefield, because of this temperament flaw, red dragonmen suffer grave losses.”

“You can regard draconic nature as a widespread temperament flaw.”

“You have saint level potential and wide prospects. Your red dragonman bloodline’s quality and concentration are both exceptional, and your draconic nature is very serious. But you haven’t succumbed to draconic nature, and your temperament is not partial towards irascible impulsiveness.”

“There is something like that?” The dragonman youngster’s mind moved greatly.

Bloodline really influenced nature and to a deep degree.

The dragonman youngster had an especially deep experience, because he could use the Bloodcore to transform into different life shapes. He remembered quite clearly that when he turned into the Surging Wave fishman form, he had a strong natural affinity for water, like he was wrapped up in it.

The dragonman youngster secretly analyzed, “According to his principle, my draconic nature should be getting heavier. Because I don’t have a stable dragonman bloodline, my current dragonman form is the combined appearance of the Flame Dragon King bloodline and my human bloodline.”

“Under normal circumstances, this kind of life shape is not stable, worse off than those artificial magic beasts on Mystifying Monster Island. It is only because of the Bloodcore’s support can I force this fusion without backfire or collapse.”

“As for why my human nature has suppressed my draconic nature, it is because I am not only using the Flame Dragon King Bloodline, but also the other dragon bloodlines I’ve absorbed. Most of them were white dragon bloodlines.”

“White dragon and flame dragon bloodlines counteract each other; therefore, my human nature maintains control.”

This was how the dragonman youngster always acted when facing Long Meng.

The main reason was that he was worried that his constitution wouldn’t perform good, and Long Meng would doubt he had a saint level bloodline

Long Meng had a serious expression, earnestly replying, “With regards to fighters like us, our bodies are natural weapons, and if we indulge our desires, don’t restrain our temperament and allow them to be expressed, then our weapons will drive us, and we won’t be the master of our weapons.”

“Always be aware of draconic nature, be vigilant of it, suppress it, master it, and ultimately, maintain a clear mind, that is the proper path to elevate our martial skills to a new state.”

“It is precisely because of that that my martial path has been elevated, and I mastered my ‘force’.”

Hearing Long Meng mention “force”, the dragonman youngster’s eyes shined, his interest thick, “I know about ‘force’! But I can’t grasp it, it’s too hard! I don’t have the faintest outline of the fundamentals.”

Long Meng laughed, “What is referred to as force is actually grasping subtlety, uniting body and mind.”

“We must know our body well and master it. The more you know of yourself, the faster you can function. You have to know when your fist descends, it can smash the porcelain and also the bricks underneath it. You must know what result you want to attain, and how much strength should be used to achieve it.”

“In terms of mentality, we must control our mind and keep it crystal clear. However, the draconic nature is usually too strong and maintaining this is very difficult. Only by mastering this, cleansing the self, and understanding one’s heart can one control their thoughts.”

“We dragonman have strong bodies, and usually exceptional bloodlines, that represents our growing draconic nature.”

“And this strong draconic nature is what makes controlling our thoughts so hard.”

“Therefore, usually, those outstanding fighters that master power don’t have the best bloodlines. They are normally beautiful inside and out, have abundant experience, experienced the world to the utmost, and washed themselves beautifully clean. Or perhaps they were cut off from the rest of the world from a young age, was instructed by a very competent teacher, diligently cultivated, and their mind was like a newborn baby for their entire life.”

“To grasp power, both body and mind needs to be mastered, you can’t do one without the other.”

Long Meng’s words luckily cleared up some of the dragonman youngster’s questions.

The dragonman youngster had obtained a lot of fighter experience from divine blessings. These experiences mostly dealt with battle qi cultivation and battle skill usage, but few of them touched on force.

Because these were Charming Blue Goddess’ blessings, and Charming Blue’s followers were concentrated on oceanic lifeforms, therefore, the dragonman youngster had two kinds of experience with force, Running Water Force and Purification Force.

Running Water Force could give a fighter a defense that circled around them like moving water, it could withstand Cutting Force and Piercing Force, dispersing them upon contact.

Purification Force could affect battle qi, breathing, blood, and consumed food, filtering and purifying them all. When used on food, it could purify it, covertly increasing its ease of digestion, and could even allow a fighter to eat magic beast meat a level above them. If one was poisoned, they could use Purification Force to transform the poison. If they had an enemy’s battle qi, magic, or divine power in them wreaking havoc, Purification Force could target them and enormously help the fighter.

However, the dragonman youngster didn’t grasp these two forces.

Even if he turned into his fishman form, he was unable to. The experience in this was incomparable to the help the battle skill and battle qi experience provided.

According to Long Meng’s theory, this was because the dragonman youngster’s body and mind were not in the right place.

“However, I learned Penetration Force with the Grand Arena Medal, it was success in one stroke.”

“My body and spirit then met the standard for it, then why were they both incompatible with the other two forces?”

The harbored misgivings hounded the dragonman youngster, “Must I master both aspects before I am able to cultivate force?”

“I cannot achieve the standard you speak of Long Meng.”

“Are there no shortcuts?”

Long Meng waved his hand, “Don’t be anxious.”

“My meaning is that by completely mastering one’s body and mind, you’ll have a greater chance of grasping force, this is the highest criterion.”

“The reason I brought up force is because I see your human nature is suppressing your draconic nature. Furthermore, this is not the first time you’ve shown that. Your mind has already reached a passing grade, and cultivating force is possible for you.”

The dragonman youngster slightly shivered.

He shamelessly put on a pretense for Long Meng. Long Meng was not Cang Xu. He had a shrewd and ruthless insight, but he sincerely followed the martial path, had a high perspective, comprehended new things by inference, and could similarly make out a bit of his own nature.

The dragonman youngster looked overjoyed, he stood up and said, “So there really is a shortcut! Then teach me now.”

“What really is force?”

“I really want to learn it.”

Long Meng chuckled, “I can’t guarantee you can learn it.”
