“Force is far rarer than battle skills, it is not universal by nature. One can master one type of force, but it won’t necessarily apply to another person. Especially when it comes to learning force, it has almost no value when it comes to instruction. This is because everyone’s body and mind is different.”

The dragonman youngster suddenly thought, “So that’s how it is. The divine blessings’ experiences on force instead hindered my grasp of Running Water Power and Purification Power?”

Long Meng slowly stood up, “Come, let me test you for a bit on what force suits you.”

Long Meng approached the young man and suddenly revealed his chest, “Use your fist to hit my chest.”

The dragonman youngster used his normal strength to directly hit Long Meng.”

“Again.” Long Meng was motionless as a mountain, “Use the same strength, don’t change the way you threw it either.”

The dragonman youngster punched again, looking flabbergasted.

Long Meng smiled, “What do you feel?”

The dragonman youngster spoke forthrightly, “The first punch felt like hitting a steel plate. The second time felt like dough.”

Long Meng nodded, “Correct, the first was hard force, the latter is soft force.”

“The most important thing is which of these two forces do you feel more distinctly?”

The dragonman youngster thought to himself, he had nearly the same affinity for both, but after some more consideration, he replied, “The first, hard force.”

The dragonman youngster questioned again, “Hard force, soft force, what are those? Why have I never heard of them?”

Long Meng didn’t hold anything back, “These two are the most basic forces. The forces we hear of daily are derived from these two basic forces.”

“For example, Penetration Force is a soft force variation, Cutting Force is a hard force application, Piercing Force relies mainly on hard force, with soft force complementing it.”

This was the first time the dragonman youngster had heard this theory.

War Merchant’s database included all kinds of battle qi techniques, battle skills, meditation methods, spells, and more, but never anything on force. War Merchant gathered this information for magic beasts, while force was the exclusive technique of intelligence races.

The dragonman youngster learned of two forces from the divine blessings’ fighter experience, but only felt their usage.

The dragonman youngster let slip as he was pondering, “Since all forces are variations and derivations of hard and soft force, then if I master these two forces, and combine them in different ways, can I master all forces?”

Long Meng shook his head, his gaze serene, “It isn’t like that.”

“The real order is just the opposite of what you just thought.”

“Only by mastering many kinds of forces can you combine your experiences, grasp the subtleties, and purify that into these two foundational forces.”

After a pause, Long Meng continued, “Normally, the forces a fighter can comprehend, then master are those that the fighter uses most often, are the best at, or has the greatest insight into.”

“For example, take a fighter whose best battle skill is Penetration Bone Finger. When he attacks with this battle skill, he controls each portion of strength in his body, and his mind is incomparably clear for an instant, it is possible that he grasps insight in that instant, thereby grasping Penetration Force.”

“Another example, a fighter who is exceedingly proficient at war hammers. They have perfect command of the war hammer, as if it was part of their body, a vivid part of their nature. Then it is possible that during daily training, he can have a flash of inspiration that can allow him to grasp Smashing Force, Gravity Force, or even Collision Force.”

“Many have said that it is more likely to happen during battle than during training.”

“Usually, the more intense a battle is, the closer the fighters engage, the more body and mind are united, thoughts will clear, leaving no impurities in them. Under this state of body and mind, grasping force is far more likely than normal.”

This is how the vast majority of fighters learn how to use force.

“Their body and mind attain a certain standard that allows them to grasp one, or perhaps two, or even three forces. They have only put one foot into the realm of force, and by no means have attained the next level of it, let alone mastered it all.”

The dragonman youngster interrupted with a question, “What is the mastery of force? Is it mastering hard and soft force?”

Long Meng nodded.

The dragonman youngster’s eyes contracted. In this, he was not Long Meng’s equal!

Long Meng took back the subject, “According to this test, you have a deep understanding of hard force, then your next objective is to learn a force derived from hard force, or a force that relies mainly on hard force.”

“Select a type you are most familiar with, the basic combat style you are most fond of, no matter if it is in attack, defense, or whether it is unarmed or with a weapon, no matter if it is based on basic combat skills or battle skills, so long as you feel it most agrees with your intentions and are most carefree with it.”

“Then constantly practice it, repeat it.”

“During this process, I will transmit into you the two fundamental forces - hard force and soft force.”

“This will enormously spur your grasp of force, and you will learn that corresponding force. But what it is specifically, I don’t know, it varies from person to person.”

“Of course, the greatest possibility is that this training fails.”

The dragonman youngster stared, “You can…pass on force?!”

This was a first for him.

“You can, so long as you grasp Resonance Force.” Long Meng nodded, his expression always tranquil.

“Resonance Force…” This was the first time he had heard of such a type of power.

He noticed that Long Meng’s combat skill mastery was far deeper than his.

His teachings on force had greatly broadened the young man’s horizons, and he gained quite a lot from them.

“Long Meng’s close quarters battle superiority is too great, if I fought him as I am now, I must do my best to avoid close combat, adopting long ranged warfare is the optimum choice.”

“I can feel that he is sincerely teaching me.”

“Why?”

“I am obviously his enemy, and if he wants to subdue me without a fight, isn’t that rather naïve?”

“Long Meng is not a naïve person!”

After interacting with Long Meng several times, the dragonman youngster had increased his understanding of him.

Since he returned from the Ocean Mother Nest, he had a profound level of insight into human nature. Before during the Snow Bird Harbor battle, he could effortlessly plan against Cong Mang. Then during the duel, he accurately gauged Mi Fang’s mentality.

The dragonman youngster could not determine the reason for this change. He judged that it was possible that it was because he was Charming Blue Goddess’ divine emissary and used many deception spells. For wasn’t cheating and concealing a power that targeted the mind?

Chapter 930: Cang Xu Converges

Ice Sculpture Capital.

In the Ten Millenia Ice Lake.

Through a gigantic floor length window, Cang Xu observed the ice lake outside of the alchemy laboratory.

It was a quiet world. The ice layer was a natural barrier, cutting off the clamor and ruckus from the capital on the surface. During the day, the sunlight shined through the ice layer, filling the entire lake water with blueness. The light reflected on the cracks and bubbled air, creating a glittering sight, like a submarine aurora borealis.

During the night, without the sunlight suppressing them, organic glimmer prevailed. All kinds of moss, aquatic plants, as well as winter fish and other animals emitted organic light, converging into a mild green that contaminated the entire ice lake from bottom to top.

This was a similar world full of vitality. Amid each layer of ice, lake water slowly circulated. Surrounded by this unique ecological system, those organisms that survived and reproduced here not only had to resist the extreme cold but also had to be miraculous too.

Cang Xu knew that this was the aftermath of Time divinity radiation.

Just the organisms here could be studied by magicians for countless years, and the water and the ice layers were of even greater research value than that. Cang Xu infiltrated this place with Jian Gui, constantly receiving outside information, he already knew of the Ice Cube Red Wine business, and heard about all sorts of novel things occurring in the capital, naturally this included the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Jian Gui quietly waved Cang Xu over, his tone faint, “Stop your observations, Lord Teacher Si Ling has summoned us.”

Cang Xu followed Jian Gui into the center of the alchemy laboratory, and paid his respects to Teacher Si Ling.

Teacher Si Ling looked mentally exhausted.

After entering the alchemy laboratory, he used the laboratory’s alchemy module to continue observing the environment outside.

He mainly focused on the newly built royal family engineering project – the Millenium Dragon Super-formation.

It was quite an achievement.

“This super-formation’s construction cost frightens the heavens, and it has a strong connection with the Time God Dragon’s corpse.”

“The formation hasn’t been activated yet, and is currently in the process of testing, at most only 5% of it will be active.”

“When it officially activates, the formation can conduct powerful detection scans of every layer of the Ten Millenia Ice Lake. This undoubtedly will assist the Ice Sculpture Royal Family enormously in their control over this super-resource point.”

“This is an unprecedented achievement that took the Ice Sculpture Kingdom several millennia!”

“After the formation is completely active, it seems it can mold an ice sculpture of the Time God Dragon that possesses unrivalled divine might.”

How much divine might it had was undeterminable to Teacher Si Ling.

The formation had other functions, but Teacher Si Ling couldn’t explore them for the time being.

“The Millenium Dragon Formation has a few formation pillars still below. My alchemy laboratory was only built on the century ice layer.”

The Ten Millenia Ice Lake was divided into a few layers.

The surface was the ten year ice lake, and it had been reinforced over the countless years through numerous magic formations to become tenacious and sturdy.

Above the ten year ice layer was the Ice Sculpture Capital. Below the ice layer was the flowing lake water. First there was frozen lake water from several years ago, then below was from a dozen years ago, below that was from tens of years ago. A hundred meters below the ten year ice layer was the hundred year lake water. The deeper the lake water was, the older it was.

Beneath the century old lake water was the second ice layer – the century ice layer.

The vast majority of alchemy laboratories were set up on this ice layer.

Under the ice layer was the centuries year lake water

The lake water reached a millennium and the ten millennium ice layer.

Beyond the millennium ice layer, the lake water would grow older, several millennia would accumulate, attaining an age of ten millennium.

Here, one would arrive at the bottom of the ice lake – a gigantic ice layer covering silt.

This was the core essence – the ten millennium ice layer – according to legend, a Time God Dragon fell down here, and it was precisely because of this divine dragon falling here that the Ten Millenia Ice Lake was a super-resource point.

“I have to examine this Millenium Dragon Formation to the best of my ability!”

“By the time this super-formation officially activates, it will be too late. I won’t be able to so effortlessly investigate it again.”

Teacher Si Ling had heard rumors and gossip about this super-formation, but this truly significant secret had been stringently guarded by the Ice Sculpture Royal Family.

Without a doubt, this super-formation would become Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s most important trump card!

“My current investigation has already reached its limit.”

“If I want to investigate more, I have to penetrate deeper into the millennium ice layer.”

“To completely understand the formation, I fear I have to get to the lake bottom’s millennium ice layer!”

Requirements to build an alchemy laboratory on the millennium ice layer was far stricter than on the century ice layer. The century ice layer had almost no magician towers on it, but the millennium ice layer had many built on it.

In the ten millennium ice layer, you could count the number of magician towers there with your hands, but each one was towering. There were also religious temples, the largest of which was the Glacier God temple. These temples were not the main temple, but each one had formidable combat strength and were created through a concerted effort of every large church in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, a powerful measure to suppress the ice lake’s bottom.

Teacher Si Ling had naturally snuck into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and deep into the ice lake because of a heavy duty.

As a long term plan, he already decided to explore the ins and outs of the formation, thus, he summoned Jian Gui and Cang Xu.

But Teacher Si Ling didn’t openly say he wanted to leave this place and go deeper into the ice lake, instead he said, “For the past few days, I’ve done my best to activate the alchemy laboratory and observe the Ice Sculpture Royal Family’s Millenium Dragon Formation and noticed something very special about the super-formation’s base, it actually seems to be the ice sculpture guardians scattered across the capital.”

“Normally, magic formation bases are fixed, but the base of the Millenium Dragon Formation is mobile.”

“However, the ice sculpture guardians the royal family have accumulated over the millennium are mainly made from the ice lake’s ice chunks, and barely anything else. Based on their origins, they are indeed an integrated whole with the ice lake, and it is feasible to use them as a formation base.”

“I also don’t know which magician had such an exquisite design that figured out this marvelous magic formation.

“It seems only such a design has the ability to allow the full strength of this formation to temporarily mold the millennia ice into a Millenium Dragon Ice Sculpture Puppet.”

“When that happens, testing the capital’s ice sculpture guardians will also increase our understanding of the Millenium Dragon Formation. You two will take my place in this matter.”

Jian Gui and Cang Xu glanced at each other, both of them immediately expressed that they would aid Lord Teacher Si Ling; it was their honor.

Jian Gui inquired on how he should do it, what kind of ice sculpture guardian to target and steal, and what channel he should use to return to the alchemy after the theft.

Teacher Si Ling replied, “You don’t need to return, wait in the capital.”

By the time he penetrated deep into the millennium ice layer, there was a risk of being discovered. If he was discovered, the Ice Sculpture Royal Family might follow the vine to get the melon and find their way to this alchemy laboratory.

Therefore, if the two remained here, they wouldn’t be safe.

Teacher Si Ling t hen said, “Our Undead Orthodoxy doesn’t just have one alchemy laboratory in the capital.”

“Use these two addresses to find the other laboratories.”

“There is no need to take risks, go grab an ice sculpture guardian. The current Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest is underway currently, and it just so happened that the exam question wants master alchemists to team up and work together to refine an alchemy puppet that can defeat a silver level ice sculpture guardian.”

“During this operation, you will swap identities to that of former year Warm Snow Cup competitors. According to the Warm Snow Cup rules, you can join this competition and participate in the second exam.”

“Thus, you will be able to openly study an ice sculpture guardian.”

Jian Gui carefully listened and committed Teacher Si Ling’s words to memory.

Cang Xu was secretly overjoyed, “Based on this plan, wouldn’t I be able to get in contact with Zi Di?”

These days, although he had been residing in this alchemy laboratory, information was not inaccessible, he could get it through the Undead Orthodoxy’s means and heard a lot of news in real time. It naturally included the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ Magician Yao Ma (Zi Di) participating in this year’s Warm Snow Cup and that she performed excellently in the first exam.

After Teacher Si Ling briefed them on their mission, he gave Cang Xu and Jian Gui disguise magic and also time to tidy up their stuff and adjust their state.

After some time, the two used a teleportation arrangement to leave.

After Teacher Si Ling sent the two off, he modified much of the alchemy laboratory’s data, then adjusted the alchemy laboratory to an inoperative state, then he snuck away and infiltrated into the ice lake.

As a result, just as Teacher Si Ling snuck close to the millennium ice layer, he triggered an alarm.

The Millenium Dragon Super-formation immediately erupted with golden flowing light.

Teacher Si Ling hastily went into hiding. More scared than hurt, he successfully concealed himself, his heart nipped with chill, aware of the activation level of the Millenium Dragon Formation.

The deeper he got, the greater the detection and defensive capabilities of the Millenium Dragon Formation.

Gold light surged, and wherever it went, ice shattered into flakes, mixing into the kilotons of ice lake water.

This kind of large activity spread out from the millennium ice layer, and in a hidden alchemy laboratory, Yuan Ci too observed it.

“More of the royal family’s super-formation is activating.”

“I can’t stay here any longer!”

“I must open the Wisteria Cache as soon as possible.”

Yuan Ci clenched his teeth, feeling immense pressure.

Of everyone, only Yuan Ci knew where the Wisteria Cache was hidden in the Ten Millenia Ice Lake. But knowing where it was wasn’t enough, five level zero wisteria secret orders were needed too.

Yuan Ci calculated, “I have one, Jiu Jin has one, Gui Teng has one, Shi Liu has one, and Cong Mang has one, that’s all five.”

“After Bing Yang lost, Shi Liu smoothly became Ice Prison’s recommended duelist.”

“Why hasn’t Cong Mang made landfall yet?”

Yuan Ci had received the latest information, which showed that Cong Mang led his pirate group against the First Navy detachment, defeating it, and he had also captured the detachment’s commander Bei Jia alive, he then arrogantly sent the First Navy Fleet a ransom message.

Yuan Ci fumed with rage. He was unable to understand why Cong Mang was sojourning on the ocean, when obviously, the Wisteria Cache was more important.

At that point in time, when the royal family’s super-formation completely activated, the entire ice lake would be tightly monitored and wanting to acquire the Wisteria Cache then would be almost impossible!

Yuan Ci didn’t know Cong Mang was already in the Ice Sculpture Capital.

Zi Di had the equivalent of three level zero orders, Fei She had one, Jiu Jin had one, and Cong Mang had one, there were already six on hand.

But Zi Di at the moment wasn’t thinking about the Wisteria Cache.

On the one hand, she didn’t know about the super-formation, and on the other, she had more important business to rush to.

According to Elder Jiu Jin’s recommendation list, Zi Di set out to contact many master alchemists personally.

But of these master alchemists, some refused to meet, some said no to her face, and some asked for a high price.

One of them during conversation with Zi Di, exposed a flaw, and under close questioning, told a secret – the Li Jian Clan’s people had gone searching for him and offered a high price if he refused to work with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The situation Zi Di was worried about appeared.

The Li Jian Clan, the Jing Xiang Clan, and even the Alchemy Guild were all exerting themselves to “encircle and subdue” the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Despite having two tentative promises to work with Zi Di, she was very worried and hesitated, unable to sign a magic contract.

What if these people who promised were actually bought off by the three powers? Then wouldn’t she be showing the wolf into the house?

The more people Zi Di saw, the more dispirited she became, and she began to doubt if her operation was worthwhile or not.

“If I can’t use a special method to truly break my predicament, it is unjustified to talk with more alchemists.”

As this notion got stronger, the bell of a door Zi Di rang swung up.

“You finally came, do you know how long I’ve been waiting for you?” Cang Xu let out a breath and secretly transmitted.

“You are?!” Zi Di was astonished.

Cang Xu had been disguised by Teacher Si Ling, and his appearance had changed a lot, Zi Di didn’t recognize him at all.

After confirming his identity, Zi Di was pleasantly surprised, she never thought that under these circumstances, she would converge with Cang Xu.

Cang Xu sighed, “I didn’t think that after Teacher Si Ling’s many teleportations, our final destination was to be the Ice Sculpture Capital.”

“I heard you were targeted and encountered a predicament.”

“I originally planned on looking for you, but I was worried it would trigger suspicion; therefore, I chose to wait for you to pay me a visit.”

“It just so happens that Teacher Si Ling has given me a mission, let’s work together on this.”

Unexpectedly, Zi Di wanted to say yet, but hesitated, “If I’m remembering, your alchemy attainment is worse than mine?”

Zi Di was looking for expert help to team up with.

Cang Xu smiled, “Miss Zi Di, don’t look down on me. For this exam question, I can contribute far more than most of the competition.”

Zi Di suddenly understood somewhat, “Are you thinking of drawing help from the undead alchemy methods?”

Cang Xu replied, “For now, don’t speak of that. I want to meet the Lord Commander first, I have to speak with him, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is in a terrible situation.”

“It isn’t that bad?” When the dragonman youngster was brought up, Zi Di couldn’t help but be proud, “After the Lord Commander returned, our situation has been overturned, sky and earth has been twisted on its head!”

“Now we are actively cutting ties with the Li Jian Clan, the Lord Commander is strong enough to protect the mount potion benefits.”

Cang Xu shook his head, deeply sighing, “In short, let me see the Lord Commander before further discussion.”

Chapter 931: Admonishment About Peril

Cang Xu entered into the Divine Domain Descent spell summoning space and converged with the dragonman youngster.

“Cang Xu, I didn’t expect to meet back together so quickly, and in the Ice Sculpture Capital!” The dragonman youngster was both startled and delighted, he broke out into a chortle and patted Cang Xu’s shoulders, sincerely glad to see him again.

“Lord Commander.” Cang Xu bowed deferentially to the dragonman youngster, as if he was a steward attending to his feudal lord.

His tone was steady, “I also didn’t foresee that this was how we would meet again. Teacher Si Ling must have come here with an immense conspiracy.”

At the time, when Cang Xu followed Teacher Si Ling, he planned on proactively approaching the Undead Orthodoxy and actively developed a relationship with them, it was undoubtedly beneficial to the survivors.

Zi Di interjected, “Cang Xu has a mission, he wants to participate in the Warm Snow Cup’s exam, he said he can help through necromancy methods to pass the second exam together.”

The dragonman youngster was inevitably worried, “Is that too much of a risk, if an undead aura is exposed…”

Cang Xu waved his hand, “Let’s not speak of this first, whether or not we can get through the second exam, is not the most important thing at the moment. I came to urgently meet you Commander because I am very worried about my corps’ situation.”

The dragonman youngster stared at him, not quite understanding what Cang Xu was saying.

Because since he returned, he wasn’t just sailing smoothly, he was making headway time and again.

He first deceived the Li Jian Clan, making a comeback for Zi Di and Zong Ge. Then in Snow Bird Harbor, he played with both sides to achieve complete control over the situation. Then he subdued Cong Mang, made arrangements for the big guy and the others, having them lying low and gathering pirates as external assistance. As for the Snow Bird Harbor branch, he also put into place proper plans, actively supporting military training and placing Hun Tong there as insurance.

He even defeated Mi Fang recently, and unexpectedly obtained Long Meng’s teachings, it could be said he was making fine progress through his methodical arrangements. But when he heard Cang Xu’s worries, it seemed there was some significant concealed danger?

The dragonman youngster knew Cang Xu’s abilities and carefully inquired about it at once.

Cang Xu shook his head and sighed, “Lord Commander, you shouldn’t have exposed your saint level qualifications.”

“Think about what the big guy experienced.”

“During the eating contest, the big guy showed signs of it, and Da Dou immediately kidnapped him. Tripleblade and the others tried to search and rescue him, then as Shuang Lian was nosing about, all powers were acting against the big guy. The big guy then was only iron level, and was greatly coveted by all”

“And now, your Lordship has exposed your saint level qualifications, that is the greatest source of risk!”

The dragonman youngster couldn’t speak for a moment.

Cang Xu sighed, sincerely admonishing him, “Lord Commander, you seem to underestimate the influence of saint levels too much, even if one is a saint level seed!”

“Perhaps it is because nowadays you have plenty of god level bloodlines and possess a god level aptitude. To you, you just exposed a bit of your talent, and since it isn’t the legend level, you should be safe and sound.”

“But reality isn’t like that.”

“A saint level is enormously impactful! In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom there are only three saint levels. Da Han, that saint level pirate, was able to throw the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s seaboard into complete confusion.”

“This is the world of the strong!”

“Even if you aren’t one now, with your aptitude, after growing, you are bound to be a key figure that affects the big picture. I would like to ask how the other saint levels might act?”

“The reason why the current situation is tranquil is mainly because we still have the pretense of arms merchants. But this skin that could intimidate other powers will only be effective temporarily.”

“The reason why they haven’t acted is because the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is facing Da Han. They don’t have the energy to attend to us for the time being.”

The dragonman youngster blushed with shame.

He bowed his head and told the truth. He had revealed a legend level aptitude.

Cang stared blankly.

The young man then informed him of the details, it was in the Snow Bird Harbor battle, his main goal was to awe and subdue Hun Tong.

Cang Xu was dumbfounded.

After a moment, he gave a long sigh, “Lord Commander, the risks here are too immense.”

“Since this has already happened, we have to strictly control the risks. We have to do our best to tie Hun Tong to our side, and monitor him at all times, where is he right now?”

The dragonman youngster replied, “Snow Bird Harbor.”

Cang Xu: …

The dragonman youngster then told him why he arranged things like this.

Cang Xu was dumbfounded again.

After a moment, he still encouraged and consoled the dragonman youngster, “Lord Commander, you can now analyze and exploit human nature, that is enormous progress.”

“You were just missing the big picture for the details this time.”

Zi Di couldn’t help but retort, “Isn’t Long Meng a saint level seed? Why isn’t he being targeted?”

Zi Di had never felt that the dragonman youngster exposing his saint level aptitude was the wrong decision. Seeing her beloved being criticized stirred her to subconsciously defend him out of love.

Cang Xu answered, “According to my analysis, Long Meng has a powerful backer. He has never admitted to possessing saint level qualifications, that is just the popular conjecture. He never publicly did what the commander did with his ‘vast amount of enhancement spells’, that was obviously overstepping the proper boundaries.”

“Generally speaking, saint level seeds are strictly defended by all forces. When a saint level is cultivated, they will undoubtedly break the equilibrium of a region.”

“However, it is also because of this that during the duel, Mi Fang judged wrong, he fought very conservatively, allowing the Commander to effortlessly win.”

Cang Xu looked at a magic image showing the duel.

After saying that, the old magician looked solemnly at Zi Di.

“Miss Zi Di, you shouldn’t be blind to the hidden dangers here. You came from an exceptional background and have a vast scope of view.”

“Don’t let love blind you.”

“In your subconsciousness, did you not hope to see your sweetheart display his splendor and ammas more accomplishments?”

“However, pushing himself into the limelight has instead harmed him.”

Zi Di’s heart shook greatly.

Cang Xu continued, “Now that the Lord Commander has publicly exposed a saint level disposition, targeting and ganging up will surely follow at his heels.”

“Before, we could depend on our fabricated arms dealer patron to cleverly intimidate many outsider forces. But now that the Lord Commander already represents a future saint level, what will all these powers think?”

“The Lord Commander’s name is famous nationwide, my corps’ magic medicine business is also flourishing, the corps is making triumphant progress and earning a fortune every day.”

“This ambitious and pushing stance clearly wants to mettle in the three saint level structure here, turning it into a contest between four.”

“Are you saying the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s three saint levels are going to watch and allow us to grow?”

“We should thank Da Han. The reason the three saint levels haven’t immediately acted and spied on my corps is because of the external pressure Da Han is producing.”

“But after this clash, when they are free to make a move, what will we experience?”

Cang Xu added, “This time, what’s the reason why Mian Li Zang could easily convince the Jing Xiang Clan to work together, along with being able to borrow the Alchemy Guild’s Hua Ni’s power to eliminate us from the Warm Snow Cup? It is precisely the immense pressure saint level potentials brings.”

“The three parties were once competitors, but they united as a result of this. However, this is only a small part of the response that awaits us in the future.”

Zi Di took a deep breath, her heart pervading with a chill.

She sank into self-blame, why hadn’t she been able to see this mistake, she never even considered it.

She reflected on herself, and found that love had muddled her brain, the dragonman youngster’s return allowed her to wallow in a feeling of happiness.

At the same time, she had become negligent from little victories, she wanted to forge further ahead and constantly gain more. Her attention was on the Warm Snow Cup, where she wanted to do better, or even the best.

Her actions were like her business, constant expansion.

Successes one after another without a hitch caused her to forget to pay attention to what was under her, and gradually overlook risks.

Now that Cang Xu had brought up this point to her, when she thought back, cold sweat appeared all over.

The dragonman youngster couldn’t bear to see Zi Di flagellate herself, so he defended, “Don’t blame her, this was what I advocated for.”

“Cang Xu, after you and I parted ways, you were unaware of what happened to me. During that time, something major occurred.”

At once, the dragonman youngster spoke of his experience, recounting Charming Blue’s divine oracle of the Dueling Godhead in detail to Cang Xu.

Cang Xu was startled, “The Dueling Godhead?!”

Chapter 932: Cang Xu’s Strategy

Cang Xu looked solemn, and in a rare instance, he put his hands behind his back and strolled about.

After circling about three times, he recovered from hearing this astonishing news.

He looked at the dragonman youngster, whose eyes wide open and staring back at him.

Cang Xu bowed his head, and after reflecting for a long time, he turned to face the dragonman youngster, “So that’s how it is, I see.”

“Lord Commander, I know you are very cautious, you shouldn’t be agitated to advance so prematurely.”

“I understand your scheme; you want to use all of your strength to seize the Dueling Godhead!”

“Hic.” The dragonman youngster scratched his chin in embarrassment.

Cang Xu then said, “Indeed, wanting to seize the Dueling Godhead means you have to gain the Godhead’s favor. And becoming a dueling champion is undoubtedly crucial to this. Even if you aren’t the ultimate winner, the greater your Lordship’s renown in the National Ceremony Grand Duel is, the greater your hopes in this endeavor.”

“This renown to some degree represents your faith and how much the dueling believers support you. There are a great deal of benefits in this.”

“This time, when you displayed a saint level aptitude, you were able to obtain more of the populace’s approval. You were not only able to relieve any doubts of your winning streak but also were able to more easily win esteem and respect.”

“The Dueling Godhead…I didn’t expect, I really didn’t expect that there would actually be such a significant treasure!”

Cang Xu suppressed his excitement and calmed down, “Lord Commander, regardless of whether what Charming Blue Goddess’ scheme is, since you have already decided to go all out in this and willing to brave the dangers, then this old man will naturally follow you and do his best to assist you.”

It was the dragonman youngster’s turn to sigh, he chuckled bitterly, not hiding anything from Cang Xu, “I wasn’t thinking of fighting for it with everything I have.”

“I only wanted to try.”

“But when I heard your analysis, Cang Xu, I came to a realization. Since the moment I exposed my saint level qualifications, I have already stepped on a path of no return, and I must walk this road at all costs.”

“Regardless of what is said, I am really happy to have your support.”

Cang Xu narrowed his eyes as he looked at the dragonman youngster, it contained plenty of frustration from the older generation and also sincere feelings for the dragonman youngster.

Memories flashed across his face, “In White Hair Trench, you saved me Lord Commander.”

“What more is there left to say?”

“Mystifying Monster Island was where we were fated to meet. If the beginning mirrors the end, then even if we are destroyed like Mystifying Monster Island, I will still follow you to the end.”

The tranquil tone contained Cang Xu’s resolve of one who had gone through myriad changes.

Zi Di looked at Cang Xu, then at the dragonman youngster, her heart incomparably happy.

She could sense Cang Xu’s sincerity. On the one hand, she was happy her sweetheart could get Cang Xu’s vow of loyalty and devotion. On the other hand, she also felt a closeness with Cang Xu when he truly became one of them.

The dragonman youngster hesitated, then said, “Thanks to your guidance, I now understand we are faced with immense risks.”

“I also want to seize the Dueling Godhead, but I am unclear as to who the enemy is.”

“I suspect the Ice Sculpture Family, they revere dueling activities, and vigorously promote it throughout the country, that is very suspicious. They might know the facts and might even be actively accumulating faith and fermenting the Dueling Godhead.”

Cang Xu smiled, and nodded without hesitation, “They know the truth, and are also a leading party in this.”

“If we want to seize the Dueling Godhead, we are bound to have to step across the gigantic obstacle that is the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“But it is likely they aren’t the most powerful of our enemies.”

“The Sheng Ming Empire is also our enemy.”

“Da Han is also very likely to become a stumbling block.”

“Teacher Si Ling has come here, and his true objective is not yet known. But why can’t it be the Dueling Godhead?”

“Since Charming Blue Goddess could give you a divine oracle Lord Commander, when the moment comes, as saint levels, could Zhou Zhang and that Snow Pear Octopus in the Ocean Mother Nest show up?”

“Calculating this in, as of now, our enemies are the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s three saint levels, the Sheng Ming Empire used Tan Mo’s warship to sneak in one saint level, the Undead Orthodoxy has a saint level, there is a pirate saint level, and there is Charming Blue Goddess’ saint levels too. If we include the saint level magic beast, there are already nine saint levels in play.”

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di: !!?

For a moment both were somewhat numb.

Don’t be fooled by the dragonman youngster’s immense progress, and his three gold level transcendent levels, when confronting a saint level, he was a baby.

What’s more, there wasn’t just one saint level, but nine!

Cang Xu seemed to pile it on, “The saint levels aren’t even the highest levels, the Millenium Dragon Formation is already built, and according to Teacher Si Ling’s investigation, this formation can create a Millenium Dragon Ice Sculpture with god level strength!”

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di couldn’t speak, they were caught up in silence together.

Zi Di especially didn’t dare to believe it somewhat, seeming to struggle as she asked, “I am a little curious, Cang Xu. Why are you sure the Ice Sculpture Royal Family knows about the Dueling Godhead and are also a leading figure in it?”

Cang Xu looked at her, “It is very clear, the duelists on Tranquil Hill Divine Domain are filled with large qualities of the royal family’s power!”

The dragonman youngster knew about Tranquil Hill through Lan Zao, and after exchanging information, Cang Xu also knew about this too.

Zi Di contemplated, “The duelists have Mei Lin, Bao Li Gen, and Gu Dong, who are Ice Sculpture Kingdom soldiers. But that doesn’t represent…”

Cang Xu interrupted her analysis, “It isn’t just those three. Teng Dong Lang the Axe Gang Leader, Jia Bing, and Lin all had a military background. They were discharged from service, but in reality, they were still the royal family’s people.”

“Oh, why do you say that?” Zi Di was puzzled.

Cang Xu lowered his voice, “One can see it based on the fact that they acted together, targeted our businesses, and raided our Snow Bird Harbor branch.”

Zi Di frowned, “Perhaps this is an alliance of camaraderie, and not necessarily the royal family’s will.”

Cang Xu shook his head, “This is an almost definitive fact.”

Zi Di’s doubts deepened, “What is the evidence?”

Cang Xu shook his head again, “I don’t have any evidence. I just obtained the key information from the Lord Commander. I know just as much as you do.”

“Although this is a mere conjecture, I am almost certain this is the truth!”

Zi Di was confused, and so too was the dragonman youngster.

Seeing the two young people’s expressions, Cang Xu knew he had to guide them properly. He expediently asked, “Do you know about the armor prohibition?”

The armor prohibition was one of Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s ten prohibitions.

The prohibition stipulated that the Ice Sculpture Kingdom made no more than 27 pieces of gold level armor. When using gold level armor in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, one had to inform the government in advance, even during the National Ceremony Grand Duel, this prohibition had to be abided by.

This prohibition was carried out to an extreme degree.

Cang Xu inquired, “Miss Zi Di, what do you believe is the reason the Ice Sculpture kingdom put into place this prohibition?”

Zi Di reacted instantly, “Armor is more threatening than bows and other weapons, this is how the Ice Sculpture Kingdom safeguards public security and maintains its rule.”

Cang Xu shook his head, rejecting it, “The biggest assurance to maintaining public security and rule is the king with saint level strength. So long as this strength exists, then how can they be afraid of gold level armor? Thus, even if there is an armor prohibition, it should restrict saint level armor, why does it restrict gold level armor? That is absolutely not a normal national policy.”

Cang Xu had served as a noble steward for many years, due to his lowly bloodline, he had attained the limits of his individual strength. His many years and energy was used to study all kinds of historical records and delve into all sorts of information.

He not only accumulated astonishing erudition, but as he read extensively, he put their lessons into practice as he worked as a steward and cultivated wisdom that clearly understood how society operated.

Although Zi Di ran a merchant alliance, she was too young. As she matured, most of her energy was put into attending college and cultivation. After her father died, she was determined to contend with experts and large powers, messing with danger again and again to avenge him. Thus, in this aspect of accomplishment, she was far from Cang Xu.

The dragonman youngster also thought back, he also gained first-hand knowledge of how saint levels fought, confirming that even if one was draped in gold level armor, it was very difficult to vie with a saint level. From Da Han’s powerful suppression of the Second Navy Fleet, one could also see the extreme dominance a saint level had in the face of gold levels.

Cang Xu replied, “This national policy of armor prohibition, this limiting of gold level armor production in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, it is quite harmful to it.”

“Ensuring public security and maintaining rule, that is just an explanation to the masses.”

Zi Di felt baffled, and she asked, “Since that is so, why did the Ice Sculpture Kingdom implement it?”

Cang Xu didn’t answer, instead he looked at the dragonman youngster encouragingly, “I’ll give you two a small hint, the armor prohibition policy was issued and put into practice 40 years ago. What do you two think happened at that point in time?”

The dragonman youngster racked his brains and found nothing.

But Zi Di was struck by the light, and she blurted out, “Was it that great rebellion?”

“That’s right.” Cang Xu nodded, “40 years ago, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom just replaced its king. An internal rebellion was triggered, a large one indeed, and although the king successfully suppressed it, the internal damages were quite severe.”

“After cleaning out the rebels and securing his throne, the king issued the armor prohibition.”

“Looking on the face of it, it was to prevent another internal rebellion and stabilize public security, but that’s not actually what it was for.”

The dragonman youngster suddenly perceived it, “Oh, I’ve seen this information before.”

When the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps arrived at Snow Bird Harbor, they bought a lot of information to increase the survivors’ understanding of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The great rebellion 40 years ago was included in it and was found to be unremarkable. So, the dragonman youngster never cared about it.

Zi Di looked at the dragonman youngster, smiled, and relieved her beloved’s anxieties, “If I didn’t travel to Blood Kin City, I wouldn’t have thought of the rebellion 40 years ago either.”

She explained, “After me and Zong Ge left Snow Bird Harbor with our group, our first stop was Blood Kin City.”

“There, we and the Axe Gang Leader negotiated. Afterwards, when we left the city, there was a teleportation problem, then Zong Ge, by means of the Pearl Bubbles, won a 3 v 1, killing the Axe Gang Leader and the others, therefore it left a deep impression on me.”

Chapter 933: Teaching Politics

“After Zong Ge was struck by the Blood Vengeance Axe, to resolve that issue, I dedicated myself to gathering a lot of information for a time to understand the history of the Blood Vengeance Axe.”

Zi Di continued to narrate, “Before the great rebellion, Blood Kin City was called Snow Fall City.”

“Snow Fall City’s lord was a member of the royal family, and when the rebellion became enough to sweep in, he feared death upon defeat and disgracefully surrendered to the rebel army.”

“When the Ice Sculpture King put down the revolt, he recaptured Snow Fall City. Originally, he wanted to punish Snow Fall City Lord, but the king’s mother pleaded for leniency, after all, he was of the royal bloodline.”

“The king still allowed Snow Fall City Lord to keep his position; however, he renamed the city to Blood Kin City.”

After Snow Fall City Lord learned of the new name, he couldn’t withstand his guilt and committed suicide that same night.”

“From that day onwards, the Blood Vengeance Axe was lost to outsiders, wandering between many owners before finally ending up in the hands of a snow elf expert, he relied on this axe to open up territory in this city and establish the Axe Gang.”

Cang Xu quietly listened, waiting until Zi Di’s speech was finished, before suddenly saying, “Miss Zi Di, before arriving in Blood Kin City, you were ambushed halfway by a mysterious gold level, likely to be the Axe Gang Leader.”

Zi Di nodded, “Although we haven’t ascertained the truth of this to this day, after Zong Ge killed the Axe Gang Leader, our investigation and analysis felt that the probability was very high.”

“As for why the Axe Gang Leader attacked us, its probably due to his personal friendship with Teng Dong Lang.”

“The reason why he recruited Jia Bing and Lin was for absolute safety. According to the information we gathered, this conformed with how the Axe Gang Leader customarily used his soldiers.”

“Luckily we had the Pearl Bubbles, otherwise…” When she said that, Zi Di revealed a trace of lingering fear.

Cang Xu said in passing, “Teng Dong Lang was a gang boss, Lin was Ice Spear City’s largest gang leader, and the Axe Gang Leader goes without saying. You don’t think that these three having the same role is too coincidental?”

Zi Di replied, “What else? Transcendents from the military don’t have any capacity for production, isn’t relying on their strength to earn a normal living?”

Cang Xu shook his head, “If that was the case, then becoming mercenaries would be more rational, why did they all become gangsters? The cultural differences between gangs and the army are vast.”

“I believe that their discharge from military service was a camouflage, an arrangement of the royal family.”

“It is simple to verify, check and add up the amount of people in it. I think there should be many such cases of this. They should have started 40 years ago and become more inundated as time passed.”

The dragonman youngster pondered, “IF that is true, isn’t it a bit strange?”

“These large quantities of discharged soldiers, why didn’t they become city guards and instead became systemically problematic criminal gangs?”

Zi Di thought of something, “By doing this, they can cut down on military spending.”

“Gangs have income. At the same time, veteran organized powers can muscle into the space the original gangs occupied, and it can also be considered to be a covert type of increased law and order.”

Cang Xu added, “This is not the Ice Sculpture Royal Family’s true goal. I personally believe the royal family is secretly expanding their armed forces, relying on the pretense of gang syndicates to clandestinely maintain troops. When their time of need comes, the king will issue a call to action, and large numbers of gangs are bound to proactively answer with generous enlistments.”

“The reason why the Ice Sculpture Royal Family did this should be to vie with the powerful Sheng Ming Empire.”

The dragonman youngster was startled, “Wait a moment, did you say vie with the Sheng Ming Empire?”

Zi Di had a weird expression, “The Ice Sculpture Kingdom and the Sheng Ming Empire have a good relationship. Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming didn’t even hesitate to send his Thirteenth Prince to serve as a hostage prince in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Where did this vying with the Sheng Ming Empire come from?”

Cang Xu had the look of one who saw beyond the facade of the world, one more emphasizing, “That’s right, the royal family is doing this to vie with the Sheng Ming Empire.”

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di looked at each other in dismay.

The two felt that Cang Xu was speaking more and more out of place.

Cang Xu then said, “The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is mainly composed of snow elves, what do you two feel about this country’s popular customs?”

The dragonman youngster said, “Snow elves are inherently arrogant, and duels are popular domestically, the popular customs are quite plucky.”

Cang Xu nodded, “With such popular customs, how will they submit to the Sheng Ming Empire? For a long time now, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom has always maintained sovereignty.”

“When Ice Sculpture Kingdom was first established, the snow elves were a unified whole, they defeated the other races led by the barbarian race and completely dominated Ice Sculpture Island.”

“After the country’s founding, they cleaned out the borders and often sent troops on expeditions.”

“Afterwards throughout history, they repeatedly defeated powerful invaders and counterattacked them back until they destroyed their enemy’s base before stopping.”

“This country’s military ethics is abundant.”

“The attitude this country has means it will absolutely never surrender easily. Despite the Sheng Ming Empire’s power, it can’t yet reduce the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to a vassal state.”

“Our emperor clearly understands this, therefore, he sent the Thirteenth Prince to serve as hostage and by means of his role as a great power, formed an inseparably close relationship between both countries.”

Zi Di interjected, “The masses all say that the Heavenly Emperor deliberately treated the Thirteenth Prince as a hostage, but in reality, it was to prepare for and provide cover for the Wilderness Continent invasion.”

Cang Xu nodded again, “Seeing through the chaotic situation before us, we can research it from a higher angle and look down at the structures from a higher vantage point.”

He deeply sighed, using some a somewhat raging intonation to say, “Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming is a man of grand ambitions, the unification of the Sheng Ming Continent did not stop his journey, and he has mobilized the entire empire’s strength to attack the Wilderness Continent, that is the theme of our era.”

“To attack the Wilderness Continent, the imperial army has to cross the ocean and has to establish stable supply routes.”

“Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming began his arrangements long ago. Sending the Thirteenth Prince as a hostage to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was one of them. The second is the imperial pantheon targeting ocean gods, suppressing and ganging up on them, Charming Blue Goddess was a casualty of this.”

“However, when the imperial army was establishing bridgeheads on the Wilderness Continent, the Pirate Throne rose at this subtle juncture and the pirates of the Main Plane immediately became incomparably rampant. What do you think of this?”

“Yes, on the Main Plane, the Sheng Ming Empire is entirely worthy of being the greatest force, it is the sole continental power. The rest of the powers absolutely don’t want to see the empire’s attack succeed and annex another continent.”

“Therefore, on the face of it, the empire is the conqueror and the beastmen resist, it is a contest between two continents, the Sheng Ming Empire vs the Beastmen Clan Alliance. In reality, it is the Sheng Ming Empire confronting the pressure of the entire world. Beside the beastmen clans, the other powers haven’t openly gone to war, that’s all.”

“If the Heavenly Emperor wants to conquer, first of all, he has to ensure his ocean transportation lines are secure. Relying on teleportation is not economical, the empire would go bankrupt halfway through the process.”

“If Divine Domain Descent was serviceable, then why haven’t numerous gods put it into common usage since the ancient days? After endless development, the Divine Domain Descent spell should have grown into a conventional method of transportation. But in fact, this isn’t the case. For the same reason, this proves this applies to other transportation methods.”

“Therefore, the good old way of sea transportation is the only solution.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, expressing his approval while secretly speculating, “Divine Domain Descent really is risky. Charming Blue Goddess was forced into this dangerous situation. Under the circumstances, she deployed the saint beast in Ocean Mother Nest to protect it. Apparently, since Charming Blue Goddess is asleep, my repeated invocations of Divine Domain Descent was authorized by the Godhead. That’s how I was able to exploit it!”

Cang Xu continued, “In order to create maritime transportation lines, the Sheng Ming Empire began to exert itself and issue a good deal of policies. Our journey through Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island are embodiments of those policies. Snake Rat Island’s Lord Tan Qiu and Two Eyes Island’s Hun Tong both received the empire’s nobleman incentive policies. They conquered an unremarkable island, paving the way for the empire’s war machine through blood and flesh, developing a vast number of maritime station points. These station points linked together can support a few main maritime transportation routes.”

“Of course, based on what we know right now, Snake Rate Island can only provide sideline support, while Two Eyes Island’s position was pretty good, it seems it was careening towards becoming one of the hubs of a transportation line.

“If Two Eyes Island is like that, what about Ice Sculpture Island?”

The dragonman youngster’s and Zi Di’s heart shook in tandem.

The two looked at each other, equally seeing the other’s sudden realization.

They began to understand the reason and essence of the confrontation between the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and the Sheng Ming Empire.

Cang Xu explained, “Ice Sculpture Island is different from the vast majority of islands, it has a considerably vast surface area, and a very long history. The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is entrenched in the ocean, and nowadays has three great saint level combat powers, and once had a legend level too, its foundation is quite deep. Their attitude and factions will undoubtedly have an enormous influence on the maritime transportation lines.”

“Standing on the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s point of view, their citizens naturally crave for freedom and long to maintain their autonomy.”

“Standing on the Sheng Ming Empire’s point of view, annexing the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is the best and most secure outcome. But the empire doesn’t have a good way of taking direct action. On the one hand, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom has a strong military, top quality alchemy technology, and a plucky character. On the other hand, the snow elves have two big external connections. One is to the Frost Continent, where the snow elves originated from. The other is to the Life Continent, that place is made of elven kingdoms large and small.”

“If the Sheng Ming Empire recklessly attacks, it might lead to these two continents drastically reacting, so the empire has to tread cautiously.”

“Therefore, we saw a good deal of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency like Snow Bird Harbor City Lord moving around in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. From just the secret spies like Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, code name [Emancipator], and also [Usurper], one can understand they want to overthrow the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s current regime! Their rapacious designs are abundantly clear.”

The young man nodded repeatedly. He was already secretly trying to track down who else belonged to the Imperial Secret Spy Agency. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had already been exposed, and [Usurper] was certain to be greater than he. The dragonman youngster initially suspected the Li Jian Clan. This clan controlled the Second Navy Fleet, and during the naval battle with Da Han’s pirate alliance, they put their own safety before principal, it really was suspicious.

Chapter 934: The Group’s Strategist

Cang Xu led the discussion between the three, changing the direction of it, “Now let us look back at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom rebellion 40 years ago.

“First, this rebellion started at a subtle opportune moment, when the current king took over.”

“On the face of it, it was indeed a power struggle between members of the royal family.”

“However, the current king has saint level strength, while his competition was only gold level, yet they never shrank back. For what reason did a gold level think they were confident in taking on a saint level?”

“I personally believe that this rebellion should have received imperial assistance, that the Imperial Secret Spy Agency played a major role in it.”

“Snow Fall City Lord was a member of the royal family, and regardless of whether he had terrible luck of being coerced into it, he was still a member of the rebellion. In short, he ultimately surrendered to the rebel army.”

“After the king put down the revolt, he changed its name to Blood Kin City. Despite approving his mother’s plea for leniency and sparing Snow Fall City Lord’s life, in reality, he died soon after. The outside world claims that after he learned of the city’s new name, he could endure his guilt and committed suicide. But is that really true?”

“It is possible that the king’s mother’s plea for leniency was just abnormal political grandstanding. Wasn’t the king sure to settle accounts, so he sent an assassin?”

“It can also be seen that the current king didn’t let off any royal family members who joined the rebellion.”

“The winner writes the history books.”

Zi Di’s eyes shined, and she said straightforwardly, “Makes sense. The Blood Vengeance Axe was once Snow Fall City Lord’s weapon. Afterwards, it fell into the hands of a snow elf expert. And he relied on this axe to grab territory and establish the Axe Gang! If the Axe Gang Leader was a power aided by the royal family, then the logic to this is very strong.”

Zi Di continued, “During the rebellion, there might have been secret clashes between saint levels. Legend level involvement is low, after all, the responsibility is too large. Ice Sculpture’s strength was greatly injured, and it allowed the current Ice Sculpture King to thoroughly recognize reality.”

The dragonman youngster said, “It seems this way, although the king assumed the throne, he sensed an imperial conspiracy, but the enemy was strong while he was weak, so he had no choice but to restrain his emotions. Because of the rebellion, he no longer trusted other members of the royal family and began to actively plant veterans all over the country disguised as criminal syndicates to reinforce his control over the nation.”

Cang Xu added, “The rebellion 40 years ago was a fierce contest between the Sheng Ming Empire and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“The result shown to the outside was disastrous losses, with seven or eight of the ten members of the royal family dead.”

“It goes without saying, I fear these secret confrontations are more treacherous and ruthless, all sacrifices are silent and nameless.”

“Although the king won this contest in the end, relying on his native advantage to defeat these outsiders, the empire wasn’t fully beaten.”

“At least the Imperial Secret Spy Agency hasn’t been completely eliminated just yet, [Usurper] is still active and developed many subordinates afterwards. We know one of them is Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.”

“The royal family was weakened, so the armor prohibition and troop restrictions were issued at that time.”

“The armor prohibition restricts Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s armament reserves, and the army restrictions prevent kingdom troop stockpiles. This is a harmful national policy.”

“I consulted the data and saw there were many proposals at the time. The Thirteenth Prince was very active, and nobles large and small to protect their benefits also must have relied on the Sheng Ming Empire secretly and proposed these national policies, displaying their power during this process.”

The dragonman youngster sighed, “The armor prohibition is publicly to protect public security and safeguard the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s rule. The army restrictions could also be interpreted as the benevolent king, unable to bear war, desired the entire country’s recovery, while simultaneously needing to placate the panic-stricken and restless noble powers as a newly throned king, so this policy was put into place. The historical truth is more often than not the opposite of what the official explanation and popular understanding is.”

Zi Di suddenly said, “Although the Thirteenth Prince is a hostage, he has great prestige. When the throne was vacant, many Ice Sculpture citizens loved and respected him enough to endorse his ascension to the throne.”

“This was not a small number of people, at the time, there was a voice throughout the kingdom that hoped the Thirteenth Prince succeeded the throne.”

“Unfortunately, the Thirteenth Prince is Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming’s son, and based on his bloodline and legal principle, he is unqualified to succeed the throne.”

“Thinking about it now, this should be a contest the Ice Sculpture Kingdom defeated the Sheng Ming Empire in.”

“Although there wasn’t a flash of knives and swords, the level of danger in it causes the body to tremble.”

With Cang Xu’s guidance, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di saw new details in old information. Their understanding was elevated to new heights.

Zi Di suddenly asked, “The Dueling Godhead accumulation, is that a grand chess piece the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is using to withstand the Sheng Ming Empire?”

Cang Xu muttered, “I am more inclined to believe that this is the Ice Sculpture Royal Family strengthening themselves. After all, the popularization of duels and the gathering of this Godhead has been going on for a long time.”

“This is the world of the strong.”

“Every organization’s scale, each regime’s prosperity and decline, is founded on individual transcendents.”

“The problem lies with bloodlines and if a transcendent individual can continue to advance in rank.”

“Regardless of how, self-improvement is never wrong, but the issue that arises is normally potential is finite, bloodlines reach their limits, and the road of self-improvement is severed.”

Cang Xu lamented, “The vastness of the Dueling God project is astonishing. When I learned of the Millenium Dragon Formation, I was utterly amazed, and upon learning of the Dueling Godhead, I have a whole new level of respect for the Ice Sculpture Royal Family. Successive generations of kings were really extraordinary, it’s no wonder they were able to govern the Ice Sculpture Kingdom into becoming a top ranking power in the Main Plane.”

“Based on the country’s territory surface area, resource quality, and other metrics, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is a mere island nation, its lands cannot compare to the Sheng Ming Empire or many other large kingdoms. The Ice Sculpture Kingdom only has one super-resource point, the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.”

“But its king and citizens are really outstanding, it is they who made the Ice Sculpture Kingdom glorious, they who built this nation into being rich and powerful.”

The dragonman youngster sank into silence. His prior suspicions were correct. The Ice Sculpture Kingdom actively promoted duels for a reason, this was no short term project, but one that lasted for at least a thousand years!

Through Cang Xu’s guidance and analysis, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di had a better understanding of the situation.

The two knew not to be fooled by how numerous and disorderly it looked from the outside, it was essentially the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and the Sheng Ming Empire confronting and contesting with each other.

They also realized that despite the lack of evidence, Cang Xu was convinced that the Ice Sculpture Royal Family knew about the Dueling Godhead. Furthermore, within the ranks of the duelists, there was a lot of Ice Sculpture Kingdom forces.

This was wisdom!

Even without direct evidence, one could deduce from other facts and excavate the truth from history’s dense fog, seeing through all kinds of disputes and chaos to find the underlying structure of the current political situation.

The dragonman youngster pondered, “Since the Ice Sculpture Royal Family is leading the Godhead project, then did the Sheng Ming Empire detect it? Who is [Usurper]? If we can track him down, we can learn the answer.”

Cang Xu analyzed, “From the current information, imperial detection is unlikely.”

“Lan Zao’s situation proves that the Imperial Secret Spy Agency’s missions into Tranquil Hill have failed repeatedly and have only made some progress for the first time ever. But Lan Zao and the others are trapped in the Dueling Divine Domain, and I fear it is very difficult to bring back information.”

“From the duelists’ activities we can see the Imperial Secret Spy Agency don’t know who they are. Otherwise, these people are all obstructions to their capture of the Dueling Godhead, how could they not act against them? Or at minimum, weakening the duelists will impair the royal family’s power.”

“Judging by the duelists’ backgrounds, the Ice Sculpture Royal Family still possesses the native advantage, leading the Imperial Secret Spy Agency by a wide margin.”

“However, we cannot underestimate the empire.”

“Firstly, we know there is a saint level shield guardian, as well as a gold level clergyman of mysterious faith that secretly boarded Tan Mo’s warship, their current whereabouts is unknown, they probably got into the island already.”

“Secondly, there have been rumors the Thirteenth Prince is returning with Qi Ci Lang accompanying him. Furthermore, it is rumored heavily that the latter wants to participate in this year’s National Ceremony Grand Duel.

“These should be measures the Sheng Ming Empire has taken to intervene. We also need to calculate in the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, I have a feeling that [Usurper], their leader, has quite the remarkable identity.”

“When Snow Bird Harbor’s magician tower was blown up, and pirates were liable to strike at any moment, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord didn’t actually contact [Usurper] whilst under this immense pressure, that instead proves indirectly that [Usurper] plays a critical central role. If we can truly ascertain this person’s identity, we will have a very clear understanding of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency’s power here.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the Sheng Ming Empire…” The dragonman youngster forced a smile, feeling the enormous pressure.

He was a mere gold level, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps compared to these two juggernauts was like a small boat in a storm. If they were somewhat inattentive, they would be rushed to dregs and torn asunder.

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but ask, “Was it a bit selfish of me to expose my saint level qualifications and fully throw myself into contesting the Dueling Godhead before I elucidated the intelligence about our enemies?”

“The current situation is this: in the worst case, don’t speak of losing the contest, you might also implicate your comrades too.”

“Our original plan was redemption. We wanted to exchange the divine artifacts and other gains with the imperial higherups for our lives and freedom.”

“We wanted to seek survival, but now we want to brave deadly risks to seize a Godhead, isn’t that putting the cart before the horse?”

Previously, the dragonman youngster was too unaware of the situation, and now that he had gotten Cang Xu’s advice, he finally had a comprehensive and clear understanding at long last.

The enemies were great powers, and the dragonman youngster felt he was tiny in comparison, causing him to sink into rare hesitation.

Cang Xu weighed up his words and observed his expressions, immediately realizing what the dragonman youngster’s mentality was.

He smiled, after exposing the truth of the current political situation, he began to encourage the dragonman youngster, “Lord Commander, you are proactive, fighting at all costs for the Dueling Godhead was absolutely the right decision.”

“Don’t lose heart, because there is a chance we might succeed.”

“Even if we fail and die, what does that matter?”

“I am delighted to follow you, and I believe Miss Zi Di also has enough courage to take this risk with you together.”

Chapter 935: Seizing the Godhead for Righteousness

From the ghost girl’s blindness to the risks exposing saint level talent brought, Cang Xu completely recognized the girl’s nature – grand ambitions and initiative!

She longed for progress too much, thirsted for success too much. She was never satisfied and was incomparably greedy for success and never felt like she had enough.

As expected, his words evoked Zi Di’s innermost endorsement.

“That’s right, Lord Commander, even if you fail, and ultimately die, I am willing to follow you.” The girl looked into the young man’s eyes with fiery radiance.

She continued to declare more heartfelt words, “I am becoming more dissatisfied with the original redemption plan.”

“We painstakingly survived Mystifying Monster Island, experienced untold dangers and difficulties, and ultimately still suffered Priest Jia Sha’s plot against us, and we had to strive to survive it all.”

“We had goodwill; it was Jia Sha and the Flame Dragon King who didn’t want us alive!”

“What’s wrong with wanting to keep on living?”

“Like what the Lord Commander said during the Snake Rat Island naval battle, we are righteous, and continued survival is the greatest justice.”

“We deserve all of these gains, including the divine artifacts. Why do we have to give to others?”

“Don’t tell me you aren’t lamenting this, Commander? At the very least I utterly despise parting with them!”

“My father died, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was annexed by the Seventh Prince’s leadership, the Sheng Ming Empire cannot avoid blame. These nobles, to some extent, are stained by my father’s blood. Us capitulating to them is only because we are weak at the moment.”

Unswerving determination spread across Zi Di’s face, “If the redemption plan is executed, we will only reach a temporary compromise and obtain time and opportunities to grow. With the Bloodcore, what are we afraid of? In this brief time, we have already built the Bloodline Purification formation, and my corporeal body has already attained a gold level bloodline. Commander, you already have a god level bloodline, and the big guy’s bloodline is at least legend level.”

“What are we afraid of?!”

“So long as we have enough time and resources to grow, we can take back much more, we should receive the profits that belong to us!”

“If we control the Dueling Godhead, your Lordship will become at minimum a demigod.”

“A demigod!”

“We will have Tranquil Hill, the Dueling Divine Domain, and with your Lordship’s divine spells, even if we die, you can resurrect us!”

“This enormous benefit is sufficient to struggle with all of our strength this time, even if we have to gamble everything!”

“All of life is a gamble, and many high level gambling tables require exceptional golden opportunities to even participate in. Before us is that kind of opportunity! If we miss it, I will regret it for my entire life!!”

At that moment, Zi Di’s gaze was luminous, exposing her aspirations to her beloved.

The dragonman youngster stared into his shining and dazzling eyes, his memory seeming to return to Mystifying Monster Island.

Yes, this was Zi Di, this was the girl he knew.

She never changed, and to create the shipwreck of the Hog’s Kiss, one needed to have courage and a gambling nature in spades. To have dealings with War Merchant, and scheme against her fiancé Zhen Jin, this girl never lacked ambition.

At this moment, the ghost girl seemed to be aflame, and her threatening gaze caused the dragonman youngster to subconsciously advert his eyes.

The young man had to concede that Zi Di’s speech reached the bottom of his heart.

Was he really willing to hand over the divine artifacts for a chance to obtain the empire’s redemption?

Before, his feelings were not clear.

But now, he controlled the [Mermaid Fairy Tale], using this divine artifact again and again with beautiful results! He even deceived both the Main Earth Elemental God and Teacher Si Ling!

The divine artifact was extremely effective.

He could infer that the divine artifact, the [Green Jade Gold Coffin] could also be of use.

To say nothing of the Bloodcore, it was the core of the core, its value far exceeding the divine artifacts!

Could they give away these useful divine artifacts?

“Perhaps the reason why I want to fight for the Dueling Godhead with all I have, the reason I have such a strong longing for it, is that I hope I can keep more of my gains?”

“However, why do I feel something is a bit off?” The dragonman youngster had a strange feeling.

The young man thought, “During the Snake Rat Island naval battle, I really did give a speech. But it seems like Zi Di misinterpreted my idea, and is excessively bold and confident that justice is on our side?”

Zi Di’s initiative spirit was too exuberant, and it caused the dragonman youngster to feel their side had become a wicked gang.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ core consisted of three people. Zi Di was a vengeful ghost girl, Cang Xu was a necromancer proficient in head changing spells, and when the young man looked at himself again, his current dragonman form was mainly reliant on the famously vicious and irritable red dragon bloodline. His strongest form, the fishman form, was a cleric backed by the deception goddess Charming Blue.

From the Sheng Ming Empire’s point of view, Charming Blue Goddess was the god of a different race, and one the Sheng Ming Empire’s gods had ganged up on and pursued, definitely making her an evil goddess in their eyes.

What went wrong, this portrayal is a little fishy.

Cang Xu was constantly watching the dragonman youngster and taking his measure, saying timely, “Lord Commander, there is now time to hesitate.”

“Although we escaped Mystifying Monster Island, our situation has never changed.”

“We bear immense pressure to survive, and as we staggered along our journey, we were often forced into the path of despair, yet we still did everything we could to struggle onwards.”

“Many times, we are forced into tight spots and can only do so much.”

“Lord Commander, I can especially understand you. I would like to ask this, in the Ocean Mother Nest, did you really want to do blasphemous prayers?”

“Were we not aware of the hidden dangers in doing that? We had no other choice, so we did it.”

The dragonman youngster was immediately engrossed firmly by Cang Xu’s words.

Cang Xu was extremely right.

He felt that Cang Xu was also speaking to his heart.

Cang Xu continued to release the dragonman youngster’s psychological pressure, “Take yourself, Lord Commander, did you not purposely lie? No, to save Xiao Guai, your risked your life to deceive the Main Earth Elemental God. For the sake of us survivors, you risked offending Charming Blue Goddess, compelled to do so because there was no other alternatives.”

“What did we do wrong?”

“Nothing!”

“What is wrong in wanting to keep living?”

The dragonman youngster stared at Cang Xu, deeply exclaiming, “Cang Xu, you really understand me.”

“Hm, hm.” Zi Di nodded again and again, “Continued survival is the greatest justice.”

“We are righteous!” The girl once again stressed.

The dragonman youngster frowned.

Cang Xu promptly added, “Why are we always forced into having no choice, why do we take risks out of desperation? It is because we are too weak.”

“We want to carry out justice, and behave righteously, how can the weak do that?”

“We have to get stronger, only when we are powerful will we have the ability to calmly implement justice and fulfill our morality.”

“The Dueling Godhead this time is a unique golden opportunity to strengthen ourselves; we must firmly grab hold of it!”

The dragonman youngster heavily nodded, pushing aside his other thoughts as he looked firm and determined, “Cang Xu, you are correct.”

“Sorry.” He looked back at Zi Di, “Because of the pressure, I hesitated.”

Zi Di gazed at her sweetheart, and tenderly replied, “I understand you, Lord Commander. You are worried about taking risks that will burden the others, so you have misgivings.”

“But actually, you don’t need to bear this burden on your own, on Mystifying Monster Island, you were the reason we are still alive.”

“We are an integral whole; our benefits are unanimous. However the ramifications of this are huge, and we have to keep this secret from the others for the time being, that’s all.”

“Don’t worry, they will understand and support us!”

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, “Currently, I have exposed my ‘saint level potential’ since in the future the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is bound to sort me out, then I might as well be decisive!

The young man clenched his dragon claw, “Once you’ve started, there is no turning back. We can’t attend to both extremes without neglecting one, so the best choice for me is to use all of my strength to seize the Dueling Godhead.”

Cang Xu smiled, “Exactly so.”

The young man looked worried, However, there really are a lot of people blocking us, and from truly formidable powers. Our hope of success this time is remote.”

Cang Xu slowly shook his head, and began to bolster the dragonman youngster’s confidence, “Based on what I saw, our chances of success might instead be great.”

“The foremost reason is that to obtain the Dueling Godhead’s favor and seize it, one has to rely on a gold level!”

“The saint level already has a foot in laws, and their strength will become a major hindrance.”

“Of the gold levels, you Lord Commander are very competitive!”

The dragonman youngster agreed with this point, “Looking at it currently, the only threat to me is Long Meng. Perhaps I can add Qi Ci Lang too?”

Cang Xu continued, “Our greatest advantage is that the enemy is in the open while we are in the dark.”

“Because due to an unexpected turn of events, we learned of many trump cards these powers have from many channels.”

From Lan Zao, the survivors learned of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, the duelists, and Tranquil Hill.

From the dragonman youngster, they learned of Charming Blue Goddess’ arrangements and about the Charming Blue Church located there.

From Cang Xu, the survivors learned of Teacher Si Ling’s operation, learning of the Millenium Dragon Super-formation in passing.

Cang Xu determined, “The Sheng Ming Empire is not powerful enough to have a simultaneous confrontation with the Wilderness Continent, the Frost Continent, and the Life Continent.

“While the Ice Sculpture Kingdom also doesn’t want an acrimonious falling out with the Sheng Ming Empire. This can be seen from the current Ice Sculpture King’s choice, who endured silently and operated in the dark.”

“Both sides have their respective misgivings. Therefore, even if the Sheng Ming Empire dispatched a saint level, I believe such a fight would never happen publicly!”

“This signifies that the scale of this dispute will be limited, both sides will restrain themselves.”

“In those two powers’ eyes, we still represent a mysterious international arms merchant. Adding on the deep conflicts wrought by the great rebellion, both sides will be focused on the other.”

“Even if a part of them is mindful, they will be more distracted by Da Han.”

“Da Han has become a subtle third party.”

“Teacher Si Ling, and perhaps Zhou Zhang is a fourth or fifth party.”

“Due to the many sides in this situation, we can exploit the racks between these confrontations to get closer to the Dueling Godhead.”

Zi Di asked, “Then what do we do?”
