After analyzing to this step, Zi Di saw and approved of Cang Xu’s observation of the big picture and his stratagem for it.

Cang Xu coughed; after talking for a while, he finally reached his goal, allowing the dragonman youngster and Zi Di to recognize the true situation while still maintaining their initiative and energetic attitude.

Cang Xu replied, “First, we need to save someone.”

“Save someone?” The dragonman youngster and Zi Di looked at each other in dismay.

Zi Di suddenly asked,” Is it Shi Liu?”

But Cang Xu shook his head.

Chapter 936: Excavating Inheritance and Poaching People

At the foot of the brilliantly capped Splice Mountain, there was a small town – Graft Town.

Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong left behind five magic plants here; they were the Fountain King Lotus in the city center, the Gold Star Vine on the north side at Splice Mountain, the Pepper Tower on the south side, the Withered Claw Tree on the west side, and the Green Umbrella Tree on the East Side.

After Magician Cai Jing, Da Bei, Zhong Bei, and the others arrived at Graft Town, they were mainly focused on inspecting these five magic plants.

Their alchemy laboratory was set up in the south side Pepper Tower.

Pepper Tower had already been remodeled into a sunroom.

The roof’s glass was a silver level alchemy module, known as the sunlight collector.

After sunlight was gathered, it could focus the light on the magic plants in the room.

Green vines extended from the Pepper Tower’s walls, at each tip of the vines grew a pepper as large as a banana.

The long peppers were bright red, and on their surface, there was a person’s face.

These faces had closed eyes, as if in a deep slumber. Some even had open mouths and snored.

Cai Jing carefully observed a pepper, using a magic line brush from time to time to fill in the gaps in the magic circuits on the peppers’ surface or carve a new magic rune.

With the sound of footsteps, Magician Da Bei walked into the top floor.

“A bit gentler, do you want these peppers to explode with your noise?” Cai Jing transmitted to Zhong Bei, rebuking, “If a chain detonation of this many peppers happens, this town will be blown to kingdom come!”

Magician Da Bei grumbled, “Can you stop fiddling with your peppers all day long?”

“We’ve been in Graft Town for a long time already, yet we still haven’t found any trace of Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance!”

“Can you focus a bit?”

Cai Jing looked up and glanced at Da Bei, then glanced back down and continued to maintain the micro-formations on the peppers, saying, “In that case, what’s your plan?”

“We have already rummaged through Graft Town and turned it upside down, not just once, but five times!”

“But we have gained nothing.”

“These five magic plants might be the clues Fan Sheng Za Zhong left behind for his inheritance, that was the conclusion our investigation arrived at.”

“Now, I am in the process of trying to find clues from the Pepper Tower.”

Magician Da Bei shook his head, “I’m only seeing you utilizing the Pepper Tower to grow your magic plants! Don’t blame me for reminding you, if this continues, we will have nothing to present to the Alchemy Guild.”

In the wake of each passing day of zero progress, the pressure on Magician Da Bei multiplied.

At the time, he went looking for Master Cai Jing’s help to counterfeit potions to attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ magic medicine business. This resulted in an unexpected turn of events that led to them joining up with Cai Jing on Flower Skirt Island and excavating Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance.

Despite Qian Xing’s attack, they got through that daunting experience without a mishap with a teleportation formation and escaped.

Since then, the two of them reached an agreement. Through Magician Da Bei’s recommendation, Cai Jing successfully joined the Alchemy Guild.

·

The Alchemy Guild greatly valued Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance and gave the mission of excavating his series of inheritances to Cai Jing, Da Bei, and Zhong Bei.

According to the clues, they surmise there were two suspicious places, one of which they picked. Graft Town. The outcome up until now was that they never found the inheritance.

Cai Jing sighed, “Then there is nothing we can do. We’ve already done our absolute best.”

“We suspected two places, one is here, and the other is Tranquil Hill. Do you know where Tranquil Hill is?”

“This small town is actually our only option.”

He was under less pressure than Da Bei.

Although the two were gold levels, Cai Jing was stronger than Da Bei. Regardless of individual fighting strength or alchemy mastery, it was the same for both.

He was also the main person in charge of exploring Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritances. Da Bei was more like the person the Alchemy Guild dispatched to supervise him.

“Don’t be too impetuous, I feel you’re hotter tempered than the explosive peppers I am configuring.” Cai Jing shook his head, “Take it easy, partner. I was secluded on Flower Skirt Island for several years. How long has it been since we arrived at Graft Town?”

Cai Jing was strong and capable; he was not worried about the Alchemy Guild replacing him for the time being.

In the entire Alchemy Guild, no one comprehended Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritances more than he, that was his greatest confidence.

To excavate the inheritance on Flower Skirt Island, he had spent several years on it. He had a lot of patience when it came to looking for the clues in Graft Town.

While Da Bei’s situation was far worse off than his.

Although Da Bei was an Alchemy Guild Elder, he wasn’t Shuang Lian, Hua Ni, or other elders with real power.

There were a great many people that could replace him, come to Graft Town, and supervise Magician Cai Jing.

Da Bei didn’t want to let this go, he understood thoroughly that if this mission was completed on his watch, his contribution would be massive. This was a significant chance for someone like him who had no talent.

The Alchemy Guild had urged him repeatedly, so Da Bei was more anxious than Cai Jing.

He was clear about the fact that the guild headquarters’ people also took a fact to this excavation mission and started to use their contacts in succession. They couldn’t touch Cai Jing, but they could dig out Dai Bei and take his place in the hole.

Da Bei bitterly tried to persuade, “I can understand we can’t find any clues. But the crucial thing is that general headquarters doesn’t understand that.”

“Lord Cai Jing, you have already joined the Alchemy Guild and are no longer a loner. You are enjoying Alchemy Guild funds and facilities, you must reciprocate those somewhat.”

Seeing Cai Jing’s head lowered and silent, Da Bei looked around a bit, then simply stated honestly, “General headquarters already has many veiled criticisms, we need to give them something that will shut some people up. No matter how…minorly forged the progress is…”

“So long as it stuffs those people’s mouths, we can be at ease for a time.”

Cai Jing sneered to himself, he already saw through Da Bei’s situation and also knew what his motives were right now.

Cai Jing was just about to say something when suddenly, hurried footsteps could be heard.

Thump, thump, thump.

Magician Zhong Bei pushed open the door, and ran into the top floor.

Cai Jing looked gloomy and almost wanted to explode.

Da Bei’s heart jolted, he reprimanded his own beloved son on behalf of Cai Jing, roaring, “Idiot, you’re doing it again! Don’t you know this place needs silence? If these explosive peppers are roused, it will cause a chain explosion that will destroy the entire town!”

“So, sorry!” Magician Zhong Bei waved his hand, looking pleasantly surprised, “There is an important matter that just happened. A stranger paid a visit and said he has a clue to Great Master Za Zhong’s inheritance!”

“Oh?” Da Bei and Cai Jing were astonished.

The two glanced at each other and promptly left the top floor.

Going down the stairs, they came to the third floor parlor. On the way, they inquired Zhong Bei of the mysterious visitor, but Zhong Bei didn’t know much about them.

Not long after, the three saw the mysterious visitor in the parlor.

It was the dragonman youngster.

No, to Cai Jing and the two others, the young man looked like an unfamiliar gold level snow elf magician.

“May I ask what your distinguished name is? You hold a clue to Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance? How did you know that was our objective this trip?” Cai Jing directly inquired.

The snow elf youngster looked serene, he icily and arrogantly sized up Cai Jing and Zhong Bei, not endearing at all.

The young man calmly stated, “You turned over Graft Town five times, and have come up empty handed. I have a clue you want.”

“I have come to collaborate with you.”

“Collaboration?” Cai Jing pursed his brows, he didn’t have a good first impression of the snow elf youngster.

A smile floated onto Da Bei’s face, and his attitude was much more cordial than Cai Jing, he probed, “Sire, might you be a member of the Alchemy Guild?”

The snow elf youngster waved his hand, “That is not important, first let me see whether you have the qualifications to work with me.”

“If you aren’t qualified, then it is a waste of time discussing it with you.”

Cai Jing’s brows wrinkled tighter, coldly taking measure of the snow elf youngster, “How will you calculate qualifications, do you want to test us?”

The young man was expressionless, “This is indeed a test. I will test you all at once, and don’t you want to test me too? Come to the dueling arena.”

It had to be said that this proposal conformed with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s culture.

Duels permeated each facet of Ice Sculpture citizens’ daily lives. Before negotiations began, to confirm qualifications a duel was held, a common occurrence.

Cai Jing and Da Bei glanced at each other, then they both looked at the snow elf youngster and nodded simultaneously.

Cai Jing said, “Then let’s go to a dueling arena!”

Although Graft Town was small, it had five dueling arenas.

They picked the largest one with complete facilities, then set up a private duel with no spectators.

The snow elf youngster and Cai Jing faced each other.

Da Bei was far weaker in combat than Cai Jing, so the latter was the only choice for this.

Da Bei was presiding, and after counting down, he roared, “Let the duel begin.”

There was only one person in the large spectator gallery, Magician Zhong Bei.

Da Bei ran back to stand behind Magician Zhong Bei.

The snow elf youngster and Magician Cai Jing didn’t rush to battle.

The snow elf youngster was calm and unruffled, “Magician Cai Jing, I heard you are good at deploying magic plants. You best take out your special skills, otherwise I will defeat you easily, you don’t want to regret it.”

Cai Jing sneered angrily at the young man’s arrogance. He narrowed his eyes, and knowing the gravity of the situation, he bit his teeth and said, “Then you shall get what you wish!”

Summoning spell – Ascending Dragon Grass.

With a short magic staff in his hand, he raised his arm, activating a magic formation, and directly unleashing the spell in the staff.

The summoning formation instantly formed in the air.

A moment later, space violently fluctuated, and a thicket of grass several meters tall was summoned into the dueling arena.

The Ascending Dragon Grass shook its slender grass stalks like it was dancing gracefully.

The grass tips were very pointy, glistened with metal luster, and were as sharp as spear tips.

Spatial magic – Space Holes.

Magician Cai Jing’s magic ring on his hand flashed, activating the spell loaded in it.

A second later, the dueling arena’s space undulated fiercely several times as a large amount of pitch-black holes appeared.

These holes weren’t that big and were concentrated around the Ascending Dragon Grass and the snow elf youngster.

The Ascending Dragon Grass’ tips entered these space holes, and a moment later, crossed through space and extended out of the space holes near the young man.

Ten grass tips stabbed at the snow elf youngster together.

The young man wielded his long magic staff.

He pressed the bottom of the staff on the ground, triggering its installed defensive magic.

A moment later, a water ball formed around his body.

The grass tips thrusted into the thick water ball, and for a moment, it was hard for them to pierce through.

Under the snow elf youngster’s spiritual control, the water ball’s surface surged with streams of water. The flow accelerated, forming a whirlpool that firmly sucked all of the grass tips into it.

The snow elf youngster began to chant, and after a few brief words of magic, a chill began to spread.

The strong chill followed along the water ball, covered the Ascending Dragon Grass’ tips, then penetrated the space holes, and began to erode the Ascending Dragon Grass’ main body.

The gold level Ascending Dragon Grass was turned to ice in mere seconds, becoming motionless.

Da Bei and Magician Zhong Bei were both shaken.

The snow elf youngster’s defense and counterattack was quite sharp. In less than ten seconds, he suppressed the Ascending Dragon Grass instead.

“He only used two spells, one water type, and one ice type. But he used no less than four spell techniques. They were quick casting, simple chanting, spell stacking, and barrier casting.”

“The crucial thing is what made those spells so strong, his magic equipment or his bloodline?”

The father and son had some scope of insight, in just one round, they immediately understood that the snow elf youngster was a formidable caster.

Summoning spell – Frost Leaf Flower.

A large white flower covered Cai Jing’s body.

Summoning spell – Absolute Zero Birch.

Two towering birch trees stood at either side of Magician Cai Jing. These birch trees had very high ice resistance.

Spell – Giant Type Life Transformation.

A second later, the life transformation spell caused the Absolute Zero Birch Trees to pull out their roots to form giant feet and twist their branches together to form fists. The tree trunks looked like a person’s face as the trees finally turned into two giants.

The giants pounced from the snow elf youngster’s left and right

The young man had been maintaining his water ball barrier, and the water ball rolled, sliding him backwards.

It left behind a trail of ice, slowing down the pursuing giants’ steps.

The young man chanted, condensing countless icicles in the air.

Several hundred icicles shot down, striking the giant trees with clacking echoes.

The birch giants were gradually worn down and fell mid pursuit.

The young man began his counteroffensive.

Cai Jing moved while doing mobile casting and attacking.

Ice type magic that was shooting towards him or covered a certain range was mostly absorbed by the Frost Leaf Flower, greatly reducing their might. He was safe and sound.

Cai Jing’s summoned magic plants couldn’t cope with the snow elf youngster.

In the audience, Da Bei and Zhong Bei were unable to take their eyes away from this.

“Lord Cai Jing’s fighting style is very distinct. He cultivated his own summons, the magic plants have different functions, using them for attack, defense, and healing. Almost all of his spells are used to aid these magic plants and more easily display their power.”

“This mysterious snow elf magician is fairly traditional. He mainly uses various kinds of ice magic, and occasionally some water magic. He is definitely a special duty battle magician, casually grasping all kinds of casting techniques and his various spells are done as naturally and smoothly as breathing. Watching him fight simply is enjoyable!”

The young man and Cia Jing attacked each other for a short time, then when Cai Jing gradually couldn’t endure the strong pressure, he revealed a trump card.

He summoned a gigantic Morning Glory magic plant.

The Morning Glory sprayed the magic medicine loaded in it.

This magic medicine was called Muddled Color, and it had a strange, yet potent effect of mixing energy.

The medicine sprayed onto the ice magic, immediately causing ice walls and ice arrows to collapse into balls of chaotic ice elements. When it sprayed onto the young man’s water ball, it nearly caused the water ball to crumble.

The snow elf youngster remained calm under danger, and laughed, “That’s more like it.”

The fiercer and more pressuring the fight was, the more efficient his magician experience would be.

Magic spell – Rapid Freeze Ray.

Magic spell – Flying Magic Mirror.

Ten magic mirrors with smooth and shiny surfaces spun like frisbees as they fluttered in all directions. Five or six Rapid Freeze Rays shot out, reflecting across the magic mirrors and diffracting into three rays. With one refraction, tiny Rapid Freeze Rays covered the entire dueling arena!

The light rays were far more numerous and complicated than the magic medicine.

The Morning Glory’s spraying was clearly weakened by the light rays, and under the swift barrage, was frozen into an ice cube.

Zhong Bei and Da Bei shifted their expression, both feeling things were far from good.

As both sides attacked each other, Cai Jing was obviously not a match. This was not a good omen.

Cai Jing did everything he could to counterattack, releasing all kinds of magic plans in turn, including the explosive peppers he was just cultivating.

These peppers were hurled and exploded with remarkable power, blowing up the snow elf youngster’s temporarily condensed giant ice wolf.

The young man immersed himself in the thrill of battle, becoming more proficient as the battle went on. As he used spells one after the other, all kinds of casting techniques were quickly polished to perfection in actual combat.

One had to at least be at gold level to put the young man under pressure.

To tell the truth, Cai Jing was not weak, he was an elite gold level. He was a great master counterfeiter, and before he joined the Alchemy Guild, he was a loner who had the skills to keep himself safe.

But as the fight continued, the young man was growing stronger. Cai Jing wanted to make a comeback, but from beginning to end, he was pushed into a downwind position. The more he tried, the more powerless he felt.

Large amounts of magic plants were frozen into ice blocks, and as Cai Jing strived to rescue them, many more magic plants were frozen.

The young man’s Bloodcore didn’t have any snow elf bloodlines in it; his form was just from deception divine spells. What was really amplifying him was the ice dragon bloodlines he had absorbed.

Through real combat testing, the bloodline boost turned out to be very good.

At least fighting someone like Cai Jing was no problem.

This allowed the young man to test how strong he was as a magician.

“That’s enough, let’s end it.” As soon as he saw Cai Jing had nothing left to reveal, the snow elf youngster waved a hand and did something big.

This was not magic, but a divine spell.

The divine spell’s vast power immediately swept across the entire dueling arena, blowing Cai Jing into the air, and nearly launching him outside.

The young man took the initiative to dominate, and Cai Jing landed onto the ground, looking quite unsightly.

Da Bei and Zhong Bei were dumbfounded.

When the young man used a divine spell at the end, its power and indescribable aura made it clear the young man’s mastery of divine spells was not inferior to his own magic.

As it turns out, during this long fight, the snow elf youngster only used half his strength.

Even Cai Jing couldn’t help but ponder, “If he used divine spells, how long would I last?”

By the time the snow elf youngster floated over to Cai Jing, the alchemist no longer was indignant about the young man’s arrogant look.

Cai Jing bowed his head in a magician’s etiquette, submitting cheerfully, “I lost, sire, your battle strength is extraordinary!”

The young man was happy.

In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, as long as one was strong enough, they could convince others through a duel, this was quite convenient!

He said to Cai Jing, “You’re pretty strong too, you are qualified to work with me. I can give you the pertinent clues, that’s right, Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance really is in Graft Town.”

The snow elf youngster put on airs.

“In that case, what do we need to pay for it?” Can Jing inquired, finding it difficult to cover up his nervousness.

The young man’s mouth curled into a thin smile: “I want you to take part in this year’s Warm Snow Cup and help the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ Magician Yao Ma pass the second exam.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?” Cai Jing was startled.

He knew of this mercenary corps.

Originally, Da Bei had found him to deal with this mercenary corps.

Cai Jin had neither malice nor a good opinion of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. He knew the Alchemy Guild’s Magician Shuang Lian seemed to collaborate with this mercenary corps. And Shuang Lian had once given him a hard time, forcing him to take out Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance piece to keep the peace.

But this conflict was mainly between Cai Jing and Shuang Lian. In fact, the conflict wasn’t that deep, and it didn’t hinder Cai Jing one bit from agreeing to this request.

“And you?” The snow elf youngster looked at Da Bei and Zhong Bei.

The father and son glanced at each other, forcing a smile one after the other.

Originally, because Zhong Bei’s supervision was lacking, Bu Quan had joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, leading to a string of events. Now, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was coming into their lives once again.

The mysterious and formidable snow elf magician requested that they work with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Cai Jing had already agreed, so Da Bei and Zhong Bei had no choice but to pick collaboration too.

Both sides signed and agreed to an auspiciously light collaboration contract, the snow elf youngster then used a divine spell to create another layer of insurance.

“Now then follow me.” The youngster secretly led the three to Jia Bing’s residence.

“So, it was actually in Jia Bing’s alchemy room!” Da Bei and the others obviously knew what this place was.

At the time, after Zong Ge won his 3 v 1, he and Zi Di accelerated their trip to clandestinely come to Graft Town to plunder Jia Bing’s alchemy laboratory.

Before departing, the two noticed Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s secret inheritance door. They tried their best, but the door wouldn’t open. As there wasn’t an opportunity at the time, they gave the door the classic combination of Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell before leaving.

They left just in time, as not long after, the duelists dispatched personnel over. Because Jia Bing guarded the secret of the inheritance, never disclosing it to anyone, the duelist didn’t find it.

Cai Jing and the others had searched Graft Town a full five times, and so long as those spells still existed, based on their own strength, they would never find it.

As the three wore expressions of utter incomprehension, the snow elf youngster lightly wiped the secret door with his hand, undoing the magic and allowing it to appear.

The three were utterly excited, Cai Jing even more so, nearly throwing himself at it.

He reached out to caress the secret door, his voice trembling, “That’s right, this is another one of Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritances!”

Don’t be fooled by how calm and leisurely he was with his peppers, as a matter of fact, he valued this series of inheritances more than Da Bei.

Because his strength and alchemy research both revolved around magic plants. Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritances were extremely helpful to him.

While Da Bei had limited strength and talent, this inheritance strengthened him only minimally. What Da Bei truly valued was the Alchemy Guild’s calculated contribution and merits after this mission was accomplished.

“First conceal this information, wait until we open the secret door, then we can tell the higherups,” said Da Bei.

After discovering the secret door, he was no longer anxious. On the contrary, he was extremely vigilant, afraid that on the eve of success, the Alchemy Guild’s higherups would transfer him away.

Cai Jing straightened his body, his eyes still fixed on the secret door, sighing, “I want to open this secret door, but it isn’t easy, and it will take a long time.”

It had taken a lot of effort to open the first secret door. Touching this secret door didn’t even provide the barest threads of information on how to open it. It would require meticulous study to create a grafted magic plant the secret door would approve of, and that would take a lot of energy and time, along with constant attempts.

Cai Jing looked at the snow elf youngster, “First I will help the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps pass the second exam. That matter is more urgent.”

Da Bei looked grave.

Helping the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would not be simple. How was Da Bei not unaware that the Alchemy Guild and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were in a bet, and that the truly powerful Elder Hua Ni was in the process of suppressing and attempting to eliminate Yao Ma (Zi Di)?

Nowadays, if the three people wanted to help Yao Ma, then their status and renown in the Alchemy Guild would be immensely impacted.

But the dragonman youngster shook his head, “If I could say I could help you solve this secret door in half a day, what would you think?”

Cai Jing, Da Bei, and Zhong Bei were simultaneously dumbfounded.

Zhong Bei was incredulous, subconsciously retorting, “How is that possible?”

Cai Jing was bewildered.

Da Bei was the most meticulous researcher, while at the same time lacking the talent, unable to know from experience how hard it was to open the secret door, therefore, he was the first to react, “Sire, what do you want to do?”

The snow elf youngster thought out what Cang Xu said, repeating those words, “In fact. I can do much more for you.”

“For example, a higher position in the Alchemy Guild. Don’t you want the same power and position as Hua Ni?”

Da Bei’s heart beat wildly, Zhong Bei’s mouth fell open.

Cai Jing locked his brows tight, his mind rising with alertness. He gazed at the snow elf youngster, “I know the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is backed by an arms merchant, you were dispatched by their backer!”

Chapter 937: The Enemy Crumbles from Within

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s Alchemy Guild’s Warm Snow Cup’s second exam site was set up in a dueling arena.

“I didn’t expect to have so many people participating in the second exam.” The dragonman youngster slightly swept his eyes around, apart from the spectators who bought tickets, there were a thousand alchemists competing.

That was a lot!

One must know that to advance to the Warm Snow Cup’s second round, iron level and bronze levels vanished, one at minimum had to be a silver level. There were also no lack of gold levels, who were currently sitting on the sidelines, almost all of them surrounded by crowds.

This was the world of the strong, alchemists and magicians were no exception.

The vast majority of alchemy proposals requested transcendent levels. If the transcendent level was too low, sometimes even test observation was impossible.

“This includes the contestants that got through the first round, there are still so many left. That there are so many left is a testament to this being a top quality alchemy kingdom on the Main Plane.”

Sitting next to the dragonman youngster was Cang Xu, who was secretly transmitting this to him.

“In terms of territory size, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is a tiny pellet compared to most kingdoms on the continents. But its alchemy strength is extraordinary, and adding on its culture of ferocity, it has always maintained a large scale army, worthy of the reputation of a great militaristic powerhouse.”

“This country’s shortcoming is poor resources. They only have one super-resource point, and many of their resource points are concentrated in ice and water elements, a severe overemphasis in these areas. They also rely on flourishing export trades to sell large amounts of excellent quality alchemy products, allowing the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to obtain an astonishing amount of foreign money. By utilizing foreign trade to support and repay their domestic activities, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom has maintained their high development costs and military spending.”

He was seldom together with the dragonman youngster, and he wouldn’t let by any chance to proactively teach the latter as much as possible to raise his cognition level and develop his international perspective.

The young man earnestly listened, actively absorbing it and itching to write it all down.

He was constantly growing and wouldn’t let off any opportunity to learn.

Through Cang Xu’s recent admonishment, the dragonman youngster noticed many of his inadequacies.

Previously, he planned against Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Tan Mo, Cong Mang, and the others. He had captured the gold level pirate alive, then gave a large scale sermon to Da Dou, Cong Mang, Hun Tong, and the others, filling him with a sense of achievement.

However, after communicating with Cang Xu, the dragonman youngster could see clearly that these accomplishments was mere ignorance and fearlessness, he was blindly charging ahead, while the hidden dangers were enormous, it just didn’t collapse on him on the spot.

Cang Xu’s admonishment allowed him to deeply familiarize himself with what was called scholarly knowledge and wisdom.

Emulating the virtuous, the young man also longed to use a talent like Cang Xu, therefore he made up his mind, conscientiously studied, and actively got closer to Cang Xu.

Despite obtaining lots of experience from divine blessings, those experiences were focused on cultivation.

His lifetime wisdom was barely increased.

Under the Alchemy Guild’s management, the second exam calmly began.

According to the arena’s arrangement, every ten groups of alchemists would get onto the wide dueling arena and publicly do alchemy.

The dragonman youngster watched, noticing that each group had three people. There were no pairs. The second exam had a massive alchemy workload, and each group was filled to the maximum limit.

The first round finished, with only three groups left. Just the first step – refining a complete alchemy puppet, eliminated 70% of the contestants.

And of the three groups left, although they had a complete alchemy puppet, after they fought the ice sculpture guardian, each of them was defeated. The most outstanding of them only lasted three rounds.

The dragonman youngster’s brows creased, making out clues.

The refined alchemy puppets had decent battle prowess and was not too different from the ice sculpture guardian. The real disparity was intelligence.

Those hastily refined puppets looked like dementia patients. The ice sculpture guardian was quite quick witted, as if it were a normal person, and this disparity in intelligence led to a distinct contrast in performance.

The best of the next five rounds was the third round, where four groups refined a battle puppet. Among them, one group’s puppet lasted ten rounds, then suddenly, it began to fume with smoke and stopped moving, allowing the ice sculpture guardian to effortlessly take the win.

From this, it could be seen that this year’s Warm Snow Cup exam was extremely difficult.

The dragoman youngster, in the audience seating, was observing and listening in all directions, hearing the surrounding people discuss and ridicule how difficult this year’s Warm Snow Cup was.

The young man sighed, “To some degree, these people were burdened by us.”

He looked towards the judging panel seating, noticing that Hua Ni was sitting in the middle, looking very gloomy.

She was the most obvious enemy in the Alchemy Guild!

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but recall what Cang Xu had said beforehand.

Cang Xu, “Before this, we need to save someone first.”

Zi Di: “Shi Liu?”

Cang Xu shook his head, and said the answer, “It is Jiu Jin.”

The Alchemy Guild’s Elder Jiu Jin had become the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ saboteur after Zi Di gained leverage over him.

Cang Xu’s answer was surprising.

The dragonman youngster thought of Zong Ge, Shuang Lian, and Bu Quan.

To the youngster it seemed that Jiu Jin was a valued elder, he had no safety issues, why did they need to rescue him?

But Cang Xu stated why, “At present, we know the Li Jian Clan has extraordinary monitoring capabilities. When Zong Ge was playing the role of the commander, they saw two Long Fus, and from that point of view, they unraveled Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.”

“At the time, Miss Zi Di grabbed hold of Jiu Jin’s weak point and blackmailed him, and the Li Jian knew about that. However, they didn’t have feasible evidence in hand like we do.”

“Even so, the Li Jian Clan can deal with Jiu Jin with this information already.”

Zi Di frowned, still puzzled.

She calculated this situation but felt it was no big deal. Jiu Jin’s undead aura had already dissipated, and the real evidence was in her hands. Even if the Li Jian Clan raised difficult questions, it wouldn’t endanger Elder Ju Jin’s life or affect the Wisteria Cache.

Of course, Jiu Jin would be suspected, pushed aside, suppressed, and at most, sacked from office. Zi Di planned that if this situation occurred, she could take advantage of it to recruit Elder Jiu Jin.

But Cang Xu shook his head, declaring, “We must save Jiu Jin! Or to be more accurate, we must save his current position within the Alchemy Guild.”

“Only in the Alchemy Guild, only in the capital, can Jiu Jin have much more of an impact. As an insider elder, Jiu Jin really helped us a lot. Correct?”

Zi Di nodded, without Jiu Jin leaking the exam questions to her, she would be unable to get through the first exam.

Cang Xu then said, “The Li Jian Clan might denounce him. If that happens, Elder Jiu Jin’s position and power will be in serious danger. Currently, we really need external support. We are outsiders and must take root as soon as possible and assimilate with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. We are climbing a mountain, and Jiu Jin is one of our few leverage points. If he loses his position, then we will suffer immense losses.”

“Jiu Jin is actually an outstandingly helpful partner.”

“First, he once worked with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance and experienced receiving its financial aid. Second, we have significant blackmail on him, and if we toss it out, he is bound to lose his standing and at best he’ll be imprisoned for life, and at worst he’ll be executed for being the accomplice of an undead. Finally, he has already yielded to us. Even if he is unwilling, he already leaked questions to us and accepted that he’ll only be falling deeper and deeper.”

After pausing for a moment, Cang Xu gazed at the two, “Passing the Warm Snow Cup’s second question is that the main event. We want to pass the first round of exams to enter the next stage of the Warn Snow Cup: displaying our alchemy products.”

“In that stage, we will finally utilize War Merchant’s legacy and have a good chance of taking first place. As a reward for being first, we can receive financial aid in the construction of a magician tower. If we build a magician tower, we will have taken root in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Apart from sufficient combat strength, a magician tower is also a production base that will safeguard our alchemy business.”

“If the Alchemy Guild doesn’t resolve and eliminate us right now, I can’t say for sure Hua Ni will give up. The greatest possibility is that she will continue to organize more elimination questions, and if we compete according to the rules, then we will be too passive!”

Cang Xu’s views drew the young man’s and lady’s fervent nods.

Cang Xu took a deep breath, “We need to establish ourselves, to take root in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom and the Sheng Ming Empire are secretly setting themselves against each other, and possess natural contractions, along with intense, surreptitious contests. Although the empire is strong, it is apprehensive about the implications. Even though the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is weaker, it has a legendary foundation and is the local ruler.”

“Thes two colossuses pitting themselves against each other is bound to create upheaval, it is precisely this opportunity and place we can grasp and exploit.”

“Snake Rat Island, Two Eyes Island, those places were too small, and already under the Sheng Ming Empire’s control. Even if they weren’t islands, they don’t have enough space for us to use.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom people are tough as nails and have a strong dueling ambience. We enjoy the convenience of duels, and your name, Lord Commander, is propagated nationwide, you have gained the populace’s widespread support, and if this was anywhere else, those things would be extremely difficult to accomplish in so short a time span.”

“The resources here are also relatively rich. Despite it being considered a small pellet-sized country, to us, it is big enough.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom also has an adequate alchemy environment. Our alchemy products here will be enormously competitive, we’ll be able to sell then for profits that we can reinvest into ourselves, improving our strength and using the surplus to expand production, forming a positive cycle.”

As he said that, Cang Xu looked towards Zi Di, throwing a look of admiration, “Thanks to Miss Zi Di, our business plan succeeded immensely, we have abundant capital, and most crucially, we can establish ourselves and accelerate our expansion.”

In the field of business, Cang Xu really admired Zi Di’s methods. If it were him, he definitely couldn’t have achieved what she did. This was far more than outstanding, Zi Di developed the unexplored market of mount potions, and stirred up the magic medicine market in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom with a massive surge of waves. The huge profits caused a colossus like the Alchemy Guild to become threatening, and personally participated in a bet with her, it was clear Zi Di was an absolute genius at this.

“In view of Miss Zi Di’s hatred for the empire’s Seventh Prince and a good deal of nobles, we need to look for appropriate imperial higherups. But building up a network usually takes a lot of time.”

“The redemption plan still needs to continue.”

“In this time period, we need to energetically grow, strive to absorb every resource and opportunity near us, and do our utmost to expand and grow. If we can become an influential power in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, this will definitely raise our bargaining weight.”

“If the redemption plan is successful, our extraordinary quality, comprehensive battle strength, and influence can be fully displayed on stage through the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Our value were be determined, and our bargaining chips in negotiations will grow, we’ll need to hand over fewer spoils of war and keep more of them.”

“If the redemption plan fails, we will help the Ice Sculpture Kingdom! Using them as a temporary protective umbrella that can hold back external wind and frost for the time being, delaying for crucial time. Of course, being forced into such a terrible situation is something I don’t want to end up in.”

Cang Xu fully integrated the present circumstances together, declaring the survivors’ grand strategy henceforth.

When Zi Di heard this, her eyes shined, approving at once, “Sure, we should do that.”

She looked at the Commander, “If the redemption plan succeeds, we can contact the Imperial Secret Spy Agency and deal with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom together.”

“If the redemption plan fails, we can give the Ice Sculpture Kingdom information and expose the Imperial Secret Spy Agency to display our sincerity for cooperation to them.”

The main focus was on being a flexible faction.

The dragonman youngster didn’t retort. Cang Xu’s strategy continued their redemption plan, combined it with the latest situation, considered success or failure, advancing or retreating in accordance to it, and was very reliable.

Cang Xu then said, “To take root in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, we cannot act like how we are right now. Hogging the magic medicine benefits is not desirable, forming alliances of benefits will produce many more hidden profits.”

“Of course, it is imperative we cut ties with the Li Jian Clan!”

“They were utterly defeated by Da Han on the sea; their deliberate conservation of their strength was a political blunder and caused the entire clan to stink to high heaven and be in quite the vile political environment within the kingdom currently. If we continue to work with them, they will encumber and hinder our plan of taking root.”

“Accounts haven’t been settled with the Li Jian Clan yet because of the National Ceremony Grand Duel, the imminent forming of the Dueling Godhead, and the external threat of Da Han and the empire, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is unable to pull any resources to deal with them, that’s all.”

Cang Xu raised a finger, “First we resolve the Alchemy Guild problem, then we cut ties with the Li Jian Clan.”

“By getting involved with the Alchemy Guild, we must help our agents, crumbling the enemy from within.”

“With regards to Elder Jiu Jin, we have to save him. We cannot allow him to collapse and must do everything we can to stabilize his current position.”

“The current danger is that the Li Jian Clan is likely to leak his real relationship with us. The reason why Hua Ni hasn’t acted yet might be to lull us into a false sense of security. The list of names we obtained was stolen by Jiu Jin copying the database.”

“This is perhaps the enemy’s trap.”

“Therefore, accepting anyone on the list is a hazard. For the next exam, excluding me, the third person will be quite crucial.”

“Here, I shall recommend someone…”

“Cai Jing!”

Chapter 938: Advancing Before Hua Ni’s Eyes

The Ice Sculpture Capital, the Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest site.

“Success!”

“We knocked down the ice sculpture guardian!”

“Oh, finally someone passed.”

“The cheers in the arena interrupted the dragonman youngster’s memories. He stared out and saw the ice sculpture guardian being defeated.

The consecutive losses caused the arena to be quite depressed.

The first alchemist group to pass caused the other competing magicians to relax and grow more confident.

The dragonman youngster carefully observed, recognizing who the winner was.

“It is the current Blood Kin City Lord.”

This city lord came to the capital half a year earlier for this competition, leaving an assistant back in Blood Kin City to administer it. While all of his energy and time was spent on preparing for the Warm Snow Cup.

This conduct and bearing was not becoming of a city lord. But as a master alchemist, he was clearly outstanding, strong enough and very ambitious.

What Blood Kin City Lord was attempting was obvious at a glance – he wanted to fight for top three in the Warm Snow Cup and obtain the reward that would let him construct his own magician tower!

“Worthy of being a gold level alchemist and magician.”

“The rune circuits he created were excellent, and he considered all of the circumstances that might happen during the battle.”

“The other two silver levels are indeed fortunate to smoothly follow Blood Kin City Lord in his success.”

“His magic circuit design fundamentals are exceedingly outstanding, if I were doing it, I would take up at least five times as much of the puppet’s internal space. So many effects, from just twenty sets of magic circuits. How did he accomplish this?”

The alchemists were discussing spiritedly.

Magic circuit designing was a basic skill to these alchemists.

As a veteran alchemist, Blood Kin City Lord was not just strong, he was exceptional.

It was with the outstanding magic circuits he designed that allowed his puppet to have enough space to load military equipment and thereby defeat the ice sculpture guardian and advance.

It could be said that Blood Kin City Lord used his formidable power to pass the second round fair and square.

Not long after, another group succeeded.

“It is Xue Tuan Zi.”

The survivors had been paying attention to her.

The dragonman youngster cared because Xue Tuan Zi was a member of the duelists. Cang Xu valued her background much more, Xue Tuan Zi was herself outstanding enough, while her grandfather was even more so, he was the master of Snow Cake Tower and a genuinely powerful elder in the Alchemy Guild, one that possessed his own magician tower.

Magician towers greatly amplified magicians’ power. A magician who had a tower was absolutely a person of high standing. No matter if it was wealth or fighting strength, neither could be looked down upon.

Xue Tuan Zi and Blood Kin City Lord were both gold level alchemy masters, but their line of thoughts were utterly different.

She designed the alchemy puppet very peculiarly, it looked like a child yeti, chubby, shoddily made, mainly focused on defense, far more than its offense.

After the battle with the ice sculpture guardian started, the alchemy puppet took a beating the entire time.

However, as it was being beaten up, it was constantly absorbing those external attacks and turned them into internal energy. After enough internal energy was saved, a violent explosion occurred.

The ice sculpture guardian was blown up, leaving behind the small yeti. According to the Alchemy Guild’s rules, it was quickly judged that Xue Tuan Zi’s group would advance.

“You can do this?” Everyone was amazed.

Xue Tuan Zi’s method was undoubtedly quite a revelation to everyone. Many people benefited from it, and new ways of thinking were formed, but it was too late.

Making changes for the second exam was very hard, and many things needed to be prepared for it.

The dragonman youngster secretly analyzed, “Xue Tuan Zi is a duelist, and her background is very good. She is seeking and provoking duels and constantly going to arenas. Therefore, her battle abilities are also outstanding.”

“Her tactics and alchemy designs combined and expressed stability that could let her advance.”

In the twelfth round, it was Zi Di’s group to go onto stage.

Cang Xu and Zi Di kept together, but these two were not the protagonists in the audience’s eyes. Most of them were casting their sight on the third person.

The gold level alchemist – Cai Jing!

“An Alchemy Guild elder is actually competing.”

“How is he qualified?”

“Haven’t you researched the rules? So long as one is an Alchemy Guild Elder, they can participate in the Warm Snow Cup’s alchemy achievement exhibition. He can take part in any round or exam question.”

Essentially, the Warm Snow Cup’s first exam was to weed out the inferior people, and filter for the true alchemy elites. If an elder level figure cannot pass, then the Alchemy Guild must be incompetent.

“Great Master Cai Jing obviously could participate in the achievement exhibition, but as it happens, he decided to participate in this exam question…”

“Damn it, to be wealthy enough to hug such a thigh.”

“Humph, we don’t know how much he was paid.”

“Speaking of which, even though Cai Jing became an Alchemy Guild elder, he has a very dark past. Many years ago, to make money, he counterfeited many products and disturbed the entire nation’s alchemy marketplace.”

Some familiar with the alchemist looked at Hua Ni.

Hua Ni narrowed her eyes, rigidly glaring at Cai Jing.

She had been scheming to eliminate Yao Ma (Zi Di), but in the end, Cai Jing took part in the competition to safeguard Yao Ma!

Cai Jing had just joined the guild, and not long after being appointed elder, he immediately made a play against Elder Hua Ni!

His guts were too fat.

At first, Hua Ni still believed that Cai Jing was unaware of the inside story and severity. Therefore, she personally contacted Cai Jing, yet Cai Jing refused without the slightest hesitation.

At once, Hua Ni fumed with unbearable anger!

Due to the rules, Hua Ni couldn’t raise difficult questions. She and Cia Jing were elders both, and even if she wanted to suppress and push aside the latter, she couldn’t do that at the moment.

In short, Hua Ni was enraged but could do nothing for the time being.

She could only tenaciously remember this slight by Cai Jing.

Of course, this also included Cang Xu.

After signing up, the groups were determined. The Alchemy Guild, Li Jian Clan, and Jing Xiang Clan all secretly contacted Cang Xu, wanting him to desert. In the end, all were cleverly put off by Cang Xu and he ultimately rejected them all.

He was undoubtedly playing with these three factions!

The exam began.

Zi Di and Cang Xu hastily moved. Very quickly, the weak alchemy foundation they showed caused many master alchemists to frown. This standard of supporting work was not presentable.

But Cai Jing was a true clutch!

He was a gold level, and although his reputation as a counterfeiting great master was bad, it conversely proved his skills. As a magician, he was able to meet Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s standards, open the secret door, and acquire the grafted magic plant inside, a task impossible for many gold level alchemists.

One must know that Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong was a druid, and his inheritance was set aside for druids.

Why did the Alchemy Guild have to appoint Cai Jing as the one who would continue to excavate Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance? Could it be because he was familiar with them? Of course not! His “unrivaled” alchemy strength in the domain of magic plants was somewhat exaggerated. But in the massive Alchemy Guild, it was really hard to find anyone who was comparable with him.

Those that were Cai Jing’s match or stronger were usually heavy weights and had more important business to attend to.

In short, Cai Jing served as the main force, displaying a critical role that allowed the alchemy here to go off without a hitch.

Zi Di’s group’s finished product looked like an ice gargoyle. It was the shape Zi Di was most familiar with, and also had mass popularity, conforming with the norms of society.

During the fight, the ice gargoyle performed well. Not only was it agile, but it also attacked first and even had rich expressions.

When it was wounded during the fight, the ice gargoyle actually resembled a living person, its frost calls of pain dumbfounding many spectators.

That was overthinking things a bit.

Cai Jing was an expert at micro-magic formations. His main method of cultivating magic plants was engraving all kinds of micro-formations on the outside and inside of magic plants, so these magic plants could hear his commands, and be used for other purposes like amplifying the magic plants’ power, or complementing the magic plants’ shortcomings, and so on.

During his duel with the dragonman youngster, the latter confirmed his strength. Like the explosive peppers, after he carved micro-formations into them, he could derive a person’s face into them.

The principle was through magic formations; he could bestow an object with spirituality.

The ice gargoyle was thus a living presentation.

And the ice sculpture guardians all had the same design – human soldier, blade in the right hand, shield in the left. They were balanced in all aspects and didn’t have any obvious shortcomings or strong points. The biggest failing was that they couldn’t fly.

The ice gargoyle could flutter and hover through the air, and not long after the battle began, it held the upper hand.

This kind of advantage was maintained, lasting until it turned it into a victory.

Thus, Zi Di’s group smoothly advanced.

“We passed!” When the dragonman youngster saw this, he laid down his worries and let out a breath.

When Zi Di returned, he merely nodded, then turned around and left the venue.

According to the plan, next he had to hand a matter immediately.

“You passed the second exam; you really have skills to go so far as to be able to persuade Cai Jing.” Elder Ju Jin secretly relaxed, since the survivors had leverage on him, he had no choice but to go along with their plans.

Jiu Jin looked towards Cai Jing, hiding a look of pity, “Elder Cai Jing’s prospects are dark. He personally took part in the exam and helped Yao Ma. What’s the difference between that and slapping Elder Hua Ni right in the face?”

“In the future, he is bound to be targeted by Hua Ni!”

Just as Jiu Jin was sighing ruefully, someone appointed by Hua Ni suddenly contacted him.

It went more or less like this: Hua Ni was considering Elder Jiu Jin’s contribution in the organization of the Warm Snow Cup. He was being “rewarded” by being promoted to the manager of the Alchemy Guild branch in Grey Stone City.

When Elder Jiu Jin heard this, he stared blankly, doubting his own ears.

Grey Stone City was very poor; they didn’t even have bird poop. Even if he was the Alchemy Guild branch leader there, it couldn’t compare to being a comfortably off elder in the capital. This was no reward; it was a penal sentence!

Finding it hard to believe, Jiu Jin ran through the crowd to the judging panel and asked Hua Ni about this.

Hua Ni coldly looked at him, her heart full of resenting what Cai Jing just did, and didn’t bother playing around. She directly said, “Jiu Jin, I haven’t investigated into your business with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps because of our past friendship.”

Jiu Jin felt like he fell into an icy hole in that split second.

“Hahaha, if Elder Jiu Jin doesn’t want to transfer, then it would be better if he excavated Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritances with me.” Cai Jing came up from behind, instead of returning to whence he came from, he instead walked over to the judging panel.

Cai Jing looked at Hua Ni and around him, then suddenly, he revealed a flower pot. Inside the flower pot was a gold level grafted magic plant.

His move immediately attracted everyone’s gaze.

Cai Jing publicly declared that this grafted magic plant was what lay behind the secret door of Great Master Fan Sheng Za Shong’s inheritance.

And as expected, those words caused everyone to cry out in surprise.

Some insiders suddenly realized where Cai Jing got the confidence to compete with Hua Ni – his meritorious service!

This immense merit was sufficient to turn a newly promoted elder like him into one that truly stood firm in the guild.

Cai Jing gazed at Jiu Jin, and continued, “I also have to thank you, Elder Jiu Jin, it was from you that I had a revelation. Therefore, this here is your merit too.”

Jiu Jin was astonished, he had no relations with Cai Jing, so why was he lying and defending for his sake?

But then Cai Jing secretly transmitted to Jiu Jin, quickly making things clear – this was all the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ plan!

For a moment, Jiu Jin was both alarmed and delighted.

Like Cang Xu expected, Hua Ni learned that Jiu Jin was being blackmailed by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps from the Li Jian Clan and was unreliable. She didn’t dismiss and replace Jiu Jin, but rather purposely let Jiu Jin take information, using that list of names as a trap, to scheme against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

On the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ side, under Cang Xu’s advisement, they saw through this trap but also didn’t contact Jiu Jin ahead of time to inform him that the survivors had already recruited Cai Jing so he could plan accordingly, this conversely, lulled Hua Ni and other enemies into a false confidence.

Hua Ni sat there on the judging panel, looking very ashen, everyone could see her ghastly mood!

Cai Jing helping Zi Di pass slapped her face.

Due to this humiliation, she tried to vent her anger on Elder Jiu Jin.

Instead of her anger being vented, Cai Jing skillfully turned things around.

Cai Jing was willing to give a part of his contribution to Jiu Jin. What grounds did Hua Ni have to send Jiu Jin away and demote him unofficially?

“What’s going on? How did Graft Town’s inheritance excavation undergo such immense progress all at once?’

Hua Ni had been paying close attention to this guild mission every day.

Because recently, there were many people begging to her, many guild members desiring to force their way in, and on the extreme end, some who wanted to directly suppress Magician Da Bei’s position for the sake of this lucrative job.

Da Bei’s external reports had always been that they had made zero progress.

This was zero progress?!

Hua Ni didn’t suspect Cai Jing was lying.

Because it was too easy to verify. Only an idiot would do that.

The other elders on the judging panel looked at each other.

The situation had repeatedly developed beyond their expectations.

Hua Ni was struck without warning and lost a good deal of face. Cai Jing’s side obviously came prepared.

Simultaneously, some in the audience looked at Magician Da Bei in the arena, their eyes carrying blame, resentment, while others were rejoicing in another’s misfortune.

Chapter 939: New Alchemy Guild Faction!

Da Bei had been appointed by the guild as Cai Jing’s supervisor.

When such massive progress was made, Magician Da Bei should have reported it.

But now, when Cai Jing publicly declared it, everyone knew.

Without a doubt, Magician Da Bei gravely neglected his duty, he must have joined Cai Jing and gotten bribed by the latter.

In short, he would be held accountable for this matter!

Magician Da Bei naturally sensed many gazing at him with ill intent, his heart filled with agony, but on the surface, he was calm and collected.

He walked out of the audience seating to the judging panel, and shouted, “As one of the participants in this, I personally experienced the entire excavation process, I can prove Elder Jiu Jin’s contribution was great and gave us a crucial enlightenment.”

When Magician Da Bei made this statement, he made clear he completely stood on Cai Jing’s side.

Contrary to belief, those words caused much of the countless malcontent gazes to dwindle.

Many people narrowed their eyes, staring at Cai Jing, Jiu Jin, and Da Bei standing near the judging panel, looking cautious.

Without a doubt, these three had at least reached a mutual alliance. From now on, these fellows would advance and retreat together in the Alchemy Guild for a long time.

Jiu Jin gave a look of thanks to Da Bei, he deliberated for a time, then publicly expressed that he was very honored that he could help Cai Jing. More importantly, he had a differing opinion on Hua Ni’s reassignment of him, and he wanted to report it to the Alchemy Guild President.

Cai Jing stated at once that he happened to have the time, so it would be better if they went together.

Undoubtably, bringing this accomplishment along would give Jiu Jin a platform to pressure the president.

Everyone’s expression changed again.

The ambience in the arena also became stifling.

Some politically savvy alchemists realized at the same time: a brand new faction was being born before their eyes!

Neither of the gold level magicians Jiu Jin or Da Bei had outstanding talent. Honestly speaking, many master alchemists on the same level looked down upon them, believing they were toadying too much for personal gain. But in the end, they were guild elders who had operated for many years, their accumulated contacts couldn’t be despised.

If the two united, they would be a threat, and they couldn’t compete openly against truly powerful elders like Hua Ni.

This was the world of the strong, and these two weren’t strong. Their alchemy didn’t make much contribution either and many of their contacts were transient.

The key was Cai Jing.

The core of this faction was undoubtedly Magician Cai Jing.

Cai Jing was outstanding in battle and alchemy mastery; thus the masses had to concede.

His greatest regret was that he was an outsider. He just joined the Alchemy Guild and wanted to gain the others’ trust and genuine acceptance, that needed time and opportunity.

But in the wake of these three allying, Cai Jing could borrow Jiu Jin’s and Da Bei’s networks, and his regret was already resolved to a degree!

Most of all, he acquired another of Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance. The latter was a grand legend level, and this was the second time Magician Cai Jing had opened his secret door. Apart from what this contribution represented, Cai Jing’s inheritance excavation strength had won greater popular approval.

Cai Jing had fully established himself!

With him and the other two combined, others were forced to face them squarely.

Many people were secretly praising Cai Jing’s brilliant skill in roping in Elder Jiu Jin.

By contrast, Elder Hua Ni had actively pushed away Jiu Jin, a truly faulty move.

Many people, even the gold level magicians, were not in the know. Hua Ni thought about fighting for Jiu Jin; however, she knew that Jiu Jin would always be under the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ thumb due to blackmail. So long as this evidence existed, she could never hope Jiu Jin would truly convert to her side. She would be unable to really trust Jiu Jin.

Hua Ni had no choice but to do this.

“This time, I’ve lost all face. You’ll all pay for this!” Hua Ni vowed in her thoughts, knowing she had no choice as she fumed with rage, “Everyone, leave. Right now is the Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest, now is not the setting to flaunt your contributions.”

“Regardless of what you think of your position Jiu Jin, or your contribution this time Cai Jing, it will be given to the Lord President to decide.”

“I wouldn’t worry about it.”

After saying that, Hua Ni’s expression inevitably withered with depression.

When everyone heard what she said, they all believed she had publicly admitted defeat.

“Then we’ll take our leave.” Cai Jing smiled, gave a magician’s courtesy, and calmly left with the posture of a winner.

Elder Jiu Jin and Da Bei followed close behind him.

His flaunting of his contribution and opposition to Hua Ni was all part of his collaboration agreement with the snow elf youngster.

He had accomplished everything that was agreed upon. Naturally, he left the dueling arena without a second thought.

“Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps…”

Before leaving, he swept his eyes across the audience seating but didn’t see Long Fu.

After leaving the dueling arena, Cai Jing was still deep in thought.

“After this incident, I have become completely antithetical to Hua Ni.”

“What is the arms merchant behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps planning?”

“Helping us will help them disrupt the Alchemy Guild’s willpower for hostilities.”

“It doesn’t matter, this is quite beneficial to me, isn’t that so?”

The reason why Cai Jing joined the Alchemy Guild was merely because it made inheritance excavation more convenient, he didn’t have many aspirations to curry favor. Although his bloodline had reached its limits, his individual strength still had plenty of room to grow.

The reason rested with that his main combat strength was from various magic plants.

Perhaps in the future he would change and become like Jiu Jin and Da Bei, but for now, he wasn’t thinking about doing so.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ backer’s method was brilliant.” Cai Jing’s eyes flashed, mulling over that shocking day.

Up until now, he was still unable to understand how the grafted plant the snow elf returned with, one obviously from his own hands, had lasted for so long. He had an outrageous answer that met the inheritance secret door’s criterion.

One must know that on Flower Skirt Island, he had spent a long time on countless attempts before he was able to obtain a finished product, He also had to use a large amount of magic medicine to maintain temporary stability, and through a dangerous path, he was barely able to meet the standard that would open the secret door.

By contrast, the grafted plant the snow elf youngster made was stable beyond imagination, it simply looked like an original lifeform.

Cai Jing spared no effort and was barely able to pass, while the snow elf youngster obviously had energy left over.

The contrast between the two was too distinct!

That undoubtedly stimulated Cai Jing’s curiosity. He longed to learn about the alchemy technology the snow elf youngster used.

If he could, he would be far more certain he could continue to excavate Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance.

Not just that, he also could use this alchemy technology in his magic plant cultivation and improve his strength by a wide margin!

There was nothing that could be done, the young man had enticed him too much!

As for Da Bei and Zhong Bei, apart from the allure of benefits, they chose to stand by Cai Jing because they were painfully forced into doing so.

Da Bei neglecting his duty like this was something he couldn’t explain to the president, he would certainly be blamed, and if it was severe enough, he’d be fired from his position as elder.

Even if he still retained his position of elder, what was the point if he would have been kicked to the boonies like Jiu Jin and disparaged from the capital?

On the one side there were massive benefits, on the other there was censure and punishment. Da Bei clenched his teeth and simply decided to rely on Cai Jing’s new mountain top.

“What are you all staring at, continue the competition!” Hua Ni frostily shouted, her expression ice cold.

She cursed secretly, “Just wait Cai Jing, do you really think you can succeed?”

“You know nothing of the kingdom’s high level politics.”

You think the president will concede? He’ll stand by and watch as you establish your faction?”

“You are too naïve! Hehe.”

Before Cai Jing returned to the Alchemy Guild headquarters, the event in the dueling arena had already been collected and summarized into a report that was delivered to the Alchemy Guild President.

The president felt his head aching.

As the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s Alchemy Guild President, he was a high ranking figure and a core member of the entire kingdom’s authority.

As a high ranking figure, he needed to be impartial and maintain the balance between all sides, he had to reward merit and punish negligence.

But if he only accomplished impartiality, he would never become the king’s confidant, be assigned as intermediary, and be in charge of the construction of the Millenium Dragon Formation.

The president knew in his mind: what high ranking people really needed to do was to protect themselves and the benefits of a particular crowd! The mount potion profits were quite considerable, and the Alchemy Guild had to value it. Therefore, they had to take control of it.

He wasn’t the only one approving of and supporting Hua Ni’s actions, the Ice Sculpture King was too.

“But I didn’t expect Cai Jing would emerge as a new force to be reckoned with, and form a brief alliance with Jiu Jin and Da Bei.”

“Fortunately, Shuang Lian is still trapped in Tranquil Hill. If she used the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as a bridge to unite with Cai Jing, then that would be terrible.”

Shuang Lian with her magician tower was aptly named a genuinely powerful cohort leader. Compared to her, Cai Jing’s foundation was superficial.

The Alchemy Guild President was at first, unaware of Shuang Lian’s situation.

But due to the royal family learning he was utilizing the guild’s valuable strength to look for clues so he could search and rescue Shuang Lian, the king personally summoned him.

“Jiu Jin and Da Bei are traitors that cannot be indulged.”

“Cai Jing’s contribution needs to be rewarded, but I absolutely cannot give up on holding Jiu Jin and Da Bei accountable because of it.”

“But doing that will enormously damage my country and my authority as president.”

The president carefully pondered and quickly thought of a plan to resolve this.

It was not necessary to hold them accountable at the moment.

First he would reward Cai Jing, then give him some fake clues to Great Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong’s inheritance, keeping him far from the capital, and at best, getting him away from Ice Sculpture Island!

“Let Cai Jing take Jiu Jin and Dai Bei with him, what the eye doesn’t see, the heart doesn’t grieve over.”

“Without them, who does the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps have in the Alchemy Guild?”

Just as he felt proud of himself, an urgent report came in and was delivered to the president’s hands.

“What?!” The Alchemy President jumped from his seat and onto his feet.

“Long Fu, Long Fu has done what?!”

In a split second, this high level figure’s calm had completely vanished, and his stratagem had turned into a corny joke.

Dueling Divine Domain.

The dragonman youngster had already arrived, and at this moment, he was standing on the summit of Tranquil Hill, looking in all directions and seeing the vast earth wherever his eyes went.

Honey Snow Tower’s outline was especially towering and conspicuous in the flat ground

Standing next to him, Gu Dong had an iron expression.

Chapter 940: Please Call me Duelist Long Fu!

Let’s turn time back a little, when Zi Di’s group passed and Cai Jing was walking towards the judging panel.

The dragonman youngster had already stood up and left the dueling arena.

He recalled his conversation with Cang Xu.

Cang Xu’s gaze was indistinct, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lacks people in the Alchemy Guild, since there aren’t any, let’s build one.”

“However, selecting Cai Jing as the core of this faction is not enough.”

“Jiu Jin and Da Bei aren’t much help; they aren’t truly core members of the high class.”

“If I were the Alchemy Guild President, I would have many ways of dealing with those with merits.”

“Therefore, we need this new faction to truly take root.”

Zi Di asked, “How should that be done?”

Cang Xu looked at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster’s mind stirred somewhat, “Speak, what do you need me to do?”

A smile crept into Cang Xu’s face, “Become a duelist, Lord Commander.”

The young man and lady were equally shocked.

Zi Di cried out in surprise, “How can that be done?”

Cang Xu’s smile grew, and he asked, “Are you not capable of it? Lord Commander, you can deceive even the Main Earth Elemental God to save Xiao Guai. Is it a question if you can deceive a nonexistent Dueling God?”

He had another thing to say but didn’t – the dragonman youngster had done repeated blasphemous prayers and obtained many divine blessings from Charming Blue’s Godhead. There was no reason he would falter against an incomplete Dueling Godhead.

The dragonman youngster sank into contemplation.

From his level of skill, becoming a duelist was no problem at all.

In addition, his memory forgery was proficient too. He was also quite experienced in blasphemous prayers and sacrifices.

“Look at Long Meng and the others, there are a few factors to become a duelist.”

“First is strength.”

“Second is being involved in duels.”

“Third is identifying oneself with duels from the bottom of one’s heart.”

“Strength is not a major factor, because as long as one is a transcendent, one can become a duelist. It’s just that low level transcendents aren’t qualified to set up a tombstone on Tranquil Hill’s summit.”

“In fact, the faith of ordinary people is a required thing for gods. According to the principle we can surmise that ordinary people can also become duelists. It’s just that in the Ice Sculpture dueling arenas, it is mostly transcendents contesting each other, while ordinary people are seldom seen in the arena.”

“The second factor is dueling activity. Each duelist has done many duels; this is a universal characteristic.”

“However, as a matter of fact, the second and third factors are essentially the same – it is faith!”

“Duels are themselves a sacrifice to the Dueling God. And acknowledgement of duels is faith unto itself.”

“Therefore, through forged memories and deception spell, you can disguise your faith.”

“With this foundation, you are likely to obtain the god’s summon and gain the choice to enter the Dueling Divine Domain!”

This speculation was something the dragonman youngster had considered before now.

In fact, since returning to Ice Sculpture Island, he had pondered this very matter.

Through this theory, he could immediately become a duelist!

But he hadn’t done so.

Because it was too dangerous!

Now faced with Cang Xu, the dragonman youngster let out his past concern, “If I become a duelist, it is likely I can enter and exit the Dueling Divine Domain, land on the top of Tranquil Hill and see those tombstones.”

“If that happens, the other duelists might become violent and proceed to gang up and attack me!”

“I am very worried that this move will provoke them too much. Therefore, previously I chose to pretend not to notice Mi Fang’s true identity and deliberately let him live.”

Cang Xu shook his head, “Lord Commander, I disagree with you in this respect.”

“Under the current situation, if you become a duelist, the other duelists won’t beleaguer you.”

Zi Di was puzzled, “If I were a duelist, I would definitely be worried about my identity and also Tranquil Hill being exposed by the newly arrived Commander. I would positively attack first!”

Cang Xu shook his head, asking a crucial question, “Miss Zi Di, do you feel that the duelists would expose Tranquil Hill of their own accord?”

Zi Di’s mind shook, realizing she had sunk into a misconception.

The duelists would never divulge the secret of the Dueling Divine Domain or Tranquil Hill!

The main reason was faith.

Faith was a unifying ideology.

Since faith had reached a set standard, would duelists, who acknowledged it from the heart, leak the pertinent secrets?

But then again, it was precisely because when one’s acknowledgement made it impossible to leak secrets that those who reached it were chosen to be duelists!

Cang Xu’s tone was pensive, “The current gold level duelists are a mixed bunch. The biggest faction is from a military background. What about the others?”

“Mi Fang is a human, a son-in-law of the Jing Xiang Clan. He Gai Tou was once a prisoner of Ice Prison and is currently running gambling houses. Yun Zhong is easygoing and has refused Mian Li Zang’s offers many times. Zhu Gan likes to fish all over the world; Qing Hong Yan is a rabbitman…

“If the royal family limited the duelists to those with a military background, we could understand why. But Mi Fang and the other outsiders?”

“Have they ever leaked these secrets?”

“The answer is no.”

“The power of faith is formidable, and from it, one’s thinking is altered and restricted. I believe they have never thought about exposing Tranquil Hill and the Dueling Divine Domain. They are like members of a happy family, unlikely to think of backstabbing their own parents.”

“This point is also proven by the Imperial Secret Spy Agency’s response.”

“The Imperial Secret Spy Agency has repeatedly tried to spy on Tranquil Hill and has failed repeatedly. This was why Lan Zao made comparatively large progress this time.”

“The Imperial Secret Spy Agency’s information gathering abilities are absolutely the best on the Main Plane. Yet even they are helpless in this aspect; this proves that the duelists all guard this secret.”

“This is their common and tacit understanding! It is the greatest place where they identify with each other.”

“If the Lord Commander’s disguise works, and he enters Tranquil Hill and becomes a duelist, the others will believe our commander won’t leak secrets, and that kind of trust hinges on how tightly they guard those same secrets.”

When Zi Di heard this, her eyes lit up, “So, it isn’t a big provocation?”

Cang Xu grunted, and nodded, “Definitely not as big as the Lord Commander exposing his saint level qualifications.”

The dragonman youngster facepalmed.

Zi Di blinked, and defended him, “The facts are already set in stone, it’s too late to say anything about it. Since the Lord Commander has already exposed his saint level qualifications, sooner or later, he will be targeted. We might as well become duelists to give the others a shock! Let it be payback for these treacherous people who always deal with us in the dark!”

Cang Xu continued, “Of course, simply relying on faith is not completely secure. Because faith can change, and people in this secular world belong to all kinds of factions.”

“Therefore, it is very possible that those picked to become duelists and enter into Dueling Divine Domain should be bound by some divine spell contract.”

“Thus, when the Lord Commander becomes a duelist and goes into the Dueling Divine Domain, the newcomer should be enticed and placated.”

“In more detail.” The dragonman youngster pressed.

Cang Xu explained, “Tranquil Hill’s duelists are actually in a similar situation to the Alchemy Guild.”

“Although they are a collective, their internal structure is a mess, and apart from the military faction, there is no other mature faction.”

“The ones actually dealing with us are the military background duelists. We killed Teng Dong Lang, the Axe Gang Leader, Jia Bing, and Lin. The loss of four gold levels produced a very deep enmity that is hard to thoroughly mediate, but it can be alleviated. We have the corpses of three of the gold levels.”

“As for the others…”

“Can we turn Mi Fang into a friend? I have come to see Mi Fang as weak. It is completely possible to pressure him, then use benefits to move him.”

“Zhu Gan and Yun Zhong have not attacked us, and are naturally undisciplined and free, we can peacefully coexist with them.”

“He Gai Tou helped Bing Yang play a dirty trick on us, and I reckon he is close to the military faction. But that doesn’t matter, his gambling houses are huge businesses, and that is his actual weak spot!”

“The most crucial person is Long Meng.”

“Long Meng decided to show benevolence, and he called over the Lord Commander to give him practical assistance. Does he really just admire you Lord Commander? Or is there a feeling from deep within his heart that stems from his high longing for duels and the arrogance of his draconic nature that drives him to mold a powerful enemy that will increase his thrill in battle?”

“There is another possibility, is Long Meng trying to rope in the Lord Commander? Is he investing in the future where the Lord Commander could become a duelist?”

The dragonman youngster’s eyes shined, Cang Xu’s words were like lightning that split open the dense fog in his brain.

Cang Xu then said, “Long Meng is a dragonman, his racial identity already proves much.”

“I surmise that apart from the royal family being involved with the duelists, the White Dragon King might be involved too. Long Meng might be their arrangement.”

Cang Xu sighed, “The Ice Sculpture Kingdom has three saint levels, the King, the Royal Grand Magician, and the White Dragon King.”

“What is the relationship and contest of benefits between these three? Is the contract of alliance between the royal family and the White Dragon Clan robust? Is the Dueling Godhead precious enough to create cracks in their alliance?”

“In short, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s political atmosphere is very subtle. This can be seen when Da Han attacked. During the naval battle, none of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s three saint levels appeared.”

“Even now, the pirates are still devastating the kingdom’s coastal waters.”

Cang Xu’s political acumen was simply unparalleled!

His analysis of human nature was even more profound.

Due to his proposal, the dragonman youngster forged the relevant memories and designed the corresponding prayer.

After Zi Di advanced, the young man needed to act.

“Dueling God, my Lord, my Paramount.”

“The intertwined snowy peaks and glaciers, the inexhaustible wind and frost, hide Your divine domain from view. Oh, Dueling God, Your honor travels through time with resplendent radiance.”

“It is You who lets heroic swords clash together, You who bestows tenacious shields.”
