“Under Your protection, warriors notice the fountainhead of strength in the light of dawn, and the windstorm of war turns into a breezing fight.”

“In Your almightiness, You forged the order of duels, each sandpit turns into an arena where the brave refine with fire and allow former enemies to turn hostility into friendship under Your awe-inspiring gaze.”

“Under Your holy gaze, I recount Your sacred creed like a poem in every duel. Hereto I pray that You let this pious one immerse himself in Your glorious favor. Please let me into Your everlasting Dueling Kingdom, let me become one of Your holy warriors that will eternally protect Your honor and power.”

The dragonman youngster kowtowed, silently praying.

After silently praying two or three times, there was spatial fluctuation.

A Divine Domain Descent spell!

This time, it wasn’t Charming Blue’s divine power driving it but Dueling divine power.

The descent spell enveloped the dragonman youngster, producing both a familiar and unfamiliar feeling.

As he slowly opened his eyes, he saw the floor tiles had become hilly rocks.

He gradually stood up straight and straightened his chest. The sound of the wind surrounded his ears, but the cold air couldn’t conceal his fiery red dragon scales.

He looked around and saw he was already at Tranquil Hill’s summit.

Two gold level auras were wildly dashing towards the dragonman youngster.

Today, it was Gu Dong’s and Yi Jiu’s turn to be on duty.

Shuang Lian’s deployment of alchemy puppets as well as gold level elementals had stormed in and nearly taken Tranquil Hill.

Since then, Mei Lin made it so there was no longer just one gold level duelist posted, but two.

Gu Dong and Yi Jiu were several hundred meters away from the dragonman youngster when they stopped moving.

Both of their eyes were equally opened wide.

They had reacted to a newcomer coming in, and with pleasant surprise, they ran over, but as they got closer, they sensed the dragonman youngster’s transcendent aura.

“This transcendent aura, it seems a bit familiar!” The two felt things were far from good.

Eventually, when they saw the rightful owner, the two’s hearts immediately sank to rock bottom.

“Crap! It’s Long Fu?!”

“That’s absurd, how can it be him? It really is him!!”

Gu Dong stood in place flabbergasted; he was a soldier and had an honest temperament. Then as he saw the duelists coming in to mix with Long Fu, he couldn’t wrap his head around it.

What’s going on, it seemed like…an enemy suddenly turned into one of them?

Yi Jiu narrowed his eyes; he was a thief. He had a very flexible bottom line and could accept Long Fu becoming a duelist.

But he did attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, killing their only “magicians” at the time.

Long Fu was the comrade of his victims.

“He didn’t know about us duelists, and now he has been chosen and has appeared on the top of Tranquil Hill…those tombstones are the best evidence!” Yi Jiu’s heart beat wildly.

The dragonman youngster was unable to take his eyes off the tombstones, constantly taking measure of the names written on them.

Then a good while later, he slowly turned around to look at Gu Dong and Yi Jiu.

“You two, it looks like you can give me an explanation?” He appeared to understand somewhat, vigilantly looking towards Gu Dong and Yi Jiu, simultaneously revealing feelings of indignity, suspicion, and other intense moods.

“We’re finished, he noticed!” Gu Dong and Yi Jiu were equally dark.

Gu Dong had terrible eloquence and didn’t speak.

Yi Jiu had a dry mouth and tongue, saying after a good while, “This place is my God’s divine domain. The Dueling Divine Domain. Tranquil Hill is my Lord’s holy land, I believe you have heard of the legend of Tranquil Hill Sire Long Fu.”

“You know me?” The dragonman youngster asked.

Yi Jiu squeezed out a smile, using a touch of praise, “Of course, you are currently a household name nationwide, a dueling star.”

“You being selected by my Lord to become a duelist is also reasonable.”

As he said that, Yi Jiu signaled Gu Dong with his eyes.

Gu Dong was in a daze, fundamentally incapable of comprehending Yi Jiu’s intent.

Yi Jiu couldn’t help but give him a disdainful look, he was then forced to say, “Lord Long Fu, don’t be tense, this holy land is safe.”

He decided to steady the dragonman youngster first, he didn’t want to fight the dragonman youngster.

More importantly, he had to immediately transmit the news out.

He shouldn’t be the explainer or host.

How to explain it all to Long Fu was troublesome, Yi Jiu was overwhelming thinking about it, so he decided to leave it to another.

The duelists could draw support from their shared faith for urgent communications; they just needed to expend divine favor to do so.

Very quickly, the explosive news that the dragonman youngster became a duelist and was already on the summit of Tranquil Hill was passed into each duelist’s mind. Through the duelists, this was rapidly reported to the high level powers behind them.

The dragonman youngster sized up the horizon with rapt attention, and after a good while, he suddenly left.

“Huh?! Lord Long Fu, where are you going?” Yi Ju promptly inquired.

Gu Dong was standing silently in the path of where the dragonman youngster was headed.

The dragonman youngster narrowed his eyes and began to emit a dangerous aura, his finger pointing at a lofty tower, “That magician tower looks just like Honey Snow Tower? So as it turns out, Shuang Lian and my corps’ Bu Quan have fallen into this place?!”

Yi Jiu’s eye twitched.

Concerning this event, he had participated in the entire thing.

“Calm down, Lord Long Fu, please calm down a little, don’t be impulsive.” Said Yi Jiu.

The dragonman youngster stared at Gu Dong, coldly shouting, “You want to intercept me? Are you sure you want to do that?”

Gu Dong was already coldly sweating all over.

The pressure on him was too much.

Despite Long Fu showing off his battle strength in duels, it hadn’t been excessive. But the duelists had reached a consensus that Long Fu was very dangerous. He had a mysterious method that effortlessly slew Jia Bing and two others. When the scene was investigated, the three gold levels didn’t show a trace of resistance!

Thanks, Zong Ge.

His blustering tactics always had a massive deterrence effect.

When the dragonman youngster confronted Yi Jiu and Gu Dong, he was able to dominate both without even attacking.

“Oh dear, I’d better explain.” After space undulated, a voice was heard.

The dragonman youngster turned around to see Long Meng.

“Sire Long Meng.” The dragonman youngster stared at him, his dangerous aura dwindling, “I also saw your name on the tombstones.”

Long Meng nodded, smiling at the young man, “As you saw, I am just like you – a Dueling God holy warrior!”

“Dueling God? What is all this about?” The dragonman youngster played the role of a greenhorn.

Long Meng took measure of the dragonman youngster, his eyes revealing his admiration, “Although I was mentally prepared for this, believing that you could become a duelist, Long Fu, you were chosen far sooner than I expected.”

He then sighed, “I know you have many doubts, so I happened to find another friend to explain things. Allow me to save you from misunderstandings caused by gossip, first let’s go to Honey Snow Tower together.”

When Long Meng said that, the young man was genuinely flabbergasted somewhat.

At once, the four left together and hurried to Honey Snow Tower.

After a certain distance, Gu Dong and Yi Jiu halted. After all, these two had once besieged Honey Snow Tower, and to not trigger misunderstandings, they were considerate.

And so, Long Meng and Long Fu slowly approached Honey Snow Tower.

An alarm quickly rang through Honey Snow Tower.

“Enemies, according to scans, it’s two gold level dragonmen fighters!” The tower spirit reported.

Shuang Lian and Bu Quan were resting, and after getting the warning, they immediately got up and entered into the main control room at the top of Honey Snow Tower.

A moment later, master and disciple cried out in alarm simultaneously, “Oh, it’s him!”

“Long Meng (Long Fu)?!”

Shuang Lian and Bu Quan were pleasantly surprised.

Then, master and disciple subconsciously looked at each other.

The atmosphere was a little awkward and bizarre.

Chapter 941: Shuang Lian and Bu Quan vs Long Meng and Long Fu

The center of the Ice Sculpture Capital, there was a lofty pure white castle centered on a tower.

It was the Royal Stronghold, the center of Ice Sculpture Kingdom politics.

Information about the dragonman youngster becoming a duelist and appeared on the top of Tranquil Hill was immediately passed to the Ice Sculpture King.

Any unforeseen events about the Dueling Divine Domain was urgent information.

The Ice Sculpture King immediately shelved his current business and quickly read the report.

“A new duelist was born; it’s Long Fu.” The Ice Sculpture King’s eyes shrank slightly, then he soon sank into contemplation.

According to common sense, critical information related to the Dueling God shouldn’t have been leaked.

However, the Ice Sculpture Royal Family took charge of this operation, and successive generations of kinds vigorously developed it and never suppressed dueling activities. The royal family had massive control over the Dueling Divine Domain.

Or in other words, over the years, members of the royal family were duelists.

It’s just that their names weren’t qualified to be listed on the tombstones, just like the Bayonet Gang’s Bing Xiao.

In fact, as successive kings energetically popularize duels, their faith inherently inclined towards the Dueling God. Even if it wasn’t their main religion, their dedication to the Dueling Godhead’s calculated value was absolutely enormous.

In this aspect, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency were far from the Ice Sculpture Royal Family.

“It really was possible for Long Fu to become a duelist. He has gained too much fame and profit from duels. Those that benefit that much from them usually love duels completely, and this kind of love can produce strong dueling faith.”

“Having this kind of faith is a good thing.”

The Ice Sculpture King’s foresight exceeded that of normal people.

To him it seems that Long Fu was a capable subordinate of an international arms merchant. Now, this capable subordinate had faith in the Dueling God, tantamount to his thinking being altered and being restricted.

This kind of restriction was undoubtedly advantageous to the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

One must know that Long Fu had saint level qualifications!

“Eradicate Long Fu?” For a split second, the king’s mind really emitted that notion, but he quickly threw it to the back of his head.

Why would he want to do that?

If he was worried about foreigners, he wouldn’t have allowed people like Mi Fang, Zhu Gan, and Yun Zhong to become duelists.

And compared to those people, Long Fu could produce more benefits.

“Long Fu’s aptitude is amazing; he is worth recruiting.”

“Perhaps there is much room to collaborate in some areas with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ arms merchant backer.”

The Ice Sculpture King was very interested in the survivors’ backer.

Just like Cang Xu analyzed, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and the Sheng Ming Empire were embroiled in a fierce game. As the ruler of his nation, the Ice Sculpture King was willing to work with any power or individual that could help him deal with the Sheng Ming Empire.

And he also understood that just relying on the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was not enough to resist the massive Sheng Ming Empire.

As for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ killing of Teng Dong Lang and the other three gold level duelists, the Ice Sculpture King’s reaction was not as hard lined as Mei Lin, Bao Li Gen, Gu Dong, and the others.

He was a high ranking person, a statesman. In his eyes, these duelists were all his pawns, and not his comrades.

He could more calmly reflect, and even callously analyze the pros and cons based on pure profit and loss comparison.

“However, who is their arms merchant backer?”

“I have already deployed many people to inquire about this, yet in spite of everything, none have made any progress.”

“Divination spells have also been used a lot, yet nothing was gained!”

The more investigations failed to find the “mysterious arms merchant”, the higher the Ice Sculpture King’s evaluation of them was.

He never doubted the arms merchant actually existed.

Because Long Fu was present.

He was an efficient subordinate with saint level qualifications, the one the “mysterious arms merchant” had dispatched to break into a new market. This illustrated that this “mysterious arms merchant” had courage and was certain to be very formidable.

“Since the arms merchant can send a future saint level, they must have provided him with considerable amounts of skills and trump cards.”

The Ice Sculpture King had a similar judgment as Mi Fang.

From this judgement, one could see Zong Ge’s 3 v 1 against the duelists as very reasonable.

This was also why the Ice Sculpture King felt like he shouldn’t attack Long Fu.

If he attacked, he’d have to face Long Fu’s many trump cards. A saint level teleportation scroll could spoil everything and would also offend the “mysterious arms merchant” and spoil a future collaboration before it could be realized.

After all, the Ice Sculpture Capital didn’t block teleportation.

Apart from that, the domestic political ambience was layered. The Sheng Ming Empire and Da Han were external pressures that made the Ice Sculpture King very cautious and unwilling to attack so recklessly.

Past battle records already proved that Long Fu was someone no normal gold level could deal with. Deploying a saint level to handle Long Fu was also not guaranteed to work, and crucially, even if they did succeed, the profits would be slimmer.

The Ice Sculpture King didn’t have the motivation to attack Long Fu, at least not right now.

“In that case, let’s focus on placating him.”

“The current trouble now is his decent relationship with Shuang Lian, he will definitely stand up for her.”

The Ice Sculpture King frowned slightly.

Shuang Lian was a truly powerful Alchemy Guild elder, and she controlled Honey Snow Tower. Remember, this was a magician tower in the capital!

This was no simple building, but a formidable military fort.

The Ice Sculpture King naturally knew about Shuang Lian and also valued her considerably.

Shuang Lian’s magician tower was built with royal family funds. Because Shuang Lian was a former champion of the Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest.

Shuang Lian’s demiplane mainly produced honey snow; she was a primary supplier of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s military!

To sum it up, Shuang Lian was one of his people.

However, the Dueling Divine Domain secret was too high level, and Shuang Lian was unaware of it.

The Ice Sculpture King also didn’t want her to know.

But as it happens, Shuang Lian did know! She was not a duelist, was not restricted by faith, and wasn’t bound by a divine spell contract.

The Ice Sculpture King’s plan to deal with her was to temporarily imprison her in the Dueling Divine Domain and not allow her any connection with the outside world to protect the secrets of the Dueling Divine Domain to the greatest degree.

On the one hand, this was a temporary measure. After the Dueling Godhead was complete, handling Shuang Lian would be easy and simple. On the other hand, the Ice Sculpture King also wanted to seize the opportunity to suppress Shuang Lian and grind his subordinate’s haughtiness and make future assignments easier.

But that was no longer possible.

The Ice Sculpture King quickly recognized that if he wanted to placate Long Fu, he had to inform Shuang Lian of some secrets and placate her too.

“It just so happened the Dueling Divine Domain’s defenses are still somewhat low, let’s have Honey Snow Tower make up for that a bit.”

After pondering, the king gave orders, including a notification to the Alchemy Guild President.

The king didn’t know that in the wake of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corp’s intervention, a new Alchemy Guild faction had been born, the king wouldn’t have paid attention to such a trivial matter.

His announcement was given to the guild president, immediately causing the latter’s plan to target Cai Jing to collapse.

The order was also transmitted to Gu Dong, disappointing him, but also letting him relax, realizing how he should treat Long Fu and was no longer at a loss of what to do.

Inside Honey Snow Tower.

Long Meng and Long Fu, as well as Shuang Lian and Bu Quan were sitting opposite each other, drinking tea.

The four of them were the only ones in the room.

Bu Quan couldn’t help but keep staring at the dragonman youngster, pleasantly surprised to the utmost. Her sweetheart appearing like this to save her made her overjoyed.

Shuang Lian stared at Long Meng, “I didn’t expect that there was such a secret you have never told me about.”

Her tone hid bitterness.

The dragonman youngster was immediately drawn by this, his eyes flickering between Shuang Lian and Long Meng.

These two had history!

Long Meng sighed, “You aren’t a duelist, and if I were to say anything, your freedom would be restricted, jailed somewhere and cut off from the outside world, much like how you are now.”

Shuang Lian coldly snorted, “But now I know, and as it turns out, this place is actually the arrangement of successive generations of kings, the scale is mindboggling!”

“Why did you decide to inform me about this now?”

Long Meng turned to look at the dragonman youngster, “Because of him.”

He briefly stated the reasons and details.

Bu Quan’s eyes flashed, holding her teacup with an excited expression, “Lord Commander, you once again saved me! I really don’t know how to thank you.”

The dragonman youngster fixed his eyes on Bu Quan, his tone sincere and also slightly ashamed, “Since you disappeared, I have always been trying to find you.”

“Despite many people saying you were already dead, I never believed that.”

“My heart told me that you must be alive. You are the one I recruited, and I cannot let any of my people die so easily!”

“Rationally speaking, I never believed that you would lose your life while you were inside Honey Snow Tower and under your teacher’s protection. You just went missing.”

“Alas!” The dragonman youngster sighed, “Truth be told, I didn’t think I’d happen to find you here.”

“When I saw a magician tower resembling Honey Snow Tower, I rushed over here!”

“Lord Commander…” Bu Quan was moved to gleaming tears.

Long Meng was shifting his eyes between Long Fu and Bu Quan.

These two had history!

“Cough, cough, cough!” Shuang Lian firmly coughed, her expression becoming strict.

Her beloved disciple’s liberal attitude really made her furious and frustrated.

What she loathed even more was that this dragoman was actually stirring up the emotions of her disciple right in front of her, simply not paying any heed to her, Shuang Lian.

At once, Shuang Lian gloomily said to the dragonman youngster, “Commander Long Fu, I still want to thank you. If you hadn’t recruited Bu Quan into your mercenary corps, she wouldn’t have come across such danger. If she hadn’t gotten into danger, I wouldn’t have come to save her. By logical deduction, the reason we are stuck in the Dueling Divine Domain is because of you.”

The dragonman youngster promptly stood up and bowed to Shuang Lian, thanking her for her assistance while also taking out a large sum of money to compensate her and Bu Quan.

“Do I care about your money?” Shuang Lian looked exceedingly disdainful.

She had money, a lot of money.

The dragonman youngster knew how she’d react, as Cang Xu had already deduced it for him.

The dragonman youngster pretended to be stunned, then clenched his teeth and sorrowfully resolved that he could try to use some alchemy blueprints as an apology.

This suggestion immediately caught Shuang Lian’s interest.

At the time, the reason why she helped the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, apart from Bu Quan persuading her, was that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ alchemy knowledge truly moved her.

If Long Fu really took out alchemy knowledge of the appropriate level, then Shuang Lian couldn’t bear to refuse this suggestion.

Bu Quan grinded her teeth, filling with worry, “Lord Commander, if it is too difficult for you, then forget it. Knowledge is precious…”

“Shut up, you!” When Shuang Lian saw her disciple siding with an outsider, she immediately became irate and bellowed out coldly.

Bu Quan was cowed by her teacher’s reprimand, utterly afraid.

Long Meng chuckled, coming over to help smooth things over, “Being young is good, Shuang Lian, there is no need to make things difficult for two young’uns.”

Shuang Lian glared at Long Meng, her eyes as sharp as blades, “Long Meng, there is something I need to ask you. You said you were a duelist and are freed to go in and out of the divine domain.”

“You already knew I was trapped here.”

“Why did you come only now? What were you doing before then?”

A deadly question, a soul wrenching question!

Long Meng was immediately struck dumb, bewildered by it.

This was the first time Long Fu saw Long Meng like this, and he felt his horizons broadening.

In the dragonman youngster’s memory, Long Meng was powerful, and in each duel, he performed directly and efficiently with extraordinary might. Long Meng was also calm, wise, and farsighted. He tutored the dragonman youngster in combat techniques, used Resonance Force to immensely help the dragonman youngster, and allowed the latter to quickly grasp many kinds of forces.

Long Meng being stifled and at a loss for words honestly caused the dragonman youngster to…secretly cheer.

“Long Meng and Shuang Lian must have a story with each other. Perhaps Long Meng is indebted to Shuang Lian.”

“This is great, I didn’t expect them to have such a layered relationship.”

With Long Meng attracting the fire, the pressure on the dragonman youngster lessened greatly.

After this conversation, he seized the opportunity to provide a plan for a collaboration between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Shuang Lian.

“My corps needs to stabilize its footing in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and is currently eager to part ways with the Li Jian Clan…” The dragonman youngster explained his situation.

He proposed that he could sell honey snow as well as other alchemy products on behalf of Shuang Lian.

Long Meng told Shuang Lian that in the short term, she still had to stay in the Dueling Divine Domain and couldn’t return to the Ice Sculpture Capital just yet.

The dragonman youngster used this as a pretext to tell Shuang Lian that while Shuang Lian was missing, many things happened in the Alchemy Guild. Hua Ni, Snow Cake Tower, and others were all targeting her with rampant and repulsive attitudes, Shuang Lian’s influence was in the process of being dwindled.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Shuang Lian reached cooperation at this time, selling honey snow and other acts on behalf of her would be simple, yet significant, it was sufficient enough to transmit the important information outwards that Shuang Lian was still alive.

This would enormously retrieve Shuang Lian’s violently dropping influence, furthermore, her adversaries within the guild wouldn’t be as rampant, no longer wanting a foot after winning an inch.

Shuang Lian turned it over in her mind, and under the wide open gaze of Bu Quan, finally agreed to this collaboration.

After taking their leave, Long Meng felt he was pardoned.

Bu Quan was reluctant to part, constantly gazing at the dragonman youngster’s back as it faded from view.

“Still looking?” Shuang Lian coldly snorted.

She was obviously also watching just like Bu Quan, staring at Long Meng’s back.

Bu Quan asked, “Teacher, it seems you are somewhat biased against the Lord Commander?”

Shuang Lian exploded, “What bias? There is no bias!”

“I have come to tell you that draconic nature is lascivious, these dragonmen are not good people, and are naturally disposed to licentiousness. Bu Quan, you are not experienced in the world, especially in romance, you were easily lured by a few words.”

“It is very detrimental to you.”

“Alas, back in the day…”

Shuang Lian didn’t speak as she recalled something.

“What happened to you. Teacher, then?” Bu Quan pricked up her ears, straining to hear what it was, but Shuang Lian didn’t speak, and she could bear to coax it out of the latter.

Shuang Lian broke from her thoughts and returned to reality, seeing her favored disciple looking up at her with wide and curious eyes.

Shuang Lian raised her index finger and flicked Bu Quan in the head, resentfully turning around, “Go back!”

“Time for combat training.”

“I’ll give you three alchemy assignments to write up on.”

Chapter 942: Mi Fang – Long Fu, You Are a Devil!

Ice Sculpture Capital.

“Clan Leader, you summoned me?” Mi Fang had the whiff of wine on him as he staggered into the study.

“Look at yourself!” The Jing Xiang Clan Leader had specifically hurried to the capital from his territory for this, and he showed no quarter in his reprimands, “Mi Fang this isn’t how you should appear. It was just one loss, yet you’ve been drowning your sorrows all day and been extremely depressed!”

“Yes, you were stripped from your position.”

“But you brought this upon yourself, not I. It was done according to clan rules.”

“Your duel with Long Fu really disappointed people!”

Mi Fang was silent and sullen.

His defeat to Long Fu cast him from heaven into hell.

The authority he gained from the mount potion business was expropriated jointly by the primed factions within the Jing Xiang Clan.

Mi Fang didn’t just lose the power he gained beyond his limits, but also the power base he once had before all this too.

The implication of this beating was very clear!

The Jing Xiang Clan Elder sighed; he stood up from his chair, walked around the desk, and faced Mi Fang.

Mi Fang took a nervous step back.

The clan leader nevertheless extended a hand and helped him sit down on the guest seat.

The clan leader’s voice became gentler, “Your loss to Long Fu was too ugly.”

“It wasn’t just your reputation that slumped, the entire clan has also suffered immensely too.”

“You will climb back up from where you fell. Mi Fang! The clan still believes in you, I also want to give you a chance to strive again.”

“I specifically came over from our territory just for you!”

“Go challenge Long Fu, fight him again in the arena! Grab your mettle and win. You need to shatter your nightmare with your own hands and stand up like a man again.”

Mi Fang’s mind and body shook, and he stared open eyed at the Jing Xiang Clan Leader.

The latter looked earnest and serious.

But Mi Fang’s heart was ice-cold. He knew Long Fu’s strength, how he easily won a 3 v 1. Challenging him again was courting disaster.

Regretfully, very few apart from him knew this truth.

The outside world universally believed that Mi Fang’s tactics were too conservative, too mindful of the gains and losses, and he fundamentally didn’t display the fighting strength he should have.

Gods above!

“If I really displayed my power, I fear I’d already be dead!” Mi Fang knew the severity, but the outside world didn’t.

Lots of his supporters, many of which were women, spurned him.

His friends couldn’t understand him; his family were unable to console him either.

“Lord Clan Leader, I am not Long Fu’s match!” Mi Fang shook his head.

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader raised his head, turning from a leaning posture to an upright one as he walked around the desk and towards the master seat.

During this process, with his back to Mi Fang, lightly stated, “That is the reason why I brought you this.”

As he sat down again, there was already a small potion bottle laid out on the desk.

Under the lighting, the potion glistened with a scarlet light and looked very strange.

Mi Fang was an outstanding pharmacist, and when he saw this potion, his expression changed, “A devil transformation potion?”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader nodded, “This is a saint level potion, it can transform you into a devil for a short time and increase your battle strength enough for you to defeat Long Fu.”

Mi Fang’s brows wrinkled, “But the residual effects of this transformation potion are very strong.”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader shrugged his shoulders, “This is the biggest help I can give you. Mi Fang, you are a pharmacist, you can bear this kind of sequelae, and it is unlikely to drop you from the gold level.”

“You must defeat Long Fu!”

“It was he who threw you into the abyss.”

“The clan also needs you to defeat Long Fu, it would revitalize our prestige.”

“Your current circumstances were bestowed upon you by Long Fu.”

Mi Fang hesitated, “No, Long Fu really isn’t my mortal enemy, and there is no need to use such desperate measures to deal with him.”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader sneered, “Come up with a bit of courage, Mi Fang!”

“You think I don’t know?”

“Through the other duelists, you plotted against Long Fu and tried to poison him.”

“Long Fu isn’t your mortal enemy, what? It was precisely because of him that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ mount potion business is so prosperous, and from beginning to end, has dominated the largest market share.”

“You must know that the Alchemy Guild has already acted. If we don’t shoot down the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in time, my clan’s future mount potion business might take a dive and be supplanted.”

The study sank into a deathly silence; the atmosphere was quite heavy.

After a good while, Mi Fang took a deep breath, “I need to think it over.”

“Think well!” The Jing Xiang Clan Leader stood up and left the study. The devil transformation potion was left quietly sitting on the desk before Mi Fang’s eyes.

Mi Fang didn’t know how he left the study.

His thinking was very chaotic, he didn’t know when the potion came into his hand.

When he came to his bedroom, he was astonished to find his wife was already waiting for him.

After a touching scene, his wife hugged him and tenderly advised, “Challenge Long Fu again, go duel him and win this crucial battle; the potion’s residual effects are unimportant, your contribution to the clan for this will ensure you are handsomely paid.”

Mi Fang suddenly felt ice-cold, and he looked at his lovely wife. His wife’s eyes were tender and soft as water, concealing her calculations.

But Mi Fang didn’t rebuke her.

His marriage to her was due to a cost benefit analysis. When he shamefully lost, his wife had to bear immense pressure from the clan.

Mi Fang slowly closed his eyes, his voice somewhat hoarse, “I am tired, let me sleep first.”

He fell into a deep sleep, waking up at noon the next day.

Fate’s tribulations continued.

He received three massive pieces of news.

The first was that Yao Ma’s group had smoothly advanced and passed the Warm Snow Cup’s second exam. Furthermore, Cai Jing and the others built a new faction, becoming the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ partner in the Alchemy Guild. The Alchemy Guild approved of Cai Jing’s contribution and had no choice but to stand by and watch as this faction was established.

The second was that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps began to sell a large amount of honey snow. Crucially, this honey snow originated from Shuang Lian’s demiplane. According to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ external explanation, the missing Shuang Lian had actually been doing confidential study and research and was unable to appear right now.

The third came from the duelists. He, Long Fu, had become a duelist!!

This all happened yesterday.

Mi Fang had a better grasp of this information than the Jing Xiang Clan Leader, and after consulting the other duelists, he quickly reconstructed the facts and truly understood the situation.

Mi Fang’s body and mind were in incomparable agony.

His foe had taken a massive step forward, while he was consigned to the infernal abyss.

In his pain, he also began to feel regret.

“Perhaps, from the start, he was wrong to attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Now that this matter has reached this point, there is no use in saying anything.”

Mi Fang’s mind was inundated with hatred.

“The reason why I am in these circumstances is all due to Long Fu!”

He was already forced to the end of his rope.

He was a Jing Xiang Clan son-in-law, already bound to this clan. Even if he wanted to cut ties and jump ship, which noble would accommodate him?

Could he leave the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and grow in another nation?

Just thinking about it caused Mi Fang to despair.

This was the place he had arduously worked for many years; he had given his youth to this ice-cold land. Could he renounce these things and start afresh?

As a gold level, it wasn’t like he didn’t have other chances to grow.

But in truth, duels weren’t popular in other places. Duels, joining his wife’s clan, these shortcuts allowed Mi Fang to save a lot of time and energy. He was already accustomed to surviving and growing in this kind of environment.

Mi Fang tried to contact Mei Lin again.

Mei Lin was exhausted. During this period, she had been attacking all over the place, taking on the incomparably difficult challenge of keeping the seaboard safe.

But the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s seaboard was very long, and it was difficult for the First Navy Fleet alone to ensure it was totally secure.

Most dreadful was Da Han hadn’t attacked yet. He was like a sword hanging over everyone’s head, no one knew when it would suddenly fall and behead them.

The psychological pressure from military affairs physically and emotionally exhausted Mei Lin.

At this moment, she was still accepting startling news – Long Fu unexpectedly became a duelist and also climbed to the top of Tranquil Hill! After that, Long Meng took Long Fu to Honey Snow Tower and both sides reached a cooperation agreement.

That instantly washed away all of Mei Lin’s prior efforts.

And what made Mei Lin even more depressed was the command she received from the Ice Sculpture Royal Family yesterday.

She comprehended the royal family’s opinion; she was to stop attacking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and instead use mainly appeasement strategies.

Due to this, Mi Fang couldn’t get any more outside support from the duelists.

After the connection with Mei Lin ended, for a split second, Mi Fang had a terrible feeling that the world had abandoned him.

“Look at the letter.” Suddenly, a voice could be heard.

“Who?” Mi Fang’s hair rose, his body and mind wildly jolting.

Gold level battle qi bubbled out, covering his body in a split second.

But he couldn’t find the source of the sound, he only found a letter on the desktop.

“When did this sealed letter appear? It obviously wasn’t there a moment ago…” A strong chill rapidly pervaded Mi Fang’s heart.

He looked at the letter with rapt attention for a good while, then extended his hand and slowly opened it.

The letter’s contents caused his eyes to suddenly contract.

Ten minutes later.

He arrived at a restaurant.

In a private room, the dragonman youngster was feasting on magic beast meat, it was half cooked and still reeked considerably of blood.

The dragonman youngster’s sharp teeth were constantly chewing, mashing the flesh between them into bits.

Mi Fang entered the private room and saw this exact scene.

It was only him and the dragonman youngster in the private room.

But Mi Fang knew it wasn’t just the dragonman youngster here, there had to be an expert hiding elsewhere.

Mi Fang was impolite, coldly sitting across from the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster was busy eating the meat and didn’t bother raising his head to look at Mi Fang as he said, “My time is limited, I will speak to you directly.”

“Mi Fang, come over to my side.”

Mi Fang didn’t anticipate this, he almost believed he misheard it.

A moment later, he angrily cackled, “Hehehehe, it’s all thanks to you I am in an awful situation.”

“You actually said you want me to join you? Hahaha!”

“What are you talking about?”

“Who do you believe you are?!”

“You are my foe!”

“It’s all because of you that I fell into this state!”

Mi Fang grew angrier as he spoke, and just as he sat down, he stood back up and roared loudly as he constantly waved his arms.

He was so flustered and exasperated that he even threw the chair behind him.

The chair smashed into pieces against the wall.

When the dragonman youngster saw this, his tone was cold, “You will die.”

Mi Fang was flushed red, huffing and puffing, his eyes contracting as he grinded his teeth, “Are you threatening me?”

“Hehehe, do you think I will be scared?”

“I am not worried about informing you that next, I will challenge you. I already have a way of winning; I will ruthlessly defeat you and take back everything that belongs to me from you. Everything!”

The dragonman youngster was unruffled by the chaos, “A way of winning? Are you talking about that devil transformation potion?”

“A saint level potion…” When the dragonman youngster said that, he lightly chuckled, “Hehehe.”

Mi Fang’s eye twitched, and his complexion became as gloomy as water.

His greatest trump card was easily uncovered by the dragonman youngster, but Mi Fang wasn’t surprised.

Because he thought of that mysterious voice as well as the strange appearance of that invitation letter. It was precisely according to that invitation letter that he secretly met with the dragonman youngster.

Since the other side could accomplish this, then making inquiries about the saint level potion was very possible.

Hatred and battle intent quickly waned from Mi Fang’s mind, replaced with endless cold thoughts and despair.

“It seems you’ve calmed down, excellent.” The dragonman youngster seemed to squeeze out a smile, it was fleeting.

He drank red wine while saying to Mi Fang, “So long as you calmly reflect, you will think that I am right. You have already lost so much. Continuing to oppose me will lose you even more, including your life.”

“Don’t be so stupid, Mi Fang.”

“Do you really believe the Jing Xiang Clan’s promise? That as long as you take the saint level potion to deal with me, that they’ll handsomely reward you?”

“What are you encountering now? Your painstaking and hard fought authority was stripped away overnight.” Your power, wealth, and status are at their behest. In the end, you attached yourself to the Jing Xiang Clan, and whatever power they gave you, they can take back at any time.”

“It is not I who is humiliating you, but them!”

“Do you not understand? Here, even though you became a member of their clan, you are still a human, an outsider.”

“You are not a snow elf.”

“Think about it carefully, does the Jing Xiang Clan really treat you as one of them?”

Mi Fang didn’t know what to say, so he was silent, like he was dead.

The dragonman youngster finished drinking his wine, then belched in satisfaction.

Then he casually pushed his meal tray towards Mi Fang on the opposite side of the dining table.

On the meal tray, there was still a small piece of mangled magic beast flesh.

“You’re hungry, eat a bit.” Said the dragonman youngster.

“No, I ate breakfast. Furthermore, I don’t have a habit of eating other people’s leftovers.” Mi Fang’s silence was effortlessly smashed, he felt humiliated and resolutely declined.

“Hehehe.” The dragonman youngster sneered out a laugh.

He looked up, leaned back on his chair, then extended a dragon claw, pointing it at himself, and then to Mi Fang.

“You and I are the same.”

“We are both outsiders.”

“We are both duelists.”

“We both have taken countless risks, and experienced numerous tribulations, so many in fact, we have lost count.”

The dragonman youngster shook his head, then lifted both hands and clenched then into fists before Mi Fang.

“We rely on our two hands to work and struggle. And those people, those out of touch people were born into everything we exhausted ourselves working for!”

“Why?”

“More hateful is that our efforts might be denied by those people and be completely lost in a night. In their eyes, we are a joke!”

“This is precisely why I am willing to take risks, why I wanted to leave home to make my way in this world.”

“I want to use hard work to establish my own force!”

“Look at you now, Mi Fang, you have nothing left to lose. Join me, work with me, and you can gain so much more, much more than you can possibly imagine.”

“Don’t tell me you don’t want to truly belong to your own force?”

“I can help you.”

“Don’t tell me you don’t want to take control of the Jing Xiang Clan and show those people your true colors?”

“I can still help you.”

“The prerequisite is that you join me.”

The dragonman youngster intentionally stopped there.

Then he clearly saw Mi Fang’s Adam’s apple roll and heard him swallowing his saliva.

His orange dragon eyes clearly reflected Mi Fang’s captivated expression; each subtle change in his expression was taken in by the dragonman youngster.

“You’re hungry.”

The dragonman youngster chuckled, “I can see now that you are very hungry.”

“Eat a bit, have my food, take a bite.”

The dragonman youngster’s voice eased, like it was hypnotic.

Mi Fang’s train of thought seemed to be chaotic and also blank.

He also didn’t know why, as if demons and gods were at work, he was seeing himself stepping towards the dining table, slowly stretching out his hand, and touching the meat on the meal tray.

Without tableware, Mi Fang picked up the magic beast flesh and put it into his mouth.

This magic beast flesh did not suit him, and the reeking blood made him nauseous.

But he still took a big bite and devoured it.

During this process, his bewilderment and despair slowly shed away, his gloom began to turn into viciousness.

His cheek puffed up, and watery blood spilled from the corner of his mouth, went down his chin, and down his neck, dyeing his exquisite, noble elven apparel red.

The dragonman youngster burst into boom laughter, and he got up to leave.

As he brushed past Mi Fang, he lightly left a sentence, “Leave the potion bottle behind, I’ll inform you of what to do next.”

Mi Fang understood.

Just by eating the meat, he had made clear his attitude, he would leave behind the potion and display his sincere submission.

Mi Fang also didn’t know why he was moving so briskly, there was no hesitation. He immediately took out the potion and clacked it onto the table.

The dragonman youngster was already at the door.

Mi Fang hastily asked, “You’re leaving at once? Are you a devil?! I left behind the potion, what are you going to give me?”

During the entire talk, the dragonman youngster never openly promised to give him anything.

“What can I give you? Hehehe.” The dragonman youngster opened the door, “I accept your dueling challenge, and promise that during this duel, I will only beat you up, I won’t kill you.”

A second later, the door closed.

Chapter 943: Young Man – Political Ability Up ↑

The negotiation outcome with Mi Fang was far more fruitful than the dragonman youngster’s prior moves.

As he left the restaurant, the dragonman youngster recalled what Cang Xu had said.

Cang Xu analyzed Mi Fang, “Of all the duelists, Mi Fang is best suited as the first place to breach.”

“On the one hand, he is in a terrible situation and is implicated in our benefits. On the other hand, more importantly, his temperament is obviously feeble.”

When the dragonman youngster heard that, his mind couldn’t help but think of how heavily armored Mi Fang was during their duel.

The dragonman youngster didn’t fully understand, “Since Mi Fang is certain he cannot defeat me, and even fears he’ll lose his life, he came fully armed and adopted an extremely conservative tactic, that was sensible.”

But Cang Xu shook his head, “To see someone clearly, you must examine their actions. Look at how they act, not just during a short time period, or superficially, rather look at how he lives. Especially what key choices they make during important impasses, that will more elucidate you of their nature.”

“We don’t know how Mi Fang lived before coming to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, but after he came here, what did he do?”

“Through his masculine charm, he exploited the resources of female snow elves and invested them into himself.”

“Through duels he earned money and reputation, he then utilized his reputation to expand his romantic charm, thereby increasing the amount of resources he milked from his female companions.”

“Finally, he chose the Jing Xiang Clan, that clan suited his growth the most.”

“Summing all this up, we can find that Mi Fang likes to take shortcuts. During duels, he is always extremely prepared, greatly emphasizing whether he wins or loses. When he tries, the gains and losses are weighing heavily in his mind.”

“He has a keen sense for opportunity; therefore, he was able to beat the other Jing Xiang Clan snow elves to become the person in charge of the mount potions.”

“Thus, he rode the wind, and it was also because of that he fell into the abyss after losing the duel. According to the intelligence, his authority in the Jing Xiang Clan has nearly vanished, this is precisely the optimal time to negotiate with him.”

When the dragonman youngster heard this analysis, he felt he had gained immensely from this, and he promptly asked Cang Xu for guidance and how he should specifically conduct the negotiation.

Cang Xu taught him, when handling people of weak, rich, and ambitious nature, he should do his utmost to display an intensely intimidating stance to gain dominance, then he could use benefits to entice and sway his mark. By accomplishing these two steps, he could achieve his negotiation goal on a basic level. If he could add a third step, it would be to increase a feeling of acknowledgement, that would be better.

Afterwards, Hun Tong scouted out new information, allowing the survivors to learn of a “saint level devil transformation potion”, it was crucial information.

Due to it, the negotiation with Mi Fang became a more pressing matter.

“This negotiation was a total victory.”

“The ‘saint level potion’ has been neutralized in advance. Although it can’t bring about a crisis, it is still a gigantic inconvenience.”

The dragonman youngster felt quite happy.

He had left the room before Mi Fang, ignoring the saint level potion left on the dining table, both to deter Mi Fang and to impress upon him a feeling of unfathomable depths, that everything was under his control.

Hun Tong was hiding in the room and would bring the potion to the dragonman youngster.

Since Cang Xu converged and pointed out the dragonman youngster’s many strategic mistakes, the dragonman youngster immediately corrected course and brought Hun Tong back from the Snow Bird Harbor branch to join at his side again.

The mysterious voice Mi Fang had heard, along with the sudden appearance of the invitation letter, was from the deception divine spell empowered Hun Tong.

After returning to the temporary capital base, the dragonman youngster was still pondering this operation.

“To solve a problem, it might be necessary to kill it!”

“Killing Mi Fang, or inciting him into rebellion, the latter is obviously more useful to me.”

“At this time, what do we currently need to do? Integrate with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, take root here.”

“Therefore, we must have a good relationship with all powers here.”

“Eradicating Mi Fang, even if I show strength, will establish enmity with the Jing Xiang Clan. Meanwhile, it will cause the other noble to be vigilant and loathe us.”

“Simultaneously, Mi Fang is also a duelist. He is not from the military, and if I kill him, it will estrange me from the other duelists, and they’ll be stringently on guard against me.”

“I have so many worthy subjects to study from Cang Xu.”

The dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

In the past, to deal with problems, he usually used violence and strength.

His experience in Ocean Mother Nest allowed the fishman youngster to comprehend the usages of deception. In the Snow Bird Harbor battle, he put that into practice.

His negotiation with Mi Fang was him following Cang Xu’s guidance, an attempt to handle a problem in a new way.

“This method is neither fighting nor deception, but upon carefully sampling it, it has ingredients from both.”

“We used the pretense of arms merchants to bluster and deceive many people. Mi Fang is no exception. Our breakthrough into the Alchemy Guild was a decisive victory. By contrast, Mi Fang was forced into a corner, it is obvious the enemy is strong, and he is weak.”

“Thus, this was the best time to negotiate.”

“The goal of this negotiation was to force the enemy into surrender and compliance. Therefore, it was not just blind force, I also had to look for common ground. Therefore, I stated we were the ‘same’. Looking at the results, it was very remarkable.”

“The reason why I could accomplish this, apart from my prior victory over Mi Fang, was thanks to Zong Ge’s 3 v 1 deterring him. More importantly, by relying on Cang Xu’s methods, I was able to resolve the difficult problem that was the Alchemy Guild.”

Cang Xu helped Cai Jing and the two others form a faction, aided the dragonman youngster in becoming a duelist, and also helped him contact Shuang Lian. In this string of operations, they accurately struck the core problem and affected the decision making of the highest echelons of the kingdom.

From this high place, they effortlessly suppressed the Alchemy Guild President and powerful elders like Hua Ni.

After this news got out, his surging renown flabbergasted hostile powers.

“How did Cang Xu achieve this?” The dragonman youngster pondered this question many times.

He asked himself for the answer, “He saw through the situation, beyond the façade and into the Ice Sculpture King’s plight and requirements, then with the help of the situation, applied leverage to make new advances on these forces to benefit us.”

“Cang Xu truly is amazing!”

The dragonman youngster felt admiration as well as vigilance.

“Racial contradictions lie between Mi Fang and the Jing Xiang Clan. Although Mi Fang joined the Jing Xiang Clan, and became a son-in-law, superficially integrating with them, as a matter of fact, he was unable to.”

“Why?”

“The Jing Xiang Clan’s snow elves don’t want to give Mi Fang an influential position, and they can’t satisfy him.”

“Essentially, this racial contradiction prevents either side from trusting the other completely!”

“If Mi Fang was a snow elf, his situation would be completely different.”

“This is a racial conflict. Every intelligent life form, because of different bloodlines and life shapes, will bring about different worldviews, value systems, and outlooks.”

“These differences are usually massive and cannot be jointly understood.”

“Because I have the Bloodcore, and can transform myself, I can personally experience how immense these differences are.”

When the young man was a fishman, he felt incomparably close to water. If he was in his dragonman form, he absolutely wouldn’t feel that way.

The young man thought of what Long Meng had told him.

Be vigilant of your draconic nature, by mastering it, it is possible to go a step further in the martial realm.

“If one was unaware of racial instincts and the need to master them, it would be difficult for people to cooperate with each other on a deep level.”

“Mi Fang’s relationship with the Jing Xiang Clan can be regarded as such a situation. But ultimately, their cooperation is fractured!”

“An overall survey of all powerful organizations in the world shows that without exception, all of their main members are of the same race. The Sheng Ming Empire is mainly made of humans, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom of snow elves.”

“In that case, what about my Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

Mi Fang’s loss, and living example caused the dragonman youngster to be more alert, more interested in the racial contradictions in his own mercenary corps.

Cang Xu evaluated the dragonman youngster and said he was an outstanding leader. It wasn’t flattery, it was practical and realistic.

The dragonman youngster was constantly changing, constantly improving.

He was continuously learning.

This time, with Cang Xu, he experienced and learned much from his negotiation with Mi Fang.

The dragonman youngster had experienced and been enlightened by politics, and so his political acumen soared!

After Mi Fang secretly negotiated with the dragonman youngster, he returned to the clan base.

That very afternoon, he publicly declared that he wanted to challenge Long Fu again, to wipe away his humiliation!

This news rapidly spread, attracting widespread interest and discussion.

“Indeed, in that duel, Mi Fang didn’t show off his strength. If I were me, I’d be unwilling to resign myself to that.”

“Older brother is older brother, he defeated himself, and although he lost, he didn’t truly admit defeat. In this battle, he has come to a great realization!”

“Did the Jing Xiang Clan force him to fight again? Mi Fang’s loss caused the Jing Xiang Clang to suffer castigation!”

“I’m afraid Long Fu won’t agree. As a dragonman, it is normal for one to look down upon their defeated opponent.”

The masses were unaware of the truth.

Mi Fang’s self-pitying female supporters, the projecting populace, and those analyzing the current political situation were more or less right.

The dragonman youngster received Mi Fang’s challenge letter, and that evening, he declared that he accepted this challenge.

The masses cheered.

“Long Fu accepted, he didn’t refuse!”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps doesn’t actually need to work with the Jing Xiang Clan. Now they have people in the Alchemy Guild.”

“I’ve always heard that Long Fu is a warrior, that he has a heroic and magnanimous nature. You can see that from each of his fights. Believe me, I can judge people accurately. Even if I am mistaken, there is no reason the others aren’t mistaken too. The masses’ eyes are bright as snow!”

Thus, the dragonman youngster once again gained the good graces of the populace.

The next day, the duel began.

The public duel containing the most magnificent Long Fu under the limelight, as well as the narrative of revenge for a past transgression, caused the dueling arena to be a full house inside.

Chapter 944: Probing Long Meng

The entire duel was marvelous, and the cheers didn’t stop as they filled the entire arena.

In the last duel, Mi Fang was defensive, now he was going on the offensive, using dazzling attacks.

Long Fu was also mainly on the offensive, defense was auxiliary.

Through Long Meng’s tutelage, he learned Gravity Force, and for defense he possessed Tempering Force and Tenacity Force.

In this battle, he was constantly using these forces.

With the help of Mi Fang’s pressure, he quickly grasped these three forces in real combat.

He didn’t use battle skills, instead focusing on using basic combat techniques to deal with Mi Fang’s various battle skills.

This caused the audience to exclaim in astonishment.

“Did you notice? Long Fu isn’t using his full strength.”

“His battle style has greatly changed; he’s not using any battle skills.”

“But his fist-work and footwork have been improved immensely, heavens, how has it improved so much?!”

Finally, the dragonman youngster used a battle skill.

Battle skill – Dragon Pearl, Exploding Flame

Exploding Flame battle qi filled the dragon pearls, imbuing each one with the property of explosiveness.

The dragonman youngster used three exploding dragon pearls against Mi Fang, causing the latter to cough up blood and fall to the ground, losing his battle strength.

He accepted it wholeheartedly.

In the previous battle, his repeated battle skills were all unable to produce the desired result. The dragon scales and armor was one reason, while the two great forces amplifying defense was a second.

The dragonman youngster’s basic combat skills overwhelmed him. Finally, Mi Fang had a high battle qi depletion rate, while the dragonman youngster’s basic combat skills used battle qi frugally.

Seeing Mi Fang was running out of battle qi, the dragonman youngster used [Dragon Pearl] to win in one final move.

“This was a marvelous duel!”

“That’s right, both sides fought gracefully and without regret.”

“Older Brother Mi Fang fought with all he had, squeezing out every last bit of battle qi. Defeat doesn’t matter; he is still our older brother!”

Losing was not important in this case.

So long as there was a duel that was decided, there would be a winner and a loser.

What was important was that the defeat was not unsightly. In the last duel, Mi Fang’s defeat was too ugly, too shameful, and he hadn’t displayed one bit of battle will and courage.

But in this fight, it was much better, much more daring and risky, that caused the masses’ evaluation of Mi Fang to universally go back up.

And the crucial thing that changed his style was one simple sentence from the dragonman youngster, he promised that he “wouldn’t kill him”.

This to Mi Fang was priceless.

He could attack with all of his strength yet felt he was no match for the dragonman youngster, this solidified his submission to the dragonman youngster more.

“In just a few days, how did Long Fu improve his combat skills so much?”

“Was this from Long Meng’s advice? Nonsense!”

“Only through the Dueling Divine Domain’s experience inheritance can this be possible. But according to the reports, Long Fu hasn’t spent much time in the Dueling Divine Domain.”

“Therefore, he is just pretending in everything, he is inherently powerful, and is only constrained by circumstance, he is only showing off a part of it, that is the reasonable take!”

The dragonman youngster’s fighting gifts were too insane, to the extent that Mi Fang made the wrong conclusion. This wrong collusion was also in accordance with popular sentiment.

However, in reality…

“He really made a lot of progress, if I hadn’t personally witnessed it, I could never have imagined it to be so.” Long Meng sighed to himself, admiring the dragonman youngster even more.

To the extent that he was more motivated to teach after this duel.

The dragonman youngster sensed that Long Meng was getting closer to him.

“Why?” The dragonman youngster pondered the cause of this change.

He thought of his saint level qualifications, of his immense progress, that he was a dragonman just like him, and also of Shuang Lian and of the concealed story Long Meng had with her.

“You can master many more forces. Your combat talent is rare, one of a kind unlike what I’ve seen before.”

“Your progress can almost be counted as miraculous.”

After Long Meng’s instruction ended, he reminded the dragonman youngster, “You have become a duelist, but haven’t been in the divine domain for very long.”

“Every duelist that enters the divine domain is imbued with a divine spell contract.”

“After the divine spell agreement is placed, we can leave the Dueling Divine Domain.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, already sensing the divine spell contract on him.

It was not a big problem for him.

He could use deception divine spells to deceive the Main Earth Elemental God and the divine artifact [Truth Slate], so naturally he could deceive an incomplete Dueling Godhead’s divine spell contract, allowing it to mistakenly believe that he was always complying within the scope of the contract.

Of course, he didn’t need to expended divine power or Pearl Bubbles right now to deceive the Dueling God contract.

He was still willing to abide by it.

Long Meng continued, “In fact, new duelists also get a benefit in kind that you haven’t received yet.”

“By staying in the divine domain, you will automatically be instilled with some fighting experience.”

“These experiences come from the divine domain’s accumulation, originating from the participants of countless duels in years past. To some extent, they had faith in duels, therefore after death, their experience was left behind here.”

“You can inherit a portion of this experience.”

“Obtaining this experience can allow you to effortlessly and quickly learn many new battle skills, far more efficiently than you can learn on your own…”

“Hic, perhaps with you, that isn’t the case.” Long Meng looked at the dragonman youngster, quickly changing his remark.

The main thing was that the dragonman youngster’s learning rate was too fast, too outstanding!

The dragonman youngster appeared delighted, “So, it turns out there is another great thing.”

Long Meng smiled, and nodded, “Just this once. Afterwards, if you want to have that kind of experience again, you’ll have to exchange divine favor for it.”

“Your situation is different from the other duelists.”

“I suggest that you continue to train diligently for a time. Your potential for forces is immense, and currently you grasp three of them, that is far from your limit.”

“Perhaps, when you can’t advance anymore or no longer see clear progress, you can receive the Dueling God’s blessing, that would increase the cost-performance ratio.”

The dragonman youngster nodded repeatedly, looking like a good student behaving modestly.

This caused Long Meng to have a better opinion of him.

Actually, the dragonman youngster was thinking, “Can’t advance? With the Bloodcore, there won’t be a day where I can’t advance forward.”

“The Dueling God is far more stingy than Charming Blue Goddess. Divine favor doesn’t automatically go up, instead I have to make contributions.”

“But it doesn’t matter.”

“So long as I conduct blasphemous prayers, I believe I can obtain much more. After all, the Dueling God doesn’t exist, and the Godhead is incomplete.”

The dragonman youngster was completely able to give the other duelists a small shock, through blasphemous sacrifices.

But after careful deliberations, he let it be.

Actually, doing this would provoke the other duelists too much.

If the image of the dragonman youngster being favored by the Dueling Godhead was formed, in the eyes of others, he’d become the most powerful competitor in the Dueling Godhead contest!

At that time, the Ice Sculpture Royal Family and the White Dragon King’s faction would want to sort out the dragonman youngster.

Long Meng would also turn from friend to foe!

Becoming a duelist was already dancing on the edge of a perilous precipice. Doing more would be jumping and falling off the cliff.

“So long as I can seize it, the entire Dueling Godhead will be mine, there is no need to do blasphemous sacrifices for a bit of divine favor.” The dragonman youngster thought.

Outwardly, he told Long Meng he wanted to return the Axe Gang Leader’s and the other gold levels’ bodies.

Long Meng was extremely grateful, “You have come to realize this, that’s great. Helping return duelists who died in battle to Tranquil Hill is the consensus of all of us.”

Long Fu then asked, “I am considering this, but should I demand some spoils of war?”

Long Meng chuckled, “You can do as you see fit.”

The young man’s eyes vaguely flashed with sharp light.

He recalled Cang Xu’s guidance, “If Long Meng didn’t agree to this demand for spoils of war, then it would prove he has a close relationship with the military faction.”

“If Long Meng agreed, then it is indirect evidence of the competitive relationship between the high class.”

“If Long Meng is indifferent, then he is an intermediary between them.”

Returning the three dead gold levels was the plan the dragonman youngster, Cang Xu, and Zi Di had discussed and agreed to. Now the dragonman youngster said it specifically for Long Meng to hear, cleverly probing the latter.

At the same time, he asking Long Meng for his guidance also could deepen his relationship with Long Meng, further weakening the domineering feeling of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

As expected, after the three gold level bodies were buried on Tranquil Hill, the royal family immediately responded, expressing they were pleased.

The dragonman youngster decided to return them without asking for any spoils of war, greatly allaying the relationship and tension between them and the duelists.

Chapter 945: Hua Ni – They Came Prepared

Soldier City.

Bao Li Gen was training, pumping iron dumbbells and huffing loudly.

“Damn you, damn you Long Fu!”

“You think that by returning their bodies you can settle the grievances between us?”

“Wishful thinking!”

“Even if the authorities make allowances for you, I will never forgive you, I will never let go of any enemy that massacred my comrades!!”

As a soldier, one with a fiery temper that flagrantly violated military discipline every day, he was unable to stand this outcome.

He expended divine favor to contact Mei Lin.

Me Lin was equally dissatisfied.

Bao Li Gen roared, “Are we letting the enemy get away with everything and even letting them leap and frisk about before our very eyes?! I will kill him; I will tear him to pieces!!”

Mei Lin sighed, she too was as resentful and restless as him. From the latest intelligence reports, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had made a string of moves. Using the starting point of the dragonman youngster becoming a duelist, they applied leverage to surrounding relationships and were in the process of vigorously roping in other duelists.

If their growth continued like this, it would be very detrimental to Mei Lin and the others.

Mei Lin replied, “Long Fu has displayed saint level qualifications, he did so deliberately at his own volition.”

“If I am not mistaken, next they will display their strength amply. This kind of strength, is not battle strength, its is more concentrated on alchemy capabilities.”

“The Alchemy Guild and the royal family will begin to work more with them because of their alchemy products.”

“When that moment comes, vengeance for our comrades will be even more hopeless.”

“But now, we still have a chance.”

“So long as Long Fu continues to participate in this year’s grand duel, we have a chance.”

“I am currently up to my ears in work, and as a matter of fact, even if I could personally duel Long Fu, I am unlikely to be his match.”

“This guy has hidden himself very deeply and has only exposed a portion of his combat strength.”

“Therefore, we can only rely on you.”

“What is the progress of your magic construct armament?”

Bao Li Gen gritted his teeth tightly, “Too slow, way too slow! The armaments department is still assembling it; they haven’t even adorned it yet!”

Mei Lin shook her head, “It should be fast. The National Ceremony Grand Duel is already beginning, you are the military’s representative, they won’t leave you out to dry.”

“You have been training for this for two years, and your day to compete is fast approaching.”

After breaking the connection, Mei Lin sank into contemplation.

According to currently available information, Bai Li Gen was far from Long Fu in battle strength.

On the face of it, Bao Li Gen was stronger than Long Fu. But secretly, both were hiding many skills.

Like Mi Fang, Mei Lin firmly believed that Long Fu advancing leaps and bounds by Long Meng’s tutelage was just a façade. The truth was that Long Fu was simply unveiling his original strength step by step. Long Meng helping Long Fu with this show proved that they had reached a cooperation and a tacit understanding.

“Although Long Fu has that mysterious 3 v 1 method, with the magic construct armament, Bao Li Gen will have peak gold level strength!”

As a member of the duelists, and also a high ranking admiral, Mei Lin knew a lot of high level information.

What was referred to as magic construct armament was an entire set of matching and closely connected equipment. It could immensely enhance the user, not just unilaterally, rather it covered attack, defense, recovery, movement, and all other aspects.

Feng Lian had a bronze mask; it was a part of his magic construct armament that reached the saint level. It could transform his body to increase in size and elevate his offenses enormously.

But because it was saint level, its energy depletion rate was terrifying.

Even Feng Lian was unable to independently support it. Therefore, when it was active, Feng Lian would frequently use wind chains to link himself to his magic ship and borrow its magic power reservoir.

On the kingdom’s army side, Bao Li Gen was outfitted with a complete set of magic construct armament.

It was gold level, yet its expenditure rate was gigantic.

Thus, it had its own energy core, Bai Li Gen would find it very difficult to satisfy the overall energy expenditure rate himself when it was activated.

This was one of the features of magic construct armament, all magic construct armament had their own independent energy supply. This kind of exogenous energy far exceeded the energy reserves of a regular gold level transcendent.

It was also because of this that the magic construct armament could amplify all aspects so powerfully.

Mei Lin was a magician, she understood thoroughly how strong magic construct armament was.

“From another point of view, magic construct gear can turn an individual soldier into a magic ship, one wearing a magician tower.”

“For the National Ceremony Grand Duel and to sell its munitions, the army has poured all of its forces into this project. Three years ago, the army selected its best, and Bao Li Gen came out on top.”

“He painstakingly trained for almost two years, and him with the magic construct gear will be worth putting hope into!”

“The better he does in duels, the better the finished product does in the Warm Snow Cup, the greater the volume of the kingdom’s munitions trade.”

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy product experts was a significant mainstay industry, and through its flourishing alchemy industries, had turned them into an international arms dealer.

Bao Li Gen’s battle strength concerned the army’s reputation and fight for benefits. Mei Lin believed that this time, the kingdom’s army would be going all out.

Alchemy Guild.

This year’s Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest was beginning its second segment – the alchemy product exhibition.

Each one person exhibition hall was next to each other, and people surged like the tide, it was a lively spectacle.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ exhibition hall was presided over by Yao Ma (Zi Di).

Zi Di showcased three products.

The first was the undead aura sensor she used to threaten Elder Jiu Jin.

A hired apprentice alchemist was in the process of using an undead material to publicly display the operation of this alchemy instrument.

The second was something Zi Di had planned to sell in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom market, the Fire and Ice Song.

It was not magic medicine, but a loudspeaker.

After it was activated, the horn would broadcast a singing voice. Within the range of the singing voice, ice elements would turn from their normal rigid state into a drifting and free flame like state.

From the outside, it looked like white flames were quickly melting the ignited ice.

The third was the Ice Crystal Protection Talisman.

The main material was ice crystals, refined into defensive equipment. It was only the size of a palm, and after being aroused, it could release a hexagon shaped defensive barrier. The barrier was not large; it was smaller than a one hand circular shield.

The wonderful thing rested with the fact that many Ice Crystal Protection Talismans could be activated at the same time, joining together the same translucent hexagon barriers.

“It’s with that instrument that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gained blackmail over that rubbish Jiu Jin!” Hua Ni’s face was steady, pondering and polishing the information she had on hand.

She now hated the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps bitterly, and really wanted revenge, thus, she paid close attention to information on them.

Since Cai Jing and the others formed a new faction, the Guild President not only didn’t suppress them, but he helped them instead. Thus, Hua Ni’s influence and prestige in the Alchemy Guild took a huge blow.

Since then, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps started to sell large amounts of honey snow on behalf of Shuang Lian, driving another knife heavily into Hua Ni.

Perspicacious people could see that Hua Ni was routed by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in a disastrous manner.

Hua Ni was slapped publicly by the masses, but she could do nothing about it.

Therefore, she wildly researched into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, thought she found an area to attack, then set up doing so. Of course, her movements were restricted to a degree, as she couldn’t make things difficult for the royal family.

“This is outstandingly clever..” Looking at the undead detector in the magic image, Hua Ni’s expression changed.

Then, she cursed again, “Jiu Jin, you wastrel.”

Information that Jiu Jin was being blackmailed by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was given to Hua Ni by the Li Jian Clan.

Hua Ni didn’t have much of a reaction to this.

Who cared if Jiu Jin was polluted by a necromancer’s aura? Couldn’t anyone trade with a necromancer? Actually, the strange thing was that this business couldn’t be brought up in public.

Magicians had flexible convictions, and all of these people were inclined towards research, so this group had a more forgiving opinion of necromancers.

Their benefits were not much in agreement with the gods’ benefits.

“This Ice Crystal Protection Talisman is also pretty good, but its design concept is ingenious. Its defensive performance and energy consumption rate is just average however.”

“The most valuable one is the second item – the Fire and Ice Song!”

At a glance, Hua Ni could see from the point of view of a professional the immense worth of the second alchemy product.

It could turn rigid ice elements into a more gaseous form, the practical applications of this was immense.

For example, it could be used to exploit iceberg and glacier resources, automatically turning ice cubes into depleted flames.

Another example, on the battlefield, many spells and magic items that used rigid ice elements could easily be disturbed by the Fire and Ice Song and even be destroyed by it.

Hua Ni most valued the paired usage of the Fire and Ice Song with honey snow.

“With the help of the Fire and Ice Song, more of honey snow can be converted into an energy source. The level of this conversion rate improvement, even with a cursory assessment, can be calculated as very considerable!”

When Hua Ni’s analysis reached this point, her brows deeply creased. She noticed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was not just limited to mount potions, they had their own original advantages to compete.

“Who is their arms merchant backer? Such strength!”

Even enemies like Hua Ni had to admit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ three products were very enticing.

“They came prepared.”

“The Fire and Ice Song especially suits our kingdom, and the Ice Crystal Talisman will be versatile equipment for the army.”

Hua Ni suddenly realized why Long Fu revealed his “saint level qualifications”.

“The strength of this collaboration, the scare of this business, I fear it really does need a saint level overseeing it.”

The truth was after Zi Di and Cang Xu investigated, they decided to withhold two of the original five products.

They didn’t want to provoke the Alchemy Guild too much.

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed an exceptional display; to show it was worth it to work with them, but not too exceptional.

Like the dragonman youngster becoming a duelist and deciding not to acquire the God’s favor.

Chapter 946: Jing Xiang Clan Begs for Collaboration

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ influence was on a meteoric rise.

The dragonman youngster became a duelist and was appeased by the royal family. Working with Shuang Lian, as well as helping Cai Jing and the others allowed them to get through the Warm Snow Cup Grand Contest unimpeded.

During the second stage of the competition, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps revealed three alchemy products, once again raising their status in the minds of others.

Zi Di took advantage of this to negotiate with the Alchemy Guild, reaching an agreement to produce these alchemy goods, including mount potions.

What power in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had the largest alchemy production lines?

There was only one answer, the Alchemy Guild.

The Jing Xiang Clan’s magic medicine production lines were far inferior to the Alchemy Guild’s, let alone other types of alchemy products.

As for the Li Jian Clan, don’t bother bringing them up.

The negotiations quickly made progress.

On the one hand, Zi Di’s applications for many alchemy laboratories was in the process of being audited and approved.

In the survivors’ plans, these alchemy laboratories could be turned into small scale production workshops.

As of now, production capacity is very scarce.

They wanted to do everything to increase production capacity.

To obtain more alchemy laboratories, Cai Jing and the others, in accordance with the survivors’ wishes, applied for the maximum number of alchemy laboratories they could.

The Ice Sculpture Capital has many alchemy laboratories.

Zi Di once inspected several famous alchemy blocks, finding many of them were abandoned.

After all, leasing these alchemy laboratories cost money.

In fact, the amount of laboratories inside the capital was far less than the amount underground. Within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake’s three layers, there were countless laboratories, along with hidden magician towers, temples, and other facilities.

This was the foundation the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was built on before it was founded.

Zi Di’s plan was clear, “The alchemy laboratories serving as workshops is only a stopgap measure.”

“After all, these laboratories not only require rent, but are time limited, and we need to provide standards-compliant alchemy results.”

Otherwise, these laboratories wouldn’t have been abandoned.

Many alchemists had had problems during testing, and were unable to get results, thus, they were disqualified from renting these places.

But to the survivors, this requirement was that demanding.

Because with War Merchant’s gigantic database, they could take out items that could meet the requirements and continue to lease them in accordance with the rules.

“We also need to buy large scale installations, organize production lines, hire many apprentice alchemists, or settle the matter in one go and refine alchemy puppet workers on a broad scale.”

“In short, we need a large alchemy factory, one that works without rest, and outputs enough to supply the Ice Sculpture Kingdom as well as other powers.”

Relying on the dragonman youngster’s military force, Cang Xu’s exquisite schemes, and Zi Di’s business plans, they completely swept away past impediments and once again entered a wide expanse of land they could rapidly grow across.

She began to purchase large scale production units, while simultaneously beginning to survey the entire nation, considering where to build her factory in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Very quickly, she took a fancy for the Li Jian Clan’s production lines.

She started negotiations with the Li Jian Clan’s person in charge, “Our collaboration won’t continue. Your recently expanded production lines are about to be abandoned, why not sell it to me instead?”

This was quite unruly, and the Li Jian Clan member in charge turned angrily pale, almost losing their noble demeanor to swear at her.

But Zi Di knew the current Li Jian Clan Leader’s temperament, directly stating, “If you can’t make the decision, then pass it on to your clan leader.”

Since the dragonman youngster had said so, the Li Jian Clan Leader, Mian Li Zang, had been waiting for Long Fu to talk to him.

As a result, they kept waiting, but Long Fu never came.
