Finally, they waited until Zi Di came over with the intense request to purchase the Li Jian Clan’s alchemy production lines.

Mian Li Zang was fairly angry, but he was a mature politician, controlled his mood, and decided to contact the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster rebuffed him, telling him: right now, my heart is on doing well in duels. Also, due to constant training with Long Meng, I don’t have time to talk with you, Mian Li Zang. Considering the Li Jian Clan’s current circumstances, I hope both sides will cut ties properly and finalize all the procedures. If there is an opportunity to work together again in the future, maybe we’ll collaborate once more.

Mian Li Zang felt humiliated.

The dragonman youngster told him this in those words: I don’t want to see your Li Jian Clan anymore, your Li Jian Clan has sunk into such deep trouble that I don’t want it near me, I don’t want it to implicate me.

“I’d rather die than sell these production lines to you!” The resentful Mian Li Zang immediately turned down Zi Di’s purchase proposal.

Zi Di was not disappointed at his refusal. It goes without saying that the probability of this going through was tiny, she placed no hopes into it.

Furthermore, the Jing Xiang Clan was quickly calling in, actively requesting that the Dragonman Lion Mercenary Corps work with them, this was a change from their attitude of “even if we lose the duel, we’ll procrastinate in high spirits”.

The Jing Xiang Clan’s changing attitude was due to two main reasons.

The first reason was that during Mi Fang’s second duel with Long Fu, he was defeated again. However, Mi Fang’s battle style was able to salvage his prestige greatly.

The second reason is that rumors of the Alchemy Guild collaborating with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were already circulating.

The Jing Xiang Clan was powerless to stop the business alliance between the Alchemy Guild and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Since they were no match, they might as well throw in the towel.

Don’t say anything about strength of character or snow elf pride.

This is the survival wisdom of nobles!

Thus, the Jing Xiang Clan’s representative’s attitude was very humble. Zi Di appeared as arrogant as possible, then explicitly instructed, “Bring Mi Fang over so we can converse.”

The Jing Xiang Clan was naturally unwilling to. The clan’s snow elves had suppressed Mi Fang with great difficulty, and this kind of son-in-law needed to be beaten down so he’ll be more useful in the future.

Zi Di moved with the dragonman youngster’s reputation, “During the second duel, our Lord Commander has acknowledged Mi Fang.”

“Mi Fang is the only one that can meet his eyes in all of the Jing Xiang Clan.”

“If you want to replace him as the negotiator, then the replacement needs to first challenge Lord Long Fu and use the duel to display their tolerance.”

The Jing Xiang Clan: …

After Zi Di’s words were sent back, the entire clan sank into a weird silence. Soon after, they shirked responsibility, none of them wanted to duel.

The Jing Xiang Clan’s bloodline, by nature, was not good at fighting, but at making medicine.

The reason why they valued recruiting Mi Fang was because of his fighting strength and the tremendous influence Mi Fang had when he won duels.

That evening, the Jing Xiang Clan Leader summoned Mi Fang again, “In your last duel, since you didn’t use the potion, return it to me.”

Mi Fang shook his head, smiling as he declined, “Lord Clan Elder, that potion is best left in my hands. It is my confidence as the clan’s representative with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps from now on.”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader frowned, “That is my clan’s property, not yours.”

Mi Fang’s smile grew, “I am also a member of the Jing Xiang Clan. Since it is my clan’s property, I have the right to use it. This potion should be in the hands of the one that bring out the most value in it.”

“If there is a clan member that wants to use this potion to challenge Long Fu, then I will hand it over to you.”

The Jing Xiang Clan Leader sank into silence.

In the stifling atmosphere, he watched Mi Fang intently.

Mi Fang fearlessly looked back at him, face to face, his smile growing stronger.

The pleasure of retaliation intoxicated him.

Through this matter, he could clearly see the Jing Xiang Clan’s base nature – cowardice!

“If there is nothing else, please excuse me.” Mi Fang strategically asked to leave.

Before he left the study, the Jing Xiang Clan Eldler finally said, “You should know this pertinent news. From now on, you are responsible for the clan’s talks with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps concerning the mount potion business.”

Mi Fang exposed a victorious grin, he let go of the door knob, and turned to look back at the clan leader, the latter was expressionless.

He bowed, lightly replying, “Yes.”

His rightful position had been returned, but Mi Fang wasn’t the slightest bit happy.

He understood thoroughly who it was that returned it all to him!

Therefore, when he represented the Jing Xiang Clan and negotiated with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he expressed a deferential attitude from deep within his heart.

Zi Di was responsible for receiving him.

“Sorry, this time, I am quite busy, and don’t have time to discuss everything with you.”

“This is my first draft of the business agreement, have a look.”

Mi Fang shook his head, “No need to, I accept.”

This attitude caused Zi Di to stare at him, but soon after she gently laughed.

The young lady sized up Mi Fang again, “You can still look at it, amend some parts, and then return to the Jing Xiang Clan.”

Mi Fang shook his head again, “The reason why I agree to it, apart from acknowledge allegiance to your Lord Commander, is that I know full well that at this moment, the Jing Xiang Clan has absolutely no qualifications to negotiate with your corps!”

Zi Di exposed a satisfied smile, clapped her hands, and exclaimed, “Excellent, Mi Fang, I have a bit more respect for you now.”

“Since I don’t have time, you can represent the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and go speak with He Gai Tou.”

“Here are the details, take a good look, appreciate the intent inside.”

“Perform well!”

As Zi Di brushed past him, she clapped Mi Fang’s shoulder while he respectfully lowered his head.

“Why were you the one to come?!” He Gai Tou’s brows creased greatly.

Beforehand, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps contacted him, wanting to work together with him. The delegate that came was not Long Fu, or even Yao Ma, but Mi Fang.

This was out of place!

But very quickly, He Gai Tou reacted, and he glared at Mi Fang, “You submitted to Long Fu?”

Mi Fang smiled, “The Lord Commander allowed me to completely realize what I should do. The Jing Xiang Clan is not my family.”

He Gai Tou clenched his teeth, feeling he had been caught unprepared.

At the same time, he was deeply aware that the Jing Xiang Clan was no longer the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ match, and in order to save their mount potion interests, they had bent the knee.

“These nobles!” He Gai Tou grinded his teeth, but he wasn’t surprised.

Chapter 947: Opening the Wisteria Cache

Mi Fang’s and He Gai Tou’s negotiation finished that same day.

Long Fu would challenge Shi Liu, then secretly supply capital to allow He Gai Tou to play the market and gain much more money.

Mi Fang would transport the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ capital, and with his own name, wager that Long Fu would defeat Shi Liu within three rounds.

This awed He Gai Tou immensely.

“Long Fu is indeed hiding his strength; he is actually confident that he can beat Shi Liu within three rounds!”

“Long Fu is also very vindictive. Last time, the Ice Prison’s representative Bing Yang made things difficult for him, and now it is his turn to target the Ice Prison.”

“He’s warning me.”

“What an overbearing guy…”

Hei Gai Tou understood that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had deliberately sent Mi Fang to negotiation as another layer of deterrence.

He Gai Tou was not like Mi Fang; he was a lone figure with no patron.

Otherwise, he wouldn’t have used the pretense of gambling to send the Ice Prison Warden bribes every year.

Otherwise, he wouldn’t have helped Bing Yang, thereby showing consideration to Mei Lin and the others.

He Gai Tou’s largest backer, or foundation, as the duelists.

“But in the end, that fucking Long Fu became a duelist!” When He Gai Tou heard that news for the first time, he exploded into obscene language.

He Gai You was the second breach in Cang Xu’s plan.

If one were to say Mi Fang was weak natured, then He Gai Tou was weak in influence.

It was precisely why he was a good target.

Long Fu’s challenge to Shi Liu did not attract as much attention as before.

Despite Hei Gai Tou, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps secretly applying force to disseminate information and gossip to raise it in the public eye.

This was because the National Ceremony Grand Duel was in its late stage, and the dragonman youngster and Shi Liu were not the only pair of gold levels dueling each other.

There was another important reason, after a period of elimination and filtering, there were many outstanding duelists that distinguished themselves. There were many new faces among them.

The National Ceremony Grand Duel was not held every year; it was a golden event that attracted many outsiders to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. At the same time, there were many domestic cultivators that were vigorously training and preparing for many years for this.

The dragonman youngster had a massive reputation, but his style and fighting methods were not gaudy, and many spectators felt he was no longer fresh.

The dragonman youngster also sensed this too.

“The greater my reputation, the more helpful it will be for my seizing of the Dueling Godhead.”

“I have to continue to raise my renown, but if I repeat myself, my renown will have reached its limit.”

The dragonman youngster was already hugely popular nationwide, everyone who should know of him did, and those who shouldn’t know of him had at least heard of him.

Next, he should allow his reputation to settle.

Make haters into fans, and make fans into mega fans, make more people acknowledge the dragonman youngster’s power…from the point of view of faith, this was deepening the level of belief!

It was precisely because of this goal that the dragonman youngster challenged Shi Liu.

The Ice Prison’s side hesitated, but Shi Liu was unable to hold himself back.

With the help of deception divine spells, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps used Elder Jiu Jin’s name to quietly negotiate with Shi Liu and acquire the other’s trust, ultimately reaching an agreement.

The duel began.

But for the first time, the dueling arena was empty.

Today, there were three gold level duels happening, the dragonman youngster’s and Shi Liu’s was just one of them.

But the dueling gambling pots were innumerable. It wasn’t just the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and He Gai Tou priming public opinion, other gambling houses and powers were proficient at such lines of work.

The fight began.

The dragonman youngster charged into Shi Liu.

Shi Liu felt things were anything but reassuring and immediately pulled back.

Battle skill – Dragon Pearl, Exploding Flame!

As the dragonman youngster charged, three dragon pearls appeared and floated around his body.

Ding.

With a dull echo, the dragonman youngster and Shi Liu fought in close quarters.

Then, explosions!

Dragons exploded in succession, lifting a surge of smoke.

This was the first round.

In the second round, Shi Liu shouted, magic power bubbling from his magic crystal as he used his earth type battle skill.

A gigantic wall broke through the smoke, standing tall across the dueling arena.

The dragonman youngster didn’t retreat.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Battle skill technique – Vibration Force!

Demolition Fist exploded like a bomb, and it by itself could blow up a large hole in the earth wall. But with the Vibration Force empowering it, the explosion formed a vibrating ripple.

The powerful, radiating wave quickly covered the entire earth wall.

The earth wall’s surface quickly dried, then turned crispy, flakes of it swirled in the air as cracks expanded, until finally, it turned into earthen fragments of various sizes.

The second round finished.

The dragonman youngster punched through the earth wall defense, and then charged at Shi Liu again, brandishing his fist.

He was too close, and Shi Liu was unable to shift away. The latter growled and charged at him to attack.

Formidable attacks struck the dragonman youngster, but the dragon scales, defensive battle skills, and Tempering Force combined to form a perfect defense.

By contrast, after Shi Liu was struck by the heavy fist, he suddenly went slack and motionless.

The dragonman youngster took advantage of this to plunge his dragon claw into his body, pull out his magic crystal, and crush it in front of everyone.

Without a magic crystal, Shi Liu’s earth elemental body loudly collapsed, turning into countless fragments, steeping the ground everywhere with strong earth elements.

In three rounds, Long Fu had won!

Everyone present was stupefied.

No one anticipated this duel finishing so quickly.

Beforehand, countless experts considered Shi Liu’s and Long Fu’s formidable defenses, all of the surmising that this would be a prolonged war of attrition.

Yet the result was this, within a brief dozen seconds, not only was victory or defeat determined, but also life or death!

“What just happened?”

“It’s over?!”

“Is Shi Liu dead? How can this be? I just sat down.”

The audience fiercely discussed, beginning to rack their brains in analysis.

“Was this a fake match?”

“Idiot! Who would die in a fake match?!”

“Long Fu is not that kind of person! Don’t slander my older brother!! You’re courting death!”

“Don’t tell me Shi Liu was such a weak duelist?”

“No, no that isn’t it. To be selected by the Ice Prison and also be a gold level, how can he be trash?”

“Right, I’ve seen Shi Liu duel several times, and has shown great battle strength.”

Everyone analyzed, and after enthusiastic discussion, they arrived at a verdict – Long Fu had become stronger!

“He has learned Vibration Force, heavens, how is he improving so much?”

“In this duel, he displayed a few forces, but they were focused on defense. Now that he knows Vibration Force, he was able to restrain an elemental’s body.”

“Shi Liu had the bad luck to come across my older brother Long Fu!”

“Were Long Meng’s teachings this good? Long Fu’s progression is simply outrageous.”

“I am starting to be interested in his next duels. We really don’t know what kind of improvements he’ll have in the next duel!!”

Victory

He Gai Tou won, he played the market and earned a hefty sum. Who would have thought before the battle that Long Fu would “kill” Shi Liu within three rounds.

Shi Liu also won. Through multiple deception divine spells, he successfully played dead and indirectly broke out of prison.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also won. First, they established a beneficial alliance with He Gai Tou, greatly pulling their relationship together. Second, the dragonman youngster’s killing of Shi Liu displayed his aggression and also gave the audience a nice surprise, causing widespread and enthusiastic discussion that improved his reputation. Third, Shi Liu realigned his allegiance. And the Wisteria Cache was within view.

In a nutshell, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps won in three ways.

“We have waited long enough; it is time to open the Wisteria Cache!” The dragonman youngster, Zi Di, and Cang Xu moved together.

Zi Di was in the open, using the form of Gui Teng to take along Jiu Jin, Cong Mang, and Shi Liu.

The dragonman youngster and Cang Xu were supporting in secret.

“The day is finally here.” When Elder Yuan Ci saw the four people, he deeply sighed.

“Lead the way now, if we procrastinate anymore, more parts of the formation will be activated and it’ll become strong, we won’t have another chance after this.” Elder Jiu Jin urged.

As an Alchemy Guild elder, although not part of its core, he had heard about the Millenium Dragon Formation.

Elder Yuan Ci nodded, he had been hiding in the Ten Millennia Ice Lake for years, and these last few days, he could sense the ice lake changing dramatically.

Yuan Ci did not deceive Zi Di, the Wisteria Cache really was hidden on the second ice layer.

The five level zero Wisteria Secret orders were collected together (Zi Di was using Fei She’s, as her order was worth three), and successfully opened the cache’s door.

The hidden chamber was not big; it had a circular wall that had high cabinets built into it.

Each cabinet drawer had a suitcase in it, containing alchemy goods and even more space.

These were silver level suitcases, and each suitcase was stuffed with gold coins, gems, valuable alchemy materials, and more.

There were too many priceless things, an inventory was required.

In the middle of the secret chamber, there was a tabletop one-and-a-half human tall, on which there were five items laid out.

A golden magic storage pouch, a white bone ring, an ice crystal crown, a scarlet cloak, and a small wooden box.

Everyone’s attention was quickly concentrated on these five treasures.

The suitcases were all filled with conventional resources, winning out in sheer quantity. The tabletop in the center was an alchemy module, acting as a seal and a concealment device to protect the five treasures on it.

When Elder Yuan Ci saw the five treasures, his eyes quickly flashed.

He pretended to laugh unconcerned, “Excellent, there are five of us, and as it happens there are five treasures, one per person.”

“This time, me and Jiu Jin contributed the most, so we will pick first.”

Yuan Ci was a silver level magician, but Cong Mang and Shi Liu were both gold levels.

To guard against the others’ objections, and to strengthen his influence, he pulled in Jiu Jin.

Jiu Jin was a gold level magician, and an Alchemy Guild elder, an authentic local bully of the Ice Sculpture Capital.

But unexpectedly, Elder Jiu Jin shook his head, “This kind of allocation is not appropriate, I don’t approve.”

Elder Yuan Ci’s face changed.

Shi Liu and Cong Mang echoed it, “We disagree too.”

Elder Yuan Ci’s expression sank like water, his fears had come to pass, and he couldn’t help but sneer out to probe, “How would you distribute these?”

The result of this probing was that Shi Liu, Jiu Jin, and Cong Mang all looked at Gui Teng ( Zi Di), looking like they were waiting for orders.

Elder Yuan Ci began to drip cold sweat.

He swallowed his saliva, subconsciously taking a few steps back.

Zi Di lightly chuckled, “Don’t be nervous, Elder Yuan Ci, you are useful to us.”

“It seems you have some understanding of these treasures; can you explain them to us?”

Chapter 948: Yuan Ci Tells of the Treasures

Yuan Ci bitterly sighed, deeply recognizing Gui Teng’s shrewdness.

He now felt very regretful, how could he be so blind to bring in Gui Teng and conspire for the Wisteria Cache with them?

Even worse, Gui Teng had roped in, no, more like subdued Jiu Jin, Cong Mang, and Shi Liu.

“How did he accomplish this?”

“This is impossible!”

“He has a backer, he must have a backer!”

Yuan Ci was angry and bitter, but due to the pressing circumstances, he was forced to reply, “I only know about three of them.”

He pointed his finger towards the golden magic storage bag, “That is the Time Gold Purse, over time, gold will condense inside the bag.”

“It is an alchemy creation from the goblin era.”

“To my understanding, most of the gold coins come from that purse.”

“I was also involved in the arrangement of the Wisteria Cache.”

“All of the gold coins condensed in the purse are marked with the symbol of the goblin empire. Therefore, if we want to use them and not expose this treasure, we will have to recast them.”

Shi Liu was expressionless, while Cong Mang’s eyes lit up.

Elder Jiu Jin knew the ropes, and looked astonished, “What is the principle of this alchemy treasure? Don’t tell me it turns time into metal? Does it touch on the endless transformations of alchemy materials? Isn’t that one of three ultimate pursuits of alchemy?!”

What was referred to as the three ultimate pursuits of alchemy was the golden touch, the elixir of eternal life, and the universal dissolving compound.

Alchemy was founded and was initially developed to turn based matter into gold, for massive economic benefit. Nowadays, this field of research had many results. Turning base matter into gold was achieved, and even be said to influence other fields. Druids and magicians had their respective divine and arcane spells that could turn matter into gold.

But the ultimate pursuit of the golden touch was never attained, or in other words, it served a greater purpose in symbolism.

Technology was always failing and succeeding, always going a step forward. Small objectives were attained, and large objectives rose to the occasion.

At first, when master alchemists were able to turn things into gold, it took materials and energy far in excess of how much gold was produced.

They began to delve into how to decrease expenditures, costs, and increase earnings.

Then, master alchemists, during the process, learned how to turn materials into new materials, which automatically caused them to ponder, what other materials could be turned into gold?

Finally, gold was no longer the universal pursuit of alchemists, they began to study other substances meticulously, and how to turn things into those materials. At this step, the meaning of the golden touch had deepened to mean “endless transformation of matter”, that was an immense proposition.

The golden touch, as alchemy developed, constantly deepened in meaning, and was always one of alchemy’s three ultimate pursuits.

And the time coin purse Zi Di just gained was an immense achievement from the research into the golden touch.

This magic purse could turn time into gold, then refine them into gold coins. The refined gold coins were not exceptional. The true core secret had to do with “time”, turning an immaterial concept into tangible gold!

Zi Di was also rather shaken, thinking to herself: So long as this alchemy technology is researched, and appeared in this year’s Warm Snow Cup, they would dominate everyone and take first place!

“I fear reverse engineering the technology of this purse is not something the average person can do.” Zi Di shook her head and sighed.

Jiu Jin also nodded and lamented, “Yep. However, this achievement can absolutely save a lot of research and development, and trialing costs. This is a readily available pointer.”

“If we wanted to develop this research project, the royal family and the guild would energetically support it, and research funds would come promptly. But this is a product of the goblin empire, we would need to hire a goblin empire historian, a senior goblin language scholar, and a goblin spell researcher.”

But Zi Di suddenly thought of War Merchant.

Unfortunately, the legend level goblin magician War Merchant was dead.

Zi Di’s thinking couldn’t help but diverge, “If this treasure was given to War Merchant, they would be considerably interested in it.”

“At least, I haven’t found any data in the tower spirit’s database on War Merchant researching into this field.”

“To him, this would be a new task.”

With that in mind, Zi Di looked closely at the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s former collaboration with War Merchant again.

Previously, she believed that when the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was in a dependent state, they decided to work with War Merchant. But now, just the sight of this Time Gold Purse changed her perception.

“The Wisteria Merchant Alliance was once massive, possessed astonishing wealth, and got ahold of a huge volume of materials or rare treasures, all of this was within the scope of their capabilities.”

“My Father sought War Merchant, and War Merchant likewise could use the Wisteria Merchant Alliance to find things he needed.”

Zi Di pondered, then looked at Yuan Ci, “Continue.”

Yuan Ci stated, “I recognize the second item, the crown. The Ice Crystal Crown is saint level equipment, one of the components of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s armament [Ice Sculpture Monarch].”

Most didn’t react to that sentence, but Elder Jiu Jin was once again shocked, crying out, “You’re not mistaken?”

“[Ice Sculpture Monarch] is a saint level magic construct armament. When a saint level transcendent equips it, their battle strength soars and they will be able to fight a legend level to a degree. It is one of my country’s legendary foundations.”

“How did your…our Wisteria Merchant Alliance obtain it?”

Yuan Ci shook his head, “This I do not know.”

Yuan Ci then pointed at the small wooden box, “That is the Gem Wish Box. It is said that when a gem meteor fell from the sky, a Grandmaster Alchemist used it to create a foundation altar. During the Wishing God’s grand religious festival, said stone evoked divine blessing and was shaped by the Wishing God.”

“It is also a saint level item, and it can exchange and synthesizes gems.”

Yuan Ci’s words were brief, but this time, the other four were staring at this mediocre looking wooden box.

With no exception, Jiu Jin, Zi Di, the coarse fellow Cong Mang, and Shi Liu deeply realize the worth of this small wooden box.

With gem exchanging, one could turn the gems one had into rarer gems.

One must know, that although both were gems, the price of a sapphire and a ruby was different on the market. For example, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was a source of white gems, so rubies were more expensive than sapphires here. In the Main Plane, stardust gems were the rarest and most expensive and frequently weren’t available to purchase.

If this small wooden box’s output was big, and didn’t consume much energy, then it was unto itself a top notch gem business.

The greatest value of the Gem Wish Box was not that, but gem synthesis.

It could use inferior gems, and through quantity, produce high level gems.

Since it was a saint level item, in other words, it could use gold level gems to produce saint level gems.

“This is a steady way of obtaining saint level alchemy materials! Heavens, the value of this.” Elder Jiu Jin lamented.

Yuan Ci painfully closed his eyes.

He coveted the Gem Wish Box.

“What about the other two treasures, do you three recognize them?” Zi Di asked Cong Mang, Jiu Jin, and Shi Liu.

The three shook their heads.

Zi Di replied, “Then let’s take them away first, and depart this place.

“Be careful.” Elder Yuan Ci promptly reminded, “This tabletop has a concealment and aura restraining effect. If we take them out without any appropriate measures, then these treasures will leak their transcendent aura.”

“I fear saint level transcendent aura will be detected by the large formation outside.”

Those words caused Elder Jiu Jin to look worried, “Elder Yuan Ci is correct!”

Zi Di smiled, “Don’t worry, I will act.”

After the door was opened, the dragonman youngster and Cang Xu followed in. The dragonman youngster was already in the secret room, while Cang Xu was left outside as support.

The two had deception divine spells on them, and Cong Mang and the other four didn’t perceive them at all.

Zi Di put the five level zero secret orders in the corresponding notches in the circular tabletop, activating it.

The locks inside issued a metallic click simultaneously, then the five treasures popped out slightly.

The moment the aura was about to leak, Zi Di lightly waved her hand, and the dragonman youngster used a deception divine spell.

The divine spell could mask aura, truly demonstrating that specialization had outstanding effects!

The minds of Yuan Ci, Jiu Jin, and the rest shook.

They didn’t sense anything when Zi Di used some transcendent method. On the face of it, Gui Teng simply waved their hand and the five treasures’ transcendent aura was totally concealed.

No one could tell!

Incomprehensible!

For a moment, Yuan Ci and others were more fearful of Gui Teng (Zi Di).

The five people worked together to take all of the suitcases out of the secret chamber.

The dragonman youngster personally used divine spells to test everywhere, and after confirming the secret room was empty, he told Zi Di.

After Zi Di received the confirmation, she had Yuan Ci close the secret room again.

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s formation is getting stronger, Yuan Ci, it is getting more dangerous to stay in the Ten Millennia Ice Lake, come with us.” Zi Di issued her plan.

Yuan Ci had no other choice and just nodded.

Before leaving, the dragonman youngster gazed into the depth of the ice lake.

The Wisteria Cache’s treasure room was established in the century ice layer, and below it was the millennium ice layer and the ten millennium ice layer.

When the dragonman youngster entered the lake and used many detection methods. After personally practicing them, he noticed that these detection methods performed uniformly terrible.

“The Time divinity suppresses all other powers.”

“Only the Ice Sculpture Royal Family’s super-formation is strong enough to push back against the divinity’s power and be able to investigate the Ten Millennia Ice Lake on a wide scale.”

“It’s a true pity.”

“If I could use the Bloodcore to absorb the Time God Dragon’s corpse in the ten millennium layer, that would be amazing!”

But the dragonman youngster only thought about it.

Doing this was too difficult.

There were saint level aquatic magic beasts in the millennium ice layer.

In the ten millennium ice layer, there were many more saint level aquatic beasts, even groups of them together.

Not only that, but the closer one got to the dragon corpse, the stronger the time divinity was, the more it eroded and transformed the environment. Without special methods to break in, a short hundred meters might take someone ten years to walk across.

Chapter 949: Long Fu vs Zhu Gan

“Furthermore, we can’t deal with the Millenium Dragon Formation.” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

The survivors’ alchemy strength could never compete with the formation.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ greatest alchemist was not Zi Di or Cang Xu, but the fishman magician Zhong Yong.

Although Bu Quan joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, she was nominally a member.

But after the dragonman youngster contacted Shuang Lian, he understood that Shuang Lian valued Bu Quan a lot and would never allow Bu Quan to truly join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Especially because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ backer was an “arms merchant”, and as outsiders, at most when their connection with the Ice Sculpture Royal Family deepened, they would only be “collaborators” or “allies”.

Shuang Lian was a powerful Alchemy Guild elder and belonged to the Ice Sculpture Royal Family’s faction.

With regards to Bu Quan’s personal growth, her brightest prospects lay with following Shuang Lian, and waiting to inherit Honey Snow Tower, the demiplane, and so on. Shuang Lian treated and cultivated Bu Quan as her sole successor.

Of course, this was what the masses saw as “bright prospects”.

If Bu Quan truly joined the survivors’ ranks, and got the dragonman youngster’s complete trust, she could benefit from a Bloodcore. Even if she didn’t get a Bloodcore, she could benefit from the Bloodline Purification Formation.

Through improving one’s bloodline, her future would naturally be greater, vaster, and brighter than being “Shuang Lian’s heir”.

This matter could not be advanced through force and depended on destiny.

From where Bu Quan stood, she was extremely willing and preferred the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, even willing to give up her own interests in consideration to the dragonman youngster.

But based on the current political situation, the survivors were unable to facilitate bringing in Bu Quan.

Because they needed to maintain their relationship with Shuang Lian, and to keep the Bloodline Purification Formation as secret as possible. As of now, even though the dragonman youngster returned, the Bloodline Purification Formation had only been used on Zi Di, Cang Xu, and Lan Zao, it hadn’t been spread to Zong Ge, Tripleblade, or the others.

The dragonman youngster, Cang Xu, and Zi Di were well aware that spreading bloodline purification outside of their core three was extremely risky. The opportunity was still not yet ripe, so after careful deliberations, they chose to take the most reliable plan.

Afterwards.

The dragonman youngster and the other two were researching the two remaining treasures.

Cang Xu held the bone ring, saying, “This is a gold level carved bone ring. The holder can have fair dealings with the Undead Orthodoxy.”

Cang Xu looked at Zi Di at that moment, “Based on this, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance is deeply involved with the undead force.”

The dragonman youngster was unconcerned, “With it, we can purchase a large quantity of undead materials. This will be very beneficial to your bloodline Cang Xu.”

“What about the cloak?”

The three studied it for a long time, Zi Di also used some alchemy methods, causing the cloak to leak a trace of devil aura.

Apart from that, the cloak looked ordinary, lacking any trace of transcendent aura.

But since it was one of the five treasures, it was certain to be extraordinary and at least on the same level as the Time Gold Purse.

Cang Xu guessed, “The alchemy technique is seamless, the cloak’s line style is totally different to those of the Main Plane, and it also has a trace of devil aura, perhaps it originates from Hell.”

That sentence caused the dragonman youngster to associate it with the new potion he had.

Mi Fang left behind the saint level devil transformation potion.

After Shuang Lian personally tested it, she confirmed this magic potion had no hidden dangers. When taken, it could temporarily transform the drinker into a saint level devil, with enormous repercussions, bloodline contamination.

But this repercussion wasn’t worth mentioning in front of the Bloodcore.

Bloodline pollution was even a good thing.

The Bloodcore could absorb it and give the dragonman youngster some saint level devil bloodline.

Of course, the devil bloodline from it was far less than could be gained from absorbing a saint level devil’s lifeblood. Anyways, a saint level devil’s bloodline served as the main material of this potion, and after taking it, one could reconstruct a portion of the saint level devil’s battle strength, something the dragonman youngster couldn’t achieve by simply absorbing the devil bloodline within the potion.

In short, this potion could serve as one of the dragonman youngster’s trump cards.

After the three studied the five treasures, Zi Di presented a set of silver metal cylinders.

Some were small, others large. Their walls were as thick as a man’s thumb, and both ends of it were wide open, and one could see right through them to the other side.

There were about 16 cylinders, the transcendent auras in them were mixed, and disorderly, but the highest was gold level.

“This is the magnetic cylinder fishing rod we purchased from the Alchemy Guild.” Zi Di introduced their usage.

The dragonman youngster opened his hand and imbued the cylinders with gold battle qi.

The cylinders floated into the air, then through magnetic links, rapidly merged together to become a metal fishing rod.

The fishing rod was three meters long, slender at the top, and thick at the bottom.

The gold battle qi in the cylinder fishing rod quickly changed into a crackle of electricity.

The electricity condensed and wrapped together forming a bundle of line, extending out beyond the top of the fishing rod, it was an electric fishing line.

“If you can’t hook the fish, you can electrocute it. This is positively handy.” The dragonman youngster threw the electric fishing line, noticing he had perfect command of it, wherever he aimed, it went.

The fishing rod was not for the dragonman youngster’s use, it was to be a gift.

A gift for Zhu Gan.

Zhu Gan was one of the duelists, but not part of the army faction. But he and Lin had a good close relationship as fishing friends.

With such a person, the dragonman youngster adopted a different negotiation method than the ones used with Mi Fang and He Gai Tou.

Not long after, Hun Tong took the magnetic fishing rod, and through teleportation, paid a visit to Zhu Gan’s home.

Hun Tong naturally had deception spells on him and was disguised as an unfamiliar person.

When Zhu Gan saw the strange gold level, Hun Tong, he instantly had a new perception of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strength.

Hun Tong produced both the dragonman youngster’s invitation to fight and gifted the magnetic cylinder fishing rod, hoping Zhu Gan could agree to a duel.

According to common sense, so long as Long Fu publicly declared a challenge, Zhu Gan would agree. On the one hand, Zu Gan and Lin had a close friendship. On the other hand, if Zhu Gan avoided battle, the masses would shame him, think of him as a coward, Zhu Gan’s renown would slump, and he would be widely disdained.

Of course, this was founded upon the idea that both sides were of similar strength.

If it were Long Meng who publicly challenged Zhu Gan, and Zhu Gan refused, the populace would feel that was reasonable.

When Zhu Gan learned the dragonman youngster invited him to battle, he became furious. But when seeing the gift, his expression suddenly grew mixed.

After hearing Hun Tong’s entreaty, Zhu Gan sighed, thoroughly confirming that this was Long Fu wanting to ease their relationship, therefore, kindness and might were applied.

Zhu Gan thought about it, then agreed.

Zhu Gan was a wood elf, not a snow elf. Although he was an outsider, as an elf, it was easier for him to gain mass popularity in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom than it was for the dragonman youngster or Mi Fang.

Zhu Gan traveled all over the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and had a pleasant life.

When he ran out of money, he dueled to obtain travel funds. His greatest pleasure was looking for fishing spots across the country and going wild angling.

Zhu Gan wasn’t that interested in the National Ceremony Grand Duel, but since Long Fu had invited him to fight, then there was no harm in agreeing.

He was quite familiar with the dragonman youngster. He bought and perused each of the dragonman youngster’s battle recordings.

“I didn’t expect Commander Long Fu to want to duel again so quickly!”

“This time, he decided to send an invitation, choosing Zhu Gan as his target.”

“Although Zhu Gan doesn’t duel much, he is strong enough to be in consideration. He is a wood elf, has a very graceful fighting style, and has the demeanor of our elven kind.”

“I am very curious, how much has Long Fu grown this time?”

The dragonman youngster’s fight with Zhu Gan attracted the attention of many, triggering a nationwide conversation. The level of heated discussion was greater than the Mi Fang rematch.

It was because during his rematch with Mi Fang, the dragonman youngster revealed new things, causing many to become interested again.

In addition, it was because Zhu Gan was a famous duelist, whose racial identity ensured a stable popularity. He hadn’t fought many times, causing the spectators to be exuberantly interested in him.

The dragonman youngster chose him as his opponent not only to have a communication with the Dueling God’s holy warrior, but to simultaneously use his prestige to increase the amount of new traffic he had.

The dragonman youngster’s duel with Zhu Gan was quite marvelous.

Both sides held the upper hand for a time, both fought in their own ways.

The dragonman youngster charged around, exploded dragon pearls, laughed in the thrill of battle, and practiced a heroic and straightforward persona. This persona was quite appealing, fit in with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s plucky personality, and was something the people naturally had many good opinions of.

Zhu Gan held a fishing rod as his main weapon and mainly fought at mid-range. He was actually similar to the whip wielding Teng Dong Lang in this aspect.

But his movement was more outstanding, able to advance or retreat accordingly, he moved like a blur and possessed the distinctly elven sense of beauty.

His battle skills were very sharp, creating thin cracks in the ground several meters deep. However, the dragonman youngster’s defense greatly weakened these battle skills’ attack results.

In this battle, the dragonman youngster was mainly relying on Tenacity Force to protect himself impressively.

In the final phase, the dragonman youngster used Binding Force and won by a surprise move.

This brought back the disinterested spectators, and when many saw the dragonman youngster had learned another “force”, they discussed it enthusiastically.

“I didn’t expect Long Fu to use a new force!”

“Yes, Binding Force is amazing against an opponent like Zhu Gan.”

Binding Force could empower battle skills, binding enemies they hit and limiting their mobility.

“Long Fu is learning more and more forces. He has proven himself worthy of Long Meng’s admiration, a man Long Meng couldn’t help but teach.”

“Currently, his defense has Tempering Force, Tenacity Force, and for attacks, he has Binding Force, Gravity Force, and Vibration Force. Look at how many forces he has.”

“In my opinion, I can feel the force even when he’s walking!”

Long Fu’s battle strength has grown too fast, it’s no wonder even Zhu Gan was defeated.”

“Alas, Zhu Gan rarely fights, he is the duelist I like the most. In this battle, despite losing, I still like him.”

“If Long Fu’s growth continues like this, what will he be able to achieve? He defeated Mi Fang and Zhu Gan, who will he challenge next?”

Chapter 950: Redirection Force

“Who do you plan to challenge next?” In a banquet after the duel, Zhu Gan asked the dragonman youngster this question too.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “Everyone is a duelist, I must prioritize showing consideration ‘to our people’, I must first test them all once through.”

He didn’t conceal it, “I plan to challenge Yun Zhong.”

“For this reason, I have prepared some arrows as gifts.”

Yun Zhong’s main weapon was a bow, and the dragonman youngster adapted to his taste and prepared gold level alchemy arrows for him.

His negotiation strategy with Yun Zhong was more or less the same as with Zhu Gan.

Because the two were unorganized individuals who esteemed freedom.

Zhu Gan nodded, the dragonman youngster’s answer didn’t go against his expectations, and he also confirmed again that Long Fu wanted to integrate himself into the Dueling God’s holy warriors.

For most organizations, if a newcomer wanted to integrate themselves, they’d have to host banquets, find a mediator to build a bridge, and take a long time to make clear their true feelings and prove themselves. But this was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, where duels were popular, especially among people like Zhu Gan, who were chosen by the Dueling Godhead to become the Dueling God’s holy warriors and acknowledged duels from the depths of their hearts.

Therefore, duels were the best stepping stone to fortune.

Even if they believed in something else, since a duel was agreed on, then nothing else had to be said, let’s fight first!

Zhu Gan had to admit that the dragonman youngster’s negotiation method was just right.

Hosting banquets for hospitality, giving presents, and more were not the most effective methods, and might even have a negative connotation.

Fight first, to confirm who was strong and who was weak, then the weaker side would yield of their own accord.

It could be said now that Zhu Gan’s arrogance was far less than it was before the duel.

Since I lost to you, obeying you is only right and proper.

He lifted his wine cup, looked at the dragonman youngster, and thanked him for the gold level magnetic cylinder fishing rod.

The dragonman youngster candidly laughed, and after clicking cups with Zhu Gan, drank his dry.

Then he said, “From start to finish, you never fought using the magnetic cylinder fishing rod, that surprised me somewhat.”

Zhu Gan chuckled, “I am an elf, and I would never do something as shameless as attacking you with the very gift you gave me!”

That kind of pride made the dragonman youngster very happy.

The banquet ended, the guest and host enjoyed themselves fully, and the conflict between them was put to a complete end.

In the end, Zhu Gan didn’t have a deep friendship with Lin, they only shared a common interest and fished together a few times.

If the dragonman youngster hadn’t become a duelist, then naturally, it was obvious at a glance who was close and who was distant.

But now, the dragonman youngster was not just a duelist, he was actively interacting and doing his best to assimilate with everyone, instantly causing Zhu Gan to carefully reflect over the situation.

Long Fu’s mysterious backer, he being looked after by Long Meng, his dealings with Mi Fang and Hei Gai Tou. Then on the other side, was the deceased Lin.

Lin’s military background was worth being worried about, but the royal family demanded that Mei Lin and the others stop what they were doing…

What would Zhu Gan choose?

Was this a difficult choice?

Of course, Zhu Gan knew what to choose, but he was in an embarrassing spot, after all, he had some friendship with Lin.

This time, the dragonman youngster had given him a ladder to calmly step down on.

First was the gift, then the dueling invitation.

The present expressed sincerity, the duel showed the truth.

Henceforth, Zhu Gan could declare to the outside: I was defeated by Long Fu, and recall his sincerity, so I accept his gift. I also did my best for my fishing friend Lin.

No one would gossip about it.

After the dragonman youngster publicly defeated Zhu Gan, public opinion of him distinctly changed.

It had to do with “who would the dragonman youngster challenge next”, and it became a heated topic of debate.

This indicated that after the dragonman youngster’s rematch with Mi Fang, as well as his duel with Zhu Gan, his quick and violent progress was established to the masses, and his cycle of soaring battle strength was robustly impressed into their memory.

Without a doubt, this massively benefited the dragonman youngster’s growing renown.

“If you want to tackle Yun Zhong, you best cultivate Redirection Force next.” Long Meng intentionally stated this during his tutoring.

“Redirection Force…” The dragonman youngster appeared vexed, “I’ve tried to do so before? I am not talented in that kind of force.”

Long Meng’s eyelids throbbed, “Your comprehension is the best I’ve ever seen in my life. Don’t say such words out loud, or you’re liable to get smacked.”

After pausing, Long Meng shook his head, “But your words aren’t completely meritless. You are inclined towards hard force and lack some understanding of soft force.”

Long Meng summed up the dragonman youngster’s learning achievements, “You now know Tempering Force and Gravity Force, they are 100% hard force. Tenacity Force and Vibration Force are majority hard force, tiny part soft force. You haven’t completely grasped Binding Force, which is mostly hard force, and minority soft force.”

“Redirection Force is almost entirely soft force; it is indeed more difficult to grasp than the other forces.”

“However, according to my calculations, if you focus your training on it for the next several days, it is possible for you to grasp it.”

The dragonman youngster frowned, “Isn’t that too slow?”

“I was hoping to learn Piercing Force and Cutting Force during this time, after all those are two of the three great classical attack forces.”

“Hahaha.” Long Meng heartily laughed, pointing a finger at the dragonman youngster, “I know you like offensive over defense. Believe me, Redirection Force will be more helpful to you than Piercing or Cutting Force.”

“Especially against an archer like Yun Zhong, even simple battle qi surges can deflect arrows.”

Long Meng continued, “Piercing and Cutting Force are indeed majority Hard Force, tiny part soft force, but they won’t help you that much in your next duel.”

“I know your nature; I am certain you don’t want to lose.”

The dragonman youngster exposed a touched expression, and slowly nodded, ‘Then I’ll listen to you.”

Long Meng grew happier when he saw the dragonman youngster heeding his advice.

Since he met Shuang Lian with the dragonman youngster, the latter had grown more pleasing to the eye.

As he tutored the dragonman youngster’s force cultivation, he noticed that the dragonman youngster was more gifted in hard force than soft force and grew to like the thick browed young man even more.

He felt: the dragonman youngster was an expert at hard force, which proved that this guy was just, honorable, direct, and bad at doing those twisted and tricky things.

Of course, he knew the dragonman youngster was a red dragonman, and now this impression had deepened greatly.

Little did he know that everything the dragonman youngster showed Long Meng was a deliberate exhibition.

The dragonman youngster’s earliest mastered force was Penetration Force. This force was majority soft force, tiny part hard force, sufficient to prove that the dragonman youngster was equally outstanding in comprehending soft force too.

“But I have to pretend a little.”

“If I had unsurpassed talent in both soft and hard force, that would be too much.”

“Even if Long Meng was personally teaching me, perhaps he would have suspicions about whether I learned forces at an earlier time and was merely putting on a show in front of him.”

The dragonman youngster didn’t want to provoke Long Meng too much.

The dragonman youngster stared at Long Meng, his expression solemn, “My plan is to do my best to challenge every duelist once.”

“Long Meng, you are my final challenge!”

Long Meng laughed again, “I wholeheartedly look forward to that day.”

“To make that day more wonderful, it seems I have to invest more effort into making you stronger.”

Long Meng’s reaction was within Cang Xu’s speculation, and when the dragonman youngster challenged him, it caused Long Meng to be motivated in teaching him.

And Long Meng’s training were not the only thing making the dragonman youngster stronger.

There was also the Bloodcore.

After this special training finished, the dragonman youngster returned to his encampment.

A large amount of wyvern materials and dragon parts were put together and laid out before the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster absorbed bloodlines again.

His types of dragon bloodlines massively expanded, and the concentration of the bloodlines he had soared.

Since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps opened the door with the Alchemy Guild, they could directly trade and buy alchemy materials from the Alchemy Guild and the royal family.

The Alchemy Guild was massive, the alchemy center of the entire kingdom, and have exceedingly rich stores. Come to think of it, the royal family’s stores were of superior quality.

Through this link, Zi Di bought a large amount of wyvern, sub-dragon, and even some dragon body parts.

Unlike last time, the dragonman youngster was mainly gathering red dragon bloodlines this time.

Quite a lot of them survived in the Alchemy Guild’s and royal family’s storerooms.

Although red dragons didn’t reproduce in Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the kingdom was a formidable trade country and had many steady trade relationships.

The kingdom relied mainly on alchemy, therefore, as they exported, they also imported bulk quantities of alchemy materials.

Through discreet inquiries, Zi Di found some wyvern materials close to red dragons, many that came from Eruption Island. There were a lot of dragon bones, claws, scales, and so on sourced from the Frost Continent.

Nowadays, the Frost Continent was ruled by the barbarian race, and the barbarian race were the greatest murderers of the dragons, and back during the barbarian and dragon war, the barbarians took the dragon’s headquarters, Dragon Island, thus, the dragons were scattered to the skies. When it came to who had the most dragon corpses, it naturally was the barbarians.

Dragon meat, blood, and so on had expiration dates, but dragon bones, claws, and other parts like that did not have that concern. Therefore, the dragon materials the Ice Sculpture Kingdom bought from the barbarian race included many dragon bones, claws, and scales.

Wyvern and sub-dragon materials were decent enough, and real dragon materials were expensive.

Zi Di purchased a large amount, yet the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t have any money shortages.

This was all through the Wisteria Cache’s gold coins!

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in the painful phase of cutting ties with the Li Jian Clan, while their magic medicine refinement with the Alchemy Guild and the Jing Xiang Clan had just begun, they hadn’t even gotten their first profits yet.

The survivors were not just in the process of purchasing large amounts of dragon materials, but they were also spending money on buying alchemy components, assembling magic medicine production lines, and preparing a suitable location to build an alchemy factory.

Grab with both hands firmly.

The endless stream of cash stunned those who paid attention to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zi Di was investing much more money directly into the dragonman youngster.

Seizing the Dueling Godhead was consequential, thus, the mount potion manufacturing and other such matters were secondary.

As an outstanding merchant, Zi Di still pondered developing new cash flows. She prepared to take out some alchemy blueprints from War Merchant’s database.

The scope of this needed to be precisely controlled, otherwise, it would form a flaw that would provoke suspicion.

After all, arms merchants sold munitions as products, not their manufacturing method.
